# Current State

This document describes the current implementation state of the repository.

Its purpose is to prevent agents and contributors from assuming that the intended design is already implemented.

This file should be updated whenever a task materially changes what exists, what works, or what remains incomplete.

> **Naming note (post-DEC-024, 2026-04-28; code rename completed 2026-04-29):** the tab labelled **Hook Lab** in the Phase 6→9 / Hook Lab v1 entries below was renamed **Lab** going forward as part of Phase 10's planning checkpoint, and the code-level rename (`HookLabTab.tsx` → `LabTab.tsx`, `HookLabCodeView` → `LabCodeView`, `#/hook` URL hash → `#/lab` with one-shot back-compat redirect, `tab="hook"` chat id → `tab="lab"` with `_normalise_tab` back-compat alias) shipped in sub-step **10.6**. Historical entries in this file (Phase 6 polish — tab shell, Hook Lab 4.1 → 4.8 sub-bullets, etc.) retain the original "Hook Lab" naming as accurate proper nouns for the v1 milestone. New entries from Phase 10 sub-steps use **Lab**. See **DEC-024** for the full policy.

---

## Summary

Current status: **Phase 5 (exploit verification) complete. Phase 4 partial (error handling + docs cleanup) complete; CI remains parked.** Phases 1–3 delivered real extraction (apktool), real Ollama client, real prompts and skills catalog, evidence_ref validation, and run artifacts. Phase 5 added exploit verification on emulator + ADB. Phase 4 partial added broader error handling (config, LLM client, ADB skills, workflow, CLI) and updated all docs for Phase 5 / three-tier skills.

**Phase 6 (RE Workbench shell) — initial implementation complete:** `androscan/web/` FastAPI app (`create_app`), REST (`/api/projects`, `/api/projects/{app_id}/runs`, `/api/dossier/...`, `/api/findings/...`, `/api/input/tap`, `/api/health`), WebSockets (`/ws/mirror` PNG polling via `adb exec-out screencap -p`, `/ws/logcat`), CLI `--serve` / `--web-port` and `python androscan.py --apk … --serve` after analysis. React + Vite source in `androscan/web/frontend/`; production assets built to `androscan/web/static/` (`npm run build`, output gitignored).

**Phase 6 polish — tab shell (step 1 of 4 for Phases 6→9 UX):** Frontend refactored into `WorkbenchProvider` + 3 tabs (`Reports`, `Inspect`, `Hook Lab`) using `react-resizable-panels` (every left/centre/right pane horizontally resizable; sub-panes vertically resizable; sizes persisted per `autoSaveId`). Per-tab `ChatDock` at the bottom of the centre pane. URL hash routing (`#/reports` etc.).

**Phase 6 polish — Reports refresh + chat back-end (step 2 of 4):** New backend modules `androscan/web/triage.py` and `androscan/web/chat.py`; new endpoints `GET /api/triage/{app}/{run}`, `POST /api/triage/{app}/{run}/{finding_id}`, `POST /api/chat`. Triage stored at `apps/<app>/<run>/triage.json` (status: confirmed / false_positive / suppressed / needs_review; severity_override; note); the report itself is never mutated. Chat applies layered guardrails (per-tab system prompt allowlist, length caps, ANSI/zero-width/secret redaction, per-kind attachment budgets, `<context>` prompt-injection wrapping, in-process rate limit, transcripts at `apps/<app>/<run>/chat/<tab>.jsonl`); LLM client extended with `response_format=None` and a `messages` override so the chat path is prose, not JSON. Frontend Reports tab now renders findings as Markdown cards with per-card triage buttons + severity override + note editor; chat dock wired to `/api/chat` with dossier+finding+triage attachments and Markdown reply rendering. `Inspect` and `Hook Lab` chat docks remain on the same backend with empty attachment bundles (steps 3–4 will fill them). New deps: `react-markdown`, `remark-gfm`.

**Phase 6 polish — Lane-1 RAG indexer (step 3 of 4, partial):** New package `androscan/rag/` (`chunking.py`, `embed.py`, `index.py`, `search.py`) building a per-app SQLite vector index of method-level chunks over the jadx decompiled sources. Chunker is brace-balanced over `.java`/`.kt` (class headers + methods, blacklisted keywords, char budgets — no tree-sitter dependency). Embedding is pluggable via an `EmbedProvider` protocol with three providers: `fastembed` (default ONNX local), `ollama` (HTTP `/api/embeddings`), and a deterministic `hash` fallback for tests / no-deps environments. Vectors are stored as packed `float32` blobs in SQLite (WAL mode); top-k is brute-force cosine (numpy when present, pure-Python otherwise) — designed to swap to `sqlite-vec` later without changing call sites. New routes in `androscan/web/rag_routes.py` (`GET /api/rag/{app_id}/status`, `POST /api/rag/{app_id}/rebuild`, `POST /api/rag/{app_id}/query`); `decompile_post` schedules an async daemon-thread build via `schedule_rag_build_after_decompile` after jadx finishes. New `llm`-tier skill `search_decompiled_sources` (query, top_k, package_prefix, file_substr) advertised in the prompt catalog and fail-open. Inspect-tab chat is enriched in `androscan/web/chat.py` (`_enrich_inspect_with_rag`) by appending the top-k retrieved chunks as `code` attachments — fail-soft when RAG is unavailable. Config: new `rag.embed_provider` / `rag.embed_model` / `rag.top_k_default` in `global_config.yaml` and `Config`, with env overrides `ANDROSCAN_RAG_PROVIDER` / `ANDROSCAN_RAG_MODEL` / `ANDROSCAN_RAG_TOP_K`. New `pyproject` extra `[rag]` (`fastembed`, `numpy`). Tests: `tests/test_rag.py` (chunking, providers, build/query, invalidate) and `tests/test_rag_integration.py` (chat enrichment + skill end-to-end), plus three new RAG endpoint tests in `tests/test_web.py`.

**Phase 6 polish — Settings tab (step 3.5 of 4, complete):** Operators now have a fourth top-level tab (`#/settings`) that surfaces all configuration and live health in one place. Backend: new pure-function probe library `androscan/web/health_probes.py` (adb/jadx/apktool/frida version checks via subprocess; Ollama `/api/tags` HTTP probe; embed-provider availability check; disk-free + writability of `apps/`; per-app probes for `pm path`, foreground activity, `dumpsys package` UID, uiautomator dump non-empty, apk-sha drift vs. analysis-time SHA, RAG status, decompile cache freshness — every probe is async, timeboxed (default 2 s, 1 s for adb-shell, 1.5 s for HTTP), never raises, and returns a `{ok, label, ...}` dict). New per-app overrides store `androscan/web/per_app_settings.py` writes `apps/<app_id>/app_settings.json` (schema-versioned, atomic via tempfile + `os.replace`, never touches `app_meta.json`); `effective_settings(global_view, per_app)` produces the merged view used by routes/UI; `apk_overrides_summary` lists which keys are overridden. `androscan/config/loader.py` extended with `CONFIG_FIELD_MAP` (one entry per `Config` field → `(yaml_section, yaml_key, env_var)`), `LIVE_RELOADABLE_FIELDS` (booleans for "this can be set on `app.state.config` without a uvicorn restart"), `global_view_from_config`, `effective_sources` (returns `yaml`/`env`/`default` per field for the source pills), `with_overrides`, `coerce_yaml_value`, `dump_to_yaml` + `save_raw_yaml` + `restore_defaults_yaml` (atomic write-back), `validate_raw_yaml`, `read_raw_yaml`, `discover_config_path`. Two new routers: `androscan/web/status_routes.py` (`GET /api/status/global`, `GET /api/status/apps/{app_id}`; both run probes via `asyncio.gather` with a 3 s in-process cache; `invalidate_status_cache()` is called on every settings save/reset/reload) and `androscan/web/settings_routes.py` (`GET/PUT /api/settings/global`, `POST /api/settings/global/raw`, `POST /api/settings/global/reset`; `GET/PUT /api/settings/apps/{app_id}`, `POST /api/settings/apps/{app_id}/reset`; `POST /api/settings/reload`). `app.py` refactored to keep the live `Config` on `app.state.config` with `_current_config` / `_set_config` helpers; the new routers read via callable providers so `restart_required` is only true for fields outside `LIVE_RELOADABLE_FIELDS`. Frontend: new `SettingsTab` (`androscan/web/frontend/src/tabs/SettingsTab.tsx`) with a left-rail nav and four sub-panels — `GlobalSettingsPanel` (form view with source pills `yaml`/`env`/`default` + env-lock indicator + restart-required pill, **and** a raw-YAML editor with server-side validation + dirty state), `AppSettingsPanel` (per-app dropdown, override-or-inherit toggles, "Reset to defaults" with force-with-warning semantics), `StatusPanel` (status cards by category with `HealthDot` per card, "Refresh now" cache-bypass), `DiagnosticsPanel` (raw API JSON for global + per-app payloads, `POST /api/settings/reload`). New `HealthDot` in the global header polls `/api/status/global` every 30 s and rolls up to a single green/yellow/red dot that deep-links to Settings on click. Two new API clients (`api/settings.ts`, `api/status.ts`) and CSS in `App.css` (`.source-badge`, `.env-lock`, `.restart-pill`, `.status-card`, `.health-dot.{green,yellow,red}`, `.settings-nav`, `.yaml-editor`). New tests: `tests/test_health_probes.py` (subprocess + urllib mocks; 20 cases), `tests/test_per_app_settings.py` (load/save/reset, merge, partial updates, atomicity), `tests/test_settings_routes.py` (integration; monkeypatches the **consumer** module — `androscan.web.status_routes` — not `health_probes`, since probes are imported by name). All 250+ Python tests passing; `tsc --noEmit` and `vite build` clean.
See **DEC-020** (settings tab design) and **DEC-021** (probe shape + asyncio.gather + cache).
**Phase 6 polish — Inspect-tab click-to-code (step 3 of 4):** Backend: `POST /api/inspect/map` (`androscan/web/app.py` + `androscan/web/inspect_map.py`) maps a tap (x, y) on the mirrored screen to the most likely UI element + handler: `dumpsys activity top` for foreground activity, `uiautomator dump /dev/tty` for view hierarchy, smallest-area-clickable bounds match for the element, regex grep over the persistent decompile cache for handler candidates (`findViewById` > `onClick_near` > `compose_id` > `reference`). Persistent decompile cache backend in `androscan/web/decompile_cache.py` (`POST /api/decompile/{app_id}`, `GET /api/code/{app_id}/tree|file`) keyed by `sha256(apk)` under `apps/<app_id>/.decompiled/<sha>/sources/` with `index.json` + `tree.json` and a daemon-thread jadx job. Mirror online/offline probe at `GET /api/device/status` (`adb get-state`). Package-scoped logcat WS at `/ws/logcat?package=…` uses **stable Linux-UID filtering** (`adb logcat --uid=<uid>` resolved via `stat /data/data/<pkg>` or `dumpsys package`) so the stream survives app restarts; falls back to `--pid=` re-resolution on older devices. `POST /api/adb/shell` proxy with `shlex` parsing + denylist (`reboot|wipe|format|fastboot|remount|…`) + 20 s timeout + 200 KB output cap. New `llm`-tier skill **`resolve_ui_element`** (`androscan/skills/resolve_ui_element.py`) — pure-function deterministic fuser that takes the element + foreground activity + raw handler candidates, scores them (kind base + foreground-activity bonus + activity-named-file bonus + early-line decay), optionally enriches with Lane-1 RAG hits over decompiled sources, and returns `{best, alternatives, rag_hits, reasoning}`. The same `resolve()` helper is called inline by `/api/inspect/map` so the response now carries a `resolution` block (best class/method/file/line + reasons + alternatives) without an LLM round-trip. **Frontend** (`androscan/web/frontend/src/tabs/InspectTab.tsx`): four-column resizable layout — Projects sidebar | Classes-&-methods tree + scoped logcat | UI-mapping/Code-browser tabs + chat dock | Mirror + adb-shell. `MirrorView` renders PNG frames from `/ws/mirror`, polls `/api/device/status` for an online/offline status dot, translates clicks from image-pixel space to device-pixel space and forwards to `/api/inspect/map`. `ElementMappingPanel` shows a sub-activity progress bar while the map is in flight, then renders the fused `resolution.best` as a prominent banner (regex/RAG source badge, kind pill, score, reasons grid, snippet, "Open in Code browser" button), the deterministic candidates list with kind pills + per-row "Open" buttons, and collapsible alternatives + RAG-hits sections. The Code-browser tab uses a custom `CodeView` (line numbers, scroll-to-line, persistent line-range highlight, find query, gear prefs); a tap auto-opens `resolution.best.file` and scrolls to it (`onOpenInBrowser` flips `scrollTarget`/`highlightRange` even on re-clicks via `requestAnimationFrame`). `ScopedLogcat` and `AdbShell` collapse to slim panels. Chat attachments now include the fused `best_handler` block alongside the element + candidates so the LLM sees the picked file/line/reasons. Tests: `tests/test_inspect_map.py` (element pick, handler grep, adb glue), `tests/test_resolve_ui_element.py` (13 tests covering pure-function helpers, deterministic scoring, RAG enrichment, fail-soft, registry path), `tests/test_decompile_cache.py`, plus updated `tests/test_web.py` assertions for the new `resolution` block. Step 3 is now functionally complete; step 4 (Smali graph + Frida overlay + scope/hooks) untouched.

## What currently exists

### Implemented
- Repository layout per DESIGN_DOC: `androscan/`, `androscan/config/`, `androscan/internal/`, `androscan/internal/report/`, `androscan/skills/`, `androscan/extraction/`, `androscan/llm/`, `androscan/modules/`, `androscan/modules/exported_components/`, `tests/`.
- Python project: `pyproject.toml` with dependencies (requests, PyYAML, fastapi, uvicorn, websockets), dev deps (pytest, httpx).
- CLI: `androscan.py` at repo root with `--apk`, `--task` (multi-valued), `--output`, `--config` (path to global_config.yaml), `--serve`, `--web-port`, `--list-models`, `--model`, etc.
- Constants: `androscan.constants` — APP_ID_MAX_LEN, MAX_TURNS_DEFAULT, ISSUE_SEVERITY_LABELS (Critical/High/Medium/Low/Informational), EXPLOITABILITY_LABELS, SECTION_RULE, tool cmd defaults.
- Config: `androscan.config.load_config(config_path?)` — merge order: defaults → `global_config.yaml` (if present) → env. Config includes ollama_*, run_folder_root, max_turns, apktool_cmd, jadx_cmd, section_rule_*, **web_host**, **web_port**, **web_screencap_interval_ms**, etc. Env: ANDROSCAN_OLLAMA_URL, ANDROSCAN_OLLAMA_TIMEOUT, ANDROSCAN_RUN_FOLDER, ANDROSCAN_WEB_HOST, ANDROSCAN_WEB_PORT, ANDROSCAN_WEB_SCREENCAP_INTERVAL_MS.
- `global_config.yaml` at repo root (optional): YAML for ollama, **llamacpp** (LCP.4 / 2026-05-06 — `base_url` / `model` / `max_tokens` parallel to the Ollama section; commented-out by default with a recommended `llama-server` startup line), paths, workflow, output, **web**; settings affect CLI, workflow, run folder, extraction, decompilation, and RE Workbench bind address/port.
- Dossier model: `androscan.internal.dossier` (ApkInfo, Dossier, exported components, deep_links); `app_id_from_dossier()`, `Dossier.from_dict()`.
- Skills layer: `androscan.skills` — SkillMeta, SkillContext, SkillResult; registry with discover(), execute(), list_llm_skills(), run_skills(). Pipeline skills: extract_manifest, prepare_dossier (real via apktool), generate_report. LLM-requestable: get_decompiled_class (real via jadx, with availability check and cache keyed by resolved class name); get_decompiled_method (real), list_classes_in_package, **search_decompiled_sources** (RAG over jadx output via `androscan.rag`; fail-open), **resolve_ui_element** (deterministic fuser over click-to-code candidates + optional RAG enrichment; pure-function `resolve()` also reused inline by `/api/inspect/map`). Prompt builder uses list_llm_skills() for catalog; prompts aligned with DESIGN_DOC §7.
- Extraction: `androscan.extraction.extract_dossier(apk_path)` delegates to skills (extract_manifest → prepare_dossier) and returns Dossier; real manifest parsing via apktool (decode APK, parse AndroidManifest.xml).
- LLM: Real **multi-provider** HTTP client in `androscan.llm.client` — `complete()` is a 3-way dispatcher routing on `Config.provider_kind() -> Literal["local-ollama", "local-openai-compat", "cloud"]` (LCP.1 / 2026-05-06): `_complete_ollama()` (the original Ollama HTTP path; LCP.6 / 2026-05-06 added opportunistic `format: <json-schema>` mode for Ollama 0.5.0+ via `androscan.llm.grammar.build_response_json_schema()` with per-base-url 400-fallback cache so older daemons transparently fall back to `format: "json"`), `_complete_llamacpp()` (LCP.2 / 2026-05-06 — dedicated HTTP path via `requests` to `llama-server`'s OpenAI-compatible `/v1/chat/completions` endpoint at `http://127.0.0.1:8033/v1` by default per DEC-027 Q4; SSE streaming; `<think>...</think>` strip applied defensively to both local paths; JSON-mode parity via `response_format: {"type": "json_object"}` mirroring Ollama's `format: "json"`; LCP.6 / 2026-05-06 added opportunistic GBNF `grammar` field via `androscan.llm.grammar.build_response_gbnf()` ALONGSIDE the existing `response_format` so older `llama-server` builds transparently fall back to JSON-mode-only via per-base-url 400-fallback cache), and `_complete_cloud()` (the existing 6-vendor cloud path; cloud providers ignore the grammar feature flag because OpenAI-compat `response_format` enforces validity at the SDK layer). Provider registry lives in `LLM_PROVIDERS` (LCP.1 — top-level dict with `local` / `cloud` sub-sections; backwards-compat alias `CLOUD_PROVIDERS = LLM_PROVIDERS["cloud"]` keeps existing imports working); each entry carries a `kind` field for the dispatcher's reverse-lookup. `is_ollama_available()` (legacy availability check) preserved; `probe_llamacpp(base_url)` health probe added in LCP.3 (2026-05-06 — mutual-exclusion routing in `_gather_global` keyed on `provider_kind()` so only one local-LLM probe runs per request). New `androscan/llm/grammar.py` module (LCP.6 / 2026-05-06): `active_skill_names()` registry-driven discovery of LLM-tier skill names (sorted, deterministic), `build_response_json_schema(skill_names)` for Ollama 0.5.0+ `format: <schema>` mode, `build_response_gbnf(skill_names)` for `llama-server` `grammar` field (hand-rolled GBNF on top of the standard JSON-value prelude lifted from `llama.cpp/grammars/json.gbnf`), `is_grammar_enabled(config)` feature-flag helper. Top-level enum constraint: `skill_requests[*].skill` is the discriminated-union enum over the 9 LLM-tier skills; permissive `params` (per-skill `params_schema` is operator-prose, not typed JSON Schema) + permissive `hypotheses[*]` shape (matches the parser's fail-soft default-fill posture). New `Config.local_grammar_enabled: bool = True` knob (LCP.6 / 2026-05-06 — YAML `llm.local_grammar_enabled`, env `ANDROSCAN_LOCAL_GRAMMAR_ENABLED`, live-reloadable; rendered under both "Local (Ollama)" + "Local (llama.cpp)" subsections of the Settings UI; not shown under "Cloud"). `build_prompt(dossier, prior_results?, llm_skills?)`, `parse_response()` for skill_requests/hypotheses. Tests use mock so CI does not require live Ollama or live llama-server. **`ISSUE-016` (JSON-validity drift on aggressive quants Q4_K_M / IQ4_XS) → Resolved (LCP.6)** in `docs/KNOWN_ISSUES.md` — grammar enforcement at the model's logits-sampling level closes the gap; off-vocab skill names + off-range integer fields are impossible at the sampling level. Operators on older Ollama (< 0.5.0) or `llama-server` builds without `grammar:` field support transparently fall back to `response_format`-only via the per-base-url 400-fallback cache (no operator action required).
- Run folder: `androscan.internal.run_folder.create_run_folder(app_id)` creates `apps/<app_id>/<run_ts>/` with human-readable timestamp; `run_folder_display_path()` returns display string. Run artifacts: report.json, run_meta.json, run.log per run; observations.json at app_id level.
- Workflow: `run_workflow(apk_path, tasks, run_folder, config?)` — pipeline skills (extract_manifest, prepare_dossier) → multi-turn LLM with skill catalog → consolidation (per-component mode) → evidence_ref validation → exploit verification → generate_report. evidence_ref validation: hypotheses with invalid evidence_refs are dropped before report. Supports per-component analysis mode (`config.per_component_analysis`).
- Skill results cache: in-memory and disk cache (apps/<app_id>/skill_results_cache.json); get_decompiled_class cache key uses resolved class name for per-component analysis.
- Exploit verification: `androscan.internal.exploit_verification.run_exploit_verification()` orchestrates per-hypothesis exploit steps. Exploit-tier skills: app_env_check (device/emulator/app verification), build_exploit_command (template catalog for 5 component profiles), capture_signals (volatile then non-volatile; signal types from vuln_module_skills_signals.json), run_exploit_command (adb shell), verify_exploit_result (LLM-based). Artifacts written under `apps/<app_id>/<run_ts>/exploit_verification/<vuln_module>/<hyp_id>/` (before/after screenshots, logcat.txt, dumpsys.txt, window_stack.txt, result.json). Report generated after verification with verified flag, reasoning, and artifact refs per hypothesis.
- Vuln–skill–signal profile: `androscan/internal/vuln_module_skills_signals.json` — modules, profiles (exported_activity, exported_service, exported_receiver, exported_provider, deep_link), signal_type_metadata with volatile/stub flags. Drives which signals each profile captures and which exploit template is used.
- RE Workbench (Phase 6): `androscan.web` — `create_app(config)`, `run_web_server(config)` (uvicorn). Serves JSON + WebSockets; React UI after `npm run build` in `androscan/web/frontend`. Frontend uses `react-resizable-panels`, `react-markdown`+`remark-gfm` for finding/chat rendering, per-tab `ChatDock` wired to `POST /api/chat` (with guardrails in `androscan/web/chat.py`); per-finding triage via `androscan/web/triage.py` (`/api/triage/{app}/{run}` GET; `/api/triage/{app}/{run}/{finding_id}` POST; stored at `apps/<app>/<run>/triage.json`). Lane-1 RAG endpoints in `androscan/web/rag_routes.py` (`GET /api/rag/{app_id}/status`, `POST /api/rag/{app_id}/rebuild`, `POST /api/rag/{app_id}/query`); decompile completion auto-schedules a background RAG build (`schedule_rag_build_after_decompile`). Inspect-tab backend endpoints: `POST /api/decompile/{app_id}` (lazy persistent jadx cache), `GET /api/code/{app_id}/{tree,file}` (Monaco source viewer), `POST /api/inspect/map` (click-to-code with fused `resolution` block from `resolve_ui_element`), `GET /api/device/status` (mirror online/offline), `POST /api/adb/shell` (sandboxed adb shell), package-scoped `/ws/logcat?package=…` with stable Linux-UID filtering, `GET /api/llm/info` for chat dock tooltips.
- Settings tab (Phase 6 step 3.5): `androscan/web/health_probes.py` (timeboxed pure-function probes — adb/jadx/apktool/frida (host CLI)/**frida-server (device)**/**frida version-skew**/Ollama/embed-provider/disk + per-app pm-path/foreground/UID/uiautomator/apk-sha-drift/RAG/**call-graph**/decompile-cache); `androscan/web/per_app_settings.py` (atomic per-app overrides at `apps/<app_id>/app_settings.json`, schema-versioned); `androscan/config/loader.py` extended with `CONFIG_FIELD_MAP`, `LIVE_RELOADABLE_FIELDS`, `global_view_from_config`, `effective_sources`, `with_overrides`, `dump_to_yaml`, `save_raw_yaml`, `restore_defaults_yaml`, `validate_raw_yaml`; routers `androscan/web/status_routes.py` (`/api/status/global`, `/api/status/apps/{app_id}`; `asyncio.gather` + 3 s cache) and `androscan/web/settings_routes.py` (`GET/PUT /api/settings/global`, raw-YAML save, `POST /api/settings/global/reset`, per-app GET/PUT/reset, `POST /api/settings/reload`); `app.py` keeps the live `Config` on `app.state.config` (`_current_config` / `_set_config`) so live-reloadable fields take effect without restarting uvicorn (`restart_required` returned to UI for the rest). Frontend: `SettingsTab` + sub-panels (`GlobalSettingsPanel`, `AppSettingsPanel`, `StatusPanel`, `DiagnosticsPanel`), `HealthDot` in the header (30 s polling, deep-links to Settings), `api/settings.ts`, `api/status.ts`. Tests: `tests/test_health_probes.py`, `tests/test_per_app_settings.py`, `tests/test_settings_routes.py`. See DEC-020 + DEC-021.
- Lane-1 RAG (Phase 6 step 3): `androscan/rag/` — `chunking.py` (brace-balanced method/class-header chunker for `.java`/`.kt`, no tree-sitter), `embed.py` (`EmbedProvider` protocol + `FastEmbedProvider` / `OllamaEmbedProvider` / `HashProvider` fallback), `index.py` (per-app SQLite at `apps/<app_id>/.decompiled/<sha>/rag.sqlite`, WAL, packed `float32` blobs, async daemon-thread builds with in-process locking), `search.py` (brute-force cosine top-k; numpy-accelerated when present, pure-Python fallback). Inspect-tab chat enriches user turns with the top-k retrieved chunks as `code` attachments before guardrails run. Configurable via `rag.*` keys in `global_config.yaml` and `ANDROSCAN_RAG_*` env vars. Optional deps under extras `[rag]` (fastembed, numpy); `hash` provider always works without extras.
- **Behavior Trace v3 — Dynamic Execution + Flowchart UI** (Phase 13 v1; complete 2026-05-08 per DEC-029): three coherent deliverables on top of the unchanged Phase 11 v2 / v2.1 static substrate. **(1) UI overhaul.** New `<ExecutionFlow>` flowchart (`androscan/web/frontend/src/components/trace/ExecutionFlow.tsx` + pure-helper `executionFlowGraph.ts`) renders the active anchor's call closure as a React Flow / `@xyflow/react`@^12.10.2 directed graph with custom `MethodNode` (220×72 / 6px-radius / corner pills GATE/ENTRY/ALLOW/DENY/NEUTRAL / source-line + overload-count + possibly-inlined chips / dashed border for inlined / stacked-card visual for overloads) + custom `VerdictEdge` (1.5px stroke + 6×6 arrowhead + verdict-flavored color + inline edge labels via `EdgeLabelRenderer`); `<Inspector>` right-side fixed-360px panel (`Inspector.tsx`) opens on node click with five operator-actionable sections (signature + summary + source line + predicate origin + bypass plans) + sticky action-row footer (`[Hook this method]` / `[Trace this gate]` / `[Open source]` / `[Discuss in chat]`); the legacy linear `DecisionTimeline.tsx` was renamed to `BehaviorTrace.tsx` at 13.5 (one-release migration window) and DELETED at 13.10 (this commit); pan/zoom/minimap stay enabled (re-skinned to workbench dark palette). **(2) Dynamic execution trace.** New `behavior_trace_multi` Frida template (`androscan/web/frida/templates/behavior_trace_multi.py`) hooks every method in the active `BehaviorAnchor`'s closure with 5 phases (`hook_failed` / `ready` / `entry` / `exit` / `error`), 11-tier arg/return stringification (primitives → strings → numeric arrays → byte arrays → enums → JSON-encodable objects → `toString()` → `Object.toString` fallback → `[object class]` envelope → `<unstringifiable>` final fallback), per-thread call-stack tracking (tracer-side `Map<threadId, Frame[]>` + monotonic global `seqCounter` so every `entry` event carries `parent_call_seq` for stitching). New routes `POST /api/trace/{app_id}/dynamic` + `WS /ws/trace/{app_id}/{session_id}` + `DELETE /api/trace/{app_id}/dynamic/{session_id}` in `androscan/web/trace_routes.py` + helper module `androscan/web/trace_dynamic.py` (closure extraction with `BEHAVIOR_TRACE_MULTI_HOOK_CAP = 30` cap inheriting §12.6's `hook_target_package_prefix` allowlist). JSONL persistence at `apps/<app_id>/<run>/dynamic_trace.jsonl` per DEC-029 lock-in; `trace.sqlite` (the §12.7 anchor cache) is NOT touched by dynamic runs (corruption isolation + drop-the-file invalidation). Frontend hook `useDynamicTrace(appId)` (`androscan/web/frontend/src/api/trace.ts`) owns the start/stream/stop lifecycle as a state machine with `firedMethods: ReadonlySet<overloadKey>` + `liveValues: ReadonlyMap<overloadKey, LiveValueRecord>` + `summaries: ReadonlyMap<overloadKey, SummaryState>` + `hookFailed: ReadonlyMap<overloadKey, HookFailureRecord>`. `<TraceModeToggle>` three-pill row (Static / Dynamic / Both) with `localStorage`-backed per-app persistence; auto-default flip from Static → Both on first event UNLESS operator manually picked. `<DynamicTraceRunControl>` Run/Stop button with threshold-color coding (≤20 green / 21-50 yellow / 51-100 orange / >100 red per DEC-029 lock-in) via `thresholdBand(hookCount)` + `thresholdTooltip(hookCount)` pure helpers + `closureMethodCount(anchor)` mirroring BE `extract_closure_methods`'s five-source flatten. Runtime-confirmed `inlined?` wiring upgrades the cool-gray static heuristic pill to warn-orange `inlined (runtime)` when Frida fails to attach the hook. **(3) Per-method LLM summaries.** New `summarise_method` LLM skill (`androscan/skills/summarise_method.py`; `tier="llm"`, `requires_confirmation=False` per DEC-022's confirmation taxonomy — read-only over decompile + write-only to its own cache slot, DO NOT REGRESS the invariant; params schema = `{class_smali, method_name, descriptor, app_id?}`). Decompiled-source enrichment via `read_source_file` + the existing `_extract_method_bodies` brace-matcher; prompt-budget truncation at `SOURCE_BODY_BUDGET_BYTES=4096`; cache lookup BEFORE source read for fast warm-cache hits; fail-open on every unavailability mode. Cached in `skill_results_cache.json` keyed under `skill_id="summarise_method"` against `(app_sha, class_smali, method_name, descriptor)` — per-app + per-content-hash slots prevent cross-APK pollution. WebSocket multiplex (`androscan/web/trace_summary.py`) emits three new event kinds on top of Frida frames: `summary_pending` (cache-miss + first method-fire), `summary_ready` (LLM returned; carries `cached: bool`), `summary_failed` (`error: "summary_timeout"` / `"empty_summary"` / `<exception_message>`); per-method dedup so 50× re-fire = 1 LLM call. `<MethodSummaryWidget>` chat card (`androscan/web/frontend/src/components/chat/widgets/MethodSummaryWidget.tsx`) is the second consumer of the `SkillResult.widgets` channel after Phase 11 v2.1.5's `<TraceEntryCandidateWidget>` — frozen `MethodSummaryWidget` dataclass added to `androscan/skills/base.py` with `kind: Literal["method_summary"]` and six fields (`class_smali` / `class_name` / `method_name` / `descriptor` / `summary` / `cached`); skill emits widget on both fresh-LLM and cache-hit paths. Inspector "Discuss in chat" button writes `pendingChatPrefill` with a method-specific prompt template that pre-arms the agentic loop with the four `summarise_method` skill params for deeper conversation. Bundle deltas (Phase 13 v1 cumulative): CSS 95.29 → 127.16 KB / +31.87 KB; JS 1280 → 1455.67 KB / +175.67 KB (~150 KB of which is `@xyflow/react`). **Phase 13 v2.0 UX iteration (complete 2026-05-19 per DEC-030; this commit; refines v1, does not supersede):** four operator-visible flowchart fixes + the consequent hover-progressive gate-count badge land on top of the v1 substrate. **Sink reshape (Q1 + Q2):** new module-level constants `GLOBAL_ALLOW_SINK_ID` + `GLOBAL_DENY_SINK_ID` exported from `executionFlowGraph.ts` coalesce all allow / deny verdict edges to one node per kind per anchor (operator-confidence-high cases collapse cleanly); per-source `sink_neutral` nodes stay (operator-required false-negative spotting anchor) but render small de-emphasized (110×36, dashed border, 60% opacity, lowercase "neutral" body, no corner pill) via `.execution-flow-node-sink-small` CSS class; each per-source neutral sink carries a `feedingGates: Array<{ sourceTitle, instructionIndex, verdictKind }>` accumulated at graph-build time; renderer composes a multi-line browser-default `title` attribute listing the feeding gates so hovering reveals which decisions converge into the sink (zero new dependencies — uses native browser tooltip). **Gate-count badge (Q1 hover-progressive disclosure):** new `ExecutionFlowNode.totalGates` + `unclassifiedGates` fields populated by new `gateCountsFor(decisions, key)` helper; per-method-card badge `${totalGates}g · ${unclassifiedGates}?` rendered always when `totalGates > 0` in the meta row alongside source-line + overload-count + inlined pills; CSS-hidden by default (`opacity: 0; visibility: hidden`) and revealed instantly (80ms transition) on `.execution-flow-node:hover` OR `:focus-within` for keyboard navigation discoverability; `-warn` modifier raises emphasis (warn-orange border + text, matches pre-existing `.execution-flow-node-inlined-pill` family) when `unclassifiedGates > 0` — operator-spottable signal for potential `branch_classifier` false-negatives. **Arrowhead fix (Q3):** `App.css:6856` `fill: currentColor` → `fill: context-stroke` (SVG2 keyword that correctly inherits the stroke colour of the referencing path; v1's `currentColor` on shared SVG `<marker>` resolved against the marker's own context not the referencing path's stroke); supported in Chrome 99+ / Firefox 90+ / Safari 16+. **Within-column entry-first sort (Q5):** `ExecutionFlow.tsx::layoutNodes` sort comparator gains `kind === "entry"` short-circuit at the top of the chain so the entry node always renders first within its rank column (preserves v1 synthetics-last + alphabetic-between rules); fixes the v1 "boxes above Entry method" complaint at rank 0. **Within-page fullscreen toggle (Q7):** new `useState<boolean>` `isFullscreen` + `useEffect` ESC keydown handler (scoped to the fullscreen state, cleans up on toggle-off + unmount) + `<Panel position="top-right">` mount-point with the toggle button (label flips on toggle: "Fullscreen" / "Exit fullscreen" + `-active` class indicator + `aria-pressed` accessibility); CSS class `.execution-flow-container-fullscreen` flips the container to `position: fixed; inset: 0; z-index: 1000;` (same layer as chat dock + notification toasts) so the flowchart fills the viewport without going OS-level fullscreen (operator-locked posture: within-page only). v2.0 is a pure FE change — backend untouched, no schema changes, no new endpoints, no new dependencies (pre-existing `@xyflow/react@^12.10.2` already exports `<Panel>` natively). DEC-029's three load-bearing v1 locks (color-only edge emphasis, three-mode Static/Dynamic/Both toggle, threshold-color hook-count ladder ≤20/21-50/51-100/>100) stay UNTOUCHED. Bundle deltas v2.0 alone: CSS 127.16 → 128.99 KB / +1.83 KB raw, gzipped 20.39 → 20.71 KB / +0.32 KB; JS 1455.67 → 1457.85 KB / +2.18 KB raw, gzipped 454.00 → 454.71 KB / +0.71 KB. **v2.0-tests** (FE test infrastructure + retroactive unit tests for `executionFlowGraph.ts`) is the committed follow-up next slot; **v2.1** (call-graph caller→callee edge injection for true call-hierarchy indentation) is operator-demand-gated on dogfood evidence (ISSUE-022).

### Partially implemented
- Modules: `exported_components` workflow is wired; task dispatch supports multiple --task values; first module is the only one implemented.
- **Workbench chat is single-pass.** `androscan/web/chat.py` does **not** loop on `skill_requests` like `androscan/internal/workflow.py` does, so the LLM cannot dig deeper mid-turn — it only sees what `_enrich_inspect_with_rag` (`_INSPECT_RAG_TOP_K = 4`) attached up-front. Symptom: questions that hinge on a specific method-level chunk that didn't make the top-4 (e.g. "what is this DB's name?" when the constructor chunk wasn't retrieved) get hedged answers like "decompile and look in the constructor / `onCreate`." Planned fix: agentic skill loop with consent-class hook, see **DEC-022** + the corresponding TASKS.md backlog entry; quick band-aid (independent of DEC-022): bump `_INSPECT_RAG_TOP_K` to 8–10. Tracked as **ISSUE-009**.

    - **4.4 Hook template library — DONE** (2026-04-27): New `androscan/adapters/frida_hooks/` package — five v1 templates (`entry_exit_log`, `ssl_pinning_bypass`, `crypto`, `shared_preferences`, `intent`) backed by a single tiny dataclass + parametrised renderer in `__init__.py`. `HookTemplate` bundles `(id, name, description, params: tuple[HookTemplateParam, ...], js_template, pentester_summary_template, sensitive_apis)`; `HookTemplateParam` is a flat `(name, description, required, default)` so v1 schemas are stringly-typed and the LLM-tier `generate_frida_hook` skill (4.7) gets the simplest possible parameter contract. `render(template, params) -> RenderedHook(template_id, js, summary, params_used)` validates against the schema in three explicit stages — reject unknown keys, reject missing/empty required, fill defaults — and then `str.format`s **the same merged params dict** through both the JS body and the pentester summary. `render_by_id` / `get_template` / `list_templates` / `register` / `discover` / `extract_format_fields` round out the public API; explicit `_TEMPLATE_MODULES` tuple drives discovery (mirrors `androscan.skills`'s pattern — auto-globbing was rejected because a stray scratch file would silently become a registered template). Errors live in a thin `HookTemplateError` taxonomy: `HookTemplateNotFound` (unknown id; the message lists the valid ids alongside) and `HookParamError` (one class for missing-required / empty-required / unknown-key — the operator wants one `except` block plus a human-readable reason, not three). Each shipped template's JS body uses `{{` / `}}` to escape literal JS braces; the documented contract is enforced indirectly by the registry-walk render test (any unescaped brace surfaces as a `KeyError` during `str.format`). New `tests/test_frida_hook_templates.py` (**67 tests**): `TestRenderer` (16 cases — happy path / required missing / empty / None / unknown / coerce-to-str / `{{` escape / `render_by_id` round-trip + unknown / `get_template` unknown / `register` collision / `list_templates` sorted / `extract_format_fields` named-only + skip-positional + strip-attribute), `TestRegistryFailClosed` (10 invariants × 5 modules = the contract DEC-023 promised: every module exports `TEMPLATE: HookTemplate`, id matches module basename, JS + summary non-empty, every `str.format` placeholder ⊆ declared params, every required param appears in JS *or* summary, every template renders cleanly with stub inputs), `TestTemplates` (per-template smoke renders verifying class/method/label substitution + summary mentions the right pentest concept — auth tokens for SharedPreferences, MITM/Burp for SSL pinning, plaintext-not-captured for crypto, deep-link/extras for intent). Full suite: **458 / 458 green** (was 391; +67 tests). `tsc --noEmit` + `vite build` clean — **headless by design** (no UI / no HTTP routes; 4.5 owns Stage→Inject + WS + JSONL persistence; 4.7 owns `generate_frida_hook` LLM skill). The renderer + `RenderedHook` shape is now the locked contract those layers will consume.
    - **4.8 Frida overlay on call graph + Hook Lab v1 docs sweep — DONE** (2026-04-27): The final Hook Lab v1 sub-step lights up the live Cytoscape overlay and finalises the docs sweep. **Frontend.** `androscan/web/frontend/src/components/CallGraphView.tsx` accepts a new optional `hitsByMethod: ReadonlyMap<string, number> | null` prop and exports a stable `hitKey(className, methodName)` join helper so producer + consumer share one canonical key. `null` = no active session pinned → graph renders unchanged in 4.2 styling; a `Map` (possibly empty) = overlay active → method nodes whose `(class.class_name, method_name)` matches a key render in **bold cyan** per DEC-023 with a `×N` hit-count suffix on the label and an extra tooltip line `frida: N hit(s)`, while non-matching nodes dim to opacity 0.45 so the eye is drawn to the hits first. Package-overview mode aggregates per-package totals — packages containing one or more fired methods get the same bold-cyan treatment plus a footer line `• N hits in M methods` in the node label; zero-hit packages dim to opacity 0.5. Reflection / focus-root styling still applies — `.hit` and `.has-hits` are declared **after** the reflective / focusroot rules so the bold-cyan border wins where both overlap (operators care about "did it fire?" more than "is it reflective?" the moment a hook lands). New `HIT_CYAN = "#56d4dd"` colour token shared between the Cytoscape stylesheet and the tippy tooltip HTML so the overlay reads as one visual unit. `androscan/web/frontend/src/tabs/HookLabTab.tsx` builds `hitsByMethod` via `useMemo` from the same `chatHooks` it already polls every 2.5 s for the chat `frida_summary` attachment — no new poll cadence, no new fetch endpoint — so the overlay refreshes cohesively with the right-pane HookStatsPanel / ScopeInspectorPanel and the chat-attachment payload. While `chatHooks` is still loading (`null`), the overlay deliberately turns on with an empty `Map` rather than `null` so the graph renders dimmed and hits flow in on the next 2.5 s tick — matches the rest of the right pane (HookStatsPanel / FridaTracePanel both surface "session active, no events yet" affordances rather than hiding their UI). **Tests.** No new tests in 4.8 — pure-frontend visual layering. `DECISIONS.md` DEC-023 gets a "Frida overlay (sub-step 4.8 specifics)" sub-bullet documenting the `hitsByMethod` contract + the polling-cadence-reuse rationale + the per-overload precision caveat, plus a "Hook Lab v1 complete" closing note; `KNOWN_ISSUES.md` LIMIT-002 marked Resolved (Hook Lab v1 has landed) and ISSUE-012 added documenting the per-overload precision caveat (hooks attribute by `(class, method)` not full descriptor — overloaded methods undercount on the overlay); `SAFETY_AND_SECURITY.md` §12.6 augmented with a new "v1 complete: what now-shipped controls do" subsection consolidating the operator-managed `frida-server` posture (unchanged from 4.3), the now-shipped per-app `hook_target_package_prefix` server-side allowlist (promoted from 4.3's "intentionally not added" to 4.5's "enforced with **403 hook_blocked**"), and the live trace-persistence path at `apps/<app_id>/<run_ts>/frida/<session>.jsonl` which inherits the surrounding run-folder permission posture. **What ships in Hook Lab v1 overall (4.1 → 4.8):** Smali static call-graph SQLite + REST routes (4.1) → Cytoscape pane with package overview / focus subgraph + click-to-source (4.2) → headless Frida adapter with ring buffer + readiness probes (4.3) → six-template hook library with locked renderer contract (4.4 + 4.6 added `scope_inspector`) → Stage→Inject UI with Monaco + `pyjsparser` pre-validation + WS trace + JSONL persistence + per-app `hook_target_package_prefix` allowlist (4.5) → hooks-stats + scope-inspector right-pane panels (4.6) → `query_call_graph` + `generate_frida_hook` LLM-tier skills + chat attachments + DEC-022's `requires_confirmation` consent class (4.7) → live Frida hits coloured onto the call graph (4.8). **What v1 explicitly does *not* ship:** modify-return / mutation hooks (v2), self-hosted Monaco (ISSUE-010 v2 sweep), `frida-server` auto-provisioning (operator-managed by design), DEC-022's bounded chat agentic loop wiring `query_call_graph` / `generate_frida_hook` into the workbench chat (`androscan/web/chat.py` is still single-pass per ISSUE-009 — `requires_confirmation` is structurally ready but the chat side of the loop is the next layer's job).
    - **4.7 LLM-tier skills (`query_call_graph`, `generate_frida_hook`) — DONE** (2026-04-27): Two new LLM-tier skills + the consent-class plumbing the chat agentic loop (DEC-022) needs to gate side-effecting calls. **Backend.** `androscan/skills/base.py` extended: `SkillMeta` gained `requires_confirmation: bool = False` (default False keeps every existing read-only skill — decompile / RAG / fuser — unchanged; future device-mutating skills opt in by setting it True). New `androscan/skills/query_call_graph.py` — read-only LLM-tier skill (`requires_confirmation=False`) wrapping `androscan.analysis.call_graph.list_graph` / `neighbors` / `paths`. Three modes (`overview` / `neighbors` / `paths`) with parameter clamping (`limit ≤ 5000`, `max_hops ≤ 12`, `max_paths ≤ 50`). Resolves `app_id` from the explicit param OR `run_folder.parent` (same fallback ladder as `resolve_ui_element`). Fail-open on missing app context, unready decompile cache, or unbuilt call graph — returns `success=True` with empty data and clear `text` so the LLM can read the empty result and pivot, mirroring `search_decompiled_sources`. Each mode ships a deterministic human-readable summary (top-10 nodes for overview, callers/callees with edge kinds for neighbors, path id-lists for paths). New `androscan/skills/generate_frida_hook.py` — first real consumer of DEC-022's consent-class hook (`requires_confirmation=True`). Headless renderer that takes `template_id` + `params` + optional `rationale` and wraps `frida_hooks.render_by_id`; converts `HookTemplateNotFound` / `HookParamError` into clean `SkillResult` failures whose `text` includes the valid-template-id list and the declared schema (`name=…?` for optional params with defaults) so the LLM can self-correct on the next turn. On success returns `{template_id, js, summary, params_used, sensitive_apis, rationale}` plus a human-readable `text` containing a JS preview (first 8 lines / 600 chars), the deterministic pentester summary, the sensitive-APIs list, and an "Operator action required: review the JS + summary above, then stage / inject from the Hook Lab UI. This skill does not attach to a process or inject the script." footer (DEC-023's Option-A confirmation UX — the skill is purely prep). Both new skills registered in `androscan/skills/__init__._SKILL_MODULES`. **Frontend.** `androscan/web/frontend/src/tabs/HookLabTab.tsx` extended: in addition to the existing 4-column layout, a new `useEffect` polls `/api/frida/sessions/{id}/hooks` + `/api/frida/sessions/{id}/events?limit=30` every 2.5 s when an active session is pinned (cancellation-safe; clears on session swap / detach), and `useMemo`-builds a `ChatAttachment[]` with three new attachments: `default` for the selected method header (class / method / java_rel_path / smali_id), `code` for the decompiled Java source (capped client-side at 6_000 chars to mirror backend `ATTACHMENT_BUDGETS["code"]` — operator's "show context" preview matches what the model sees post-truncation), and `frida_summary` for a JSON document combining the active session's hooks aggregate (per-(class, method) hits + top return values + last_seen_ts) and the last-30 trace-event tail (ts / kind / payload). `HookLabCodeView` lifted its `source` state up via a new `onSourceLoaded?: (source: string | null) => void` callback so the parent has the full text without re-fetching. `buildHookChatContextSummary` rewritten to enumerate exactly what's being sent (selected method, code-attachment cap, hook-row count, event-tail count, refresh cadence) — operators see the actual payload composition, not a stale boilerplate. **Tests.** **+32 backend tests**: `tests/test_skills.py` (+4 — `requires_confirmation` field default for existing read-only skills, LLM-catalog inclusion of both new skills, invariant that pipeline + exploit-tier skills never accidentally ship `requires_confirmation=True`); new `tests/test_query_call_graph_skill.py` (+16 — registration / not-in-exploit-catalog, mode validation, missing-mode / unknown-mode / missing `node_ref` / missing `source` + `target`, fail-open on bare run folder + missing decompile cache + unbuilt call graph + unknown explicit `app_id`, `overview` round-trip with package-prefix filter and limit clamping, `neighbors` round-trip + unknown-node empty payload, `paths` round-trip + max_hops/max_paths clamping, explicit `app_id` resolves through apps root); new `tests/test_generate_frida_hook_skill.py` (+12 — registration / `requires_confirmation=True` invariant + first-consent-class-skill check, missing / blank / unknown `template_id`, params-must-be-dict, schema-aware errors on missing-required + unknown-key with declared-schema readout, end-to-end render against `entry_exit_log` asserting `js` / `summary` / `params_used` / `sensitive_apis` / `rationale` and the "Operator action required" notice, `RenderedHook` JSON-serialisable invariant via `json.dumps(r.data)`, optional `rationale` not echoed when omitted). Full suite: **566 / 566 green** (was 534; +32 tests). `tsc --noEmit` + `vite build` clean (1.14 MB / 357 KB gzipped — unchanged from 4.6). DEC-022 gets a **"4.7 activation"** note (consent-class hook now has its first real consumer in `generate_frida_hook`); DEC-023 gets a **"LLM-tier skills (sub-step 4.7 specifics)"** sub-bullet documenting the fail-open contract for `query_call_graph`, the `generate_frida_hook` no-injection invariant, the chat-attachment payload composition, and the `requires_confirmation` flag's role in the future agentic loop. **What 4.7 explicitly does *not* ship:** wiring these two skills into `androscan/web/chat.py`'s agentic loop — that's still single-pass (ISSUE-009) and the multi-turn `skill_requests` plumbing is DEC-022's job (sub-step 4.8 / Phase 7+). Cytoscape graph overlay highlighting hit nodes is also still 4.8.
    - **4.6 Scope inspector + hooks/stats panel — DONE** (2026-04-27): Sixth v1 hook template + two introspection routes + two new right-pane panels behind a tab strip; everything is read-only by design (modify-return is still v2). **Backend.** New `androscan/adapters/frida_hooks/scope_inspector.py` — same 3-param shape as `entry_exit_log` so the call-graph node prefill flow keeps working, but the hook walks `this.getClass().getDeclaredFields()` per call (with `Field.setAccessible(true)` and per-field try/catch so a single getter that throws never voids the whole snapshot) and emits two new payload variants the aggregator pattern-matches on: `{phase:"entry", class, method, args, this_class, this_fields:{f: String(v), ...}}` + `{phase:"exit", class, method, return, this_fields:{...}}`. Field-level read failures land inline as `"<unreadable: …>"` rather than aborting; the JS body declares `Java.use` / `Java.perform` / `Class.getDeclaredFields` / `Field.setAccessible` in `sensitive_apis` for operator audit. Two new pure helpers in `androscan/web/frida_routes.py`: `_summarize_hooks(session)` iterates `session.events()` once, groups by `(payload.class, payload.method)`, counts `phase=="entry"` events as hits, tallies `phase=="exit"` returns into `top_returns: [{value, count}]` capped at 5 with stable insertion-order tiebreak, sorts rows by hits desc → last_seen desc → name (returns are capped at 256 chars so a 200 KB stringified buffer can't blow up the response); `_summarize_scope(session)` additionally requires `payload.this_fields` to be a dict (the discriminator that makes scope_inspector events distinct from entry_exit_log's), and keeps the most-recent entry + most-recent exit *independently* per `(class, method)` so a method that's entered-but-not-yet-returned still shows useful entry data. Two new routes wired through `build_frida_router(...)`: `GET /api/frida/sessions/{id}/hooks` returns `{session_id, hooks: [...]}`; `GET /api/frida/sessions/{id}/scope` returns `{session_id, snapshots: [...]}`. Both 404 on unknown session, both work *without* the `[frida]` extra installed (they only read the in-memory ring — no Frida I/O). **Frontend.** `androscan/web/frontend/src/api/frida.ts` extended with `HookStat` / `ScopeSnapshot{Entry,Exit}` types and `getSessionHooks` / `getSessionScope` helpers (no new deps — same `_read` envelope as existing routes). New `HookStatsPanel.tsx` polls `/hooks` every 2.5 s and renders one row per `(class, method)` with hits / template id badge / last-seen relative time / top-3 return values; gracefully handles 404-on-detach as the empty state. New `ScopeInspectorPanel.tsx` polls `/scope` every 2.5 s and renders one collapsible `<details>` card per `(class, method)`; computes the entry-vs-exit `this_fields` diff client-side, highlights the changed keys with a tinted background, surfaces a `N mutated` pill on the card header, and renders args + this_class + return value side-by-side under entry/exit subheaders (no Monaco — the data is shallow JSON, deliberately keeping the panel air-gap-friendly while ISSUE-010 stays open). `HookLabTab.tsx`'s right-pane bottom slot is now a 3-button tab strip — `Trace | Hooks | Scope` — defaulting to Trace so existing operators see no behaviour change; Hooks + Scope are disabled with a tooltip when no session is active. `App.css` gains ~360 lines under `.right-pane-tabs-*` / `.hookstats-*` / `.scope-*` namespaces; the `scope-field-changed` highlight uses `color-mix(in srgb, var(--accent) 15%, transparent)` so it tracks the theme's accent. **Polling rationale (DEC-023 sub-bullet):** `/hooks` and `/scope` are pure aggregations over the *same* ring buffer the trace WS consumes; a parallel WS would be redundant data and the 2.5 s lag is invisible to a human eye scanning a summary table. Polling also means a paused trace WS doesn't pause the Hooks/Scope panels — the underlying ring keeps receiving events. **Tests.** **+9 backend** in `tests/test_frida_routes.py` (empty session, multi-hook aggregation with top_returns ordering, malformed-payload defensiveness, scope filters non-this_fields events, latest entry + exit kept per method, 404 cases) + **+1 in `tests/test_frida_hook_templates.py`** (`scope_inspector` smoke render asserting JS contains `getDeclaredFields` + `setAccessible` + `this_fields` + the three phases + summary mentions `read-only` / `field` / `this_fields`) + **+7 free** from the registry-walk fail-closed test from 4.4 auto-parametrising over the new template. Full suite: **534 / 534 green** (was 517; +17 tests). `tsc --noEmit` + `vite build` clean (1.14 MB / 357 KB gzipped — modest growth from 4.5's 1.13 MB / 354 KB, attributable to the two new components and ~360 lines of CSS). DEC-023 gets a "Scope inspector + hooks/stats panel (sub-step 4.6 specifics)" sub-bullet documenting the new template + aggregator contract + polling rationale + tab-strip layout decision. **What 4.6 explicitly does *not* ship:** modify-return / mutation UI (v2), the LLM-tier `generate_frida_hook` skill (4.7), and the Cytoscape graph overlay highlighting hit nodes (4.8 — same `/hooks` endpoint will feed it).
    - **4.5 Hook builder + Stage→Inject flow — DONE** (2026-04-27): The Hook Lab Stage→Inject flow now spans backend + frontend. **Backend.** New `androscan/web/frida_routes.py` exposes 8 routes (factory-built via `build_frida_router(get_config, get_client, run_folder_root, web_apps_root)` and wired into `androscan/web/app.py`): `GET /api/frida/templates` + `/{id}` (read-only registry; works without `frida` installed), `POST /api/frida/render` (renders via `render_by_id` then runs JS through `parse_frida_js`; returns `{rendered, summary, parse: {ok, error?, line?, column?, available}}`), `POST /api/frida/sessions` (creates a session with `app_id` / `template_id` / `params` / `target_package` / `pid?` / `persist?` — defaults `target_package` to the app's `hook_target_package_prefix` from `effective_settings(global_view, per_app, app_package=…)`, enforces the prefix allowlist server-side with **403 hook_blocked**, pre-parses the rendered JS with `pyjsparser` and returns **400 render_parse_error** with `{message, line, column}` on failure, attaches via `FridaClient.attach`, calls `set_persistence_path(apps/<app_id>/<run_ts>/frida/<session>.jsonl)` when `persist=True`, and `load_script` to start the trace), `GET /api/frida/sessions` + `/{id}` (live session listing with stats), `DELETE /api/frida/sessions/{id}` (`session.detach()` flushes the JSONL writer first), `GET /api/frida/sessions/{id}/events` (ring-buffer snapshot for catch-up), `GET /api/frida/sessions/{id}/export` (`StreamingResponse` over the on-disk JSONL — 404 when persistence was off), and `WS /ws/frida/sessions/{id}/trace` (replay-then-stream — drains the ring on connect, then registers a non-async `on_event` hook that bounces events into an `asyncio.Queue(maxsize=2000)` via `loop.call_soon_threadsafe`; queue overflow drops one item and emits a `{type: 'drop'}` notice rather than blocking the Frida message thread). New JS pre-validation module `androscan/adapters/frida_hooks/_jsparse.py` (`parse_frida_js(source) -> ParseResult{ok, error, line, column, available}`) wraps `pyjsparser.PyJsParser().parse()` in a lazy `_pyjsparser_module()` test-seam mirroring DEC-018's RAG / DEC-023's `_frida_python` pattern; returns `available=False` (not a hard error) when `pyjsparser` isn't installed so the default suite stays optional. JSONL trace persistence wired into `FridaSession`: `set_persistence_path(path)` spawns a daemon writer thread fed from an unbounded `queue.Queue`, decoupling Frida's message thread from disk I/O; `detach()` poisons the queue and `join`s the writer so all events flush; serialisation / I/O failures bump a `persist_dropped` counter via `_jsonl_fallback`. **Per-app hook settings.** New `_HOOK_KEYS = {hook_target_package_prefix, auto_attach_on_session_start}` block in `androscan/web/per_app_settings.py`; `effective_settings(global_view, per_app, app_package=None)` defaults `hook_target_package_prefix` to the app's `app_meta.json` package id when nothing is set; `coerce_partial_update` strict-validates type / non-empty / max length / null-clears; `apk_overrides_summary` picks up hook overrides; `androscan/web/settings_routes.py` threads the app's package id through via a new `_app_package_from_meta` helper. **Frontend.** Added `@monaco-editor/react@^4.6.0`. New typed client `androscan/web/frontend/src/api/frida.ts` covers the full REST surface and ships `useFridaTrace(sessionId)` — owns the WS lifecycle (connect / replay / stream / reconnect-on-error), capped buffer at 2000 events, pause/resume with overflow buffering, drop-coalescing, and a `dropped` counter surfaced in the panel header. New components: `HookBuilder.tsx` (template picker + auto-generated param form prefilled from the call-graph node selection; debounced server-side render → Monaco read-only JS view with inline `pyjsparser` markers via `setModelMarkers(model, "androscan-jsparse", …)`; deterministic pentester-summary card; single Inject button gated on `parse.ok || !parse.available` + missing-required + render-in-flight); `FridaTracePanel.tsx` (WS-driven event list with sticky-tail auto-scroll, substring filter against `label / phase / message / class / method / error`, pause/resume/clear/export buttons — Export is an `<a download>` pointing at `/api/frida/sessions/:id/export`); `FridaSessionsList.tsx` (2.5 s polling + eager refresh after Inject/Detach, per-row template id / package / pid / event count / drop counts / detach button). `HookLabTab.tsx` restructured into three columns: graph (left) + (CodeView, HookBuilder, ChatDock) stacked centre + (SessionsList, TracePanel) stacked right; selected graph node prefills `class_name` / `method_name`; successful Inject pins the trace pane to the new session id; ~480 lines of new CSS in `App.css` under explicit `.hookbuilder-*` / `.frida-session-*` / `.trace-*` namespaces. **Monaco bundling note (deferred to v2).** `@monaco-editor/react`'s default loader lazy-fetches Monaco from a jsdelivr CDN — the main bundle stays at ~1.13 MB / 354 KB gzipped instead of doubling. Self-hosting is a single `loader.config({paths: {vs: '/monaco/min/vs'}})` plus a postbuild copy step; deferred to v2 with a `KNOWN_ISSUES.md` note rather than landing in 4.5. **Tests.** **+45 backend** in three new files: `tests/test_frida_hook_jsparse.py` (11 cases — happy path, syntax errors with line extraction, defensive non-string / empty / whitespace, `pyjsparser`-missing path returns `available=False`), `tests/test_frida_routes.py` (24 cases — full HTTP surface via `TestClient` against a copied stub `frida` module: templates list/get/404, render happy/missing/unknown/template-404, sessions create-happy/persist-false/allowlist-403/unknown-app-404/widened-prefix/template-404/param-error-400, list/get/delete/404, events ring snapshot, export JSONL stream + 404 when persist=false, WS replay-then-stream + WS unknown-session close, 503 frida_unavailable for session routes while templates/render still work), plus extensions to `tests/test_frida_client.py` (+8 — JSONL round-trip, parent-dir creation, called-twice / after-detach raises, drop counter on unserialisable payload + unwritable path, `detach()` flushes the writer, no-path mode unchanged, `app_id` / `template_id` metadata round-trip) and `tests/test_per_app_settings.py` (+13 — defaults, save/load, `effective_settings` defaulting `hook_target_package_prefix` to `app_package`, explicit override / `auto_attach_on_session_start`, `apk_overrides_summary` picks up hook overrides, `coerce_partial_update` validates type / empty / null-clears / max length / `auto_attach` boolean). Full suite: **517 / 517 green** (was 458; +45 tests + 14 from settings updates today). `tsc --noEmit` + `vite build` clean (1.13 MB / 354 KB gzipped — Monaco loader is CDN-based at runtime, see above).
- **Phase 10 — Behavior Trace (Lab tab gate-identification mode)** — **v1 complete 2026-04-29** (sub-steps 10.0 → 10.8 all landed). Implementation plan locked in **DEC-024** (planning commit 2026-04-28); see § **Phase 10 — Behavior Trace v1 — sub-step backlog** in `docs/TASKS.md` for the strictly-linear 8-sub-step rollout. Hook Lab tab renamed **Lab** going forward (forward-only; historical references to "Hook Lab" preserved as accurate proper nouns for the v1 milestone; code rename `HookLabTab.tsx` → `LabTab.tsx` landed in 10.6).
    - **10.0 Planning checkpoint + DEC-024 — DONE** (2026-04-28): Docs-only commit. DEC-024 added to `docs/DECISIONS.md` (workflow framing, platform-neutral data model, static→LLM split, template-bound bypass planner, closure caps, intra-procedural slicing limitation, Cytoscape demotion, per-app `trace.sqlite`, risk taxonomy, Hook Lab → Lab rename policy). Phase 10 backlog table 10.0 → 10.8 added to `TASKS.md` (10.0 marked `[x]`); Phase 10 stub added to `DESIGN_DOC.md`; naming-policy pointer added to top of this file. Stale-fixed Phase 8 / Phase 9 status rows in `TASKS.md` (both shipped via Hook Lab v1 sub-steps 4.1 → 4.8 — were still flagged "Not started"). Cross-link bullets added in DEC-016 + DEC-023 → DEC-024.
    - **10.8 Mirror → Trace integration + chat hook + docs sweep — DONE** (2026-04-29): The v1 wrap-up — closes the loop from the Inspect tab's click-to-code resolver into Trace mode, surfaces the active `BehaviorAnchor` to the Lab chat dock as a first-class attachment kind, and lands the Phase 10 docs sweep across the six canonical files. **Mirror → Trace cross-tab handoff.** New "trace ↗" button on the Inspect tab's `BestBanner` (next to the existing "open" button; rendered only when the new `onTraceBehaviour` prop is supplied — `BestBanner` stays a pure presentational component). The button hands the fuser's `ResolutionCandidate` back to `InspectTab.handleTraceBehaviour`, which converts the picked file path into a Smali class descriptor via the new `javaRelPathToSmaliClass(relPath)` utility (`com/example/Foo.java` → `Lcom/example/Foo;`) and combines it with `best.method_name` (when present — only RAG candidates carry it; deterministic-grep candidates don't) via the matching `javaRelPathToSmaliMethodPrefix(relPath, methodName)` helper to produce a *prefix* — `Lcom/example/Foo;->onClick(` when method known, else `Lcom/example/Foo;->`. The descriptor list is intentionally left for the operator to fill in: the deterministic resolver doesn't carry per-overload parameter descriptors, so seeding a partial signature is the most we can do honestly without guessing. The handler then writes `setPendingTraceEntry({appId, entryPrefix, sourceLabel: "Inspect → <file>#<method>" | "Inspect → <file>:<line>"})`, calls `setLabMode("trace")`, and finally `setTab("lab")` — a single click moves the operator from the click-to-code result straight into the Trace mode form, with the entry-method input prefilled and the new "Seeded from Inspect → MainActivity:42" pill above it. **`pendingTraceEntry` plumbing** mirrors `pendingCodeNav` / `pendingHookPrefill` exactly. New `PendingTraceEntryInput` type + ts-bumped `PendingTraceEntry` consumed type + `setPendingTraceEntry(input | null)` callback on `WorkbenchContext` (auto-bumps `ts` on every set so a stale-then-fresh seed reliably re-fires the consumer). `LabTraceMode.tsx` reads it on mount + on `(pendingTraceEntry?.ts, appId)` change: prefills `entryDraft` + `hopsDraft`, sets `seedLabel`, **auto-fires the trace** only when the seeded value is a complete signature (heuristic via `looksLikeCompleteSmaliSignature(s)` — checks for a closing paren followed by a recognised return descriptor `^\[*([VZBSCIJFD]|L[\w/$]+;)$`), otherwise just prefills so the operator can finish the descriptor list. Cleared via `setPendingTraceEntry(null)` after consumption (mirrors `pendingCodeNav`). The seed label drops the moment the operator edits the input or fires Trace, and a `×` button on the pill clears both label and draft. **Lab chat dock — `trace` attachment kind.** New `trace` `ChatAttachment` populated from the active `BehaviorAnchor` (the one currently loaded in Trace mode). Cross-mode plumbing: `LabTab.tsx` lifts `activeAnchor: BehaviorAnchor | null` into its own state; `LabTraceMode` republishes via a new `onActiveAnchorChange?(anchor | null)` callback (effect-based, fires exactly once on the build → loaded transition); `ManualHooksMode` accepts `activeAnchor` as a prop and threads it into both `buildHookChatAttachments` and `buildHookChatContextSummary`. The attachment renderer (`_renderTraceAttachment(anchor)` in `LabTab.tsx`) emits human-readable plain text — entry-method header (`class.method(params)return` form), summary line (`hops=N · decisions=M · plans=K (+J advanced) · TRUNCATED · INCOMPLETE`), optional rationale prose, then a numbered decision timeline (one line per gate: `class.method @N [kind] branch=verdict(score), … ← <origin> [LOW-CONF]`), and the **top-3 default-tier bypass plans** (`template_id risk=… → target` + params + rationale). Three soft caps: `CHAT_TRACE_BUDGET = 6_000` chars (matches `CHAT_CODE_BUDGET`), `CHAT_TRACE_TOP_PLANS = 3` (operators who want the full ranked list read the Trace UI directly), `CHAT_TRACE_MAX_DECISIONS = 40` per-anchor (with a `+ N more decision(s)` trailer when a closure exceeds it — prevents a 200-decision app from monopolising the budget). `buildHookChatContextSummary` extended with a 2-line block under the existing "Selected method" group: `Active behaviour trace: <class.method> · hops=N · M decision(s) · K plan(s) (+J advanced)` and the budget caption (`Trace attachment: included (capped at 6_000 chars; top 3 plans + first 40 decisions).`); empty-state copy when no anchor is loaded. The `Cleared the moment the operator changes app` contract is enforced via a `useEffect([appId])` in `LabTab.tsx` so a second project's chat dock never inherits the first one's trace context. **Backend** — `androscan/web/chat.py` `ATTACHMENT_BUDGETS["trace"] = 6_000` (matches `code` — same shape, same ceiling); the `lab` system prompt extended with a sentence telling the model to treat the trace attachment's decision-timeline list as authoritative ground truth for the gates governing the entry method and prefer recommending bypass plans rooted in those gates over speculation from decompiled code alone (alias-aware via `_normalise_tab` so legacy `tab="hook"` requests get the new prompt too). `androscan/web/frontend/src/components/ChatDock.tsx` `KIND_LABELS["trace"] = "Behavior trace"` so the "show context" preview surfaces the attachment under a sensible header. **Tests.** **+4 backend** in `tests/test_chat_guardrails.py` (the `trace` budget key is set + matches `code`; oversized trace truncates at the `trace` budget, not the default 2,000-char cap; under-budget trace passes through verbatim wrapped in `<context kind="trace">`; lab system prompt mentions `trace` and the alias prompt is identical). No new frontend tests per project convention (Hook Lab v1 sub-steps 4.1 → 4.8 + 10.6 / 10.7 set the same precedent — TS + bundle build are the FE coverage). Full suite: **749 / 749 green** (was 745; +4). **Frontend verification.** `tsc --noEmit` clean; `vite build` clean — 709 modules transformed, 1.20 MB JS / 75 KB CSS / 373 KB gzipped (modest growth from 10.7's 1.19 / 74 / 372 attributable to the trace-attachment renderer + the seed-pill CSS + the cross-tab handler). No new runtime dependencies, no schema changes outside the `ATTACHMENT_BUDGETS` map. **Docs sweep landed in this commit:** `docs/STATE.md` (this entry + Recent completed work entry); `docs/TASKS.md` (Active Task → "Phase 10 — Behavior Trace v1 complete (2026-04-29)" + 10.8 row marked `[x]`); `docs/DESIGN_DOC.md` (Phase 10 stub status promoted from "planning checkpoint 10.0 done 2026-04-28" to "**v1 landed 2026-04-29**" with cross-link to this STATE.md sub-bullet); `docs/DECISIONS.md` DEC-024 (Status line gets a `**v1 complete 2026-04-29**` closing note while staying `Active` for the cross-platform follow-ups DEC-024 explicitly defers to v2); `docs/KNOWN_ISSUES.md` (new ISSUE-013 captures the v1 intra-procedural-slicing false-negative posture per DEC-024; new ISSUE-014 captures the per-overload precision gap on `predicate_origin` two-register tie-breaks); `docs/SAFETY_AND_SECURITY.md` §12.7 (new "Trace mode (Phase 10)" subsection — read-only static layer, single per-anchor LLM call, template-bound bypass plans, dynamic effects flow exclusively through the existing Hook Lab v1 Stage→Inject controls + the per-app `hook_target_package_prefix` allowlist). **Final v1 test count for Phase 10:** ~104 new tests across 10.1 → 10.8 (10.1 +13 + 10.2 +16 + 10.3 +19 + 10.4 +29 + 10.5 +22 + 10.6 +18 (+14 trace routes + 4 chat alias) + 10.7 +0 + 10.8 +4) — over the 80-test target from DEC-024, the spread weighted toward the static layer (10.1 → 10.4) where determinism makes regression coverage cheap. **What 10.8 explicitly does *not* ship:** auto-fire on the seed when the prefix isn't a complete signature (the heuristic is conservative on purpose — false positives would burn an LLM call per click; partial seeds just prefill the form); a backend resolver that converts `(class_name, method_name)` to a smali signature with descriptors using the call-graph store (deferred to v2 — the operator's typing path is fine for v1, and the call-graph autocomplete affordance belongs in a follow-up that touches the form input itself); SSE/async build queue for the auto-fire path (still synchronous per 10.6 — bounded ≤ 30 methods + 1 LLM call); per-decision LLM calls (still forbidden per DEC-024); free-form LLM JS (still forbidden — staged plans round-trip through the same template-bound `frida_hooks.render_by_id` validation as 10.5 / 10.7).
    - **10.7 Trace mode frontend (the headline UI) — DONE** (2026-04-29): The first sub-step that consumes the locked `BehaviorAnchor` wire shape end-to-end and lights up the Trace mode operator surface. **Six new components** under `androscan/web/frontend/src/components/trace/`. **`BehaviorAnchorCard.tsx`** — header card: entry method's `class.method(params)return` signature with descriptors muted (Java-form class.method pops, raw smali descriptors stay scannable for verification); "Open in Inspect" button writes `pendingCodeNav` via `classNameToJavaRelPath(class_name)` + `setTab("inspect")` for cross-tab drill-in to the Java source; `truncated` / `incomplete` banner conditionals; LLM-authored `rationale` rendered as italic prose via `<em>` (only when non-empty — empty when the LLM was skipped or failed); status footer line `hops=N · M decision(s) · K plan(s) (+J advanced) · from cache | freshly built`. **`DecisionTimeline.tsx`** — top-down `<ol>` of `DecisionPoint`s in `anchor.decisions` order; one inline `DecisionCard` per decision keyed by `(method.class_name.method_name, instruction_index, position)`; per-card method header (`class.method`) + `kind` opcode badge + per-branch `BranchOutcomeBadge` row joined to `branch_outcome.verdicts` by index; three pill states derived from the wire payload — **`flagged`** (decision's `instruction_index ∈ low_confidence_decision_indices` from the parent anchor → heuristic classifier reported confidence < 0.6, signals "needed AI help"), **`LLM-refined`** (`branch_outcome.confidence ≈ 0.75` — the `LLM_RECLASSIFY_CONFIDENCE` constant the 10.5 skill stamps on LLM-reclassified outcomes), **`low confidence`** (post-analysis `branch_outcome.confidence < 0.6` AND not LLM-refined — verdict still heuristic, treat with care). The flagged + LLM-refined pair can co-occur (a heuristically-low-confidence gate that the LLM later refined to 0.75): the card's outline reflects the pre-LLM state, the badges render normally, the operator sees both signals. Collapsible expand-to-show body with `predicate_registers` (smali `vN` / `pN` chips), `branches` (one per `Branch.label → target_label`, fall-through rendered as `(fall-through)`), `predicate_origin` (renders via `PredicateOriginView` when populated; explicit "unresolved (slicer hit max-walk or unsupported path)" affordance when `null` — honest about the v1 intra-procedural slicer's limits per DEC-024), cross-branch reasons. **`BranchOutcomeBadge.tsx`** — pure presentational pill: `deny` red filled / `allow` green filled / `neutral` grey outline (DEC-024's locked colour contract); tooltip surfaces signed `score` + per-branch `reasons` for click-free audit; dashed-outline + dimmed treatment when `lowConfidence` (operator can read the pill colour at a glance but knows the heuristic confidence is low). **`PredicateOriginView.tsx`** — discriminated-union renderer for the 5 `PredicateOrigin.kind` variants. `method_call` is clickable (writes `pendingCodeNav` + flips the tab to Inspect for the called method's class — the most common operator action when reading a gate); `field_read` shows `iget` / `sget` tag + field signature inline (no nav — the field's defining class is implicit); `const` shows the literal verbatim with the `const-*` opcode tag (operator's mental model of the source survives); `param` shows the raw smali register `pN` + a "method parameter — bypass via the calling site" caption (defers the operator to the calling site for an actionable bypass — Trace mode v1 doesn't do inter-procedural slicing); `composite` shows the smali opcode that produced the value + the v1-limitation note. **`BypassPlanCard.tsx`** — per-plan card. Header: `risk` pill (low → `var(--ok)` green, medium → `var(--warn)` yellow, high → `var(--err)` red), monospace `template_id`, "Stage in Manual Hooks" button on the right edge. Body: `rationale` prose, "Hooks: <method>" + "Bypasses: <gate> #N" captions when `target_method` / `source_decision_method` populated (lets the operator tell at a glance which method the hook will actually attach to — Plan A targets the predicate's source method, Plan B targets the gate method itself), `<details>` "Parameters (N)" expander with a 2-column dl grid of `(key, value)` from `params`, `<details>` "Notes (N)" expander for the secondary `risks` strings. **"Stage in Manual Hooks"** button writes `pendingHookPrefill = { appId, templateId, params, sourceLabel: "Trace plan: <method_name>" }` AND calls `setLabMode("manual-hooks")` so the operator lands in HookBuilder with the form pre-populated and the mode visibly switched. **`TraceBanners.tsx`** exporting `TraceTruncatedBanner` (renders when `anchor.truncated` — surfaces operator-supplied `hops` + the `MAX_TRACE_METHODS=30` ceiling so the message is concrete: "Bumping `hops` may reveal additional gates, subject to the 6-hop ceiling and the 30-method cap baked into the skill") + `TraceIncompleteBanner` (renders when `anchor.incomplete` — explains the v1 intra-procedural slicer limit and frames LLM-refined verdicts as the strongest signal). **`LabTraceMode.tsx` rewritten** from the 10.6 placeholder into the real Trace mode shell. Top-of-pane form: `entry-method (smali signature)` text input (placeholder shows the canonical shape `Lcom/example/Foo;->onClick(Landroid/view/View;)V`), `hops` number stepper (clamped 1..6, default 3), `Trace` submit button, `Force re-trace` button (greyed when no active entry / build in flight). Result region switches on the `useTraceAnchor` state machine: `idle` → "type a smali entry method" prompt; `loading` → "Loading cached trace…"; `missing` → empty card with "Build trace" CTA explaining the skill walks the closure + classifies + asks the LLM to refine (the operator's first-time mental model); `building` → "Building trace — this fires one LLM call per anchor and may take a few seconds"; `error` → red-tinted card with the status code + detail + a "Retry as Build" button (covers both the GET-failed and POST-failed paths via `state.phase`); `loaded` → `<BehaviorAnchorCard source={state.from}>` + a `Decision timeline (N)` `<section>` containing `<DecisionTimeline>` + a `Bypass plans (N)` `<section>` containing the per-plan `<BypassPlanCard>` list (with an empty-state copy when `plans.length === 0`) plus an `<details>` "Advanced (N higher-risk plan(s))" expander wrapping `advanced_plans`. `CachedAnchorsList` picker (smaller `<ul>` of cached anchors from `GET /api/trace/{app_id}/anchors`; one row per `(entry_smali_id, hops, created_at)` — click loads, × deletes; the active row gets a subtle `var(--accent)` border treatment). `TraceStatusFooter` carries the same readiness fan-out the 10.6 placeholder shipped (decompile / call graph / trace cache + cached anchor count). Reload tick bumped after every successful Build / Delete so status + cached list stay fresh without manual refresh. **`useTraceAnchor(appId, entry, hops)` React hook** added to `src/api/trace.ts` — owns the GET-then-fall-back-to-POST lifecycle. State machine returned as a discriminated union (`{kind: "idle" | "loading" | "loaded" | "missing" | "building" | "error", ...}`); ref-based stale-response guard (`keyRef = "appId\\u0001entry\\u0001hops"`) mirrors `HookBuilder.renderInflightKeyRef`'s pattern so a fast operator changing the target doesn't get clobbered by a slow late response; `build(force?)` callback fires `buildTraceAnchor(..., force)` and slots the result back into the same state machine; `clear()` resets to `idle` without nuking the active target. **`api/trace.ts` types tightened** — was `unknown[]` placeholders in 10.6; now `MethodRef` / `FieldRef` / `Branch` / `BranchVerdict` / `BranchOutcome` / `DecisionKind` (string-union of the 14 opcode names) / `DecisionPoint` / `BypassPlan` / `PredicateOrigin` (discriminated union over `kind: "method_call" | "field_read" | "const" | "param" | "composite"`) all match `dataclasses.asdict` output verbatim. **`WorkbenchContext.tsx` extended** with two new cross-mode primitives. **`LabMode` + `setLabMode`** lifted out of `LabTab.tsx`'s local state (with `localStorage["androscan.lab.mode"]` persistence still owned in the context provider's `setLabMode` callback) so external callers — the trace components themselves, plus 10.8's Mirror "Trace this behaviour" button — can flip the mode programmatically without drilling imperative refs through the LabTab tree. **`pendingHookPrefill` + `setPendingHookPrefill`** mirror the `pendingCodeNav` plumbing exactly: `BypassPlanCard` writes from Trace mode (template id + params dict + source label), `HookBuilder` consumes on mount/change and clears via `setPendingHookPrefill(null)`. `ts` field bumped on every `setPendingHookPrefill` call so a stage→edit→stage-again sequence reliably re-fires the consumer (matches `pendingCodeNav.ts`). **`LabTab.tsx` updated** to read `labMode` / `setLabMode` from context (was local state); `LAB_MODE_STORAGE_KEY` constant + `loadStoredLabMode` helper deleted (moved to context provider). The `LabMode` type re-exported from `LabTab.tsx` for backwards-compatible naming. **`HookBuilder.tsx` wired with the prefill consumer** via a ref-based handoff (`stagedParamsRef`). The bug avoided: changing `selectedTemplateId` re-fires the existing template-change effect, which rebuilds `paramValues` from declared template defaults and would clobber the staged params.
