Fix: stash staged params in a ref *before* setting the template id; the template-change effect drains the ref into the rebuilt param dict (overlaying staged values on top of defaults) and clears the ref so subsequent unrelated template changes start clean. When the staged template equals the currently-selected template the consumer applies the prefill directly via `setParamValues((prev) => ({...prev, ...staged}))` since the template-change effect won't fire. New `stagedSourceLabel: string | null` state surfaces a "Staged from Trace" pill on the HookBuilder header (with the operator-readable source label e.g. `Trace plan: isPremiumUser`); the pill drops the moment the operator picks a different template (`onChange` on `<select>` calls `setStagedSourceLabel(null)`) or edits any param (`onParamChange` clears it). **CSS.** ~430 lines added to `App.css` under explicit `.trace-*` namespaces: `.trace-form` (3-column grid: entry input + hops stepper + button row), `.trace-empty` (idle / loading / missing / building / error placeholder), `.trace-empty-missing button` + `.trace-error button` (CTA buttons in `var(--accent)` outline), `.trace-result` / `.trace-section`, `.trace-anchor-card` (border-strong frame, header + meta + rationale stacked), `.trace-anchor-method` / `-class` / `-name` / `-descriptor` / `-inspect-btn`, `.trace-anchor-rationale` (left-bordered accent quote), `.trace-banner-truncated` / `-incomplete` (warn / err soft fills via `color-mix(in srgb, ...)` to keep the colour-token contract honest), `.trace-decision-timeline` / `-card` / `-card-flagged` (warn left border) / `-header` / `-method` / `-kind` / `-pill-flagged` / `-pill-llm` / `-pill-warning` / `-verdicts` / `-expand` / `-body` / `-details` (2-column dl), `.trace-decision-register`, `.trace-branch-badge-deny` / `-allow` / `-neutral` / `-low-confidence` (dashed-outline override), `.trace-predicate-origin-tag` / `-link` / `-descriptor` / `-literal` (literal in `var(--ok)` to visually echo "this is a constant value"), `.trace-bypass-plan-list` / `-card` / `-header` / `-spacer` / `-template` / `-stage` (hover state via `color-mix(in srgb, var(--accent) 22%, transparent)`), `.trace-bypass-plan-risk-low` / `-medium` / `-high` (each with the matching colour-token soft fill + bordered pill), `.trace-bypass-plan-rationale` / `-targets` / `-params` / `-params-grid` / `-param-row` (CSS Grid `display: contents` trick to render dl rows in a 2-col grid), `.trace-bypass-plan-risks ul`, `.trace-advanced-plans summary` (warn-coloured to signal "higher risk lives here"), `.trace-cached-anchors` / `-anchor-active` / `-anchor-pick` / `-anchor-delete`, `.hookbuilder-staged-pill` (accent pill that lives in the HookBuilder header). All colour usage routes through the existing `:root` tokens (`var(--ok)` green, `var(--err)` red, `var(--warn)` yellow, `var(--accent)` blue, `var(--accent-soft)` for soft fills, `var(--muted)` for captions, `var(--panel-2)` for card backgrounds, `var(--border)` / `var(--border-strong)` for separators); `color-mix(in srgb, ...)` used for the soft tints (matches the rest of `App.css` post-`.lab-*` work in 10.6). **No new backend tests** in 10.7 — the JSON wire-shape contract is locked by 10.6's `tests/test_trace_routes.py`; no test runner is configured for the frontend per the project convention (Hook Lab v1 sub-steps 4.1 → 4.8 set the same precedent). Full suite: **745 / 745 green** unchanged from 10.6. **Frontend verification.** `npx tsc --noEmit` clean (no TS errors); `npm run build` clean — **709 modules transformed** (was 703 in 10.6; +6 new component files + the hook), 1.19 MB JS / 74 KB CSS / 372 KB gzipped (modest growth from 10.6's 1.18 MB / 63 KB / 367 KB attributable to the ~600 lines of new TSX + ~430 lines of new CSS). No new runtime dependencies, no schema changes, no edits to backend Python. **What 10.7 explicitly does *not* ship:** the Mirror → Trace cross-tab "Trace this behaviour" button on the Inspect tab's `BestBanner` (10.8 — needs new `pendingTraceEntry` plumbing); a new `trace` `ChatAttachment` kind for the Lab chat dock (10.8 — runs once the Trace mode UI is real and the chat dock has something to forward); auto-fetching source-code snippets per decision (deferred to v2 — operators click "Open in Inspect" on the anchor card today; per-decision fetch cost would push the timeline render off the synchronous path); per-decision LLM re-classification (still forbidden per DEC-024 — the once-per-anchor LLM call from 10.5 is the contract); free-form LLM JS for staged plans (still forbidden — staged plans round-trip through the same template-bound `frida_hooks.render_by_id` validation as 10.5).
    - **10.6 REST endpoint + tab shell + Hook Lab → Lab code rename — DONE** (2026-04-29): Backend + frontend converge on the locked `BehaviorAnchor` wire shape. **Backend.** New `androscan/web/trace_routes.py` factory `build_trace_router(config_provider, app_dir_resolver) -> APIRouter` mirroring the `graph_routes.py` shape (same DI seams, same `_cache_dir_for(app_id)` precondition pattern, same `app_dir_resolver` 404 contract). Five endpoints under prefix `/api/trace`: `GET /{app_id}/status` (cache + decompile + call-graph readiness fan-out — same response shape as `graph_routes` so the Settings tab + the LabTraceMode placeholder can render both with one component); `GET /{app_id}/anchors` (lists cached `(entry_smali_id, hops, created_at)` rows ordered by `created_at DESC` from `trace_cache.list_anchors`); `GET /{app_id}/anchor?entry=<smali_id>&hops=<n>` (pure cache read — never invokes the skill, never runs the LLM, never writes; 404 when not cached so the frontend's empty-state UX can branch cleanly off the status code); `POST /{app_id}/anchor?entry=<smali_id>&hops=<n>&force=<bool>` (the **only** endpoint that triggers the `parse → slice → classify → plan → LLM → persist` pipeline — invokes the `trace_behavior` skill synchronously via `skills.execute("trace_behavior", ..., SkillContext(run_folder=app_dir / "trace-build"))` so the skill's `_resolve_app_dir(rf.parent)` lands on the real `app_dir`; surfaces the skill's `data is None` fail-open as 404 with the skill's text in `detail`); `DELETE /{app_id}/anchor?entry=<smali_id>&hops=<n>` (`trace_cache.delete_anchor` + 204; 404 when no row matched so the frontend can distinguish "I deleted it" from "it was already gone"). Hard caps in the route layer: `_MAX_HOPS = 6` (rejected as 422 rather than silently clamped — operators benefit from the explicit signal that they hit the ceiling), `_MAX_ENTRY_LEN = 500` (matches `graph_routes`' `MAX_PATH_HOPS` posture). Synchronous skill invocation on purpose — per-anchor cost is bounded (≤ 30 methods + 1 LLM call) and operators expect the call to block; an async build queue is a v2 concern (10.7 / 10.8 may add SSE if the UI starts firing builds in the background while the operator works in another mode). Wired into `androscan/web/app.py` next to `build_graph_router(...)` via `app.include_router(build_trace_router(_current_config, _app_dir))`. **Hook Lab → Lab chat rename.** `androscan/web/chat.py` `ALLOWED_TABS` extended from `{"reports", "inspect", "hook"}` → `{"reports", "inspect", "lab", "hook"}` (NOT a swap — `"hook"` retained as a back-compat alias so existing transcript files written before the rename still validate when replayed); `_TAB_SYSTEM_PROMPTS["hook"]` renamed to `_TAB_SYSTEM_PROMPTS["lab"]` (prompt body unchanged — operator UX shouldn't shift). New `_TAB_ALIASES = {"hook": "lab"}` table + pure `_normalise_tab(tab) -> str` helper that every keying site routes through (`system_prompt_for` looks up `_normalise_tab(tab)`; `append_transcript` writes to `chat/<_normalise_tab(tab)>.jsonl`; both `process_chat_request` + `stream_chat_request` normalise immediately after pulling `body["tab"]` so the rate-limit bucket + the `record["tab"]` field + every downstream call sees the canonical id). Net effect: a legacy client sending `tab="hook"` gets the same prompt, lands in the same rate-limit bucket, and writes to the same `chat/lab.jsonl` file as a modern client sending `tab="lab"` — operators upgrading mid-flight never see split state. **Frontend.** `androscan/web/frontend/src/tabs/HookLabTab.tsx` deleted + replaced with `LabTab.tsx` (~600 lines; same internals as the deleted file but split into two top-level component branches plus the new mode-switcher shell); internal `HookLabCodeView` renamed to `LabCodeView`; `<ChatDock tab="hook">` literal swapped to `<ChatDock tab="lab">`; the `autoSaveId`s for the inner `PanelGroup`s renamed `hook-h` / `hook-center-v` / `hook-right-v` → `lab-manual-h` / `lab-manual-center-v` / `lab-manual-right-v` so the layout state survives a fresh build (`react-resizable-panels` keys saved sizes by autoSaveId — under the old name they'd be orphaned in `localStorage`). New `LabTraceMode.tsx` (Trace mode placeholder for 10.6 — fetches `GET /api/trace/{app_id}/status` on mount + on app change, surfaces `decompile_status` / `call_graph.status` / `trace_cache.status` / `anchor_count` in a small `<dl>` grid + a loud "10.7 will fill this" banner; will be fully replaced by the `BehaviorAnchorCard` / `DecisionTimeline` / `BypassPlanCard` triple in 10.7). New `androscan/web/frontend/src/api/trace.ts` (~140 lines; ships `BehaviorAnchor` / `TraceCacheStatus` / `TraceStatusPayload` / `TraceAnchorRow` types matching the backend wire shape verbatim, plus `fetchTraceStatus` / `listTraceAnchors` / `fetchTraceAnchor` / `buildTraceAnchor` / `deleteTraceAnchor` helpers; uniform `ApiResult<T> = { ok: true, data: T } | { ok: false, status, error }` envelope so callers handle network errors + 4xx + 5xx the same way; FastAPI's `{ detail: "..." }` body shape is parsed and surfaced in `error` so the LabTraceMode placeholder can render `"404 — entry not cached"` rather than a generic "request failed"). **Three-mode left-rail switcher** inside `LabTab` (`Trace | Manual Hooks | Graph`, defaulting to `Trace`): vertical pill nav pinned to the left edge (~7.5rem wide, `.lab-mode-rail` styling reusing the existing `.right-pane-tab` accent-bar treatment), state persisted via `localStorage["androscan.lab.mode"]` (per-browser, not per-app — operators tend to settle into one mode rather than jumping per-app). `Trace` mode renders `<LabTraceMode appId={appId} />` (the placeholder). `Manual Hooks` mode renders the legacy three-column Hook Lab layout verbatim (CallGraph + CodeView + HookBuilder + Chat + Sessions + Trace/Hooks/Scope tabs) so operators with established muscle memory get the same flow they had pre-10.6. `Graph` mode renders a dedicated full-pane `<CallGraphView />` (same component, new context — operators who want to deep-dive the call graph without the 32%-width constraint of Manual Hooks mode pop into here). Mode hops cleanly conditional-render — picking a different mode unmounts/re-mounts the prior mode's tree, so Manual Hooks' polling / WS resources tie to actual visibility (matches the top-level tab unmount pattern; only the persistent `Settings` tab uses CSS hidden). **Cross-cutting frontend wiring.** `types.ts` `TabId` enum updated `"hook"` → `"lab"` (with a comment block forbidding new code from re-introducing `"hook"`). `App.tsx` imports `LabTab` instead of `HookLabTab`; the `tab === "hook" && <HookLabTab />` line becomes `tab === "lab" && <LabTab />`. `components/TabBar.tsx` updates the tab list entry from `{ id: "hook", label: "Hook Lab" }` → `{ id: "lab", label: "Lab" }`. `context/WorkbenchContext.tsx` `TABS` array updated `"hook"` → `"lab"`; chats record initial state key updated `hook: []` → `lab: []`; new `_LEGACY_TAB_REDIRECTS = { hook: "lab" }` table + one-shot `tabFromHash()` rewrite that calls `window.history.replaceState(null, "", "#/lab")` when the URL hash is `#/hook` (so a bookmark of the legacy id silently jumps to the canonical id without growing the browser history; subsequent share/copy of the URL hands out the canonical id). Stale doc-comment references to "HookLabTab" in `HookBuilder.tsx` / `CallGraphView.tsx` / `App.css`'s `.right-pane-tabs` namespace block updated to "LabTab" — no behaviour change, just keeps grep-by-name honest. `App.css` gains ~110 lines under the new `.lab-tab-shell` / `.lab-mode-rail` / `.lab-mode-button` / `.lab-mode-content` / `.lab-graph-mode` / `.lab-trace-mode` / `.lab-trace-stub` / `.lab-trace-status-grid` namespaces; the rail uses the same `var(--accent)` left-border treatment as `.tab-active` so the visual hierarchy stays consistent (top tabs select a tab; this rail selects a mode within the Lab tab). `frontend/README.md` `Layout` section rewritten — `#/hook` mention swapped for `#/lab` + `#/settings`, `HookLabTab.tsx` reference replaced with `LabTab.tsx` (with the three-mode-switcher description + the `LabTraceMode.tsx` follow-up + the `localStorage["androscan.lab.mode"]` persistence note), new bullet for `LabTraceMode.tsx`, new bullet for `src/api/trace.ts`, plus a sentence in the routing description noting the `#/hook → #/lab` back-compat redirect. **Tests.** **+14 backend** in new `tests/test_trace_routes.py` (router-registration smoke; status fan-out for cache-missing + cache-ready + zero-anchors; list anchors empty + populated; GET anchor cache-miss → 404 + cache-hit returns the canonical wire shape pinned by direct comparison to the POST result; GET anchor empty-entry → 422; POST builds + persists + visible to the cache reader; POST `force=true` upserts without appending; POST unresolved entry → 404 with "not found" in `detail`; DELETE success → 204 + subsequent list shows no rows; DELETE missing → 404; cross-cutting unknown app_id → 404 from the shared `app_dir_resolver`). LLM is stubbed via an autouse `monkeypatch.setattr("androscan.llm.client.complete", _stub)` returning a well-formed empty JSON object — every test exercises the deterministic static layer's output, never the LLM round-trip (that's `test_trace_behavior_skill.py`'s job). Re-uses the same `_seed_app_with_graph` pattern as `test_graph_routes.py` (real apktool tree under `apps/<app_id>/.decompiled/<sha>/smali_out/`, real `call_graph.build_index`, real synchronous TestClient call). **+4 backend** in `tests/test_chat_stream.py` for the Hook Lab → Lab back-compat alias contract: `tab="lab"` writes `chat/lab.jsonl` (canonical filename); legacy `tab="hook"` request also writes `chat/lab.jsonl` AND no `chat/hook.jsonl` appears (alias collapsing on the write side); `system_prompt_for("lab") == system_prompt_for("hook")` (alias collapsing on the prompt side); a burst alternating `tab="lab"` + `tab="hook"` consumes a single rate-limit bucket — second call trips a 1-turn/min limiter (alias collapsing on the rate-limit side, prevents double-quota by alternating ids). Full suite: **745 / 745 green** (was 727; +18). **Frontend verification.** `npx tsc --noEmit` clean (no TS errors); `npm run build` clean (703 modules transformed, 1.18 MB JS / 63 KB CSS / 367 KB gzipped — modest growth from 10.5's 1.17 MB / 62 KB attributable to the ~600 lines of LabTab + LabTraceMode + the trace.ts client). No new runtime dependencies, no schema changes outside the new `/api/trace` REST surface, no edits to `smali_parser.py` / `dispatch.py` / `call_graph.py`. **What 10.6 explicitly does *not* ship:** the actual Trace mode UI components — `BehaviorAnchorCard.tsx` / `DecisionTimeline.tsx` / `BypassPlanCard.tsx` / `TraceTruncatedBanner.tsx` / `TraceIncompleteBanner.tsx` (10.7); the Mirror → Trace cross-tab "Trace this behaviour" plumbing via `pendingTraceEntry` (10.8); the `trace` chat-attachment kind for the Lab chat dock (10.8); a first-launch tooltip explaining the new default Trace mode (10.7 — UX polish belongs with the real Trace UI); SSE / async build queue for POST (v2 — current synchronous block is fine for ≤ 30 methods + 1 LLM call); per-decision LLM calls (still forbidden per DEC-024 — POST invokes the skill which makes one LLM call per anchor); free-form LLM JS (still forbidden — POST validates plans through the same template-bound `frida_hooks.render_by_id` round-trip as 10.5).
    - **10.5 `trace_behavior` LLM-tier skill — DONE** (2026-04-29): The capstone of Phase 10's static layer. New skill `androscan/skills/trace_behavior.py` (LLM-tier, `requires_confirmation=False` per DEC-022 / DEC-024 — read-only, no device touching, no APK mutation, no Frida injection) registered in `_SKILL_MODULES` (15 → 16 skills). Public skill API: `execute({"entry_method": <smali_id>, "app_id"?: <id>, "hops"?: <int>, "force"?: <bool>}, context) -> SkillResult`. Pipeline: (1) resolve `app_dir` + `cache_dir` via the same fallback ladder as `query_call_graph` / `resolve_ui_element` (explicit `app_id` wins, else `run_folder.parent`); (2) cache lookup against `apps/<app_id>/.decompiled/<sha>/trace.sqlite` keyed by `(entry_method.smali_signature, hops)` — hit returns immediately with `[cached] ` text prefix and full `data` payload, skipping all of (3)–(8); (3) call-graph forward BFS from the entry node, depth ≤ `hops` (clamped to `trace.max_hops_hard_cap`), collecting up to `MAX_TRACE_METHODS = 30` internal (non-external) callees; sets `truncated=True` on either cap hit; up-front existence-check on the entry node so unknown smali_ids fail-open instead of emitting a zero-decision ghost anchor; (4) parse the apktool tree once via `smali_parser.parse_classes` + `decisions.parse_decisions`, then for each method in the closure run `slicing.slice_predicate_origins → branch_classifier.classify_branch_outcomes → bypass_planner.plan_bypasses` — the union of all decisions + plans is aggregated into the anchor; `incomplete=True` when at least one decision has unresolved `predicate_origin` (slicer hit `max_walk` or origin came from a path the v1 slicer doesn't follow); (5) identify LLM workload — `low_confidence_indices` = decisions with `branch_outcome.confidence < LLM_RECLASSIFY_THRESHOLD = 0.6`; `planless_keys` = decisions where the deterministic planner returned no plans AND the heuristic outcome had non-zero confidence (asking the LLM to invent plans for completely-neutral gates would generate noise); (6) **one LLM call per anchor** (per-decision LLM calls explicitly rejected per DEC-024 — that pattern blows up the token budget on real apps with 30 methods × 5 decisions each) via `androscan.llm.client.complete(..., response_format="json", stream=False)` with the inline `build_trace_behavior_prompt(...)` that emits a strict JSON-schema system prompt + a deterministic per-decision rendering (signature, instruction_index, kind, registers, predicate_origin kind, branches, per-branch verdict + score + reasons); LLM response parsed into `{rationale, reclassifications, proposed_plans}`; (7) re-classification application — for each `(method, instruction_index, branch_label, verdict)` entry with valid `verdict ∈ {"deny", "allow", "neutral"}`, replace the `BranchVerdict.verdict` (heuristic `score` is preserved for audit, an `"llm-reclassified: <reason>"` entry is appended to `reasons`), bump `BranchOutcome.confidence` to `LLM_RECLASSIFY_CONFIDENCE = 0.75` (above the heuristic moderate tier 0.85's lower bound but distinct enough that 10.7's UI can render an "LLM-refined" badge without ambiguity), invalid rows silently dropped (logged at WARN); (8) proposed-plan validation — every LLM-proposed plan must (a) have `template_id ∈ {"force_return_value", "force_method_skip", "force_string_compare_equal"}` and (b) round-trip through `frida_hooks.render_by_id(template_id, params)` without raising; failed plans are silently dropped + logged so the LLM can self-correct on subsequent calls (no exception bubbles out of the skill); accepted plans are wrapped with `BypassPlan(risks=("LLM-proposed plan; review the rendered JS before injecting.",), source_decision_method/_index/target_method` filled from the matched decision); (9) risk-partition via `bypass_planner.partition_by_risk(plans, config.trace_bypass_risk_max)` → `(default_plans, advanced_plans)` (advanced shows behind an "Advanced" expander in 10.7); (10) persist via `trace_cache.write_anchor(cache_dir, anchor)` (INSERT OR REPLACE, idempotent); persist failures are logged but never fail the call (operator can re-run; the in-memory payload is still useful for this turn); (11) return `SkillResult(success=True, data=<json-decoded anchor dict>, text=<summary with deny/allow/neutral counts + plan counts + truncated/incomplete flags + optional rationale + optional `[llm-skipped: <reason>]`>)`. **Fail-soft posture (mirrors `query_call_graph` per DEC-024):** missing app context, unbuilt decompile cache, missing call graph, or unresolved entry method → `success=True` with `data=None` and a clear `text` explanation; LLM transport failure / non-JSON response / response-not-an-object → return the deterministic-only payload with `[llm-skipped: <reason>]` in `text` (the static layer alone is enough for an operator to make progress without LLM augmentation). **Per-app SQLite cache** in new module `androscan/internal/trace_cache.py` — pure file-I/O over a `cache_dir: Path` argument with no Smali / call-graph imports so 10.6's REST routes can read directly without spinning up the full skill. Schema v1: `meta(key, value)` + `anchors(entry_smali_id, hops, payload_json, created_at, PRIMARY KEY (entry_smali_id, hops))`. Six-function API: read side `get_status` (returns `TraceCacheStatus(status, db_path, schema_version, anchor_count, error)` with schema-mismatch surfaced as `status="failed"`), `read_anchor`, `list_anchors`; write side `init_db` (idempotent), `write_anchor` (lazy-init via `init_db` so callers don't have to), `delete_anchor`, `clear_all_anchors`. JSON marshalling lives here too — `anchor_to_json(anchor) -> str` (sort_keys=True for deterministic output) + `anchor_from_json(str) -> BehaviorAnchor` with a `_PREDICATE_ORIGIN_BY_KIND` dispatch table that decodes the union variants of `PredicateOrigin` (mirrors the JSON-shape contract 10.2 + 10.3 + 10.4 already established). Path: `apps/<app_id>/.decompiled/<sha>/trace.sqlite` — sharing the decompile-cache directory means re-decompile (sha change) auto-invalidates the trace cache too; no explicit purge needed. **`BehaviorAnchor` extended** in `androscan/analysis/trace_types.py` with three additive optional fields: `advanced_plans: tuple[BypassPlan, ...] = ()` (high-risk-filtered partition for 10.7's "Advanced" expander), `rationale: str = ""` (free-form LLM-authored "why this anchor matters" prose; empty when LLM call was skipped/failed), `low_confidence_decision_indices: tuple[int, ...] = ()` (the `instruction_index` values flagged for LLM re-classification — lets 10.7's UI surface "LLM-refined" affordances without re-walking the heuristic catalog). All three default to safe empties so 10.1's minimal-shell construction pattern still works. **Two new config knobs** wired through every loader site: `trace_max_hops_default: int = 3` (default closure depth used when caller doesn't supply explicit `hops`) and `trace_max_hops_hard_cap: int = 6` (absolute ceiling — even `force=True` can't exceed it, bounds the worst-case per-anchor cost). Both added to `Config` dataclass + `Config.default()` + `CONFIG_FIELD_MAP` (`trace.max_hops_default` / `trace.max_hops_hard_cap` YAML keys + `ANDROSCAN_TRACE_MAX_HOPS_DEFAULT` / `ANDROSCAN_TRACE_MAX_HOPS_HARD_CAP` env vars) + `LIVE_RELOADABLE_FIELDS` (read per-call by the skill, no in-flight state to bounce — an active closure walk completes under the old cap, the next call uses the new value) + `_merge_from_yaml` (parses extended `trace:` section via `_safe_int`, fail-soft to default on missing/empty/invalid) + env-override branch + `with_overrides` clamp + `coerce_yaml_value`'s `int_fields` set. `global_config.yaml` extended with the two new keys + comments documenting their semantics. **Tests.** **+22 tests** in `tests/test_trace_behavior_skill.py` (slightly over the planned 17 — the LLM mocking + cache round-trip cases pull weight): catalog/registration (3 tests — skill in `list_llm_skills`, NOT in `list_skills_by_tier("exploit")`, schema documents all four params); input validation (2 — missing `entry_method` returns failure, invalid `hops` value clamps to default); fail-open paths (4 — bare run-folder, decompile not ready, call graph not ready, unknown entry method); closure walk + per-method static layer (2 — single-method closure for `Plans.gateBoolPredicate()V` emits 1 decision + force_return_value + force_method_skip plans; closure walk writes to `trace.sqlite` + visible via the pure cache reader); cache hit + force=True (2 — second call hits cache without re-invoking LLM via an unreachable stub; `force=True` re-walks even when cached and overwrites the row); LLM round-trip (3 tests via monkeypatched `androscan.llm.client.complete` returning a stub `_LLMResponse` — re-classification lifts `branch_outcome.confidence` to `LLM_RECLASSIFY_CONFIDENCE` and appends "llm-reclassified" to verdict reasons; LLM-proposed plan with valid `template_id` round-trips through `frida_hooks.render_by_id` + surfaces in `data.plans`; LLM-proposed plan with invalid `template_id` silently dropped); LLM fail-soft (2 — `complete()` raises → deterministic payload + `[llm-skipped: transport: ...]` marker; non-JSON response → deterministic payload + `[llm-skipped: json-parse: ...]` marker); risk partitioning (1 — `trace_bypass_risk_max="low"` config routes Plan B's MEDIUM `force_method_skip` to `advanced_plans` while Plan A's LOW `force_return_value` stays in `plans`); config-knob smoke (3 — defaults match spec, both new knobs in `LIVE_RELOADABLE_FIELDS`, both in `CONFIG_FIELD_MAP`). All 22 tests pass via the same `_seed_apps_with_graph` pattern as `test_query_call_graph_skill.py` (real apktool tree under `apps/<app_id>/.decompiled/<sha>/smali_out/smali/`, real `call_graph.build_index` + real per-method static analysis end-to-end). Test stub helpers `_LLMResponse` / `_stub_llm_unreachable` / `_stub_llm_must_not_call` / `_first_decision_index` are reusable for future trace-behavior regressions in 10.6 / 10.7 / 10.8. Full suite: **727 / 727 green** (was 705; +22). No FE changes, no schema changes outside the new `trace.sqlite`, no new optional dependencies, no edits to `smali_parser.py` / `dispatch.py` / `call_graph.py`. **What 10.5 explicitly does *not* ship:** REST endpoint + tab shell + Hook Lab → Lab code rename (10.6 — `androscan/web/trace_routes.py` will read directly from `trace_cache` without invoking the skill, exactly the separation the pure-SQLite layer enables); Trace-mode frontend with `BehaviorAnchorCard` / `DecisionTimeline` / `BypassPlanCard` (10.7); Mirror → Trace cross-tab integration + docs sweep (10.8); per-decision LLM calls (DEC-024 forbids — a single anchor-scoped call is the contract, not a loop); free-form LLM JS (DEC-024 forbids — only the three template ids in `force_return_value` / `force_method_skip` / `force_string_compare_equal` are accepted); inter-procedural slicing in the closure (the slicer is intra-procedural per 10.2; the closure walk merely visits more methods, each one still sliced in isolation); LLM-driven cache invalidation policy beyond the `force=True` flag (re-decompile invalidates via sha change, no explicit per-anchor TTL in v1).
    - **10.4 Bypass planner — DONE** (2026-04-29): New module `androscan/analysis/bypass_planner.py` — pure deterministic synthesiser that maps a single `(DecisionPoint, BranchOutcome)` to zero or more template-bound `BypassPlan` instances. Public surface: `plan_bypasses(decision, instructions=(), label_index=None) -> tuple[BypassPlan, ...]` (the per-decision API; 10.5's `trace_behavior` skill loops over the closure and aggregates), plus `partition_by_risk(plans, threshold) -> (default_plans, advanced_plans)` (operator-facing risk-threshold split — UI surfaces `advanced_plans` behind an "Advanced" expander per DEC-024) and the locked taxonomy constants `VALID_RISKS = ("low", "medium", "high")` + `DEFAULT_RISK_THRESHOLD = "medium"` + `risk_at_or_below(plan_risk, threshold)` (strict total-order comparator with fail-soft fallback to `"medium"` on unknown values). **`BypassPlan` extended** in `androscan/analysis/trace_types.py` with three additive back-reference fields: `target_method: Optional[MethodRef]` (the method actually hooked — Plan A targets the predicate's source method, Plan B targets the gate method itself, Plan C targets the synthetic `Ljava/lang/String;->equals(Ljava/lang/Object;)Z` ref); `source_decision_method: Optional[MethodRef]` (the gate method the plan was generated for); `source_decision_instruction_index: Optional[int]` (which `DecisionPoint` within that method — methods with multiple decisions need the disambiguator). All three default to `None` so 10.1's minimal-shell construction pattern still works. **Three new Frida hook templates** under `androscan/adapters/frida_hooks/` — all template-bound, no free-form JS per DEC-024, all picked up automatically by the existing `TestRegistryFailClosed` fail-closed registry walk: `force_return_value` (params: `class_name` / `method_name` / `return_value_expr` / `event_label`; LOW risk; hijacks one named method and returns the operator-supplied raw JS literal — original method body NOT executed, side effects skipped), `force_method_skip` (params: `class_name` / `method_name` / `return_descriptor` / `event_label`; MEDIUM risk; replaces every overload with a no-op stub returning the descriptor's natural zero — `false`/`0`/`null`/`0.0`/nothing-for-void — the only template that handles void gates honestly since Frida refuses non-void implementations on `V` methods), `force_string_compare_equal` (params: `target_literal` / `event_label`; MEDIUM risk; hooks `String.equals(Object)` AND `String.equalsIgnoreCase(String)` app-wide, force-returns `true` only when EITHER side of the comparison stringifies to `target_literal` — the literal-gated nature is what makes the override survivable; a plain `force_return_value` on `String.equals` would break class loading + hash table lookups). All three follow the DEC-023 sub-step 4.4 contract verbatim: declared params + JS `{{` brace escaping + non-empty `pentester_summary_template` + auto-registered via `_TEMPLATE_MODULES` (bumped from 6 → 9 modules; `TestRegistryFailClosed::test_module_list_is_non_empty` updated `>= 5` → `>= 9`). **Plan emission rules (locked v1):** for each `(decision, outcome)` where `outcome.confidence > 0` AND there's both a DENY *and* an ALLOW verdict (no flip target → no plans), and where `decision.is_switch` is False (switches punt to v2 — per-case forced literals are non-trivial), three independent rules each may contribute one plan in deterministic order: **Plan A — flip the predicate value** (only when `predicate_origin` is `MethodCallOrigin` AND source method is NOT `String.equals` / `equalsIgnoreCase` — those route to Plan C — AND the planner can synthesise a literal for the source method's return descriptor): emit `force_return_value` targeting the *predicate's source method* with `return_value_expr` derived from `(decision.kind, allow-branch direction, source method's return descriptor)` via the locked `_SINGLE_REG_TRUE_TAKES_ZERO` table for `if-*z` opcodes — `Z → "true" / "false"`, `I/J/B/S/C → "1" / "0"`, `F/D → "1.0" / "0.0"`, `L...;` zero side → `"null"`, `L...;` non-null side → no plan (can't reliably synthesise an instance), `V` → no plan (can't force-return on void); risk LOW. Two-register predicates (`if-eq` etc.) and switches are filtered upstream → no Plan A. **Plan B — force-skip the gate method** (only when `decision.method.return_descriptor == "V"`): emit `force_method_skip` targeting the enclosing gate method; risk MEDIUM. Useful for `void enforceLicense()` style gates that throw on deny — skipping the method short-circuits the throw. **Plan C — string-literal bypass** (only when `predicate_origin` is `MethodCallOrigin` AND source method is `String.equals` / `equalsIgnoreCase` AND a recoverable `const-string` literal exists in either branch's basic block OR via a backward scan over the gate method body up to the decision — gates frequently load the secret right above the equals call): emit `force_string_compare_equal` with `target_literal` filled from the const-string text verbatim; risk MEDIUM. **Tests.** **+29 tests** in `tests/test_bypass_planner.py` (eight Plan A tests covering all five descriptor branches + ConstOrigin / FieldReadOrigin / ParamOrigin / CompositeOrigin skips; two Plan B tests for void-vs-non-void gate; three Plan C tests for literal-found / literal-missing / Plan-A-routes-away; five planner-skip tests for no-outcome / zero-confidence / both-deny / switch / no-instructions; seven risk-taxonomy tests for the locked enum + total-order comparator + fail-soft + the three threshold-partition cases; one end-to-end assertion that Plan A + Plan B output renders cleanly through `frida_hooks.render_by_id`; three config-knob tests for the new `trace_bypass_risk_max` field). **+3 new per-template smoke renders** in `tests/test_frida_hook_templates.py::TestTemplates` (each new template's substring assertions + sensitive-APIs metadata check). **+21 free** from the parametrized `TestRegistryFailClosed` walk auto-picking up the three new templates (7 walks × 3 modules). New fixture `tests/fixtures/trace_smali/smali/com/trace/Plans.smali` (12 methods + constructor — every method shaped so the pipeline `parse → slice → classify → plan_bypasses` emits a fully-deterministic plan list). **New config knob** `trace_bypass_risk_max` (string, default `"medium"`) wired through every loader site: `Config` dataclass + `Config.default()` + `CONFIG_FIELD_MAP[trace_bypass_risk_max] = ("trace", "bypass_risk_max", "ANDROSCAN_TRACE_BYPASS_RISK_MAX")` + `LIVE_RELOADABLE_FIELDS` (read per-call by 10.5's skill, no in-flight state to bounce) + `_merge_from_yaml` (parses new `trace:` section, fail-soft to `"medium"` on missing / empty) + env-override branch + `with_overrides` clamp + `coerce_yaml_value`'s `str_fields` set. `global_config.yaml` gets a new `trace:` section documenting the default + the valid enum + the fail-soft policy. Loader stays permissive (any string accepted at config-load time); planner / 10.5 skill validate against `{"low", "medium", "high"}` downstream with fail-soft fallback to `"medium"` (mirrors how `llm_provider` is handled). Full suite: **705 / 705 green** (was 649; +56). No FE changes, no schema changes, no new optional dependencies. **What 10.4 explicitly does *not* ship:** the LLM-tier `trace_behavior` skill that consumes the planner output and persists `BehaviorAnchor` to `trace.sqlite` (10.5); FieldRead → `patch_field` template (Java reflection-based field patching is messy in Frida — instance-vs-static, getter patterns, the right time to patch — Plan B + the operator-driven `generate_frida_hook` path cover the v1 use cases); two-register predicate flip (slicer's priority rule returns one operand; the other is unknown to the planner — LLM may suggest a hand-rolled hook); object-side non-null synthesis (Plan A handles the "force null" direction but punts non-null — operator authors via Manual Hooks); switch decisions (per-case forced literals are non-trivial — operator hand-crafts via Manual Hooks); REST routes (10.6); Trace-mode frontend (10.7). No edits to `smali_parser.py` / `dispatch.py` / `call_graph.py` or any SQLite schema.
    - **10.3 Heuristic branch outcome classifier — DONE** (2026-04-29): New module `androscan/analysis/branch_classifier.py` — pure deterministic per-branch deny/allow/neutral classification. Public surface: `classify_branch_outcomes(method_decisions) -> MethodDecisions` (frozen-dataclass-replace, mirrors 10.2's API exactly so 10.5 can chain `parse_decisions → slice_predicate_origins → classify_branch_outcomes` without intermediate bookkeeping) plus `classify_one_decision(decision, instructions, label_index)` for tests / 10.5's per-anchor walk. Two new dataclasses added to `androscan/analysis/trace_types.py`: `BranchVerdict(branch_label, verdict, score, reasons)` and `BranchOutcome(verdicts, confidence, reasons)`. `DecisionPoint` extended with `branch_outcome: Optional[BranchOutcome] = None` (additive default → all 29 of 10.1's + 10.2's tests still pass unchanged; verified before any 10.3 test was authored). **Heuristic catalog (locked v1):** strong DENY (-1.0): `throw vN` / `Ljava/lang/System;->exit` / `Landroid/os/Process;->killProcess`. Strong ALLOW (+1.0): `L...;->setResult(...)` (any args). Moderate DENY (-0.7): `L...;->finish()V` / `finishAffinity` / `finishAndRemoveTask` *only when no preceding `setResult` in the same basic block* (the legitimate "report a result then close" allow-flow is suppressed via order-aware walk); `const-string vN, "..."` whose literal matches the curated deny-keyword regex. Moderate ALLOW (+0.7): `L...;->startActivity(ForResult)?(...)`. Weak DENY (-0.3): branch-length ratio ≥ 3:1 with shorter branch ≤ 3 instructions, applied *only* when both branches are otherwise neutral (the early-exit-gate pattern). Restricted to 2-branch ifs — switches use per-case verdicts directly, no cross-branch ratio. **Verdict thresholds:** `score ≤ -0.5 → "deny"`, `score ≥ +0.5 → "allow"`, else `"neutral"`. **Confidence tiers (deterministic, pinned by tests):** based on `max(|branch.score|)` across all branches — `≥ 1.0 → 1.00` (strong), `≥ 0.7 → 0.85` (moderate / string-keyword), `≥ 0.3 → 0.45` (weak only — *below* the `LLM_RECLASSIFY_THRESHOLD = 0.6`; 10.5's `trace_behavior` skill will invoke the LLM to re-classify), else `0.00` (no signals — LLM re-classifies). Pre-classification, `branch_outcome` is `None`; post-classification it's always populated (even when verdicts are all neutral and confidence is 0.0) so 10.5 distinguishes "didn't run the classifier" from "ran it and found nothing". **Curated deny-keyword regex** extends DEC-024's spec `\b(premium|locked|jailbroken|unauthori[sz]ed|forbidden|denied|tamper|root|debug)\b` with two strict-additive improvements: word boundaries around the alternation prevent partial-word false positives (`rootView` → no match for `root`, `debugger` → matches via the explicit `(?:ger|ging)?` suffix); optional `(?:ed|ing)?` suffixes on `tamper` / `root` / `debug` so canonical pentest vocabulary like `rooted` / `tampered` matches (without these, the spec would miss "Device is rooted" — the textbook example). Both extensions are operator-facing improvements that strictly enlarge the match set; v2 may add more pentest vocabulary (`frida`, `xposed`, `emulator`, etc.) per operator feedback. **Basic-block walker** stops at the first terminator (`return*` / `throw` / `goto*` / `if-*` / `*-switch`) — the terminator IS part of the block since `throw` is itself a deny signal. Fall-through branches (`target_label is None` for `if-*` false branch + switch `default`) start at `decision_instruction_index + 1`. Unresolved labels → empty block → 0-score → neutral verdict (defensive; well-formed apktool output never hits this). **Tests.** **+19 tests** in `tests/test_branch_classifier.py` (slightly over the planned 18 budget): `branch_outcome` defaults to None on 10.2 output (pre-classify baseline contract); `classify_branch_outcomes` doesn't mutate input (frozen-replace contract); per-signal assertions for throw / System.exit / killProcess (strong DENY, score -1.0, confidence 1.0) + setResult (strong ALLOW) + startActivity (moderate ALLOW, 0.7); `finish` without `setResult` yields moderate DENY (-0.7, confidence 0.85); `setResult` preceding `finish` suppresses the finish signal (net score +1.0 — pure ALLOW, no "finish without setResult" reason in the verdict's reasons list); string-keyword matches for `rooted` (suffix-aware), `PREMIUM` (case-insensitive), `unauthorised` (British spelling); `rootView` substring NOT matched by `\broot(?:ed|ing)?\b` (word-boundary contract — neutral verdict, no signal); length-ratio fixture (1 instr vs 8 instr, ratio 8:1) yields weak DENY (-0.3) on shorter side, NEUTRAL on longer side, confidence 0.45 *below* the LLM threshold (asserted explicitly via `LLM_RECLASSIFY_THRESHOLD` constant), method-level reason surfaces the cross-branch ratio; symmetric-no-signals → NEUTRAL/NEUTRAL/0.0; packed-switch with throw/setResult/neutral cases + neutral default classifies each case independently (case 0 DENY, case 1 ALLOW, case 2 + default NEUTRAL) with confidence 1.0 from case 0's strong throw signal; clean two-branch DENY/ALLOW split (throw on one side, setResult on the other) — both verdicts non-neutral, both scores ±1.0, confidence 1.0; **confidence-tier pinning test** asserts the four documented values (1.00 / 0.85 / 0.45 / 0.00) one-per-fixture so 10.5 / 10.7 can rely on them as a stable contract; `BranchOutcome` JSON round-trip via `dataclasses.asdict` + `json.dumps` (the contract 10.5's `trace.sqlite` cache + 10.6's wire format both depend on). New fixture `tests/fixtures/trace_smali/smali/com/trace/Outcomes.smali` (15 methods exercising one heuristic each + a constructor; hand-crafted so each method's expected verdict + score + confidence is fully deterministic). One pre-existing 10.1 test loosened (`summary.switches == 2 → >= 2`) since the new `Outcomes.smali` introduces a third packed-switch — original intent was "at least the packed + sparse from SwitchCases", lower bound keeps the assertion robust against fixture growth. Full suite: **649 / 649 green** (was 630; +19). No FE changes, no schema changes, no new optional dependencies. **What 10.3 explicitly does *not* ship:** cross-method analysis (basic-block walker stays inside one method), inter-procedural string flow (only immediate-block `const-string` literals are matched against the keyword regex; strings from resources / fields / runtime decoding aren't followed), locale-aware string matching (regex is English-only — DEC-024 v1; localised gates correctly fall to LLM re-classification in 10.5), `setResult(RESULT_OK)` vs `setResult(RESULT_CANCELED)` discrimination (both currently signal "result reported"; 10.5's LLM can refine via the const slice that precedes the invoke).
    - **10.2 Backward slicing for predicate origin — DONE** (2026-04-28): New module `androscan/analysis/slicing.py` — pure, intra-procedural backward slicer that walks each `DecisionPoint`'s `predicate_registers` backward through the method body to its closest defining instruction. Public surface: `slice_predicate_origins(method_decisions, *, max_walk=64) -> MethodDecisions` (frozen-dataclass-replace, no input mutation) plus a single-decision helper `slice_one_decision(decision, instructions)` for 10.5's per-anchor walk. Five `*Origin` dataclasses added to `androscan/analysis/trace_types.py` per DEC-024: `MethodCallOrigin(method, invoke_kind)`, `FieldReadOrigin(field, is_static)`, `ConstOrigin(value, smali_op)`, `ParamOrigin(register)`, `CompositeOrigin(reason)`. Each carries a `kind` discriminator field so `dataclasses.asdict` + `json.dumps` round-trips the union variant unambiguously — 10.5's `trace.sqlite` cache + 10.6's wire format both deserialise via a `kind`-keyed dispatch with no custom encoder. `DecisionPoint` extended with `predicate_origin: Optional[PredicateOrigin] = None` (additive default → 10.1's tests still pass unchanged). Slicer behaviour: follows `move(-wide|-object)?(/from16|/16)?` chains transparently; `move-result-*` looks back one step to the preceding `invoke-*` and emits `MethodCallOrigin` from its target signature; `iget-*` / `sget-*` emit `FieldReadOrigin` with `is_static` set per opcode; `const(-*)?` emits `ConstOrigin` with the raw literal text preserved verbatim (`"premium"` not `premium`); `move-exception` and any unrecognised defining opcode (arithmetic / `instance-of` / `cmp-long` / `array-length` / `new-instance` / casts / etc.) emit `CompositeOrigin(reason=opcode)`. Skip-set excludes `iput-*` / `sput-*` / `aput-*` / `invoke-*` / `goto*` / `return*` / `throw` / `monitor-*` / `check-cast` / `nop` / `fill-array-data` / `filled-new-array*` (these don't define registers). Walk exhaustion via `max_walk` (default 64) → `predicate_origin = None` honestly per DEC-024 rather than a fabricated origin. Walk reaches start-of-method body with a `pN` register tracked → `ParamOrigin`; with a `vN` register tracked (uninitialised — defensive) → `None`. **Two-register predicate combination policy (DEC-024 v1):** for `if-eq` / `if-ne` / `if-lt` / `if-le` / `if-gt` / `if-ge`, the slicer slices each operand independently then combines via priority `MethodCall > FieldRead > Param > Const > Composite > None`; the more-actionable side wins (operators want to manipulate the non-constant operand of value comparisons). Ties break to the LHS so source order produces a stable result. v2 may widen `predicate_origin` to a `tuple[PredicateOrigin, ...]` when both sides matter equally. **Small amendment to `decisions.py`:** `MethodDecisions` gained a new optional `instructions: tuple[str, ...] = ()` field carrying the raw stripped text of every "real" instruction line indexed by `instruction_index`; populated via a tiny `_emit_instruction(raw)` helper in `_MethodState` that's now the single source of truth for the index counter and the instructions list. Memory cost: only methods with at least one branch retain their bodies — typical APK is a few hundred KB. This is what enables the slicer to be a pure function over `MethodDecisions` without re-reading files. **Tests.** **+16 tests** in `tests/test_decisions_slicing.py`: `predicate_origin` defaults to None on 10.1 output; `instructions` is populated and points at if-* / switch lines at the right indices; per-variant slicer assertions for MethodCallOrigin / FieldReadOrigin (iget) / FieldReadOrigin (sget) / ConstOrigin (int) / ConstOrigin (string with literal preservation) / ParamOrigin / CompositeOrigin (arithmetic) / CompositeOrigin (instance-of) / CompositeOrigin (move-exception); move chain `iget v1 → move v0, v1 → if-eqz v0` resolves to the underlying FieldRead (slicer follows aliases); two-register `iget vs const` returns FieldRead via the priority rule; two-register both-MethodCall returns the LHS via tie-breaking; `max_walk=2` exhaustion returns None (and the same method with `max_walk=64` resolves to ConstOrigin); JSON round-trip per variant (5 variants × 1 assertion each) verifies the `kind` discriminator is preserved. New fixture `tests/fixtures/trace_smali/smali/com/trace/Slices.smali` (~13 methods + helpers, hand-crafted so each origin kind has a known terminus). All 13 of 10.1's tests still green after the `decisions.py` amendment (verified). Full suite **630 / 630 green** (was 614; +16). No FE changes, no schema changes, no new optional dependencies. **What 10.2 explicitly does *not* ship:** branch outcome classification (10.3), bypass plan synthesis (10.4), aliasing / field-flow / escape analysis (DEC-024 limitation), inter-procedural slicing (v2), SSA / phi-node reconstruction across control-flow joins (v2 — most-recent textual definition wins). No edits to `smali_parser.py` / `dispatch.py` / `call_graph.py` or any SQLite schema.
    - **10.1 Decision-point extraction — DONE** (2026-04-28): Two new modules under `androscan/analysis/`. **`trace_types.py`** ships the platform-neutral data model per DEC-024: `MethodRef` and `FieldRef` (canonical references with Java-form names + raw Smali descriptors; `smali_signature` round-trip joins against `call_graph.nodes`); `DecisionKind` (string-valued enum covering all 12 if-* opcodes + packed/sparse switch); `Branch` (operator-readable label + `target_label: Optional[str]` where `None` means fall-through); `DecisionPoint` (the deliverable of 10.1 — `method` / `instruction_index` / `source_line` / `kind` / `predicate_registers` / `branches`); `BehaviorAnchor` and `BypassPlan` shipped as minimal shells locking the field names 10.4 / 10.5 will populate without breaking the data-model contract. **`decisions.py`** is a third Smali pass over the same apktool tree `smali_parser.py` already walks twice (`parse_classes` + `parse_invokes` are unchanged). `parse_decisions(roots, classes) -> (list[MethodDecisions], DecisionsParseSummary)` emits one `MethodDecisions` per method with at least one branch (linear methods are excluded — callers join against the full method list when they need enumeration). Switch data blocks (`:pswitch_data_0` + `.packed-switch` / `:sswitch_data_0` + `.sparse-switch`) are parsed inline via a deferred-back-fill pattern (`_pending_switches` dict in `_MethodState`) so each switch decision's `branches` are populated with one `Branch` per case label + a `("default", None)` fall-through; the per-method `label_index: tuple[(label_name, instruction_index), ...]` lets 10.3's basic-block walker resolve any branch target without re-parsing. Instruction indexing is conservative (lowercase-opcode lines only; `.directives`, `:labels`, `# comments`, blank lines do not advance) so labels point at real next-instruction indices. Fail-soft: malformed switch blocks → `_DecisionParseError` caught at file level, surfaced via `DecisionsParseSummary.parse_errors`, rest of the file keeps parsing. **No edits to `smali_parser.py`, `dispatch.py`, `call_graph.py`, or any SQLite schema** — additive only. **Tests.** New `tests/test_decisions_extract.py` (**13 tests**): MethodRef + FieldRef round-trip + ValueError on malformed input; linear method excluded from output; summary counters match emitted decisions; `if-eqz` 2-branch shape with correct register + true-target + fall-through; all six zero-comparison kinds covered in one method; all six two-register kinds covered with predicate-register tuples; source-line attachment tracks most recent `.line` directive; label_index resolves branch targets to next-instruction indices; packed-switch emits one branch per case + default; sparse-switch preserves original key strings (`"case 0xa"`); multi-dex coverage via `smali_classes2/HiddenGate.smali`; `DecisionPoint` round-trips through `dataclasses.asdict` + `json.dumps` (the contract 10.5's cache + 10.6's wire format both depend on). Three new fixture .smali files under `tests/fixtures/trace_smali/` (`smali/com/trace/Gates.smali`, `smali/com/trace/SwitchCases.smali`, `smali_classes2/com/trace/HiddenGate.smali`). Full suite: **614 / 614 green** (was 601; +13 tests). No FE changes. **What 10.1 explicitly does *not* ship:** backward slicing for predicate origin (10.2), heuristic deny/allow/neutral classification (10.3), bypass plan synthesis (10.4), `trace_behavior` LLM skill + `trace.sqlite` cache (10.5), REST routes + tab-shell rename (10.6), Trace-mode frontend (10.7), Mirror → Trace integration + docs sweep (10.8). No new optional dependencies; no edits to existing modules.

Q2 (A) depth-only pill — UI surfaces just the count ("via 2 helper method(s)"), no full-path tracking (richer tooltip listing methods walked through deferred to v3). Q3 (A) surgical `StrReplace` YAML edits — bypasses `loader.py.dump_to_yaml` so ISSUE-015 (Settings Save reformats YAML) doesn't bite; preserves comments + section ordering. Q4 (A) automated v1-vs-v2 corpus measurement test — locks the regression floor in CI rather than waiting for real-app dogfood data. **Slicer depth-tagging** (`androscan/analysis/slicing.py`, ~60 LOC delta over the 11.5 base): `_DescentBudget` gains `current_descent_depth: int = 0` (mirrors `remaining_depth` but in opposite direction — incremented on entering `_descend_into_callee` / `_walk_field_write_sites`, decremented in their `finally` blocks so sibling decisions in the same caller restart at depth 0). New `_tag_descent_depth(origin, budget)` helper applies `dataclasses.replace(origin, descent_depth=budget.current_descent_depth)` to `MethodCallOrigin` / `FieldReadOrigin` cap-stop terminals; idempotent (returns origin unchanged if already tagged at the same depth) so success-path unwinds don't double-tag. Every early-exit branch in `_maybe_descend_method_call` (budget=0 / cycle / external / not stateless / inner-failed) and `_maybe_descend_field_read` (budget=0 / no current_class / cross-class / cycle / inner-failed) now routes through `_tag_descent_depth` before returning. **Schema additions** (`androscan/analysis/trace_types.py`): `MethodCallOrigin.descent_depth: int = 0` + `FieldReadOrigin.descent_depth: int = 0` (both with detailed docstring notes on the Phase 11 / DEC-025 origin + frontend depth-pill semantics + the schema-bump rationale). **Cache schema bump** (`androscan/internal/trace_cache.py`): `SCHEMA_VERSION` `"1"` → `"2"` with a multi-paragraph schema-version-history comment block. v1-cached anchors silently re-build on first 11.x open via the existing route-layer "missing → build" fallback (`get_status` already returns `error="schema_version mismatch"` on a v1 read; no migration code, no UI banner). **Config schema** (`androscan/constants.py` + `androscan/config/loader.py`): two new `Config` fields wired through `CONFIG_FIELD_MAP` + `LIVE_RELOADABLE_FIELDS` + `Config` dataclass + `Config.default()` + `_merge_from_yaml` + `load_config()` + `with_overrides()` + `coerce_yaml_value()`: (1) `Config.ollama_num_ctx: int = 16384` (env: `ANDROSCAN_OLLAMA_NUM_CTX`; bumps Ollama's default 8192 context window to absorb v2's ~2× input prompt growth from deeper PredicateOrigin chains); (2) `Config.trace_max_slice_depth: int = 2` (env: `ANDROSCAN_TRACE_MAX_SLICE_DEPTH`; mirrors `slicing.MAX_SLICE_DEPTH`; clamped to `slicing.HARD_CAP_DEPTH = 4` defensively in `_DescentBudget.fresh`). Also bumped `OLLAMA_NUM_PREDICT_DEFAULT` from 8192 → 12288 (DEC-025 spec — absorbs ~1.5× response payload growth) + `OLLAMA_NUM_PREDICT_TIERS` from `[8192, 16384]` → `[12288, 16384]`. **LLM client** (`androscan/llm/client.py`): both `_stream_ollama` and `_complete_ollama` now forward `num_ctx` to Ollama's `options` dict alongside the existing `temperature` / `num_predict`. **Skill wiring** (`androscan/skills/trace_behavior.py`): replaces hardcoded `_DescentBudget.fresh()` with `_DescentBudget.fresh(max_depth=int(getattr(config, "trace_max_slice_depth", slicing.MAX_SLICE_DEPTH)))` — `getattr` fallback preserves test fixtures using a stripped Config. **`global_config.yaml` surgical inserts** (Q3 (A)): three new keys via direct `StrReplace` edits — `ollama.num_ctx: 16384` insert, `ollama.num_predict: 8192 → 12288` bump, `trace.max_slice_depth: 2` insert — plus accompanying explanatory comment blocks; bypasses `loader.py.dump_to_yaml` entirely so ISSUE-015 (Settings Save reformats YAML) doesn't auto-clobber. Smoke-tested via `yaml.safe_load` + `load_config()` round-trip. **Frontend** (`androscan/web/frontend/src/components/trace/PredicateOriginView.tsx` + `App.css` + `TraceBanners.tsx` + `api/trace.ts`): new `DepthPill` sub-component renders `via N helper method(s)` (for `MethodCallOrigin`) or `via N field write(s)` (for `FieldReadOrigin`) when the slicer's `descent_depth >= 1`, with a `title` tooltip explaining the depth (Q2 (A) — depth-only, no path tracking).
