CSS bundle +~0.5 KB gzipped. **Operator impact:** every cap-stop / cycle-blocked / external-blocked terminal carries a "via N helpers" pill so operators see at a glance whether v2 descent fired and how deep it went; the LLM has wider context (16K) + output (12K) headroom for v2's deeper chains; the cache schema bump invalidates v1 anchors so first-open after deploy gets fresh v2-shape payloads. **ISSUE-013 status: Resolved (Phase 11 v2)** in `docs/KNOWN_ISSUES.md` — corpus regression floor locked via the v1-vs-v2 measurement test; the > 50% production-dogfood threshold from DEC-025 still pending real-app verification. **LLM prompt-budget revisit:** measure-first approach (no preemptive `_format_decision_for_llm` tightening) — chose to ship the bumped budgets and observe whether real-app traces actually squeeze the per-anchor token budget; tighten in 11.7 or a follow-up if measurement shows the bumps weren't enough. **Spec deferrals:** Q1 (B) all-variants depth tagging (would carry depth on success-path Const/Param/Composite terminals — operator argument exists but spec literal won), Q2 (B) descent path tracking (richer tooltip listing methods walked through — depth-only pill is simpler v1 UX), Q3 (B) schema-only YAML changes (preferred surgical inserts since ISSUE-015 doesn't auto-clobber comment blocks added directly to YAML). **Next sub-step:** 11.7 — docs sweep + DEC-025 closing note (Phase 11 v2 wrap-up). CI.0 (Phase 4 CI) remains a parallel non-blocking thread.
    - **11.5 Tier 2 (d) part 2 — Same-class field-write-site walking — DONE** (2026-04-30): Second half of ISSUE-013 close-out (the first half was 11.4's bounded inter-procedural method descent). **Slicer extension** (`androscan/analysis/slicing.py`, +~280 LOC): refactored `_maybe_descend` into a dispatcher that fans out by origin variant — `_maybe_descend_method_call` (11.4's existing path, unchanged behaviour) for `MethodCallOrigin`, new `_maybe_descend_field_read` for `FieldReadOrigin`, terminal variants are no-ops. Added new `_walk_field_write_sites(origin, *, current_class_smali, budget, ...)` walker that finds the most-recent write to the read field per the **constructor-priority rule** (Q1 (A)) — collects every matching `iput-*` (instance) or `sput-*` (static) write site across the class's methods, partitions into `<init>`/`<clinit>` vs other methods, prefers the textually-last write within the constructor partition (matches operator intuition that the last `<init>` assignment is what the field's value will be after construction), falls back to the textually-last non-constructor write only when no constructor write exists. Inner re-slice runs `_slice_register` on the written register at the chosen write site and recurses through `_maybe_descend` so a field-write source that itself resolves to a `MethodCallOrigin` or another `FieldReadOrigin` can be further descended (subject to the shared `_DescentBudget` — closed economy with 11.4's method descent). New `_RE_IPUT` / `_RE_SPUT` regexes parse the source register out of `iput vSrc, vObj, ...` / `sput vSrc, ...` lines (note: `vSrc` is the source register being written, opposite role to iget's vDest). New `_class_from_method_sig` + `_java_class_to_smali` local helpers thread `current_class_smali` through the descent chain — needed for the same-class gate per Q2 (A) strict policy. Field cycle key `(class_smali, field_name, type_descriptor)` lives in the same `budget.visited` set as method-descent cycle keys; the third tuple slot ("descriptor" for methods, "type" for fields) is structurally distinct (parens-wrapped vs bare token) so the two never collide. **Same-class gating** (Q2 (A) strict): `field.class_name → smali` must == `current_class_smali`; no superclass walking in v1 (deferred to v3 / Phase 12 if telemetry shows demand). **Receiver-agnostic matching** (Q3 (A)): write sites matched by `(field_class_smali, field_name)` only — `vObj` ignored. The textbook `this.mField` field-cached idiom is correct under this rule (only-write-site scenario); aggressive aliasing cases may pick a write to a different instance's field. **Lazy walk per descent** (Q4 (A)): no eager indexing pass; walk runs on every field-read descent (bounded by closed-economy budget — max 2 descents in v1; promote to indexed lookup in 11.7 only if megaclass cost surfaces). **Closed-economy budget composition**: a top-level slice with `MAX_SLICE_DEPTH = 2` budget can compose any of (2 method hops; 2 field-write hops; 1 field + 1 method; 1 method + 1 field) — verified end-to-end via the `gateFieldWriteFromMethodCall` and `gateFieldWriteFromDeepChain` fixtures. **Method-call dispatch's `_descend_into_callee`** updated to thread `current_class_smali` of the *callee's* class into the inner `_maybe_descend` (the inner slice runs over the callee's body, so its same-class field-write check must be against the callee's class — explicit semantic clarification in the docstring + inline comment). **Fixture additions** (`tests/fixtures/trace_smali/smali/com/trace/Helpers.smali`, +7 methods + 5 fields): instance field-cached predicate (`gateInstanceFieldRead` + `mPremiumFlag` written in `<init>`); static field-cached via `<clinit>` (`gateStaticFieldRead` + `sFeatureEnabled`); multi-write field constructor-priority test (`gateMultiWriteFieldRead` + `mMultiWriteFlag` written in BOTH `<init>` (0x1) AND `initMultiWriteFlag` (0x0) — Q1 (A) picks 0x1); cross-class field read (`gateCrossClassFieldRead` reading `Lcom/trace/Slices;->sFlag:Z`); declared-but-never-written field (`gateUnwrittenFieldRead` + `mNeverWritten`); field-write-from-method-call composition (`gateFieldWriteFromMethodCall` + `mFromMethod` written from `pureGetA()` return — exercises 1 field + 1 method hop = 2 hops total, exactly at the v1 budget cap); deep-chain budget exhaustion (`gateFieldWriteFromDeepChain` + `mFromDeepChain` written from `pureChainHopOne()` which itself chains 3 method hops — descent stops at `pureChainHopTwo` per the budget cap). Existing `<init>` extended to write `mMultiWriteFlag = 0x1` so the constructor-priority test has a real candidate. **Tests** (`tests/test_decisions_slicing.py` — +8, 51 total slicer tests green): instance field via `<init>` resolves to ConstOrigin "0x1"; static field via `<clinit>` resolves to ConstOrigin "0x1"; constructor-priority wins over setter (asserts ConstOrigin "0x1" not "0x0"); cross-class field descent blocked (FieldReadOrigin preserved); no-write-site graceful failure (FieldReadOrigin preserved); closed-economy 1-field+1-method composition (terminal at MethodCallOrigin "pureGetB" at depth-2 cap); closed-economy budget exhaustion mid-chain (terminal at MethodCallOrigin "pureChainHopTwo"); v1 backwards-compat path preserved when descent kwargs omitted. **Verification:** all 856 non-device pytest tests pass (848 baseline + 8 new); ReadLints clean; all 99 existing 10.x slicer + skill tests still green; all 27 11.4 descent tests still green (the v1→11.4→11.5 layering is purely additive — 11.4's `_maybe_descend_method_call` was extracted unchanged from the dispatcher refactor). **No skill-side wiring changes needed**: the `trace_behavior` skill already passes `classes_by_smali` + `decisions_by_method_sig` + the shared `_DescentBudget` from the 11.4 wiring; field-write descent is reached via the unchanged `_maybe_descend` entry point. No new SQLite schema, no new config knobs (deferred to 11.6 alongside the LLM budget bumps + `trace.max_slice_depth`). **Operator impact:** every decision whose v1 slicer terminated at a `FieldReadOrigin` for a same-class instance / static field with a constructor-init write now resolves to the deeper terminal (typically `ConstOrigin` for one-time-init flags — the textbook ISSUE-013 false-negative class). Combined with 11.4, the pipeline now resolves the two largest false-negative categories ISSUE-013 documented (helper-method-wrapped predicates + constructor-init field-cached predicates). **Spec deferrals:** superclass walking (Q2 (B)), receiver-aware iput matching (Q3 (B)), eager field-write indexing (Q4 (B)) all stay v3 / Phase 12 candidates — adopt only if telemetry shows demand on real apps. **Next sub-step:** 11.6 — bypass-planner re-run + LLM-tier `trace_behavior` budget revisit + cache schema bump (`trace.sqlite` `SCHEMA_VERSION` 1 → 2) + ISSUE-013 close-out + `trace.max_slice_depth` config knob + frontend depth-pill UI. CI.0 (Phase 4 CI) remains a parallel non-blocking thread.
    - **11.4 Tier 2 (d) part 1 — Bounded inter-procedural slicer descent + type-driven `is_stateless` analyzer — DONE** (2026-04-30): Largest behavioural change of v2 — closes the first half of ISSUE-013 (the second half is 11.5's same-class field-write-site walking). **Slicer extension** (`androscan/analysis/slicing.py`, +~330 LOC over the v1 369 LOC base): (1) New module constants `MAX_SLICE_DEPTH = 2` (config knob `trace.max_slice_depth` deferred to 11.6 per planning-checkpoint Q4) + `HARD_CAP_DEPTH = 4` (defensive ceiling regardless of operator config). (2) New `_DescentBudget` mutable `@dataclass` with `(remaining_depth: int, visited: set[tuple[str, str, str]])` — mutability per planning-checkpoint Q1 ("closed economy" semantics in DEC-025 implies the same instance flows across passes); `.fresh(max_depth)` factory clamps to `[0, HARD_CAP_DEPTH]`. (3) New `_STATELESS_LIB_DENYLIST: dict[str, frozenset[str] | None]` constant — three-valued lookup (whole-class entry = `None`; per-method allowlist = `frozenset`; not in dict = unknown / fall back to body-walking). v1 seed per planning-checkpoint Q2: `Ljava/lang/Math;` (whole), 8 primitive boxing classes (whole), `Ljava/lang/String;` getter allowlist (length / charAt / substring / equals / hashCode / isEmpty / indexOf — `concat` / `replace` etc. excluded since they allocate), `Lkotlin/jvm/internal/Intrinsics;` (whole), and the planning-checkpoint addition on top of the spec's seed: `Ljava/lang/Object;` getter allowlist (hashCode / equals / toString / getClass — extremely common in `if obj.equals(...)` predicates). (4) New `is_stateless(method_sig, *, classes_by_smali, decisions_by_method_sig, reflective_method_sigs, visited)` type-driven analyzer (~120 LOC) — walks the method's instruction stream looking for side effects: `iput-*` / `sput-*` / `aput-*` (field / array writes), `monitor-*` (synchronization — defensively stateful per the spec), `throw vN` (exception throw), `invoke-*` to non-stateless callees (recursive — reuses the visited set; cycles classify as stateful per the v2 defensive default — "if we hit a cycle without confirming statelessness, don't trust it"); methods flagged `may_have_unresolved_reflection` are stateful regardless of body shape. Constructor calls (`<init>` / `<clinit>`) are treated as stateless on the analyzer's side (they don't write the caller's state — any escape would already be caught by the caller's own `iput-object`). When the body isn't in `decisions_by_method_sig` (linear stdlib helper), falls back to the deny-list; when the deny-list also doesn't know the method, returns False (defensive — descending into a method we can't see is the bad case). (5) New `_descend_into_callee(origin, *, budget, ...)` helper — finds the callee body's `return-*` (with source register; `return-void` skips), marks the callee visited + decrements budget, calls `_slice_register` on the return source register, and recurses via `_maybe_descend` on any inner `MethodCallOrigin`. Restores the depth counter on the way out so a *sibling* decision in the same caller method can still descend into a different helper (the visited entry stays — same callee never re-descended in one top-level slice). (6) New `_maybe_descend(origin, *, budget, ...)` orchestrator — gates on `MethodCallOrigin` + budget + visited + classes_by_smali (external bail) + `is_stateless`; falls back to the original on any failure (operator never sees a worse terminal than v1). (7) `slice_predicate_origins` public API extended with four optional kwargs (`classes_by_smali`, `decisions_by_method_sig`, `reflective_method_sigs`, `descent_budget`) — when `classes_by_smali` AND `decisions_by_method_sig` are both supplied, descent is enabled; when either is None, slicer behaves exactly like v1 (every existing 10.x test still passes against the unchanged signature path). **Decompile parser extension** (`androscan/analysis/decisions.py`): `parse_decisions(..., include_branchless=True)` now emits a `MethodDecisions` for every method (with empty `decision_points` for branchless methods) — required so the slicer's descent can reach pure helper-method bodies (a `return-only` getter has no decisions of its own but its body is exactly what descent needs to see). Default behaviour preserved (no test breaks; the v1 contract of "only methods with decisions" is opt-out). **Skill wiring** (`androscan/skills/trace_behavior.py`): Calls `parse_decisions` with `include_branchless=True`, builds `classes_by_smali` from the parsed `ClassDecl`s, fetches `reflective_method_sigs` via the new `call_graph.list_reflective_method_sigs(cache_dir)` helper (a small SQLite read over `nodes WHERE may_have_unresolved_reflection = 1`), and instantiates a single shared `_DescentBudget.fresh()` that's reused across every per-decision slice in the closure (closed economy — same hub helper visited via decision A isn't redundantly re-descended via decision B). Branchless methods explicitly `continue`'d in the per-closure loop so the v1 aggregation behaviour is byte-identical. **New helper** (`androscan/analysis/call_graph.py`): `list_reflective_method_sigs(decompile_cache_dir) -> frozenset[str]` — returns every method `smali_id` flagged `may_have_unresolved_reflection`, empty frozenset on missing / unreadable DB so the caller can pass it directly to the slicer without branching. **New fixture** (`tests/fixtures/trace_smali/smali/com/trace/Helpers.smali`, ~26 methods): exercises every descent + statelessness path — single-hop getter (`gateOneHopGetter` → `pureGetFlag`); two-hop chain (`gateTwoHopChain` → `pureGetA` → `pureGetB`); three-hop chain capped at depth 2 (`gateThreeHopChainCapped`); descent blocked by stateful callee (`statefulIputCallee` / `statefulSputCallee` / `statefulAputCallee` / `statefulMonitorCallee` / `statefulThrowCallee`); deny-list short-circuits (`pureStringLength` / `pureMathAbs` / `pureObjectHashCode` / `pureKotlinAreEqual`); deny-list per-method enforcement (`statefulStringConcat` — String.concat NOT in allowlist); pure arithmetic (`pureArithmeticOnly`); cycle (`cycleA <-> cycleB`); cross-class descent (`gateCrossClassDescent` → `Lcom/trace/Slices;->getFlag()Z`); external callee terminates (`gateExternalAndroidCallee` → `Landroid/util/Log;->d(...)I`). **Tests** (`tests/test_decisions_slicing.py` — +27 tests, 43 total slicer tests green): 5 descent positive paths (one-hop getter resolves to ConstOrigin "premium"; two-hop chain resolves to ConstOrigin "0x1"; three-hop chain capped; max_depth=0 disables descent; cross-class via classes_by_smali); 3 descent negative paths (stateful field-write callee preserved as MethodCallOrigin; external callee preserved; v1 backwards-compat when descent kwargs omitted); 6 `is_stateless` direct unit tests (pure const return; iput False; sput False; aput False; monitor False; throw False; pure arithmetic True); 5 deny-list short-circuit tests (Math.abs True; String.length True; Object.hashCode True; Intrinsics.areEqual True; String.concat False); 2 reflection deny tests (is_stateless False on reflective sig; descent blocked when helper is reflective); 1 cycle termination test (cycleA / cycleB both False); 4 `_DescentBudget` semantics tests (visited set prevents redundant re-descent across two slice calls sharing the same budget; `fresh(999)` clamps to HARD_CAP_DEPTH; `fresh(-5)` clamps to 0; module constants `MAX_SLICE_DEPTH == 2` + `HARD_CAP_DEPTH == 4` pinned). **Verification:** all 848 non-device pytest tests pass (821 baseline + 27 new); ReadLints clean; existing 10.x slicer + skill tests all green (99 in the 10.x bucket — descent is a no-op against v1 fixtures since they don't have stateless helper chains, byte-identical aggregated decisions). **Worst-case fixture cost sanity check** (per planning-checkpoint Q3): `Log.d` is in `Landroid/util/Log;` which is `is_external = true` in the call graph — descent terminates immediately on external callees, no quadratic blow-up. Real worst case is an app-internal hub helper called from N decisions in the closure; per-closure cost is bounded by `_DescentBudget.visited` (each unique callee descended at most once per top-level slice) — verified in `test_descent_budget_visited_set_prevents_redundant_redescent`. **Operator impact:** every decision whose v1 slicer terminated at a `MethodCallOrigin` for a stateless in-app helper now resolves to the deeper terminal (typically a `ConstOrigin`, `FieldReadOrigin`, or `ParamOrigin` — which 10.4's bypass planner can target with `force_return_value` directly). Operators see the actionable terminal, not the surface-level call. **Spec deferrals (planning-checkpoint Q4):** `trace.max_slice_depth` config knob deferred to 11.6 per the planning checkpoint — 11.4 ships with the hardcoded module constant only. **Next sub-step:** 11.5 — Tier 2 (d) part 2, same-class field-write-site walking (`_walk_field_write_sites(field_read_origin, *, class_decl, budget) -> PredicateOrigin`); reuses the same `_DescentBudget` (closed economy). CI.0 (Phase 4 CI) remains a parallel non-blocking thread.
    - **11.3 Tier 1 (c) — `BehaviorAnchor`-aware overlay on Manual Hooks Cytoscape pane — DONE** (2026-04-30): Third (and final) operator-UX deliverable from DEC-025's Tier 1 polish bundle, and the **first 11.x sub-step that touches the Python tree**. **Backend.** New route `GET /api/trace/{app_id}/anchored-methods` in `androscan/web/trace_routes.py`, factory-built via the existing `build_trace_router(...)` plumbing (no new DI seams). Returns `{app_id, sha, methods: [{class_smali, method_name, hops, created_at}, ...], total, error}` derived from `trace_cache.list_anchors` + a per-row `read_anchor` payload unpack to enumerate every `decisions[*].method`, deduped on `(class_smali, method_name)` keeping the most-recent `(hops, created_at)` per method (most-recent wins on `created_at`; ties broken by larger `hops` since a deeper trace is more thorough). `MethodRef.class_name` (Java form) is converted to the Smali descriptor inline so the response carries the same descriptor shape the call-graph store keys on. **Status codes per spec:** 404 on unknown `app_id` (via the shared `app_dir_resolver`); 409 when decompile cache isn't ready (mirrors every other route in this module); **404 on "no `trace.sqlite` exists yet"** (the "operator has never built any traces" case — distinguishes from the "built then cleared" case which returns 200 + empty methods list); 200 + `error` field set when at least one cached anchor's payload fails to decode (partial results still returned so the overlay still renders the methods we *can* read). **No new SQLite schema** — the route is a query over the existing `anchors` table and stays correct under 11.6's `SCHEMA_VERSION` bump because `read_anchor` already handles both v1 and v2 payload shapes via the additive-defaults pattern. **Backend tests** (`tests/test_trace_routes.py` — +7 tests, 22 total trace-route tests green): unbuilt-cache 404, unknown-app 404, decompile-not-ready 409, happy path after build (asserts `class_smali` form, `hops`, `created_at`), empty-after-clear 200, dedupe across two anchors that share methods (`gateBoolPredicate` + `gateIntPredicate` from the `Plans.smali` fixture), payload-decode-failure surfaces `error` field via direct `sqlite3` UPDATE corrupting the row's `payload_json`, route-registered smoke. Full pytest suite green: 821 passed. **Frontend.** `androscan/web/frontend/src/api/trace.ts` gains a `listAnchoredMethods(appId)` client + new `AnchoredMethod` / `AnchoredMethodsResponse` types. `CallGraphView.tsx` gains an optional `anchoredMethods?: ReadonlyMap<string, AnchoredMethodMeta> | null` prop (mirrors the `hitsByMethod` pattern, uses the same `hitKey(className, methodName)` join helper for cohesion); when non-null and a method node's `(class.class_name, method_name)` is in the map, the node gets a new `has-trace-anchor` Cytoscape class (small purple corner badge — inline SVG of a filled `TRACE_PURPLE` circle as `background-image` positioned top-right) and an extra tooltip line `<purple ⚓> trace cached: <hops> hop(s) · <created_at relative time>`. The new Cytoscape stylesheet rule is layered AFTER the `.hit` / `.has-hits` rules per spec so the bold-cyan Frida overlay still wins where both apply (Frida hits beat trace anchors visually because they're live data — DEC-023 / 4.8 sets the same precedence rule); rule is carefully scoped to non-conflicting `background-image` properties so the cascade doesn't fight `.hit`'s border-color/shadow even with later-rule-wins ordering. New `TRACE_PURPLE = "#9d6dff"` colour token shared between the Cytoscape SVG (via `encodeURIComponent`) and the tooltip's `.cy-trace-anchored-glyph` CSS rule (same hex literal, kept in lockstep by code review since the two surfaces live in different rendering layers and there's no shared module they can both pull from). `LabTab.tsx`'s `ManualHooksMode` fetches the anchored set on `appId` change via `useEffect` + `listAnchoredMethods`, builds the `Map<string, AnchoredMethodMeta>` in a `useMemo` keyed on `hitKey(<smali-to-java-converted class>, method_name)`, and threads it through `<CallGraphView />`. Refresh on every successful trace build / delete is handled implicitly by the existing conditional-rendering pattern: `ManualHooksMode` is only mounted when `labMode === "manual-hooks"` (LabTab unmounts it otherwise), so any trace cache mutation that happens in Trace mode is naturally visible the next time the operator switches back — the mode-switch re-mounts the component and fires the effect fresh. 404 from the route (unbuilt cache) and any non-200 transient error both collapse to overlay-off (treat-as-`null`) rather than flickering a banner. **NOTE on two spec-vs-reality divergences (both inline-commented in source for an explicit audit trail):** (1) the 11.3 row in `TASKS.md` types the `anchoredMethods` prop as `ReadonlySet<string>` (membership-only), but the same row mandates a tooltip that needs per-method `hops` + `created_at` metadata — we use a `Map` so the tooltip text actually works (the spec's "Set" framing is shorthand for "key-only for membership tests"); (2) the spec references "the existing `formatRelativeTime` helper" but no such helper exists in the codebase today — we add a small local `formatRelativeAge(epochSeconds)` helper next to `hitKey` (output: `just now` / `Ns ago` / `Nm ago` / `Nh ago` / `Nd ago` / `Nw ago`) so the tooltip stays compact (a tooltip badge has no room for a full ISO datetime). On the **Cytoscape badge glyph itself**, we use a small purple filled circle as `background-image` rather than the literal ⚓ character because rendering a unicode emoji via SVG `<text>` is OS-dependent (the emoji font often substitutes its own colour palette and ignores the SVG's `fill` attribute) — the literal ⚓ is preserved in the HTML tooltip where `.cy-trace-anchored-glyph` gives full CSS colour control. The combined visual reads as "purple corner badge + purple ⚓ in the tooltip" — same semantic, two surfaces. **Package-overview mode:** `buildPackageOverviewElements` accepts the new prop for signature symmetry with `buildFocusElements` but doesn't use it in v1 (the package aggregate doesn't render per-method nodes, so the per-method ⚓ overlay has no meaningful surface there) — a per-package roll-up is a future polish if telemetry shows demand. **Frontend verification:** `tsc --noEmit` clean; `vite build` clean — 710 modules transformed (unchanged from 11.1 / 11.2; zero new transitive dependencies — every new import is from already-bundled modules); JS bundle 1,217 kB / gzip 380 kB (+~270 bytes gzipped from 11.2 — the new `listAnchoredMethods` client + `AnchoredMethodMeta` type + the `useEffect` / `useMemo` pair in `ManualHooksMode` + the per-node anchor lookup + tooltip line in `buildFocusElements` + the inline-SVG data URL + the new `GRAPH_STYLE` entry); CSS bundle 82.4 kB / gzip 14.2 kB (+~140 bytes gzipped from 11.2 — the new `.cy-trace-anchored-glyph` rule). ReadLints clean. **Operator impact:** every method node in the call graph now carries a visible signal of whether it's been touched by any cached `BehaviorAnchor` — closes the "I built a trace, but I can't easily see which methods it covered while I'm working in Manual Hooks mode" UX gap. The tooltip's `<hops>` and relative-age give operators a quick read on "is this trace fresh or stale?" without leaving the call-graph pane to dig through the Trace mode `CachedAnchorsList`. **Next sub-step:** 11.4 — Tier 2 (d) part 1, the bounded inter-procedural slicer descent + type-driven `is_stateless` analyzer (the largest behavioural change of v2). 11.4 starts with a **brief Ask-mode planning checkpoint** at the top to confirm the `_DescentBudget` shape + `_STATELESS_LIB_DENYLIST` initial seed before the slicer code lands (mirrors 10.6's `BehaviorAnchor` JSON wire-shape checkpoint). CI.0 (Phase 4 CI) remains a parallel non-blocking thread.

DEC-025 also formalises the v3 / Phase 12 deferral list: ISSUE-014, auto-verify bypass after injection, free-form LLM JS for bypass plans, per-app TTL on cached anchors, iOS / WebView / native-binary `BehaviorAnchor` adapters.     - **11.2 Tier 1 (b) — "Trace this method" on call-graph nodes — DONE** (2026-04-30): Second operator-UX deliverable from DEC-025's Tier 1 polish bundle. Frontend-only addition to `androscan/web/frontend/src/components/CallGraphView.tsx`'s right-click context menu — third button alongside the existing "Focus subgraph here" + "Open in Inspect" entries. **Wiring.** New `traceMethod(node, klass)` `useCallback` sibling of the existing `openInInspect` handler: defensively bails on `!appId || node.is_external`, otherwise writes `setPendingTraceEntry({appId, entryPrefix: node.smali_id, sourceLabel: "Graph → <class>.<method>"})` and chains `setLabMode("trace")` + `setTab("lab")`. Reuses the 10.8 `pendingTraceEntry` plumbing established for the Inspect → Trace handoff — `LabTraceMode.tsx`'s consumer recognises `node.smali_id` (the pre-computed canonical full signature stored on every method node per `call_graph.nodes.smali_id`) as complete via `looksLikeCompleteSmaliSignature` and **auto-fires the trace** on landing, so the operator goes from right-click → trace running in two clicks. **Disabled-state on external nodes.** New menu item is rendered with `disabled={isExternal}` + `className={...includes "cy-context-menu-item-disabled" when external}` + tooltip *"External callee — no source available to trace"*. **NOTE on spec-vs-reality:** the 11.2 spec text says the new entry mirrors "the same disabled-state pattern the Cytoscape view already uses for the existing 'Open in Inspect' entry on external nodes" — but the existing "Open in Inspect" entry actually bails *silently* in its handler (`if (!appId || !klass) return`) rather than rendering visibly disabled. We followed the spec's *intent* (visible disabled state + actionable tooltip on external nodes) rather than mirroring the silent-bail; inline source-comment in `CallGraphView.tsx`'s `traceMethod` callback flags this so the audit trail is explicit. **CSS.** One new namespace rule in `App.css`: `.cy-context-menu-item-disabled { opacity: 0.5; cursor: not-allowed; }` — visually identical to the existing `.callgraph-btn:disabled` pseudo-class rule above it (the menu button is also a `.callgraph-btn`, so the visual treatment cascades regardless), but the namespace exists as a discrete semantic hook so future menu items that *aren't* `.callgraph-btn` instances pick up the same affordance and so the disabled-state semantics are greppable as a concept. **No backend changes, no schema changes, no new components, no new tests** per the 11.2 contract — `node.smali_id` is already pre-computed on every method node by the call-graph indexer and the consumer-side plumbing was locked in 10.8. **Frontend verification:** `tsc --noEmit` clean; `vite build` clean — 710 modules transformed unchanged (zero new transitive dependencies — `setPendingTraceEntry` and `setLabMode` were already exposed by `WorkbenchContext` and pulled into `CallGraphView.tsx` from the existing `useWorkbench()` import), 1.22 MB JS / 82 KB CSS / 379 KB gzipped (+~280 bytes gzipped JS for the `traceMethod` callback + the new menu button, +~10 bytes gzipped CSS for the one new namespace rule). **Operator impact:** every method node in the call graph now offers a one-click path into Trace mode with the entry method pre-populated and the trace auto-firing — closes the "Inspect → Trace handoff via the typed entry-method form" UX gap on the call-graph side (operators were typing smali signatures by hand to start a trace from a graph node they could already see). External callees get a visible disabled affordance + tooltip rather than a confusing no-op click. **Next sub-step:** 11.3 — `BehaviorAnchor`-aware overlay on Manual Hooks Cytoscape pane (backend route + frontend overlay; the first 11.x sub-step that touches the Python tree). CI.0 (Phase 4 CI) remains a parallel non-blocking thread.
    - **11.1 Tier 1 (a) — Decision-timeline UX clarity polish — DONE** (2026-04-30): Frontend-only operator-UX bundle on `androscan/web/frontend/src/components/trace/DecisionTimeline.tsx` + matching CSS in `App.css`. **Three deliverables, no schema changes, no new components.** (1) **Inline disclosure block** above the `<ol className="trace-decision-timeline">` clarifying the ordering contract: *"Listed in static traversal order (BFS over the call graph, then source order within each method) — not runtime execution order."* — addresses the v1 friction where operators read the visual ordering as runtime execution order (the visual ordering matches the slicer's BFS over the call graph, but that's not the same as what executes when the gate fires at runtime; the disclosure makes the contract explicit). Rendered only when `decisions.length > 0` (the empty-state branch already explains *why* there are none, the disclosure isn't useful there). (2) **"Verify with runtime trace" button** on every `DecisionCard`, sitting in a new `.trace-decision-actions` flex row alongside the existing "Show predicate origin" expand button. On click: writes `pendingHookPrefill = {appId, templateId: "entry_exit_log", params: {class_name, method_name, event_label}, sourceLabel: "Trace verify: <class>.<method>"}` and flips `labMode` to `"manual-hooks"` — same `pendingHookPrefill` plumbing 10.7 established for `BypassPlanCard`'s "Stage in Manual Hooks" button. The auto-generated `event_label` is `<safeMethodName>_verify` where `safeMethodName` strips `<>` from constructors (`<init>` → `init_verify`) for grep-friendliness in the trace pane filter UX. **NOTE on spec divergence:** the 11.1 row in `TASKS.md` writes `params: {…, label}` as shorthand, but `entry_exit_log`'s actual parameter is `event_label` (per `androscan/adapters/frida_hooks/entry_exit_log.py`); we use the real param name so the prefilled HookBuilder form is valid on arrival rather than landing on a missing-required-field error. Inline source-comment in `DecisionTimeline.tsx` flags the divergence so the audit trail is explicit. Disabled with tooltip "No app selected" when `appId` is null (mirrors `BypassPlanCard.onStage`'s gating). (3) **High-confidence operator-action hint** above the actions row, rendered only when `BranchOutcome.confidence >= 0.85` AND at least one branch verdict is `deny` or `allow` (not `neutral`) — neutral-only outcomes get no hint because there's nothing to "flip", the operator should investigate the predicate instead. Hint text: *"This is a candidate gate — review the bypass plans below or stage `force_return_value` to flip the verdict."* New module-level `HIGH_CONFIDENCE_THRESHOLD = 0.85` constant sits next to the existing `LLM_RECLASSIFIED_CONFIDENCE = 0.75` and `LOW_CONFIDENCE_THRESHOLD = 0.6` constants for a single-source-of-truth confidence-tier reference. **CSS.** Three new namespaces in `App.css` under the existing `.trace-decision-*` family, following the established colour-token discipline: `.trace-decision-timeline-disclosure` (accent-blue tinted background + 2px accent-blue left border, muted text, 0.75rem); `.trace-decision-action-hint` (`var(--ok)` 12% soft fill + 40% bordered + 3px left border, 0.78rem with inline `<code>` tags styled to match); `.trace-decision-actions` (flex row with 0.75rem gap, wraps on narrow viewports); `.trace-decision-verify-btn` (transparent background + accent-blue outline, fills on hover, dims on disabled — mirrors `.trace-bypass-plan-stage`'s affordance density). **Frontend verification:** `tsc --noEmit` clean; `vite build` clean — 710 modules transformed (the spec target was 709 unchanged, but baseline drift since 11.0 already moved the count to 710 — verified via stash + rebuild that this commit adds zero new transitive dependencies; the only new import is `useWorkbench` which was already in the bundle graph), 1.22 MB JS / 82 KB CSS / 379 KB gzipped (+~1 KB CSS for the three new namespaces, +~0.5 KB gzipped JS for the verify-button handler — both within the spec targets). **No backend changes, no backend tests, no schema changes** per the 11.1 contract. Operator workflow next: 11.2 lands the call-graph "Trace from here" context-menu entry (depends on this sub-step's `pendingHookPrefill` plumbing only indirectly — 11.2 reuses the parallel `pendingTraceEntry` plumbing from 10.8). Phase 4 CI (CI.0) remains a parallel non-blocking thread, expected to land before 11.4 ships.

## What is known to work

- `pip install -e ".[dev,rag]"` and `pytest` from repo root succeed. (`[rag]` pulls in `fastembed` + `numpy` for semantic retrieval; `--setup` uses the same extras.)
- `python androscan.py --serve` starts RE Workbench on `http://127.0.0.1:8420` (or `web.*` from config). With `androscan/web/static/` built (`npm run build` in `androscan/web/frontend/`), browser UI loads projects, mirror, logcat; requires **adb** for mirror/tap/logcat.
- `python androscan.py --apk <path_to_apk> --task exported_components` runs real extraction → dossier → multi-turn LLM (Ollama) → consolidation → validation → exploit verification (emulator + ADB) → report under `apps/<app_id>/<run_ts>/`. With real APK, Ollama, and emulator running, report contains hypotheses with valid evidence_refs and verified/unverified status.
- `--task` can be repeated (e.g. `--task a --task b`). CLI checks Ollama reachability before analysis; clear error and setup tip if unreachable.
- Config loads from defaults, optional global_config.yaml (or --config path), and env overrides; default config has expected attributes.
- Real extraction: apktool decodes APK; AndroidManifest.xml is parsed to build dossier (exported activities, services, receivers, providers, permissions, deep links). `app_id_from_dossier` yields sanitized package name.
- LLM: Real Ollama HTTP client; parser handles valid JSON with skill_requests and hypotheses; invalid JSON returns empty response without crash. Tests use mock so no live Ollama in CI.
- Workflow integration tests: multi-turn with mock (skill_requests then hypotheses), evidence_ref validation, run_meta and run.log written.
- Run summary shows severity in brackets (e.g. `[High]`), "Findings: n (1 high, 1 low)" wording, and "(confidence: …)" per finding; component name is resolved from dossier when `component_name` is missing (evidence_ref → e.g. `exported_activities[0]` → activity name), else first evidence_ref or "—".
- evidence_ref validation: hypotheses with any invalid evidence_ref are dropped before writing the report (`androscan.internal.evidence_ref.validate_ref`).
- Run artifacts: `report.json` (validated hypotheses with verified flag, verification_reasoning, verification_artifact_dir), `run_meta.json` (apk_path, app_id, run_timestamp, started_at, finished_at, hypotheses_count), `hypotheses.json` (pre-verification validated hypotheses); `run.log` with [task], [ERROR], [WARNING], [INFORMATIONAL], [retry], [thinking], exploit-stage entries; persistent `observations.json` at `apps/<app_id>/observations.json`. Exploit verification artifacts under `exploit_verification/<vuln_module>/<hyp_id>/` (before/after screenshots, logcat.txt, dumpsys.txt, window_stack.txt, result.json). Skill results cache at `apps/<app_id>/skill_results_cache.json` (cache key for get_decompiled_class uses resolved class name).

---

## What is incomplete or unverified

- CI does not yet run tests on every push/PR (Phase 4 CI parked).
- Integration test with fixture APK (assert dossier shape from extraction) is parked; optional for later.

---

## Active architectural realities

- Presentation: CLI (`androscan.py`) plus **optional local web UI** (`--serve`, FastAPI + React static bundle when built).
- Orchestration: `internal/workflow.run_workflow()`; composes skills (pipeline then LLM multi-turn then exploit verification), config, llm. Extraction delegates to skills. Exploit verification delegates to `internal/exploit_verification.run_exploit_verification()`.
- Dependency direction: CLI → workflow → skills, internal (dossier, run_folder, evidence_ref, exploit_verification), llm.
- Extraction is real (apktool); LLM is real (Ollama HTTP); skills include real get_decompiled_class (jadx) and get_decompiled_method. Exploit-tier skills use adb for device interaction.
- Three skill tiers: pipeline (extract_manifest, prepare_dossier, generate_report), llm (get_decompiled_class, get_decompiled_method, list_classes_in_package), exploit (app_env_check, build_exploit_command, capture_signals, run_exploit_command, verify_exploit_result).

---

## Known gaps / technical debt

- Phase 4 CI: pytest on push/PR — parked.
- `exported_provider` capture_signals: `content_provider_query` and `app_data_snapshot` are stub (fall through to STUB_MESSAGE); logcat and exploit command are real.
- Run folder artifacts are in place; optional fixture-APK integration test is in backlog.

---

## Unsafe assumptions for new agents

- Extraction reads the APK via apktool; LLM calls Ollama; exploit verification uses adb. Tests use mocks/fixtures and do not require live Ollama, a real APK, or a connected emulator.
- report.json, run_meta.json, hypotheses.json, run.log are written per run when workflow completes; observations.json is at app_id level. Exploit verification artifacts are under exploit_verification/<vuln_module>/<hyp_id>/.

## Recent completed work

- 2026-05-20 Phase 11 v2.1 follow-up patch — **v2.1.11 — Frida-server Settings card surfaces "running but unverified-root" amber state + per-mode Diagnose playbook** (single atomic ship vehicle; FE-only; closes an operator-visible UX bug surfaced during dynamic-trace dogfood on the LCP-track infra a few minutes after a successful v2.1.10 deploy). **What broke.** Operator hit a 503 from `POST /api/frida/attach` (`frida.attach(pid=2107, package='com.example.weakbank.low') failed: unable to connect to remote frida-server: closed`) while the Settings → Status card simultaneously reported `frida-server` as green / "running". Root-cause diagnosis (this Agent-mode session, in-place via `Grep` / `Read` on `androscan/web/health_probes.py` + `androscan/web/frida_routes.py` + `androscan/adapters/frida_client.py`): the probe's Layer 3 (`frida-ps -U`, host-side wire-protocol enumeration) had succeeded but Layers 1 (`pidof frida-server`) and 2 (`ps -A | grep frida-server*`) had both missed — typical of a renamed binary (e.g. `/data/local/tmp/srv` rather than `/data/local/tmp/frida-server`), a `frida-gadget` injected into the target app, OR a non-root `frida-server` whose comm name doesn't match the Layer 1/2 prefix filter. Operator's own diagnostic command `adb shell "ps -A | grep -iE 'frida|gadget'"` (captured in the dogfood screenshot) confirmed cause (C) — only `re.frida.helper` was visible (the per-attach ptrace shim `frida-server` forks), owned by user `shell` not `root`, with PPID pointing at an unnamed parent process. The helper was stuck because it had hit the ptrace barrier on its parent's non-root attach attempt and stalled mid-handshake (textbook signature of cause (A) — non-root server — overlapping with cause (C)'s renamed-binary signature). **What was operator-visible-misleading.** `FridaServerStatusCard` (`androscan/web/frontend/src/tabs/SettingsTab.tsx` lines 1387-1406) painted the dot green whenever the probe reported `card.ok === true`, AND the probe's `ok` mirrored `running` (any layer hit → green) — so a Layer-3-only detection with `uid === null` rendered identically to a Layer-1 / Layer-2 hit with `uid === "root"`. The pre-v2.1.11 `uidWarning` extras line was gated on `card.uid != null && card.uid !== "root"`, which silently skipped the `uid == null` case (the unverified-root branch). The card's `needsStart` signal (which drives the **Start frida-server (as root)** button) had the same gate, so the button was hidden for the unverified case too — operators were left with no in-UI path to recover from the broken state. **What v2.1.11 ships.** **Three changes in `SettingsTab.tsx`** (single file, ~120 LOC net add): (1) **`StatusCardView` gains an `overrideDot?: "ok" | "warn" | "fail"` prop** that lets callers paint the dot color when they know more about the card's health than `card.ok` conveys. All other callers (every other status card across Global / Per-app surfaces) keep their existing dot derivation (`card.ok ? "ok" : skipped ? "warn" : card.error ? "fail" : "warn"`) — `overrideDot` falls back to that ladder when omitted. Future-friendly seam (e.g. an LLM card that wants to flip to amber when ping_ms > a threshold could reuse this without touching the generic StatusCardView shape). (2) **`FridaServerStatusCard` collapses both amber sub-cases into a single `unverifiedRoot` signal** (`card.running && (card.uid == null || card.uid !== "root")`) that drives both the amber-tint pass (`overrideDot={unverifiedRoot ? "warn" : undefined}`) AND the extended `needsStart = !card.running || unverifiedRoot` Start-button visibility. Two distinct `uidWarning` lines for the two sub-cases (`running as ${uid} — app attaches will fail unless restarted as root` for known non-root; `uid: unknown — host-side enumeration only; root cannot be verified, app attaches may fail` for unverified) — same amber tint either way, but the diagnostic playbook below branches on this same signal. The pre-v2.1.11 host-confirmed copy ("running (host-confirmed via frida-ps — renamed binary or frida-gadget)") is rewritten to ("running (host-side enumeration only — renamed binary, frida-gadget, or non-root server)") — adds the third cause AND ties the label to the amber state. (3) **New `FridaServerDiagnoseHint` component** (~60 LOC; rendered alongside the Start button + Install playbook in `actions`; reuses the `.frida-install-*` CSS classes for visual parity with the red-state Install playbook + zero new CSS). Two render modes: **`"known-non-root"`** surfaces the kill command (`adb shell "pgrep -f frida-server \| xargs -r kill -9"` — matches the route's 409-error-detail text from `frida_routes.start_frida_server` lines 654-660 so the operator can preview the command instead of having to click-fail-copy-from-error-text-click) so the operator can kill the stale `shell`-uid `frida-server` before retrying Start. **`"unverified"`** surfaces a two-step playbook: step 1 `adb shell "ps -A \| grep -iE 'frida\|gadget'"` (find the actual PID + binary name — the same command the operator used in their dogfood screenshot), step 2 `adb shell "su 0 kill -9 <PID>"` (kill it explicitly; `<PID>` is a plain-text placeholder, not auto-substituted — auto-substitution would lie about device state at render time; the operator must read step 1's output first). Both playbooks omit the "start as root" command because the Start button next to the hint is the single source of truth for that step (sourcing the canonical binary path from the route's `_FRIDA_SERVER_BIN` constant — no risk of drift). **Status type doc-block updates** in `androscan/web/frontend/src/api/status.ts` `frida_server.uid` (8 lines): the spec's pre-existing "yellow warning when this is anything other than `"root"`" already implied the `null` case should warn; v2.1.11 brings the implementation in line and the doc-block now explicitly documents the `null`-as-unverified-presumed-non-root treatment + the `FridaServerDiagnoseHint` per-mode playbook + the extended button visibility (`needsStart` whenever `running === false` OR uid isn't confirmed `"root"`). **No backend changes, no `health_probes.py` changes, no `frida_routes.py` changes** — the backend probe's permissive-by-design `ok` posture (any layer hit → green so liveness alone never reads as broken) stays as-is; the "ok-but-broken-for-attaches" nuance is a UX-side concern that lives at the rendering boundary where the operator sees it. **Verification gates:** `pytest` 1290 passing (no test changes from v2.1.11 itself — the bug was UI-side rendering / signal-derivation; no backend behaviour shift, no schema change, no API change; the +166 from v2.1.10's 1124 baseline come from Phase 13 sub-step ships 13.0 → 13.10 + v2.0 + v2.0.1, all unrelated to the v2.1 follow-up release umbrella); `tsc --noEmit` clean; `vite build` clean (1511.76 KB JS / 472.85 KB gzip / 131.18 KB CSS / 21.03 KB gzip — v2.1.11's ~120-LOC SettingsTab.tsx delta + ~8-line status.ts doc-block update is below the rounding noise on a build whose absolute size is now Phase-13-v2.x-dominated; v2.1.10's pre-Phase-13-v2 baseline of 1238.79 KB JS / 94.54 KB CSS is no longer the meaningful comparison point — every absolute delta against that baseline is dominated by Phase 13 v1's React Flow / `@xyflow/react` adoption + v2 / v2.0.1's flowchart-UX iteration); `ReadLints` clean across both modified files (`SettingsTab.tsx` + `status.ts`). **Operator impact:** (1) Settings → Status card now visually distinguishes "verified-up-as-root" (green) from "reachable-but-can't-confirm-root" / "verified-non-root" (amber) — operators no longer get the contradictory "green probe + 503 attach" experience that triggered this patch. (2) For the amber sub-cases, the **Start frida-server (as root)** button is now visible (was hidden for the unverified branch pre-v2.1.11), giving the operator a one-click recovery path for the known-non-root case (where the route's kill-on-`pgrep` fix succeeds) and a button-then-diagnose path for the unverified case (where the operator likely needs the diagnose playbook first because `pgrep -f frida-server` would miss a renamed binary). (3) The per-mode Diagnose playbook surfaces the exact kill commands inline + previewable (vs. buried in the post-click 409 `lastError` div) — the click-fail-copy-from-error-click loop collapses to copy-paste-click. **Branch posture:** ratified single-commit ship vehicle (the bug was small enough to land in one ship; design conversation was a back-and-forth Ask-mode session in this same Agent-mode session, not a strict-linear sub-step gate). v2.1 follow-up release stays "complete 2026-05-05" (closed at v2.1.6) — v2.1.7 / v2.1.8 / v2.1.9 / v2.1.10 / v2.1.11 are all post-release patches under DEC-025's umbrella, not new sub-steps within the v2.1 backlog. **Next sub-step:** none directly. Phase 13 v2.x dogfood continues to drive incremental fixes; LCP.7 (model-sourcing UX) remains the next planned-but-not-active sub-step. **Architectural lesson worth flagging for future contributors:** **green probe ≠ green UX**. The backend health probe is intentionally permissive about classification (`ok=True` whenever ANY of N detection layers hits, because the layers were designed for liveness, not feature-readiness — Layer 3 proves "frida-ps -U works" but cannot prove "device.attach(pid) will succeed"). The UI's rendering boundary is the right place to add the "ok-for-X-but-not-Y" nuance because that's where the operator-visible action (attach) maps to the operator-visible status (the card dot). Future status cards should consider what their `card.ok` actually proves and whether the UX gains from an `overrideDot` pass when the rendering side has stricter signal (e.g. unverified-root for frida-server, ping-latency-too-high for LLM, free-disk-too-low for filesystem). The `StatusCardView.overrideDot` prop introduced here is the seam for that pattern across the Settings tab.

- 2026-05-19 Phase 13 v2.0.1 — **Behavior Trace fullscreen-toggle CSS hotfix on top of v2.0 (DEC-030 closing-note appended with v2.0.1 record)** (single-file CSS hotfix; same calendar day as the v2.0 ship; v2.0 commit `44e110b` was the predecessor). Operator dogfooded v2.0 within minutes of deploy and surfaced TWO follow-up signals: (1) **fullscreen regression** — the within-page fullscreen toggle (DEC-030 Q7 = (a)) only covered the top ~560 px of the viewport instead of filling the full page; (2) **soft dogfood signal on overall shape** — "graph looks almost similar to before", confirming the v2.1 promotion trigger DEC-030 locked at (a) subjective/dogfood-based has fired. **v2.0.1 addresses signal (1) only** per operator's explicit option (i) at the v2.0.1 planning fork: "commit the fullscreen hotfix NOW as a tiny v2.0.1 patch on top of `44e110b`, then plan the next iteration as a separate ratification surface" — minimal hotfix; signal (2) goes into the v2-next planning checkpoint. **Root cause** — the base `.execution-flow-container` rule (`App.css:6594-6608`, in place since 13.6) pins `width: 100%` + `height: 560px` so the in-page flowchart card has a stable predictable height. The v2.0 `-fullscreen` modifier (`App.css:7833-7840`) added `position: fixed; inset: 0; z-index: 1000;` but did NOT override the base `width` / `height`. CSS spec: when `position: fixed` is set with a non-`auto` `width` or `height`, the corresponding `right` / `bottom` offsets from `inset` are ignored (the explicit dimension wins, and the opposite-side offset is computed as whatever the layout needs). Result: the fullscreen container fixed itself at `(0, 0)` with `width: 100vw` (since `100%` of the viewport-containing-block = `100vw` when fixed) but stayed clamped to `height: 560px` — workbench chrome leaked through below the flowchart. **Fix** — explicit `width: 100vw; height: 100vh;` added to `.execution-flow-container-fullscreen`. The explicit values win over the base `width: 100%; height: 560px;` via cascade order (the `-fullscreen` rule is later in `App.css`). `auto` was considered as an alternative ("let `inset: 0` resolve width/height intrinsically") but explicit viewport units are more robust against future container-parent changes — flex children + complex cascades can have edge cases where `auto` resolves to the intrinsic flowchart height rather than the viewport. **Code change** — single file: `androscan/web/frontend/src/App.css` (~+18 LOC including doc-block; `+width: 100vw; +height: 100vh;` plus a multi-line comment explaining the cascade interaction so future maintainers don't accidentally regress by removing the explicit units). CSS-only patch; no JS change, no schema change, no test impact. **Bundle delta (v2.0.1 alone):** CSS 128.99 → 129.01 KB / **+22 bytes raw** (gzipped unchanged within round-off); JS unchanged at 1457.85 KB. Net trivial; well below any budget. **Verification.** `tsc --noEmit` clean (no TS change); `vite build` clean (CSS hash bumps, JS unchanged); pytest stays at **1290 passed** (BE not touched); zero lint errors across all touched files (one file). **Dogfood-input record for v2-next (signal 2):** operator answered Q1 = (d) "all three" at the "what feels similar" fork — three friction signals to address in the next ratification surface: **(a) flat-layout friction** — column-rank still feels like a single tall column without indentation; operator expected method calls to look like a real hierarchy (caller above, callees indented below); ISSUE-022 / v2.1 territory; the v2.1 promotion trigger has now formally fired. **(b) neutral-count clutter** — 5-6 small neutral sinks per flowchart still adds up; operator wants further merging (collapsed "N neutral outcomes" chip per source method) or default-hidden behind a toggle; not previously tracked, opens new design space. **(c) gate-count badge invisible** — hover-progressive-disclosure was a misread of operator behaviour; operator doesn't hover-explore by default, so the badge never registered; operator wants always-visible at rest with `-warn` modifier still visually distinct for unclassified gates; effectively retracts the Q1 = (g) hover-progressive lock from v2.0 for the badge specifically (sink-hover tooltip behaviour is preserved since the small sink is the affordance the operator hovers when they want detail). **v2-next planning checkpoint open questions:** (1) split the three signals into separate sub-steps (a → v2.1 = call-graph injection per the originally-scoped DEC-030 v2.1 spec; b → v2.2 = neutral coalescing; c → v2.0.2 = badge always-visible toggle, smallest ship vehicle so could land first as a quick win) OR do all-three-at-once in a single broader-v2.1 sub-step? (2) for signal (a), the call-graph injection mechanism (BE `androscan/analysis/call_graph.py::neighbors()` route shape, FE `kind: "call"` edge variant styling — thin gray, no arrowhead, no label per the DEC-030 v2.1 stub); (3) for signal (b), the coalescing mechanism (collapsed "N neutral outcomes" chip per source method vs default-hide-behind-toggle vs drop-entirely-with-a-config-override); (4) for signal (c), the always-visible-badge rendering posture (always-show + `-warn` modifier when `unclassifiedGates > 0` vs. always-show only when `unclassifiedGates > 0`); (5) bundle-impact ceiling for the additive code. **`docs/DECISIONS.md`:** DEC-030 closing note appended with the v2.0.1 record (status remains `Active (2026-05-19)` — refines DEC-029, does not supersede; SVG2 keyword that correctly inherits the stroke colour of the referencing path (v1's `currentColor` resolved against the marker's own context not the referencing path's stroke because the shared SVG `<marker>` is defined once at document level); supported in Chrome 99+ / Firefox 90+ / Safari 16+ — within AndroScan's desktop-pentester browser target; pre-existing `MARKERS` registry in `ExecutionFlow.tsx:552` was specifically reserved for the per-verdict-marker fallback path `(b)` if `(a)` proved insufficient — the v2.0 lock makes it unnecessary. **Q5 = (b) within-column entry-first sort** — `ExecutionFlow.tsx::layoutNodes` sort comparator gains `kind === "entry"` short-circuit at the top of the chain (`if (a.kind === "entry" && b.kind !== "entry") return -1; New helpers `smali_class_to_java_dotted(class_smali)` (strips inner-class suffix, returns `""` on malformed input — fail-open posture) + `build_method_summary_widget(class_smali, method_name, descriptor, summary_text, cached)` exported from the skill so tests can pin the widget shape directly. New FE type `MethodSummaryWidgetData` in `types.ts` mirrors the BE dataclass field-for-field. New component `androscan/web/frontend/src/components/chat/widgets/MethodSummaryWidget.tsx` (~165 LOC) renders the card: Smali signature header + `(cached)` muted pill when applicable + summary paragraph + three action buttons matching the Inspector's action row (Hook this method / Trace this gate / Open source) — all three use the existing cross-surface pendings (`pendingHookPrefill` with `entry_exit_log` template / `pendingTraceEntry` with the full Smali signature / `pendingCodeNav` to Inspect tab). `<ChatWidgetRenderer>` switch arm registered for `case "method_summary"`. Inspector "Discuss in chat" affordance: new button below the Summary section's state-driven body (`onDiscussInChat`) writes `pendingChatPrefill` with a method-specific prompt template that pre-arms the agentic loop with the four `summarise_method` params (class_smali / method_name / descriptor / app_id) embedded as Markdown-quoted strings — operator reviews / sends, the LLM picks `summarise_method`, the skill returns text + the new `MethodSummaryWidget`, and the chat dock renders the interactive card on the same assistant turn. Always visible (not gated on summary state) so the operator can ask about a method even before its summary has landed or after one has failed. **Tests:** `tests/test_summarise_method_skill.py` +8 tests in new `TestPhase13_9_MethodSummaryWidget` class (3 cases for `smali_class_to_java_dotted` — top-level / inner-class / malformed input fail-opens to ""; 2 cases for `build_method_summary_widget` — locked field shape + cached flag round-trip; 3 cases for `execute()` — fresh-LLM emits `cached=False` widget / cache-hit emits `cached=True` widget / failed paths emit no widget). **Verification:** `tsc --noEmit` clean; `vite build` clean (CSS 123.66 → 127.92 KB / +4.26 KB; JS 1455.64 → 1460.46 KB / +4.82 KB); `pytest -q` 1282 → **1290 passed** (+8 widget-shape tests, no regressions across the 1282 baseline); zero lint errors across all touched files. **Out of scope for 13.9** (defer to 13.10 docs sweep): per-thread layout reshape into thread lanes (deferred from 13.8); `VITE_BEHAVIOR_TRACE_LEGACY` flag removal + `DecisionTimeline.tsx` deletion; marching-ants animation on fired edges (DEC-029 explicitly locked the static stroke); pan-to-fit-on-selection; `<MethodSummaryWidget>` chat-card "Refresh summary" affordance.).
- 2026-05-08 Phase 13 — **Behavior Trace v3 — Dynamic Execution + Flowchart UI overhaul — sub-step 13.8 — FE mode toggle (Static / Dynamic / Both) + dynamic overlay + summary streaming + live observation panel — wires the WebSocket events from 13.3 / 13.4 into the flowchart + Inspector** (single atomic ship vehicle; FE only — no BE changes, no schema changes, no new BE tests; the FE-side hook is verified via the existing `tsc --noEmit` + `vite build` gates per the 13.8 spec). **No planning checkpoint at the top of 13.8** — the wire shape was locked at 13.3 (multiplexed `summary_*` event vocabulary on the existing Frida envelope) and 13.4 (production `summarise_method` skill behind the `SummaryCallable` seam); 13.8 is the consumer-side wiring of those existing surfaces. Pre-implementation explore subagent surfaced critical contract corrections that the hook implementation absorbed: top-level `kind` is `"send"` (Frida transport) for trace events with `payload.phase` carrying the operator-facing `"entry"` / `"exit"` / etc. (NOT a top-level `kind: "entry"`); `summary_ready.payload.summary` (NOT `text`); `entry.payload.thread_depth` (NOT `depth`) + `entry.payload.seq` (NOT `entry_seq` — which lives on `exit` / `error` as the back-reference); `exit.payload["return"]` (literal JSON key, TS reserved word); `payload.class` is Java-dotted (the FE converts to Smali for `ExecutionFlow` node-id matching); the `ws_url` field returned by `POST /api/trace/{app_id}/dynamic` still points at legacy `/ws/frida/{session_id}` (the FE now derives `/ws/trace/{app_id}/{session_id}` itself from `appId` + `session_id`); DELETE returns `200 {ok: true, session_id}` (NOT 204); WS lifecycle = accept-first-then-validate (1008 close on unknown session / no provider; ring-buffer replay on connect including summary side-effects on replay so cached summaries surface for past `entry` events). **Frontend deliverables (5 files modified, 1 file created).** New `useDynamicTrace(appId)` hook in `androscan/web/frontend/src/api/trace.ts` (~+550 LOC) owns the POST `/api/trace/{app_id}/dynamic` (start) + WebSocket `/ws/trace/{app_id}/{session_id}` (multiplex) + DELETE `/api/trace/{app_id}/dynamic/{session_id}` (stop) lifecycle as a single state machine (`idle` → `starting` → `running` → `stopping` / `disconnected` / `error`). State vector exposes `firedMethods: ReadonlySet<string>` (overload-keys keyed by descriptor-stripped Smali) + `liveValues: ReadonlyMap<string, LiveValueRecord>` (latest fire's `args` / `ret` / `threadId` / `threadDepth` / `fireCount` / `lastFireTs` per method) + `summaries: ReadonlyMap<string, SummaryState>` (pending / ready+cached / failed states) + `hookFailed: ReadonlyMap<string, HookFailureRecord>` (populated on `hook_failed` events; v1 consumer is the run-status copy, full inlined-pill wiring lands in 13.9) + `readyStats` (`hooked` / `attempted` / `failed`) + `dropCount` (ring-buffer overflow) + `error`. Hook-side normaliser `normaliseTraceMessage(WsMsg) → NormalisedTraceEvent | null` unwraps the Frida `kind: "send"` envelope to flat `phase: "entry"` / `"exit"` / `"ready"` / `"hook_failed"` / `"error"` events + the multiplexer's `kind: "summary_pending"` / `summary_ready` / `summary_failed` synthetic events into one discriminated union (consumer no longer has to remember the `kind` + `payload.phase` double-discriminator). Per-event reducer `updateForEvent(state, event)` is pure + module-scoped + immutable-style for testability + per-event seam visibility. Generation-counter ref guards against stale-message clobbers across `start()` / `stop()` / `reset()` / `appId`-change cycles. Cleanup-on-unmount closes WS but doesn't fire DELETE (BE's session-timeout GC catches abandoned sessions; firing DELETE from React cleanup races the unmount and leaves an unhandled-rejection if the DELETE fails). Three exported helpers for cross-component reuse: `smaliSignatureFromEvent(classJava, method, descriptor)` (full-signature key) + `overloadKeyFromEvent(classJava, method)` (descriptor-stripped collapse-key) + `normaliseTraceMessage` (test seam). New `androscan/web/frontend/src/components/trace/TraceModeToggle.tsx` (~150 LOC) — three-pill toggle row (Static / Dynamic / Both) with `localStorage`-backed per-app persistence (key prefix `androscan/trace/mode/<appId>`); `initialTraceMode(appId, hasDynamicData)` pure helper for the `useState` initialiser (priority: localStorage hit → hasDynamicData ? "both" : "static"); auto-default flip from `"static"` → `"both"` on first event UNLESS the operator manually picked (operator-pick wins forever for that app id — tracked via local `operatorPicked` flag that flips on the first `onModeChange` call); Dynamic / Both pills disabled with a "run a dynamic trace first" affordance copy until at least one event lands. `executionFlowGraph.ts` exports `methodKey` / `overloadKey` / new `overloadKeyFromNodeId(nodeId)` helpers for cross-component lookups (was module-private — needed at the `ExecutionFlow` build step to convert node ids → overload keys for the `firedMethods.has()` lookup). `ExecutionFlow.tsx` (~+100 LOC) — accepts new optional `mode` / `firedMethods` / `liveValues` props (so 13.6 / 13.7 call sites without props keep their original rendering); per-node fired-emphasis decoration baked into `data` at build-time via `useMemo` so the custom `MethodNode` / `VerdictEdge` components don't need a separate React context; `MethodNode` adds bottom-right `.execution-flow-node-depth-pill` rendering `d:N · t:M` (× count when fireCount > 1) on fired nodes when mode allows; `VerdictEdge` accepts `fired` + `liveLabel` decorations + applies fired = accent-blue solid stroke (`!important` to override verdict-kind color) and untaken-in-dynamic = gray-dashed at 55% opacity per DEC-029 lock-in; new `composeLiveLabel(LiveValueRecord)` helper formats edge live-value chip from `args` (capped at 4, each truncated to 24 chars) + optional `→ ret` suffix (also truncated) under a 56-char total budget; chip rendered via second `EdgeLabelRenderer` block 18px below the verdict label so the two don't collide. `Inspector.tsx` (~+90 LOC) — Summary section now consumes the `summaries` prop with four state-driven renders (no event = placeholder copy / `pending` = spinner + "Generating summary…" / `ready` = paragraph + optional "cached" pill / `failed` = error + retry hint); new `Live observation` section (`.inspector-live` namespace) renders only when `mode` includes the dynamic overlay AND the selected method has fired (`live != null`) — shows args list (truncated chips with full-value tooltips) + return value + thread/depth context + fire count; module imports the canonical `overloadKey` from `executionFlowGraph` to look up summaries + liveValues by overload key. `LabTraceMode.tsx::TraceResultRegion` (~+60 LOC) — calls `useDynamicTrace(appId)` once per active anchor, computes `hasDynamicData = state.firedMethods.size > 0`, owns `mode: TraceMode` state initialised via `initialTraceMode(appId, hasDynamicData)`; anchor-change `useEffect` (keyed on `(entry_method.class_name, entry_method.method_name, hops)`) now ALSO calls `dynamic.reset()` to clear fired-method accents when the operator seeds a new entry / runs a new build / changes hops (without this, the previous anchor's runtime state would bleed into the new anchor's overlay since overload keys could even collide across anchors for unrelated methods sharing a class+method namespace); `TraceModeToggle` mounted in the Execution Flow `<header className="trace-section-head">` right cluster alongside new `<DynamicTraceRunControl>` sub-component (~+80 LOC); `<ExecutionFlow>` + `<Inspector>` both receive `mode` + dynamic state through their new optional props. `DynamicTraceRunControl` sub-component — Run / Stop button (label flips per connection state: `Run dynamic trace` / `Starting…` / `Stop dynamic trace` / `Stopping…`), live-status copy on the right (`N hooked / M attempted` after `ready`, `hooks installing…` while live, `WebSocket dropped — re-run to resume` on disconnect, `error` text on failure), drop-count badge when ring-buffer overflowed; threshold-based color coding (≤20 green / 21-50 yellow / 51-100 orange / >100 red per DEC-029) deferred to 13.9. New CSS namespaces appended to `App.css` (~+330 LOC): `.trace-mode-toggle-*` (3-pill row + active-state accent-blue background with bg-colored text + disabled state), `.lab-dynamic-trace-*` (Run / Stop button + status copy + drop-badge), `.execution-flow-edge-fired` (accent-blue solid stroke `!important`), `.execution-flow-edge-untaken-dynamic` (gray-dashed 55%-opacity), `.execution-flow-edge-live-chip` (per-edge live-value chip with accent-blue tint background + 220px max-width + ellipsis truncation), `.execution-flow-node-fired` (accent-blue node border + inset 1px accent-blue glow), `.execution-flow-node-depth-pill` (bottom-right depth pill on fired nodes — accent-blue tint, monospace, 9px font), `.inspector-summary-*` (state-driven Summary section with accent-blue left-border for ready / err-color left-border for failed / spinner-aligned pending / cached-pill chip), `.inspector-live*` (Live observation section with dashed top-border separator + 64px-1fr definition-list grid + per-cell ellipsis chips with full-value tooltips). Per-thread depth visualisation v1 = corner depth pill on fired nodes (`d:N · t:M`) — preserves the operator's "this called this called this" mental model via the existing left-to-right column-rank layout + per-node depth context; full layout reshape into per-thread lanes deferred to 13.9 (operator dogfood will tell us whether the corner pill is enough or whether the full reshape is needed; the hook already populates `threadId` + `threadDepth` on every `LiveValueRecord` so the data is there for either approach). **No backend changes, no new BE tests** per the 13.8 spec ("the FE-side `useDynamicTrace` hook is exercised via the existing `tsc --noEmit` + `vite build` gates"). **Verification.** `tsc --noEmit` clean; mode-toggle keyboard navigation (arrow-key cycling) → v2 candidate. **`docs/DECISIONS.md`:** no new DEC; 13.8's locks are implementation choices of DEC-029 (the mode-toggle three-pill design + the fired-edge / fired-node accent + the live-value chip + the depth pill were all locked at DEC-029's UX section). - 2026-05-08 Phase 13 — **Behavior Trace v3 — Dynamic Execution + Flowchart UI overhaul — sub-step 13.7 — `Inspector` pane (right-side fixed-360px panel that opens on `ExecutionFlow` node click; five operator-actionable sections + sticky action-row footer)** (single atomic ship vehicle; FE only — no BE changes, no schema changes, no new BE tests; the `BehaviorAnchor` / `DecisionPoint` / `BypassPlan` / `PredicateOrigin` data model is unchanged so the new component reads from existing typed FE→BE surfaces with zero backend churn). **No planning checkpoint at the top of 13.7** — the layout (right-side 360px pane sibling to `<ExecutionFlow>`) and content (five sections + action row) were locked at DEC-029 + the canonical `phase-13-trace-mockup.canvas.tsx` mockup; consumer plumbing (`pendingHookPrefill` / `pendingTraceEntry` / `pendingCodeNav` from `WorkbenchContext`) is the same surface Phase 11 v2.1's `BypassPlanCard` + `BehaviorTrace.onVerify` + `BehaviorAnchorCard.onOpenInInspect` already use. Pre-implementation scoping subagent confirmed the contracts: `pendingHookPrefill: { appId, templateId, params, sourceLabel? }` (consumer = HookBuilder, clears on consume); `pendingTraceEntry: { appId, entryPrefix, hops?, sourceLabel? }` (consumer = LabTraceMode, auto-fires `useTraceAnchor` cache GET on full Smali sig — cache-miss path surfaces operator-clickable Build button); `pendingCodeNav: { appId, relPath, className, method? }` (consumer = InspectTab, opens file in Code Browser; **no line-number field in v1** — line-aware nav is a v2 candidate); `PredicateOriginView({ origin, appId })` and `BypassPlanCard({ plan })` are reusable verbatim. **Frontend deliverables (3 files modified, 1 file created).** New `androscan/web/frontend/src/components/trace/Inspector.tsx` (~360 LOC) — owns the right-pane component. Module-local pure helpers (zero React deps; unit-test seam if 13.x ever needs to extract): `methodSig(MethodRef) → string` byte-equal to `executionFlowGraph.methodKey` so the caller's `selectedNodeId` matches the lookup; `overloadKey(MethodRef) → string` descriptor-stripped collapse-key — methods on the same class with the same name but different descriptors collapse onto the same node so the Inspector lookup also collapses (matches `executionFlowGraph`'s overload-key-dedup); `allMethodRefs(anchor) → MethodRef[]` flatten the same five sources `executionFlowGraph` flattens (entry + decisions + plan source + plan target + advanced plans), deduped at the inspector layer by overload-key; `resolveSelection(anchor, nodeId) → { canonical, decisions, plans, sourceLine, primaryPredicate } | null` (defensive `null` for synthetic sinks even though `ExecutionFlow.onNodeClick` filters them upstream — v1 belt-and-braces); `formatSignature(MethodRef) → string` Class.method(I)Z header. Component renders five sections inside `<aside className="inspector">`: (1) **Signature** — `<code>` block + muted `class_name` line + `×N overloads` pill when `overloadCount > 1` + warn-colored `possibly inlined` pill + a fuller "R8 may have inlined this; check `hook_failed` events" callout block when applicable (the callout uses `border-left: 2px solid var(--warn)` so it visually matches the workbench's other warning blocks); (2) **Summary** — empty-state placeholder ("Summary not yet generated. Run a dynamic trace to generate per-method LLM summaries (cached for the active app SHA).") since 13.8 will wire the live `summary_pending` / `summary_ready` / `summary_failed` event consumer from the `WS /ws/trace/{app_id}/{session_id}` channel + bootstrap-fetch the cached summary for the selected method on Inspector mount via a small new BE route (the cache layer + `summarise_method` skill already exist from 13.3 / 13.4); (3) **Source** — clickable `<button.inspector-source-link>` showing `<class>.java:<line>` (line number rendered for orientation but `pendingCodeNav` doesn't carry a line number in v1, so cross-tab nav lands at the file top — line-aware nav is a v2 candidate via extending `PendingCodeNavInput` with a `line` field + threading it through `InspectTab.handleSelect`'s existing `setScrollTarget` machinery); (4) **Predicate origin** — `<PredicateOriginView origin={primaryPredicate} appId={appId}>` reused verbatim with a "(N additional decisions on this method — see Behavior Trace list below)" muted note when multiple decisions land on this method (the v1 model — predicate origin per node — collapses overload-aware decisions into the first-seen origin; the muted note lets the operator know to look at the linear list for the full set); (5) **Bypass plans** — `<BypassPlanCard>` reused verbatim for plans whose `target_method` OR `source_decision_method` overload-key matches the selected method; `.inspector-plans .trace-bypass-plan-card` rule-body slims the card chrome (8px padding + 11px font) so the 360px column doesn't feel cramped (single-class targeting keeps specificity matched against the canonical `.trace-bypass-plan-card` rule body — no specificity drift). Action row at the bottom (sticky `<footer.inspector-actions>` with 2-column grid + 1-fr-2-fr fall-through grid for the Open Source row): `[Hook this method]` writes `pendingHookPrefill` with `templateId: "entry_exit_log"`, `params: { class_name, method_name, event_label: "<method>_inspector" }`, `sourceLabel: "Inspector: <class>.<method>"` and flips `labMode` to `"manual-hooks"` — mirrors `BehaviorTrace.onVerify` byte-for-byte but with `_inspector` suffix on the event label so the runtime trace's logcat output distinguishes Inspector-originated hooks from list-view "Verify with runtime trace" hooks; `[Trace this gate]` writes `pendingTraceEntry` with `entryPrefix: methodSig(canonical)` (full Smali signature so `useTraceAnchor` auto-fires the cache GET; cache-miss path surfaces the operator-clickable `[Build trace]` button) + `sourceLabel: "Inspector → <class>.<method>"`; `setLabMode("trace")` is idempotent in v1 since the Inspector only renders inside Trace mode but kept for symmetry with the cross-tab sites (`InspectTab.handleTraceBehaviour`, `CallGraphView.traceMethod`, `TraceEntryCandidateWidget.onTraceClick`); `[Open source]` writes `pendingCodeNav` for the canonical method's class + flips tab to `"inspect"` — same plumbing as `BehaviorAnchorCard.onOpenInInspect`. `[×]` close button at the top of the panel fires `onClear()` which the consumer wires to `setSelectedFlowNode(null)`. Empty-state placeholder ("Click a method node in the flowchart to inspect its signature, predicate origin, bypass plans, and source line.") when `selectedNodeId === null`. Synthetic-sink guard renders a "Synthetic sink nodes don't carry a method reference." copy in the unlikely event one slips through (defense-in-depth — `ExecutionFlow.onNodeClick` filters synthetic sinks before firing). New CSS namespace `.inspector-*` appended to `App.css` (~+220 LOC) — flex-column 360px-fixed panel (`flex: 0 0 360px; min-width: 360px; max-width: 360px;`) with `var(--panel-2)` background, header bar with workbench-style uppercase letter-spaced title + `[×]` close button (matches the `.trace-section-head` family), sectioned body with 14px inter-section gap, signature `<code>` block on `var(--bg)` with bordered chrome + word-break, two pill variants (`.inspector-overload-pill` muted + `.inspector-inlined-pill` warn-colored), inlined-callout block with `border-left: 2px solid var(--warn)`, source-link button with hover-color-change discipline (cascades to the inner `<code>` so the file name itself goes accent-blue on hover), predicate-body 6px gap stack, plans-section 8px gap stack, action row with `border-top: 1px solid var(--border)` + `var(--panel)` background to visually separate from the scrollable body + 6px gap 2-column grid, action button with hover-color-change + 100ms transition, `.inspector-action-source { grid-column: 1 / -1 }` for the full-width Open Source row matching the canvas mockup's primary-on-top + secondary-on-bottom layout. New `.execution-flow-row` wrapper (12px gap, `display: flex; flex-direction: row; align-items: stretch;`) hosts the `<ExecutionFlow>` (flex 1) + `<Inspector>` (fixed 360px) side-by-side; `.execution-flow-container` updated with `flex: 1 1 auto` + `min-width: 0` (so the flowchart can shrink below its intrinsic width without overflowing the container) + bumped height from 480px → 560px so the Inspector's 5-section body has visual headroom even with the action-row footer eating 50px. `LabTraceMode.tsx` (~+30 LOC) — flipped `selectedFlowNodeId: string | null` to `selectedFlowNode: ExecutionFlowNode | null` (lifts the full graph node so the Inspector reads `overloadCount` + `possiblyInlined` + the synthetic-sink guard fields directly from the graph layer with zero re-derivation; saves a redundant `buildExecutionFlowGraph(anchor).find(...)` call on every render); added a stale-selection-clear `useEffect` keyed on `(entry_method.class_name, entry_method.method_name, hops)` so the Inspector resets when the operator seeds a new entry / runs a new build / changes hops (without this, a stale `selectedFlowNode.id` would carry over and the Inspector would resolve to `null` under a "selected" header — the empty-state UX is cleaner); mounted `<Inspector>` as a sibling of `<ExecutionFlow>` inside the new `.execution-flow-row` flex container; wired `appId` from local state + `onClear={() => setSelectedFlowNode(null)}`. **No backend changes; no schema changes; no new backend tests** — Inspector is purely an FE consumer of the unchanged trace data model + the existing `WorkbenchContext` pendings. **Verification.** `tsc --noEmit` clean; `vite build` clean — CSS bundle 115.71 KB → 119.29 KB (+3.58 KB for the new `.inspector-*` namespace + the small `.execution-flow-row` wrapper); `compact` prop on `BypassPlanCard` if dogfooding shows the unfurled card too dense for the right-pane context → 13.9 candidate. **`docs/DECISIONS.md`:** no new DEC; 13.7 is purely an implementation choice of DEC-029 (the Inspector's five sections + the three-button action row are locked at DEC-029's "Inspector pane" sub-section + the canonical `phase-13-trace-mockup.canvas.tsx` mockup). - 2026-05-08 Phase 13 — **Behavior Trace v3 — Dynamic Execution + Flowchart UI overhaul — sub-step 13.6 — `ExecutionFlow` flowchart component (React Flow / `@xyflow/react`) — new visual surface above the legacy Behavior Trace list during the 13.6 → 13.8 build-out** (single atomic ship vehicle; FE only — no BE changes, no schema changes, no new BE tests; the `BehaviorAnchor` wire shape is unchanged so the new component reads from the existing trace cache without any backend churn). **Brief Ask-mode planning checkpoint at the top of 13.6** (per the 13.6 spec line in `TASKS.md`) picked the substrate from three options: (a) hand-rolled SVG + a small `computeDAGLayout` helper ported from the canvas mockup, (b) React Flow / `@xyflow/react` with custom node + custom edge components, (c) hybrid (hand-rolled SVG for v1 with a documented v2 escape-hatch for React Flow). **Operator picked option (b) — React Flow.** Rationale: ~150 KB JS bundle bump traded for built-in pan / zoom / minimap, drag-pan inertia, and a familiar consumer-side API; the locked DEC-029 styling (220×72 cards / 6px radius / 1.5px stroke / 6×6 arrowhead / color-only edge emphasis) is enforced via custom node + custom edge components that override the library defaults rather than fighting them. **Frontend deliverables (1 file modified, 3 files created).** New pure-helper module `androscan/web/frontend/src/components/trace/executionFlowGraph.ts` (~340 LOC;
