zero React + zero React Flow deps so the node-extraction logic stays unit-testable by isolation) — converts `BehaviorAnchor` → `{ nodes, edges }` with the following translation rules: one node per `MethodRef` referenced anywhere in the anchor (entry method + per-decision enclosing methods + bypass-plan target methods + bypass-plan source methods), deduped on overload-key (`L<class>;-><method>`) so methods with the same name on the same class but different param descriptors collapse into a single stacked-card node carrying `overloadCount`; node `kind` is `entry` / `gate` / `sink_allow` / `sink_deny` / `sink_neutral` / `method` per the DEC-029 corner-pill taxonomy; one edge per `(decision, branch_verdict)` pair routed to either the cross-referenced bypass-plan target method (when `BypassPlan.source_decision_method` + `source_decision_instruction_index` matches the decision AND the verdict is `allow`) or a synthetic per-source verdict-flavored sink (`__sink_allow__::<source_id>` / `__sink_deny__::<source_id>` / `__sink_neutral__::<source_id>` so multiple deny branches on the same source method collapse into one operator-meaningful "denial" block); `unverdicted` edges (no `branch_outcome` available) route to a synthetic neutral sink so every method has SOMETHING flowing out; coarse layout-rank assigned via BFS from the entry node so the renderer can do a column-grid layout without an external lib like dagre; `possiblyInlined` heuristic: method shows up as a decision/plan target but NOT as a decision source → flagged for the dashed-border + "possibly inlined" pill (13.9 will refine this against runtime `hook_failed` events from the dynamic-trace WebSocket — v1 is conservative). **`HIGH_CONFIDENCE_THRESHOLD = 0.85`** for the `hasGateDecision` flag — duplicated locally from `BehaviorTrace.tsx` with a "single source of truth lives in `branch_classifier.py`; 13.10 may unify the FE constants into a shared module" comment. New `androscan/web/frontend/src/components/trace/ExecutionFlow.tsx` (~330 LOC) — the React Flow renderer. Module-level constants: `NODE_WIDTH = 220`, `NODE_HEIGHT = 72`, `COLUMN_GAP = 80`, `ROW_GAP = 30`, `ARROWHEAD_SIZE = 6`, `EDGE_STROKE_WIDTH = 1.5` — all locked at DEC-029 values. Custom `MethodNode` component renders the locked 220×72 / 6px-radius / three-line stacked-card visual with: corner pills (`GATE` for entry-with-gate-decision and gate-kind methods, `ENTRY` for plain entries, `ALLOW` / `DENY` / `NEUTRAL` for synthetic verdict sinks); first-line title (Java-form `Class.method`); second-line muted class chip (last segment of the FQCN); third-line meta row with `line N` source-line chip + `×N overloads` chip + `possibly inlined` chip when applicable; dashed border for `possiblyInlined` nodes; box-shadow stacking for `overloadCount > 1` to render the stacked-card visual without duplicating the card markup. Custom `VerdictEdge` component uses React Flow's `getSmoothStepPath` (rounded right-angle bends, matches the mockup's visual) for the geometry; `BaseEdge` with `style={{ strokeWidth: 1.5 }}` enforces the locked stroke width regardless of React Flow's default theme; inline edge labels (`allowed` / `denied` / `passes through` / empty for unverdicted) render via `EdgeLabelRenderer` with verdict-flavored chips and `pointer-events: none` so the chips don't intercept clicks. Each edge sets `markerEnd: { type: MarkerType.ArrowClosed, width: 6, height: 6 }` so React Flow renders a 6×6 SVG `<marker>` element regardless of its own default sizing (which would be 12.5×12.5). `nodesDraggable={false}` + `nodesConnectable={false}` lock the layout (the operator can't accidentally re-position nodes); pan / zoom / minimap stay enabled (React Flow's `<Controls>` + `<MiniMap>` re-skinned to the workbench dark palette via the `.execution-flow-*` namespace). `onNodeClick` callback fires only for non-synthetic nodes (synthetic sinks don't carry a `MethodRef` so they're not Inspector-clickable); the consumer (`LabTraceMode`) lifts the selected node id to its own state for 13.7's Inspector pane to read. `selectedNodeId` is plumbed in as a controlled prop so the consumer drives selection state (not React Flow's internal selection model). `proOptions={{ hideAttribution: true }}` removes the React Flow watermark. `fitView` + `fitViewOptions={{ padding: 0.2, maxZoom: 1.0 }}` so the flowchart auto-fits to the container on first paint and on anchor change; `defaultEdgeOptions={{ type: "verdict" }}` makes the custom edge type the default registration. `LabTraceMode.tsx` (~+45 LOC) imports `ExecutionFlow`, adds two new state hooks: `executionFlowCollapsed` (defaults open) + `selectedFlowNodeId` (defaults `null`; v1 has no consumer beyond the click-to-set, but 13.7's Inspector pane will read it without prop-drilling); inserts a new collapsible `<section className="trace-section">` between `BehaviorAnchorCard` and the existing Behavior Trace list section; section header reads `"Execution Flow (N decisions)"` with the same chevron pattern (`logcat-toggle-btn` + `IconChevronUp` / `IconChevronDown` + `aria-controls="execution-flow-body"` + `aria-label` + `title`) as the rest of the trace UI. **Renders BOTH the flowchart AND the linear list during the 13.6 → 13.8 build-out** so operators can dogfood the new visual surface against the familiar list view; 13.9 will demote the linear list behind a feature flag once the flowchart UI is operator-confirmed. New `.execution-flow-*` CSS namespace appended to `App.css` (~+220 LOC) — overrides React Flow's default light-theme chrome (background fill + grid dots + `<Controls>` button colors + minimap colors + attribution-hidden) to the workbench dark palette; styles the custom node card (`var(--panel-2)` background + `var(--border-strong)` border / `var(--accent)` for selected + entry / `var(--warn)` for gate / `var(--ok)` for sink_allow / `var(--err)` for sink_deny / `var(--muted)` for sink_neutral / dashed border for inlined / box-shadow stacking for overloads); styles all five corner-pill variants (`-entry` / `-gate` / `-allow` / `-deny` / `-neutral`); styles the source-line + overload + inlined chips; pins the locked verdict palette on edges + edge labels (`allow` = `var(--ok)` green / `deny` = `var(--err)` red / `neutral` = `var(--muted)` gray / `unverdicted` = `var(--border-strong)` dim-dashed with `stroke-dasharray: 4 3`); `svg.react-flow__edges marker path { fill: currentColor }` makes the arrowhead inherit the edge's stroke color so the arrowhead is verdict-colored without per-edge `<marker>` definitions. New dependency `@xyflow/react@^12.10.2` + transitive `@xyflow/system` added to `package.json`. **No backend changes; no new backend tests** — the wire shape (`BehaviorAnchor`) is unchanged; the existing 1282 backend tests cover the route surface 13.6 reads from. **Verification.** `tsc --noEmit` clean; `vite build` clean — CSS bundle 110 KB → 116 KB (the +6 KB is the new `.execution-flow-*` namespace); JS bundle 1280 KB → 1435 KB (the +~150 KB is `@xyflow/react`, expected and matches the planning-checkpoint estimate); pytest `1282 passed` unchanged (BE not touched); zero lint errors across all touched files. **Out of scope (deferred to later sub-steps per DEC-029):** Inspector pane (right-side fixed-width panel reading the `selectedFlowNodeId` state, with five operator-actionable sections — signature / summary / source line / predicate origin / bypass plans) → 13.7; Static / Dynamic / Both mode toggle + live-value chips on edges (`pin="1234" → false`) consumed from the dynamic-trace WebSocket → 13.8; threshold-colored "Run Dynamic Trace" button + refined `possiblyInlined` inference against runtime `hook_failed` events → 13.9; pan-to-fit-on-selection (auto-pan when the consumer changes `selectedNodeId`) → v2 candidate; replace the `MARKERS` registry comment with actual per-verdict `<marker>` definitions if React Flow's `currentColor` inheritance proves insufficient → 13.8 if needed. **`docs/DECISIONS.md`:** no new DEC; 13.6 is purely an implementation choice of DEC-029 (the flowchart surface + locked styling + corner-pill taxonomy + edge palette were all locked at DEC-029). The substrate choice (React Flow over hand-rolled SVG) is documented inline in `ExecutionFlow.tsx`'s module doc-block as the canonical reference for any future flowchart component in the workbench. **`docs/TASKS.md`:** Active Task block flipped from "13.5 done; 13.6 next" → "13.6 done; 13.7 next"; Phase 13 row in § Sub-task status (implementation) table extended with the 13.6 deliverables; 13.6 row in § Phase 13 sub-step backlog flipped from `[ ]` → `[x]` with full landing-detail footprint; 13.7 row tightened to note that the Inspector pane will mount as a sibling of `<ExecutionFlow>` inside the `execution-flow-body` div and read `selectedFlowNodeId` from `LabTraceMode`. **`docs/STATE.md`:** this entry. **`docs/SAFETY_AND_SECURITY.md`:** unchanged in 13.6 — pure UI addition; no new attack surface, no new device-touching code, no new network endpoints (the new module reads from the existing trace cache via the existing typed FE→BE surface).

- 2026-05-08 Phase 13 — **Behavior Trace v3 — Dynamic Execution + Flowchart UI overhaul — sub-step 13.5 — Frontend rename "Decision Timeline" → "Behavior Trace" with one-release rollback flag** (single atomic ship vehicle; FE only — no BE changes, no schema changes, no new tests; pure rebrand of the operator-facing surface ahead of the substantive 13.6 / 13.7 / 13.8 flowchart-UI ships). **No planning checkpoint at the top of 13.5** — the rename was unambiguous (DEC-029 locked "Decision Timeline" → "Behavior Trace"), the contract surface (the `summary_pending` / `summary_ready` / `summary_failed` events streaming on `WS /ws/trace/{app_id}/{session_id}`) is locked by 13.3 + 13.4, and the rollback strategy was straightforward (gate the new render path behind a `VITE_*` env flag with the legacy file untouched as the byte-equal rollback target). **Frontend deliverables (5 files modified, 1 file created).** New `androscan/web/frontend/src/components/trace/BehaviorTrace.tsx` (~270 LOC) — behavior BYTE-EQUAL to the legacy `DecisionTimeline.tsx`, only the component name (`DecisionTimeline` → `BehaviorTrace`; `DecisionCard` → `BehaviorTraceCard`) and the CSS namespace differ (`.trace-decision-*` → `.behavior-trace-*`). All structural logic, low-confidence / LLM-refined / candidate-gate framing, "Verify with runtime trace" `pendingHookPrefill` plumbing, and `PredicateOriginView` mounting unchanged. Threshold constants (`LLM_RECLASSIFIED_CONFIDENCE = 0.75`, `LOW_CONFIDENCE_THRESHOLD = 0.6`, `HIGH_CONFIDENCE_THRESHOLD = 0.85`) duplicated locally with a "keep in sync until 13.10" comment so the rename PR doesn't pull the legacy file's hardcoded value chain into the new component (cleaner cleanup at 13.10 — drop the legacy file, the dupes become the canonical declarations). Legacy `DecisionTimeline.tsx` left UNTOUCHED so the rollback target stays byte-identical to the pre-13.5 render — flipping the flag yields v2.1 verbatim with zero risk of accidental behavioral drift sneaking in via the rename PR. **Feature-flag wiring.** `LabTraceMode.tsx` adds `BEHAVIOR_TRACE_LEGACY` module-level constant derived from `import.meta.env.VITE_BEHAVIOR_TRACE_LEGACY` (off by default; case-insensitive `1`/`true`/`yes`/`on` enable it — `String(...).toLowerCase().match(/^(1|true|yes|on)$/) !== null`, defensive against vite's `string | boolean | undefined` env-var type). Section header text flips between "Decision timeline (N)" ↔ "Behavior Trace (N)"; `aria-controls` + `aria-label` + `title` + DOM `id` all paired to the flag (`trace-decision-timeline-body` ↔ `behavior-trace-body`); component selection at the JSX render site is `BEHAVIOR_TRACE_LEGACY ? <DecisionTimeline ...> : <BehaviorTrace ...>` with identical props bag (the import-side type is satisfied by both since they share the props shape). **CSS namespace doubled** via comma-grouped selectors in `App.css` (~30 selector pairs added; single rule body each, identical specificity throughout since every rule is a single-class selector — no specificity drift, no semantic CSS change). Selector grouping was chosen over duplicating the rule blocks because (1) single source of truth for the actual style declaration; (2) trivial cleanup at 13.10 (just drop the legacy half of each comma-grouped selector); (3) zero specificity-drift risk vs. duplicated rule blocks where a future patch might land in only one half. Pairs cover the full namespace: `.trace-decision-timeline,.behavior-trace-list` (the `<ol>`); `.trace-decision-card,.behavior-trace-card` + `-flagged` variant; `.trace-decision-header,.behavior-trace-header` + `-line`; `.trace-decision-method`/`-kind`; the four `.trace-decision-pill*` variants; `.trace-decision-verdicts`; `.trace-decision-expand` + `:hover`; `.trace-decision-body`; the three Phase 11 v2.1.1 polish classes (`-timeline-disclosure` ↔ `-disclosure`, `-action-hint` + `code` child, `-actions`, `-verify-btn` + `:hover:not(:disabled)` + `:disabled`); `.trace-decision-details` + `dt` / `dd`; `.trace-decision-register`; `.trace-decision-branch-list,.trace-decision-reasons,.behavior-trace-branch-list,.behavior-trace-reasons` (4-way grouping). **Doc-comment updates.** `LabTab.tsx` parent-tab doc-comment notes the rename + the legacy flag for one-release rollback; `BranchOutcomeBadge.tsx`'s "Used by `DecisionTimeline`" comment expanded to "Used by `BehaviorTrace` (and the legacy `DecisionTimeline` rendered behind `VITE_BEHAVIOR_TRACE_LEGACY`)"; `api/trace.ts`'s consumer-list doc-comment updated similarly; the FE README's top-level component-map entry for `LabTab.tsx` extended to call out the 13.5 rename + the legacy flag. **No backend changes, no schema changes, no new tests** — the rename is FE-only and the wire-shape contract is locked by the existing `tests/test_trace_routes.py` + `tests/test_trace_summary.py` (1282 BE tests pass unchanged). **Verification.** `tsc --noEmit` clean; `vite build` clean (CSS 95.29 KB / 16.02 KB gzipped — uptick reflects the ~30 comma-grouped selector pairs; JS bundle unchanged at 1,243.53 KB / 385.70 KB gzipped); pytest `1282 passed` unchanged (BE not touched); zero lint errors across all 7 touched files (the new component, the legacy component left as-is, `BranchOutcomeBadge.tsx`, `LabTraceMode.tsx`, `LabTab.tsx`, `api/trace.ts`, `App.css`). Operator-visible: section header reads "Behavior Trace (N)" instead of "Decision timeline (N)"; everything else is byte-equal until 13.6 lands the flowchart UI. **Out of scope (deferred to later sub-steps per DEC-029):** ExecutionFlow flowchart component (13.6); Inspector pane (13.7); mode toggle (Static/Dynamic/Both) + WebSocket consumer (13.8); threshold-colored run-trace button + inlined? wiring (13.9); docs sweep + DEC-029 closing note (13.10 — also removes `DecisionTimeline.tsx` + `VITE_BEHAVIOR_TRACE_LEGACY` + the comma-paired `.trace-decision-*` halves of the CSS rules). **`docs/DECISIONS.md`:** no new DEC; 13.5 is purely an implementation choice of DEC-029 (the rename was already locked at DEC-029's "Naming" lock-in line — "Decision Timeline" → "Behavior Trace"). The rollback-flag pattern (env-flag-gated parallel component file with byte-equal pre-rename render) is documented inline in `BehaviorTrace.tsx`'s module doc-block as the canonical reference for any future "rename behind a flag" sweep. **`docs/TASKS.md`:** Active Task block flipped from "13.4 done; 13.5 next" → "13.5 done; 13.6 next"; Phase 13 row in § Sub-task status (implementation) table extended with the 13.5 deliverables; 13.5 row in § Phase 13 sub-step backlog flipped from `[ ]` → `[x]` with full landing-detail footprint. **`docs/STATE.md`:** this entry. **`docs/SAFETY_AND_SECURITY.md`:** unchanged in 13.5 — pure UI rename; no new attack surface, no new device-touching code, no new network endpoints.

- 2026-05-08 Phase 13 — **Behavior Trace v3 — Dynamic Execution + Flowchart UI overhaul — sub-step 13.4 — `summarise_method` LLM skill registered in `_SKILL_MODULES` with `requires_confirmation=False`; decompiled-source enrichment via `read_source_file(app_dir, smali_to_source_rel_path(class_smali), sha)` + the existing `_extract_method_bodies` brace-matcher from `get_decompiled_method.py` (re-used, no duplication); swapped behind 13.3's `SummaryCallable` seam in `androscan/web/app.py` via the new module-level `_build_summarise_method_callable(...)` factory** (single atomic ship vehicle; BE only — no UI surface, no schema changes; FE rendering of `summary_*` events lands in 13.7 / 13.8). **No planning checkpoint at the top of 13.4** — the cache-key shape was already locked at 13.3 (byte-equal across the cutover), the skill registration was straightforward (mirrors `trace_behavior` / `query_call_graph`'s tier-3 patterns), and the only design choice left was the prompt enrichment via decompiled-source snippet (re-uses the existing decompile cache + `_extract_method_bodies` directly — no jadx re-run since the dynamic-trace flow REQUIRES a cached `BehaviorAnchor` which REQUIRES a warm decompile cache, so the cache is guaranteed warm by the time this skill fires). **Seam evolution.** `SummaryCallable` extended from 3-arg `(class, method, descriptor) → str` to **4-arg** `(app_id, class, method, descriptor) → str` — required so 13.4's skill can resolve the per-app `app_dir` (decompile-cache reads need it) + the per-app cache key (`app_sha` derivation from `app_meta.json`). 13.3's `default_summary_callable` updated to accept-and-ignore `app_id` (its name-based prompt doesn't need it; explicit `del app_id` for "intentionally unused" signal); WS-handler call site in `trace_routes.py::_fire_summary` updated to pass `app_id` from the WS URL through to the callable; 14 test stubs across `tests/test_trace_summary.py` (4) + `tests/test_trace_routes.py` (10) updated to the new 4-arg signature with zero behavioral regression. **Backend deliverables (3 files modified, 1 file created).** New module `androscan/skills/summarise_method.py` (~410 LOC) owning: `SUMMARY_SKILL_ID = "summarise_method"` constant (byte-equal to `trace_summary.SUMMARY_SKILL_ID`, pinned by a test — duplicated rather than imported across the skills/web boundary to keep the skills layer self-contained), `SOURCE_BODY_BUDGET_BYTES = 4096` prompt-budget cap (with operator-visible `// ... (truncated for prompt budget)` marker so the LLM never confidently summarises a half-truncated body), `_SYSTEM_PROMPT` (steers the model toward 3-5-sentence operator-actionable prose; instructs the LLM to express uncertainty when only the name + signature is available rather than confabulate), `USER_PROMPT_HEAD_TEMPLATE` (the `Class:` / `Method:` / `Descriptor:` three-line head used in every prompt), `smali_to_source_rel_path` (Smali-form → jadx source rel-path; inner classes fall back to outer-class file since jadx emits inner classes inside the outer-class file — the brace-matcher handles them naturally), `build_summary_prompt` (composes head + optional source body within budget), `_resolve_app_dir` (mirrors `trace_behavior._resolve_app_dir`'s `run_folder.parent.parent` ladder), `_read_method_source` (fail-open on every unavailability path: malformed Smali / decompile cache not ready / class file missing / method not in source), `_normalise_descriptor` (strips internal whitespace before cache lookup so semantically-identical descriptors with stray spaces hit the same slot — predictable cache hit-rate), `_summary_cache_params` (byte-equal shape to `trace_summary.summary_cache_params`, pinned by a test), `_lookup_cached` / `_store_cached` (cache wrappers with treat-empty-as-miss + fail-soft on I/O errors), and the public `execute(params, context)` entry point implementing the full pipeline (resolve app + sha → cache lookup BEFORE source read for fast warm hits → fail-open source enrichment → LLM call via `androscan.llm.client.complete(prompt, system_content, stream=False, response_format=None)` → cache write only on success). `androscan/skills/__init__.py` extends `_SKILL_MODULES` with the new skill module string. `androscan/web/app.py` adds module-level factory `_build_summarise_method_callable(config_provider, app_dir_resolver)` returning the production `SummaryCallable` — wraps the registered skill, builds a synthetic `apps/<app_id>/__ws_trace__/` run-folder (the skill's `_resolve_app_dir` only uses `run_folder.parent.parent` to find `apps/` root + the app_id, so the synthetic path is enough to drive the resolution without creating a real run folder per WS connection), bounces sync `execute()` into the default executor via `loop.run_in_executor(None, ...)`, and surfaces `SkillResult(success=False)` as `RuntimeError` so the WS handler's `summary_failed` path emits operator-facing reasons verbatim. Single-layer-timeout discipline locked: route-layer's `asyncio.wait_for(timeout=SUMMARY_TIMEOUT_S)` stays canonical (no double-timeout race against the executor). `app.py` wires it as the 4th arg of `build_trace_router(...)`, replacing 13.3's `default_summary_callable` for production. **Tests.** `tests/test_summarise_method_skill.py` (NEW; **+29 tests** across registration + meta shape (3 — registry membership / `tier="llm"` + `requires_confirmation=False` + params schema / skill-id parity with `trace_summary.SUMMARY_SKILL_ID`); pure helpers `smali_to_source_rel_path` (5 — top-level / inner-class / double-nested-inner / malformed sentinels / empty), `build_summary_prompt` (4 — no source / with source / oversized truncation / head-template invariant), `_normalise_descriptor` (1) + cache-params byte-equality with `trace_summary.summary_cache_params` (1); skill happy path (5 — LLM-call w/ source-enriched prompt / cache write / cache hit skips LLM / cache-key invariant under descriptor whitespace / per-app-sha cache separation); fail-open / fail-soft paths (8 — missing class_smali / missing method_name / no app context / missing decompile cache / method not in source / LLM exception / empty LLM response / failed-LLM-not-cached); production-adapter wiring (3 — signature lock / skill delegation / `success=False` → `RuntimeError`). `tests/test_trace_summary.py` (4 stubs updated to 4-arg signature). `tests/test_trace_routes.py` (10 stubs updated to 4-arg signature; `_stub_summary_unused` AssertionError message extended with `app_id` for traceability; `_never_called` updated). **Test count:** 1253 baseline → **1282** HEAD (`+29` net; the 14 stub-signature updates were behavior-preserving, no test added or removed). **Lint:** zero errors across `androscan/skills/summarise_method.py` + `androscan/skills/__init__.py` + `androscan/web/app.py` + `androscan/web/trace_summary.py` + `androscan/web/trace_routes.py` + `tests/test_summarise_method_skill.py` + `tests/test_trace_summary.py` + `tests/test_trace_routes.py`. **Out of scope (deferred to later sub-steps):** rename "Decision Timeline" → "Behavior Trace" in the FE → 13.5; `ExecutionFlow` flowchart + `Inspector` pane FE → 13.6 / 13.7; mode toggle + WebSocket consumer → 13.8; threshold-colored run-trace button + inlined? wiring → 13.9; docs sweep + DEC-029 closing note → 13.10. Cache prompt enrichment with call-graph context (caller / callee class names + decompiled snippet of the caller) is deliberately NOT in v1 — the LLM gets just the method-under-summary's body within the 4 KB budget; if dogfooding shows the budget too tight for legitimately-interesting methods, v2 promotes `SOURCE_BODY_BUDGET_BYTES` to a config knob. **`docs/DECISIONS.md`:** no new DEC; 13.4's locks are implementation choices of DEC-029 (the `SummaryCallable` seam was already locked at DEC-029, and 13.4's swap-in is a one-line change in `app.py` per the DEC-029 plan). The 4-arg signature evolution is documented inline in `trace_summary.py`'s `SummaryCallable` doc-block as the canonical reference. **`docs/TASKS.md`:** Active Task block flipped from "13.3 done; 13.4 next" → "13.4 done; 13.5 next"; Phase 13 row in § Sub-task status (implementation) table extended with the 13.4 deliverables; 13.4 row in § Phase 13 sub-step backlog flipped from `[ ]` → `[x]` with full landing-detail footprint; 13.5 row tightened to flag that the FE rename is now ready to consume the `summary_*` events surfaced by 13.3+13.4. **`docs/STATE.md`:** this entry. **`docs/SAFETY_AND_SECURITY.md`:** unchanged in 13.4 — the new skill is read-only (`requires_confirmation=False`) per DEC-022, doesn't touch the device, doesn't mutate the APK, doesn't open new network surfaces beyond the existing `androscan.llm.client.complete` LLM round-trip (which is governed by the existing LLM provider config). The 13.10 docs sweep will surface the skill registration + the `SummaryCallable` seam evolution in §12.x.

- 2026-05-08 Phase 13 — **Behavior Trace v3 — Dynamic Execution + Flowchart UI overhaul — sub-step 13.3 — `WS /ws/trace/{app_id}/{session_id}` multiplexed-events surface (replay-then-stream Frida events + three new `summary_pending` / `summary_ready` / `summary_failed` event kinds, with `skill_results_cache.json` cross-session persistence under `skill_id="summarise_method"`)** (single atomic ship vehicle; BE only — no UI surface, no schema changes; FE wiring lands in 13.5 / 13.7 / 13.8). **No planning checkpoint at the top of 13.3** — the wire shape was implied by DEC-029's lock-in (the additive `summary_*` `kind` vocabulary on the existing Frida envelope) and the implementation choices were straightforward extensions of `frida_routes.ws_trace`'s replay-then-stream pattern + the `skill_results_cache` API. **Wire-shape and seam adjustments incorporated from explore-subagent's report on existing `frida_routes.py` patterns + `skill_results_cache` API:** (1) **Endpoint path is `WS /ws/trace/{app_id}/{session_id}`** (not the dependency-line-implied `/ws/trace`) — explicit `app_id` segment is required for the cache-key derivation (cache reads need `app_sha` from the per-app `app_meta.json`; bundling that into the URL keeps the WS handler stateless, matches `frida_routes.ws_trace`'s style of carrying identifiers in the path rather than in connect-time JSON). (2) **`build_trace_router` factory now returns `(rest_router, ws_router)`** — same split-prefix posture as `build_frida_router` since the WS prefix differs from the REST prefix. Updated three call sites: production `androscan/web/app.py` + two existing `tests/test_trace_routes.py` 503-path tests. Backwards-compatible at the **call shape** layer (an old image gets a clean `TypeError` at startup rather than a silently-half-wired router); strictly-additive at the **wire** layer (old WS clients filtering on the existing trace `kind`s ignore `summary_*` silently — DEC-022 / Phase 11 v2.1.5 discipline). (3) **Summary callable is a fourth optional factory parameter** (`SummaryCallable = Callable[[str, str, str], Awaitable[str]]`) — DI seam for tests + for 13.4's skill-tier swap-in. Production default = `default_summary_callable(config_provider)` (thin inline `androscan.llm.client.complete` call wrapped in `loop.run_in_executor` + `asyncio.wait_for(timeout=30.0)`); v1 prompt is name-quality only (class + method + descriptor; no decompiled source) — 13.4 will enrich with a source snippet via `decompile_cache` + register a formal `summarise_method` skill, but the seam stays so 13.4's swap-in is a one-line change in `app.py`. (4) **Per-method dedup** via in-memory `Set[str]` keyed on `MethodRef.smali_signature`-shaped strings (`method_key()` helper) — same method firing 50× in a single session triggers one LLM call, not 50; cross-session dedup happens in the `skill_results_cache` layer. (5) **Cache-miss flow emits `summary_pending` before spawning the `asyncio.create_task`** so the UI sees the pending state even on fast LLM round-trips; **cache-hit flow skips `summary_pending` entirely** (no flicker for the operator's UI). (6) **Three new failure modes locked at the wire layer:** `error: "summary_timeout"` (`asyncio.TimeoutError`), `error: "empty_summary"` (LLM returned whitespace-only), `error: "<exception_message>"` (other exceptions). **Failed summaries are deliberately NOT cached** — failures may be transient; let the next session re-fire. (7) **WebSocket lifecycle mirrors `frida_routes.ws_trace` exactly:** accept → 1008-close on unknown session → ring-buffer replay → register `on_event` hook → forever-pump bounded `asyncio.Queue(maxsize=2000)` → restore previous hook on disconnect → cancel any in-flight summary tasks (so the executor doesn't keep running an LLM call for a client that's already gone). (8) **`app_sha` resolution is best-effort** — missing `app_meta.json` → cache layer disabled (logged), summary multiplex still emits live but never warms the cache; this fail-soft posture matches `decompile_cache.get_status` for the same condition. **Backend deliverables (3 files modified, 2 files created).** New module `androscan/web/trace_summary.py` (~280 LOC) owning: `SUMMARY_SKILL_ID = "summarise_method"` constant (cache discriminator — chosen ahead of 13.4's skill-registration so 13.4's writes hit byte-equal-keyed cache entries; **no cache rebuild required at the cutover**), `SUMMARY_TIMEOUT_S = 30.0`, `SUMMARY_PENDING_KIND` / `SUMMARY_READY_KIND` / `SUMMARY_FAILED_KIND` constants, `SummaryCallable` async type alias, `java_class_to_smali` (Java-form ↔ Smali-form, idempotent on already-Smali input), `method_key` (Smali-signature shape), `summary_cache_params` (4-tuple cache key), `lookup_cached_summary` / `store_summary` (treat-empty-as-miss discipline + fail-soft on cache I/O errors), `default_summary_callable` (production wiring). `androscan/web/trace_routes.py` extended with the WS endpoint (~270 LOC inside the factory closure: `_dispatch_summary_for_entry` per-event hook, three `_emit_summary_*` helpers, `_fire_summary` background task with full exception ladder, replay + live-pump branches both routing through `_dispatch`); imports + factory signature + return type updated; module-level `_now_ts` helper added for test seam. `androscan/web/app.py` rewired to unpack the `(rest, ws)` tuple and `include_router` both. **Tests.** `tests/test_trace_summary.py` (NEW; **+21 tests** across `java_class_to_smali` (4 — dotted/Smali/$inner/empty), `method_key` (2 — round-trip + empty-descriptor), `summary_cache_params` (2 — full-shape + class-form normalisation), cache round-trip discipline (5 — miss/hit/empty-as-miss/no-op-on-empty/overload separation/per-app separation), the async `default_summary_callable` (4 — content/whitespace-strip/timeout/exception propagation), and wire-shape constants (3 — kind values + skill id + timeout). `tests/test_trace_routes.py` (**+15 WS integration tests** covering: registration smoke; replay-then-stream pre-existing events; 1008 unknown-session; 1008 no-`frida_client_provider`; cache-miss → pending+ready; cache-hit → ready-only with `cached: true`; per-method dedup (2 entries → 1 summary); exception → `summary_failed`; timeout → `summary_failed`; empty → `summary_failed`; non-`entry` phases (`ready` / `exit` / `hook_failed` / `error`) DON'T fire summaries; no-summary-callable wiring still streams trace events but skips multiplex; live-event path also fires summaries; successful summary writes back to `skill_results_cache.json` for cross-session reuse; failed summaries are NOT cached. New `_FakeFridaSession` extensions: `events()` / `stage_replay_event()` / `emit_live_event()` / mutable `on_event` hook so tests can drive both replay-buffer and live-pump branches. New helper `_ws_app_with_summary_stub` wires a minimal FastAPI app with `dataclasses.replace(Config.default(), run_folder_root=str(tmp_path))` so cache writes land in the test's tmpdir. **Test count:** 1217 baseline → **1253** HEAD (`+36` net: 21 helper unit + 15 WS integration). **Lint:** zero errors across `trace_summary.py` + `trace_routes.py` + `app.py` + both test files. **Out of scope (deferred to 13.4 / later sub-steps):** registered `summarise_method` skill in `_SKILL_MODULES` with `requires_confirmation=False` → 13.4; decompiled-source snippet in the LLM prompt → 13.4; rename "Decision Timeline" → "Behavior Trace" in the FE → 13.5; `ExecutionFlow` flowchart + `Inspector` pane FE → 13.6 / 13.7. The `ws_url` field returned by the 13.2 `POST /api/trace/{app_id}/dynamic` endpoint **still points at `/ws/frida/{session_id}` for v1** — the FE rewire to `/ws/trace/{app_id}/{session_id}` lands in 13.5. **`docs/DECISIONS.md`:** no new DEC; 13.3's locks are implementation choices of DEC-029 (the additive `summary_*` event vocabulary was already locked at DEC-029) — the design rationale is documented inline in `trace_summary.py`'s module docstring as the canonical reference. **`docs/TASKS.md`:** Active Task block flipped from "13.2 done; 13.3 next" → "13.3 done; 13.4 next"; Phase 13 row in § Sub-task status (implementation) table extended with the 13.3 deliverables; 13.3 row in § Phase 13 sub-step backlog flipped from `[ ]` → `[x]` with full landing-detail footprint; 13.4 row tightened to note that 13.3 already shipped the cache wiring under the same skill id, so 13.4 is now strictly a swap-in behind the `SummaryCallable` seam. **`docs/STATE.md`:** this entry. **`docs/SAFETY_AND_SECURITY.md`:** unchanged in 13.3 — the new WS surface inherits the existing per-app hook prefix allowlist enforcement from 13.2 (the WS handler doesn't accept arbitrary class targets — it only emits summaries for methods that ALREADY appear in `phase: "entry"` Frida events, which themselves are gated by the 13.2 closure-extraction allowlist). The 13.10 docs sweep will surface the WS multiplex + `summary_*` event-vocabulary in §12.x.

- 2026-05-08 Phase 13 — **Behavior Trace v3 — Dynamic Execution + Flowchart UI overhaul — sub-step 13.2 — `POST /api/trace/{app_id}/dynamic` endpoint + JSONL persistence at `apps/<app_id>/<run>/dynamic_trace.jsonl`** (single atomic ship vehicle; BE only — no UI surface, no WebSocket changes; frontend overhaul lands in 13.5 → 13.9). **No planning checkpoint at the top of 13.2** — the wire shape was locked by 13.1's plan-mode checkpoint; 13.2 is straightforward route + Frida session orchestration plumbing built on top of the template that already exists. **Wire-shape adjustments from the original 13.0 brief, surfaced by an explore subagent on the existing `frida_routes.py` patterns:** (1) request body uses `{entry: str, hops: int, hop_cap: int, spawn: bool, event_label: str?}` rather than POSTing the whole `BehaviorAnchor` payload — the anchor lives in `trace.sqlite` already (10.6 locked `(entry, hops)` as the canonical cache key) so the route reads via `trace_cache.read_anchor(...)` (404 on cache miss with a hint pointing at `POST /api/trace/{app_id}/anchor`); (2) stop endpoint is **`DELETE /api/trace/{app_id}/dynamic/{session_id}`** (NOT `POST .../stop`) to mirror the existing **`DELETE /api/frida/sessions/{session_id}`** Hook Lab pattern exactly — same fail-soft contract (404 on unknown session; 200 + log-but-don't-raise on detach exceptions); (3) closure extraction order locked at **entry_method → decisions[*].method → plans[*].target_method → plans[*].source_decision_method → advanced_plans[*]** with dedup by `MethodRef.smali_signature` (overloads stay distinguishable); v1 cap is simple head-truncation (entry method always survives `hop_cap=1`); (4) per-app **`hook_target_package_prefix`** allowlist enforced via direct call to `frida_routes._resolve_target_prefix` — same fail-closed posture as Hook Lab (403 if no prefix configured + no manifest package id; the route deliberately does NOT allow operator-supplied packages, the prefix IS the package for v1, per-class targeting is a v2 affordance once the UI has a "trace this dependency" mode). **Backend deliverables.** New module `androscan/web/trace_dynamic.py` (~150 LOC, three pure functions: `extract_closure_methods(anchor) -> tuple[MethodRef, ...]`, `cap_methods(methods, hop_cap) -> tuple[MethodRef, ...]`, `methods_to_json(methods) -> str` — the JS-array-literal-formatted JSON expected by the `behavior_trace_multi` template's `methods_json` substitution slot). New `StartDynamicTraceBody` Pydantic model + new constants `_MIN_HOP_CAP = 1` / `_MAX_HOP_CAP = 500` / `_DEFAULT_HOP_CAP = 50` / `_EVENT_LABEL_PREFIX = "behavior-trace"` + the two new routes in `androscan/web/trace_routes.py` (~250 LOC including docstrings; the start route's 9-step lifecycle: `app_dir_resolver(app_id)` (404 on unknown app) → `_cache_dir_for(app_id)` (409 on decompile not ready) → `_validate_entry(entry)` (422 on bad smali) → `trace_cache.read_anchor(cache_dir, entry, hops)` (404 on cache miss) → `extract_closure_methods + cap_methods + methods_to_json` → `load_app_settings + _resolve_target_prefix` (403 on no prefix) → `render_hook_template("behavior_trace_multi", {...})` (422 on `HookParamError`, 500 on `HookTemplateNotFound`) → `client.is_available()` (503 on False) → `client.attach(package, spawn=...)` (503 on `FridaUnavailableError`, 502 on generic Exception) → `session.set_persistence_path(run/dynamic_trace.jsonl)` (best-effort; logs and continues if it fails) → `session.load_script(rendered.js)` (502 on failure WITH detach-on-failure cleanup so a transient JS error doesn't leak Frida sessions). `build_trace_router` factory grew an optional third arg `frida_client_provider: Optional[Callable[[], Any]] = None`; passing `None` (or omitting it) makes the dynamic routes return **503** cleanly so existing two-arg test callers + future read-only deployments work unchanged. **`androscan/web/app.py`:** moved the `_frida_provider` definition above the `build_trace_router(...)` call so both routers share the same lazy provider closure; comments updated to point at the new contract. **JSONL persistence:** path locked at **`apps/<app_id>/<run>/dynamic_trace.jsonl`** per DEC-029 — single file per run (no `frida/` subdirectory, no per-session-id filename), each dynamic trace gets a fresh `create_run_folder(...)` call so concurrent traces never collide. Wired through the existing `FridaSession.set_persistence_path(...)` writer (line-buffered append, `dropped` counter for ring-buffer overflow, `persist_dropped` counter for serialisation failures — DEC-029 gets all of Hook Lab v1's persistence guarantees for free). **Response shape pinned:** `{session_id, app_id, template_id, package, pid, started_at, hook_count, closure_size, hop_cap, event_label, ws_url, persist_path, anchor: {entry_method, hops}}`. `ws_url` points at the existing `/ws/frida/{session_id}` for v1 — 13.3 will introduce `/ws/trace/{session_id}` with `summary_pending` / `summary_ready` / `summary_failed` multiplexing on top; until then the FE can subscribe to the Frida channel directly and filter by `event_label`. **No new tracker tables in `trace.sqlite`** — dynamic trace is per-run, not per-anchor. **Status codes locked:** 200 / 404 (unknown app, no cached anchor) / 422 (bad entry, hop_cap out of range, empty closure after cap) / 409 (decompile not ready) / 403 (no `hook_target_package_prefix`) / 502 (attach generic failure, load_script failure) / 503 (Frida not available, no `frida_client_provider` wired). **Tests.** `tests/test_trace_dynamic.py` (NEW; +17 unit tests covering closure extraction including overload distinguishability + dedup-by-smali_signature + entry-first invariant + advanced-plans-last + None-method defensiveness, `cap_methods` head-truncation + zero/negative cap behaviour, `methods_to_json` shape + empty-input + order preservation + JS-array-literal validity + void no-args descriptor, and a closure→cap→encode roundtrip pinning the route's pipeline contract end-to-end). `tests/test_trace_routes.py` (`+25` tests covering route-registration smoke for both new paths; happy-path response wire shape pinning every field; JSONL path layout invariant `apps/<app_id>/<run>/dynamic_trace.jsonl`; operator-supplied `event_label` passes through to rendered JS; `hop_cap=1` hook-count truncation; 404 on unknown app + on missing cached anchor (with hint pointing at `POST /anchor`); 409 on missing decompile cache; 422 on empty / oversize entry + on `hop_cap=0` / `hop_cap=501`; 403 on no-hook-prefix; 503 on `is_available=False` + on `FridaUnavailableError` from attach; 502 on generic attach exception; 502 on `load_script` failure WITH detach-on-failure cleanup verification; 503 on no-`frida_client_provider`-wired path for both POST + DELETE; DELETE happy path; DELETE 404 on unknown session + on unknown app; DELETE swallows detach-side exceptions). Used a `FridaClient`-subclass fake (`_FakeFridaClient` + `_FakeFridaSession`) injected onto `app.state.frida_client` rather than the heavier `_frida_python` stub from `tests/test_frida_routes.py` — surgical to the route layer's contract, doesn't bring device-handshake plumbing into the test scope. **Test count:** 1177 baseline → **1217** HEAD (`+40` net: 17 helper unit + 25 route integration + 0 net change in `test_behavior_trace_multi.py`); zero regressions. **Lint:** zero errors across `trace_routes.py` + `trace_dynamic.py` + `app.py` + both test files. **Out of scope (deferred per DEC-029):** WebSocket multiplexing of summary events on `/ws/trace` → 13.3; `summarise_method` LLM skill + `skill_results_cache` integration → 13.4; rename "Decision Timeline" → "Behavior Trace" in the FE → 13.5; `ExecutionFlow` flowchart + `Inspector` pane FE → 13.6 / 13.7; mode toggle + dynamic overlay FE → 13.8.

- 2026-05-08 Phase 13 — **Behavior Trace v3 — Dynamic Execution + Flowchart UI overhaul — sub-step 13.1 — multi-method Frida tracer template + tiered arg/return serialiser** (single atomic ship vehicle; BE+template only — no UI surface, no HTTP route, no WebSocket changes; those land in 13.2 → 13.9). **Plan-mode planning checkpoint** at the top of 13.1 (mirrors 10.6 / 11.4 wire-shape checkpoints) locked the JS template shape ahead of implementation. **Q1 — per-method `Java.use()` failure handling.** All three reasons surfaced separately (`class_not_found` / `method_not_found` / `impl_set_failed`); per-failure events emitted **live during hook setup** under a new `phase: "hook_failed"` (distinct from `phase: "error"` for runtime invocation errors), carrying `{class, method, descriptor, reason, error}`; aggregate counts only in the `phase: "ready"` event ({methods_attempted, methods_hooked, methods_failed}), no nested `hooks_failed[]` array. UX (deferred to 13.7 / 13.9) is aggregate — Run Trace card shows `Hooking 34 methods… (3 failed)` live then `26 / 30 hooked · 4 failed` post-`ready`; per-node `Possibly inlined` Inspector callout with reason-specific copy. **Q2 — depth-counter: option (b) locked.** Tracer-side `Map<threadId, Frame[]>` with monotonic global `seqCounter`; every `entry` event carries `parent_call_seq` (seq of nearest unmatched entry on same thread; `null` if top-level), every `exit` carries `entry_seq` linking back to the matching entry; Java view (`java.lang.Thread.currentThread().getId()`) for `thread_id`; dangling exits (Frida attaches mid-call) emit `entry_seq: null`. **Q3.A — tier ordering: option (a) locked.** `String(value)` first, fall back to reflection only if the result matches the default-`toString()` fingerprint `^[\\w$.]+@[0-9a-f]+$`. **Q3.B — thresholds:** confirmed at DEC-029 defaults — `STR_TRUNCATE_AT = 256`, `BYTE_FULL_HEX_LIMIT = 32`, `BYTE_PREVIEW_LIMIT = 16`, `MAX_FIELDS_PER_OBJECT = 8`, `MAX_LIST_ENTRIES = 5`. **Q3.C — bespoke handlers:** confirmed v1 set — null, primitives, String, byte[], Bundle, Intent, List, Map. **Locked wire shape (5 phases, all flat per-`send({...})` payloads):** `hook_failed` (per-failure during setup) / `ready` (one summary after setup) / `entry` (per Java method invocation; carries `seq, thread_id, thread_name, thread_depth, parent_call_seq, args`) / `exit` (paired with entry by `entry_seq`; carries `return`) / `error` (per runtime exception mid-call; re-throws after emission so app behaviour is unchanged). **Locked 11-tier serialiser dispatch order** (first match wins; per-step `try/catch`): null → primitives → strings → byte[] → Bundle → Intent → List → Map → user-defined-class-with-`toString()` → reflection-over-declared-fields (capped at 8 fields, recursion depth 1) → `String(value)` final fallback. **Backend deliverables (3 files modified / created):** new `androscan/adapters/frida_hooks/behavior_trace_multi.py` (~412 rendered JS lines; `TEMPLATE: HookTemplate` with two params — `methods_json` (JS-object-literal-formatted JSON array of `{class, method, descriptor}` built by the caller via `json.dumps`; the `str.format` substituter does not re-interpret braces inside the substituted value, so the JSON braces survive intact — same trick `custom.py`'s `js_body` uses) and `event_label`); `androscan/adapters/frida_hooks/__init__.py` `_TEMPLATE_MODULES` tuple appended with the new module path; new `tests/test_behavior_trace_multi.py` (+45 tests across 7 test classes) covering smoke render, parameter validation, wire-shape vocabulary (every locked phase + every failure reason + every event-payload field present in rendered JS), state-machine helpers (`seqCounter` / `pushFrame` / `popFrame` / `threadStacks` / `nextSeq` symbols), serialiser tier handlers + their dispatch order in the `summarise()` body (verified via `rfind` to isolate dispatcher call from definition), every locked threshold value, JS syntax validity via `_jsparse.parse_frida_js` (catches brace-doubling drift at CI time without needing Frida or a JVM), summary content (every phase / every reason / every tier mentioned + the R8-inlining callout). The existing `tests/test_frida_hook_templates.py` fail-closed registry walk auto-picked up the new template across 5 parameterised invariants. **Verification:** `pytest tests/test_behavior_trace_multi.py tests/test_frida_hook_templates.py tests/test_frida_routes.py tests/test_generate_frida_hook_skill.py tests/test_frida_hook_jsparse.py -v` → 227 passed, 0 failed. `ReadLints` on the three modified files → 0 lints. **Out of scope for 13.1 (lands later):** HTTP route `POST /api/trace/{app_id}/dynamic` (13.2); WebSocket multiplexing on `/ws/trace` with `summary_*` events (13.3); `summarise_method` LLM skill + `skill_results_cache` integration (13.4); frontend rename + `ExecutionFlow` + `Inspector` + mode toggle + threshold-colored run-trace button + `inlined?` Inspector callout (13.5 → 13.9); live JVM smoke fixtures for the serialiser (deferred to 13.2's integration test once the route can wire the template through to a `FridaSession` — `_jsparse` covers syntax at CI; per-tier `try/catch` is the v1 robustness story for runtime). **`docs/DECISIONS.md`:** no new DEC; 13.1's locks are sub-decisions of DEC-029 — the Q1-Q3 answer set is documented inline in `behavior_trace_multi.py`'s module docstring as the canonical reference. **`docs/TASKS.md`:** Active Task block flipped from "13.0 done; 13.1 next" → "13.1 done; 13.2 next"; Phase 13 row in § Sub-task status (implementation) table extended with the 13.1 deliverables; 13.1 row in § Phase 13 sub-step backlog flipped from `[ ]` → `[x]` with full landing-detail footprint. **`docs/STATE.md`:** this entry. **`docs/SAFETY_AND_SECURITY.md`:** unchanged in 13.1 — the new template will become operator-reachable only when 13.2's HTTP route ships; the 13.10 docs sweep will add the §12.x update for multi-method tracer's per-app `hook_target_package_prefix` allowlist + threshold-color UX.

- 2026-05-08 Phase 13 — **Behavior Trace v3 — Dynamic Execution + Flowchart UI overhaul — sub-step 13.0 — planning checkpoint + DEC-029 + canvas mockup** (single atomic ship vehicle; docs + canvas only — no code changes). **Why a new Phase 13 ahead of Phase 12.** Operator dogfooding 2026-05-07 → 2026-05-08 surfaced two friction signals on top of the v2 / v2.1 static substrate that are orthogonal to Phase 12's deferred precision menu and large enough to warrant their own phase: (1) the existing "Decision Timeline" UI is unintuitive for pentesters who aren't strong developers — the linear list of `DecisionPointCard`s with `true NEUTRAL` / `false NEUTRAL` badges, predicate registers (`v3`, `p0`), and Smali instruction indices (`#11`, `#31`) reads as low-level static-analysis output rather than an operator-actionable view of "what does this method do and where can I bypass it"; (2) static analysis alone can't tell the operator what *actually* fired at runtime — operators ask "is this start-to-finish?" / "which methods actually run when I tap Login?", questions only a runtime trace can answer. **Operator direction was explicit** ("Ignore phase 12 stuff for now, we'll come to it later after phase 13") — Phase 12 / DEC-026 (the static-precision menu: ISSUE-014, auto-verify, free-form LLM JS, per-app TTL, iOS adapters, Smali patching, LLM-budget real-app measurement) stays untouched and is revisited after Phase 13 ships. **Three coherent deliverables locked at DEC-029** (ratified 2026-05-08 after a three-round plan-mode iteration over the WeakBank `MainActivity.login(…)` example): (1) **UI overhaul** — rename "Decision Timeline" → "Behavior Trace"; replace the linear timeline with a flowchart + Inspector pane; remove predicate-register / instruction-index / `true NEUTRAL` / `false NEUTRAL` noise from the primary UI (can resurface behind an "Advanced" toggle in a future sub-step if asked). (2) **Dynamic execution trace** via a new `behavior_trace_multi` Frida template that hooks every method in the active `BehaviorAnchor`'s closure (subject to the threshold-color hop-cap), captures entry/exit events with a per-thread depth counter, stringifies arguments and return values via a tiered helper (primitives → `java.lang.*` common types → user-defined classes via reflection → fallback `toString()`; truncation at 256 chars / 32 bytes / depth 1 / field 8), streams events over the existing `/ws/trace` WebSocket, and persists to `apps/<app_id>/<run>/dynamic_trace.jsonl`. (3) **Per-method LLM summaries** via a new `summarise_method` skill (`requires_confirmation=False`); live-on-first-hit for methods that fire dynamically OR are explicitly clicked in Inspector; cached in `skill_results_cache` keyed on `(app_sha, class_smali, method_name, descriptor)`; multiplexed on the trace WebSocket as `summary_pending` / `summary_ready` / `summary_failed` events. **Locked design details from the 2026-05-08 plan-mode iteration** (these are committed, not open questions): three modes (`Static` default before any dynamic run / `Dynamic` / `Both` overlay default after first dynamic run; selected mode + selected node persist via `localStorage` per app); **edge styling = color-only emphasis** (same 1.5px stroke + 6×6 arrowhead for ALL edges; fired = accent blue + full opacity + solid; untaken in `dynamic` = gray dashed at 55% opacity; untaken in `both` = verdict color (allow=green, deny=red) dashed at 55% opacity; static-only = verdict color, full opacity, solid — the operator explicitly rejected thicker fired edges and a larger arrowhead during the iteration ("Blue arrow looks fine, but don't make it thick"), so the lock is documented in DEC-029 + this STATE.md entry to keep it sticky); node design (220×72px, 6px radius, corner pills for `ALLOW` / `DENY` sinks + `GATE` candidate gates, stacked-card visual for overloaded methods, dashed border + `inlined?` corner label for R8-suspected methods, live values like `pin="1234" → false` rendered inline in dynamic/both modes); Inspector pane on the right (fixed 360px) with five operator-actionable sections — signature + LLM summary + source line + predicate origin + bypass plans (predicate-register / instruction-index / neutral-badge noise removed); run-trace button color thresholds (≤20 hooks green / 21-50 yellow / 51-100 orange / >100 red — operator decides; no hard cap). **`BehaviorAnchor` / `DecisionPoint` / `BypassPlan` / `PredicateOrigin` schemas unchanged** (no schema bump on `trace.sqlite`; SCHEMA_VERSION stays at `"2"` from Phase 11 v2 — dynamic-trace data lives in the parallel JSONL file at `apps/<app_id>/<run>/dynamic_trace.jsonl`, NOT in `trace.sqlite`). **Explicitly NOT in v1 scope** (revisit in 13.x or in a follow-up DEC): marching-ants animation on fired path; branch-outcome inference from dynamic data; speculative pre-summarisation of every static method; separate "Tree of calls" panel (the flowchart IS the tree); mutation hooks (modify return values mid-run). **Sub-step backlog 13.0 → 13.10** (strictly linear, one sub-step per Agent-mode session; mirrors Phase 11's 11.0 → 11.7 cadence): 13.0 (this commit, planning checkpoint + DEC-029 + canvas mockup + this docs sweep); 13.1 (multi-method Frida tracer template + tiered arg/return serialiser — BE+template; preceded by a brief Ask-mode planning checkpoint to lock the JS template shape); 13.2 (`POST /api/trace/{app_id}/dynamic` endpoint + JSONL persistence — BE); 13.3 (WebSocket multiplexing — extend `/ws/trace` with `summary_*` events — BE); 13.4 (`summarise_method` LLM skill — BE+LLM); 13.5 (frontend rename "Decision Timeline" → "Behavior Trace" + demote `DecisionTimeline.tsx` behind a feature flag — FE-only); 13.6 (`ExecutionFlow` component — FE; preceded by a brief Ask-mode planning checkpoint to pick the substrate — hand-rolled SVG via `computeDAGLayout`-ported math vs. React Flow); 13.7 (`Inspector` pane — FE); 13.8 (mode toggle + dynamic overlay + per-thread depth visualization + summary streaming — FE); 13.9 (threshold-colored run-trace button + `inlined?` detection wiring + chat-widget renderer for `summarise_method` — FE+small BE); 13.10 (docs sweep + DEC-029 closing note + screenshots + remove `VITE_BEHAVIOR_TRACE_LEGACY` flag). **Canonical mockup at `~/.cursor/projects/Users-sujoy-chakravarti-TW-Work-AI-security-Cursor-AI-AndroScan/canvases/phase-13-trace-mockup.canvas.tsx`** — written from scratch in this same commit using the canvas SDK exclusively (`computeDAGLayout` + `useCanvasState` + `useHostTheme` + `Tooltip`); ~430 LOC including the WeakBank login-flow sample data + `FlowDiagram` SVG renderer + `InspectorPane` React component + the three-mode toggle. The production frontend in 13.6 → 13.9 must visually match the mockup within the limits of React-on-DOM vs. canvas SDK's SVG-via-`computeDAGLayout` substrate. **Verification:** `git diff --name-only` shows only `STATE.md` + `TASKS.md` + `DECISIONS.md` + `phase-13-trace-mockup.canvas.tsx`; no code changes; no test changes; no `global_config.yaml` changes; existing pytest / `tsc --noEmit` / `vite build` / `ReadLints` posture unchanged. **Next sub-step:** **13.1 — multi-method Frida tracer template + tiered arg/return serialiser**, preceded by a brief Ask-mode planning checkpoint to lock the JS template shape (per-method `Java.use()` failure handling, depth-counter implementation, arg/return serialiser tier ordering). Mirrors the 10.6 / 11.4 wire-shape checkpoints. **Phase 12 / DEC-026 stays untouched** per operator direction; LCP.7 (model-sourcing UX) is the natural next-after-Phase-13 candidate.

- 2026-05-07 Phase 11 v2.1 follow-up patch — **v2.1.10 — three-bug operator-dogfood patch: Trace + Manual Hooks state preservation across Lab-mode hops, narrow-pane Trace JSON column repair, Settings scroller migration to page-right edge** (single atomic ship vehicle; FE-only; same calendar day as v2.1.7 / v2.1.8 / v2.1.9). **Bug 1 — Lab-mode state lost on hop-and-return.** Pre-v2.1.10, `LabTab.tsx` conditionally rendered `<LabTraceMode />` / `<ManualHooksMode />` / `<GraphMode />` keyed on `labMode === <id>`, which meant the inactive modes were fully unmounted. Operators reported losing their entire Trace-mode in-progress state (entry draft, hops, loaded `BehaviorAnchor`, decompile tree, similar-classes candidates, picker results) every time they popped out to Manual Hooks for a quick hook tweak and came back. The same applied to Manual Hooks (Frida session, hook builder draft, decompiled source cache, ChatDock scroll position) on the inverse hop. **Fix:** Trace + Manual Hooks are now always-mounted via a new `.lab-mode-content-pane` wrapper that uses `display: contents` for the active mode and `[hidden]` (UA `display: none`) for the inactive one — children behave as direct flex items of `.lab-mode-content` exactly as they did pre-v2.1.10, but their React state survives the mode hop because the components never unmount. Graph mode stays conditional per operator scope choice on the v2.1.10 design questionnaire (its single-pane CallGraphView mounts a Cytoscape Web Worker that's measurably expensive to keep idle, and the operator's pan / zoom state already serialises through the cytoscape instance's own viewport cache the Inspect graph also uses). The `LabTab.ManualHooksMode` `anchoredMethods` overlay used to refresh on remount-after-Trace-build (the lines 436-441 doc-block); v2.1.10 replaces that pattern with a new `traceCacheVersion: number` + `bumpTraceCacheVersion()` counter on `WorkbenchContext` (mirrors the local `cachedReloadTick` / `sessionsRefreshTick` patterns inside `LabTab.tsx` itself but lifted to context because Trace and Manual Hooks are now siblings in the React tree rather than mutually-exclusive conditional renders, so they can't share a local prop-thread). `LabTraceMode` calls `bumpTraceCacheVersion()` from the post-Build effect (`state.kind === "loaded" && state.from === "build"`) and from `onDeleteCached` on `r.ok`; `ManualHooksMode`'s `anchoredMethods` effect adds `traceCacheVersion` to its dep array so the overlay refreshes immediately after a Trace-mode mutation. Counter is monotonic across `appId` changes (keeping reasoning simple — "counter went up → re-fetch") and never reset; the consumer's effect already keys on `appId`, so a stale counter from a previous app is harmless. **Bug 2 — Trace JSON column rendered as a tall narrow column instead of a row in narrow Lab panes.** The `.trace-row-meta` CSS Grid (`grid-template-columns: 1.2rem 6.5rem 3.4rem 4rem auto 1fr`) had no compressibility constraints on the `auto` label track — an unbreakable label like `onCreate$lambda$0_trace` (no spaces / hyphens / break opportunities) forced the track to its full max-content width (~17 rem at 0.74 rem font), leaving the `1fr` payload track at near-zero in narrow panes (Manual Hooks right column defaults to 22 % of Lab tab width = ~19 rem total). When the operator expanded a row, the payload's `overflow-wrap: anywhere` rule then wrapped JSON every ~8-10 chars, rendering as a tall narrow column. **Fix:** added `min-width: 0; max-width: 14rem; overflow: hidden; text-overflow: ellipsis; white-space: nowrap;` to `.trace-label` so the label compresses with `…` truncation when the row is narrow, freeing the payload column to take the remaining width. The full label is still readable via the row's existing `title={compact}` browser tooltip + the chevron-expand renders the label inline in the payload's first key (e.g. `{label:"onCreate$lambda$0_trace",phase:"entry",…}`). 14 rem cap chosen to comfortably fit a typical Smali method name (`onCreate`, `findViewById`, `verifyPin`, etc.) without truncation while still giving the payload column the headroom it needs for JSON to wrap onto wide lines rather than narrow columns. **Bug 3 — Settings sub-tab vertical scrollbar offset from the page right edge.** The Settings tab's panel chain (`.settings-tab` grid 240px | 1fr → `.settings-panel-host` overflow:hidden → `.settings-panel` max-width:1100px margin:0 auto padding:16px 24px 0 → `.settings-panel-header` + `.settings-panel-body` overflow:auto) put the scroll on the inner `.settings-panel-body`, so the scrollbar sat at the inner panel's right edge — typically 24 px (panel right padding) inside the right column on a narrow viewport, more on a wider one (centered panel adds 30+ px of margin on each side). Operator reported the offset; the screenshot showed a ~50 px gap between the scrollbar and the browser window's right edge. **Fix:** promoted the scroller from `.settings-panel-body` up to `.settings-panel-host` (`overflow-y: auto; overflow-x: hidden;`); reduced `.settings-panel-body` to a plain content block (dropped `flex: 1` + `min-height: 0` + `overflow: auto`); made `.settings-panel-header` `position: sticky; top: 0; z-index: 2; background: var(--bg);` so it pins to the top of the host's scroll viewport (same operator UX as before — header always visible while body scrolls — but the scrollbar now lives on the host element which is the right edge of the `1fr` column inside `.settings-tab`, i.e. the absolute right of the page minus the 240 px nav). The header's `margin-bottom: 16px` is folded into `.settings-panel-body { padding-top: 16px }` so the visual spacing stays the same (the margin would otherwise leave a transparent strip below the sticky header where scrolled content could show through). Affects all four Settings sub-panels (Global / App / Status / Diagnostics) since they share the `.settings-panel` shell — bonus consistency win. **Verification:** all 1124 pytest tests pass (no test changes from v2.1.10 itself — all three bugs are UI-mounting-shape / CSS, not API behaviour; the +31 from v2.1.9's 1093 baseline come from two interim commits — `526016b` ollama-think CLI flag + `ee54902` consolidation fix — that landed between v2.1.9 and v2.1.10 on the same calendar day, both unrelated to the v2.1 follow-up release umbrella); `tsc --noEmit` clean; `vite build` clean (1238.79 KB JS / 385.33 KB gzip / 94.54 KB CSS / 15.89 KB gzip — JS +0.28 KB raw / +0.10 KB gzip vs v2.1.9 baseline; CSS +0.21 KB raw / +0.04 KB gzip; the `traceCacheVersion` plumbing + the `.lab-mode-content-pane` / `.trace-label` / `.settings-panel-*` CSS rule additions all sit comfortably inside the LCP.0 ceiling of +5 KB JS / +2 KB CSS); `ReadLints` clean across all 4 production-code files. **Operator impact:** (1) Lab-tab mode hops now feel non-destructive — switching to Manual Hooks for a quick hook tweak and back to Trace preserves the loaded anchor + entry draft + browse / advanced disclosure state; only Graph mode still resets on hop (intentional, per scope choice). (2) Narrow Trace panes (Manual Hooks right column, narrow Lab pane resize) now render JSON payloads on wide lines as designed; the label truncates with `…` and is still readable via tooltip / chevron-expand. (3) Settings sub-tab scrollbar lives at the right edge of the right column, matching the convention every other long-content panel in the workbench follows. **Branch posture:** ratified single-commit ship vehicle (the three bugs were small enough to land in one ship; they share the v2.1 follow-up release umbrella and post-release-patch posture); no v2.1.10 sub-step planning checkpoint (the design conversation was a back-and-forth Ask-mode session in this same calendar day, not a strict-linear sub-step gate). v2.1 follow-up release stays "complete 2026-05-05" (closed at v2.1.6) — v2.1.7 / v2.1.8 / v2.1.9 / v2.1.10 are all post-release patches under DEC-025's umbrella, not new sub-steps within the v2.1 backlog. **Next sub-step:** none directly. The Lab + Settings tabs are now in good operator-ergonomic shape for dogfooding; telemetry from real-app traces under the LCP track infrastructure is the gating input for promoting Phase 12 — Behavior Trace v3 / cross-tab follow-ups (DEC-026) back to Active. CI.0 (Phase 4 CI) remains a parallel non-blocking thread, currently deprioritized per operator's "Lab engine before CI" direction (2026-04-30); promote when Lab engine reaches a stable v3 baseline.

- 2026-05-07 Phase 11 v2.1 follow-up patch — **v2.1.9 — surface per-mode `<AppPicker />` only in empty-state when no app selected (closes v2.1.8 visual-stacking regression)** (single atomic ship vehicle; FE-only; same calendar day as v2.1.8). **What broke:** v2.1.8's per-Lab-mode `<AppPicker />` UX request was implemented as full-width header strips (Manual Hooks + Graph used a new `<header className="lab-mode-head">` with title-on-the-left + AppPicker-on-the-right; Trace used the existing `<header className="pane-head">`'s right-side actions slot). Operator dogfood that morning caught that Manual Hooks's strip stacked vertically directly under the global header's AppPicker — two identical "Select project…" dropdowns on consecutive vertical lines — read as a layout bug rather than a deliberate redundancy. The same visual stacking would occur for Graph mode, but the operator scope choice on the v2.1.9 design questionnaire was Trace + Manual Hooks only (Graph keeps its v2.1.8 strip — single-pane CallGraphView with no non-empty body to render when `appId` is null, different ergonomics). **What shipped.** **Trace mode** (`androscan/web/frontend/src/tabs/LabTraceMode.tsx`): the `<span className="pane-head-actions"><AppPicker /></span>` block was removed from the existing `<header className="pane-head">` (reverts the pane-head to its pre-v2.1.8 shape — title + subtitle only); the existing one-line `{!appId && <p className="muted small">No app selected — pick a project from the dropdown above to start tracing behaviour.</p>}` empty-state was replaced with a more prominent centered card (`<div className="lab-empty-state" role="status" aria-live="polite"><div className="lab-empty-state-card"><h3>No app selected</h3><p>Pick a project below to start tracing behaviour. The same dropdown lives in the top-right header — both surfaces are kept in sync.</p><AppPicker /></div></div>`). The empty-state sits above the existing `{appId && (<>...</>)}` block; once an app is selected, no in-pane picker renders at all and the operator relies on the global header for mid-session project switching. **Manual Hooks mode** (`androscan/web/frontend/src/tabs/LabTab.tsx` `ManualHooksMode`): the v2.1.8 `<header className="lab-mode-head">` strip + `<div className="lab-manual-hooks-mode">` flex-column wrapper were both removed; the entire 3-column `PanelGroup` is now gated on `appId` via an early-return `if (!appId) return (<EmptyState />)` block at the top of the render — the EmptyState uses the same `.lab-empty-state-card` class as Trace mode for visual consistency (mirror copy: "Pick a project below to start hooking. The same dropdown lives in the top-right header — both surfaces are kept in sync."). Whole-mode empty-state was chosen over per-panel empty-state because none of the Manual Hooks panels (CallGraphView / HookBuilder / Sessions / CodeView / Chat) can do useful work without an `app_id` — they all gate on it internally and would render placeholder messaging in 4 of 5 panels otherwise, cluttering the empty state. **Graph mode** is **explicitly unchanged** — the `<header className="lab-mode-head">` strip + AppPicker remain per the operator scope choice on the v2.1.9 questionnaire. **CSS** (`androscan/web/frontend/src/App.css`): `.lab-manual-hooks-mode` rule dropped (the wrapper it styled is gone); `.lab-mode-head` + `.lab-mode-head-title` + `.lab-mode-head .app-picker` rules kept (Graph still uses them); new shared `.lab-empty-state` + `.lab-empty-state-card` + `.lab-empty-state-title` + `.lab-empty-state-body` + `.lab-empty-state-card .app-picker` rules added (centered card pattern with `max-width: 32rem`, bordered `var(--panel-2)` background; the picker stretches to full card width via `width: 100%` to read as a deliberate primary action). **Doc-block** in `LabTraceMode.tsx`: a new v2.1.9 paragraph appended to the file-header doc-block explaining the placement migration + cross-referencing the v2.1.8 visual-stacking regression + noting that Graph mode keeps its v2.1.8 strip per scope choice. **Verification:** all 1093 tests pass (no test changes — bug was UI-mounting-shape, not API behaviour); `tsc --noEmit` clean; `vite build` clean (1238.51 KB JS / 385.23 KB gzip / 94.33 KB CSS / 15.85 KB gzip — JS +0.44 KB raw / +0.08 KB gzip vs v2.1.8 baseline; CSS +0.50 KB raw / +0.07 KB gzip; the new `.lab-empty-state*` rules slightly outweigh the dropped `.lab-manual-hooks-mode` rule); ReadLints clean across all 3 production-code files. **Operator impact:** the two "Select project…" dropdowns on consecutive vertical lines no longer ship; the picker now appears exactly when it's contextually useful (operator has no app selected and needs to pick one) and disappears entirely once an app is loaded. Mid-session project switching relies on the global header at the top-right (always visible, single source of truth — eliminates the visual redundancy entirely). **Branch posture:** ratified single-commit ship vehicle (no v2.1.9 sub-step planning checkpoint — the design conversation was a back-and-forth Ask-mode session in this same calendar day, not a strict-linear sub-step gate); v2.1 follow-up release stays "complete 2026-05-05" (closed at v2.1.6) — v2.1.7 / v2.1.8 / v2.1.9 are all post-release patches under DEC-025's umbrella, not new sub-steps within the v2.1 backlog. **Next sub-step:** none directly. The Lab tab is now in good shape for operator dogfooding — telemetry from real-app traces under the LCP track infrastructure is the gating input for promoting Phase 12 — Behavior Trace v3 / cross-tab follow-ups (DEC-026) back to Active. CI.0 (Phase 4 CI) remains a parallel non-blocking thread, currently deprioritized per operator's "Lab engine before CI" direction (2026-04-30); promote when Lab engine reaches a stable v3 baseline.

- 2026-05-07 Phase 11 v2.1 follow-up patch — **v2.1.8 — embed `<ChatDock>` into Trace mode + per-Lab-mode `<AppPicker />` strips** (single atomic ship vehicle; fixes the v2.1.4 / 10.8 collision the v2.1.7 patch couldn't cover end-to-end). **What broke:** Phase 10.8 deliberately removed the `<ChatDock>` from Trace mode (operators were supposed to mode-hop to Manual Hooks for chat — `LabTraceMode.tsx` doc-block lines 52-57 stated the contract); v2.1.4's "Ask AI" button was wired to `setPendingChatPrefill({tab: "lab", ...})` assuming a `ChatDock tab="lab"` was reachable from Trace mode, but the only consumer (`LabTab.ManualHooksMode`'s `chatRef.expand()` effect at lines ~258-267 + the `<ChatDock>` it renders at line 557) was unmounted whenever `labMode === "trace"`. Net effect: clicking "Ask AI" in Trace mode silently set the prefill on `WorkbenchContext` with no consumer mounted to receive it — operator-visible no-op. v2.1.7 (commit `8d84e64` 2026-05-06; never got a STATE.md entry — was a stealth patch fixing the empty-Smali case of the same button) tackled the symptom, not the wiring. v2.1.8 fixes the wiring. **What shipped.** **Frontend** (`androscan/web/frontend/src/tabs/LabTraceMode.tsx`): wraps the existing single-pane trace surface in a horizontal `react-resizable-panels` `PanelGroup` (`autoSaveId="lab-trace-h"` so size + collapsed state persist via `localStorage` mirrors the `lab-manual-h` pattern) — the existing trace UI on the left (`defaultSize={70}, minSize={40}`), a new collapsible `<ChatDock tab="lab">` on the right (`defaultSize={30}, minSize={12}, collapsedSize={3}`). New `chatRef: ImperativePanelHandle` + `chatCollapsed: boolean` state; the same `chatRef.expand()` effect on `pendingChatPrefill?.ts` that lives in `LabTab.ManualHooksMode` is duplicated here so the two modes have parallel expand-on-prefill consumers (only one mode is mounted at a time so they never race). New `traceChatAttachments` + `traceChatContextSummary` builders contribute the active `app_id` + active `BehaviorAnchor` (when `state.kind === "loaded"`) to the chat — smaller surface than Manual Hooks's builder (no selected method / decompiled source / Frida session, those are Manual Hooks concerns) but reuses the same shared `renderTraceAttachment` so the `trace` attachment shape is byte-identical regardless of which mode triggered the chat. **Per-mode `<AppPicker />`** (operator UX request bundled with the chat-embed work): each of the three Lab sub-modes now carries its own `<AppPicker />` instance — Trace mode adds it to the existing `<header className="pane-head">` via `<span className="pane-head-actions">`; Manual Hooks + Graph modes get a new thin `<header className="lab-mode-head">` strip on top of their content (Manual Hooks wraps the existing `PanelGroup` in a flex column `<div className="lab-manual-hooks-mode">`; Graph adds the strip directly to its existing `<div className="lab-graph-mode">`). All three local pickers + the global header `<AppPicker />` share `appId` via `WorkbenchContext` for **bidirectional sync without explicit plumbing** — picking from any of the four surfaces updates the other three in lockstep, same pattern that already keeps Reports's `ProjectsSidebar` synced with the global header. **Backend module extraction** (`androscan/web/frontend/src/util/traceChatAttachment.ts` — NEW): `_renderTraceAttachment` + `CHAT_TRACE_BUDGET` (6_000 chars) + `CHAT_TRACE_TOP_PLANS` (3) + `CHAT_TRACE_MAX_DECISIONS` (40) lifted out of `LabTab.tsx` and exported as `renderTraceAttachment` + the three constants. `LabTab.tsx` rewires its existing chat-attachment builder + context-summary helper to import from the new module instead of defining them locally; `LabTraceMode.tsx` imports the same `renderTraceAttachment` from the shared module. Both modes share the per-tab chat history (`WorkbenchContext.chats["lab"]`) by virtue of both rendering `<ChatDock tab="lab">` — they're never co-mounted (mutually-exclusive `labMode === "..."` branches), so there's no rendering conflict, and the unified history reads naturally as one "Lab conversation" rather than per-mode scratch surfaces. **CSS** (`App.css`): new `.lab-mode-head` strip styling for Manual Hooks + Graph modes (Trace re-uses the existing `.pane-head`); new `.lab-manual-hooks-mode` flex-column wrapper; new `.lab-trace-shell` flex-row root for the Trace-mode `PanelGroup`. **Doc-block updates** in `LabTraceMode.tsx`: the 10.8 "Trace mode itself still has no chat dock" paragraph is replaced with a v2.1.8 "Trace mode now carries its own ChatDock" paragraph that explicitly cross-references the v2.1.4 / 10.8 collision; the v2.1.4 doc-block grows a v2.1.8-specific subsection noting that the `chatRef.expand()` consumer now lives in BOTH `LabTab.ManualHooksMode` AND `LabTraceMode` (with an explicit "the two effects are mutually exclusive — only one mode is mounted at a time so they never race" note). **Verification:** all 1093 tests pass (no test changes needed — the bug was UI-mounting-shape, not API behaviour); `tsc --noEmit` clean; `vite build` clean (1238.07 KB JS / 385.15 KB gzip / 93.83 KB CSS / 15.78 KB gzip — JS +2.59 KB raw / +0.51 KB gzip vs DEC-028 baseline; CSS +0.41 KB raw / +0.07 KB gzip; both well within the LCP.0 ceiling of +5 KB JS / +2 KB CSS); ReadLints clean across all 4 production-code files. **Operator impact:** Ask AI works in-place from Trace mode (chat panel expands on the right with the prefilled prompt; operator can edit + send without leaving the mode); per-mode AppPicker means operators no longer have to scroll or move focus to the global header to switch projects mid-investigation — the dropdown is right there in the mode they're already looking at. **Branch posture:** ratified single-commit ship vehicle (no v2.1.8 sub-step planning checkpoint — the design conversation was a back-and-forth in this Agent-mode session, not a strict-linear sub-step gate); v2.1 follow-up release stays "complete 2026-05-05" (closed at v2.1.6) — v2.1.7 + v2.1.8 are post-release patches, not new sub-steps within the v2.1 backlog. **Next sub-step:** none directly. The Lab tab is now in good shape for the operator's real-app dogfooding under the LCP track infrastructure — telemetry from that use is the gating input for promoting Phase 12 — Behavior Trace v3 / cross-tab follow-ups (DEC-026) back to Active. CI.0 (Phase 4 CI) remains a parallel non-blocking thread, currently deprioritized per operator's "Lab engine before CI" direction (2026-04-30); promote when Lab engine reaches a stable v3 baseline.

- 2026-05-06 Phase 11 v2.1 follow-up patch — **v2.1.7 — Ask AI button works without text in (hidden-by-default per v2.1.1) Smali entry field** (commit `8d84e64`; FE-only; never got a STATE.md entry at the time — captured retroactively here for the as-built record per v2.1.8's discovery of the v2.1.4 / 10.8 collision pattern). The v2.1.4 spec gated the "Ask AI" button on `trimmedEntry` being non-empty (the `disabled={!trimmedEntry}` shape collided with v2.1.1's hide-Smali-by-default decision because the operators who most needed the rescue rope were the ones who didn't know which class to trace, and the entry field they were supposed to type into was hidden behind the Advanced toggle). v2.1.7 removed the empty-entry gate and added a free-form prompt template ("I'm looking for a trace entry method in this app. ") for the empty case — same UX as typing into the chat dock directly, just one click closer. The patch resolved the empty-input UX collision but left the deeper v2.1.4 / 10.8 collision (Trace mode has no `<ChatDock>` to consume the prefill) untouched — that's the gap v2.1.8 closes today.

- 2026-05-06 LCP follow-up commit — **DEC-028 ratification + `global_config.yaml` restructure + `model` → `default_model` rename + default-model bumps + `llama-server` recommended-startup correctness fix + `probe_llamacpp` `/health` switch** (single atomic ship vehicle; +1 net test; LCP.7 opened as planning-checkpoint-pending stub for the model-sourcing UX follow-up). **`global_config.yaml` (full rewrite):** all LLM-related sections (`ollama:`, `llamacpp:`, the implicit `llm:` parent for `provider` / `cloud_*` / `local_grammar_enabled`) now nest under a single `llm:` parent — final shape `llm: { provider, local_grammar_enabled, ollama: {...}, llamacpp: {...}, cloud: { model, api_key, temperature } }` mirroring LCP.1's `LLM_PROVIDERS` registry; all three sibling-fanout sections collapsed into one (operator quote: "looked disjointed and all over the place"). YAML keys `model` → `default_model` in both `llm.ollama:` and `llm.llamacpp:` to communicate the "default chat / analysis model unless per-app override fires" semantics that `Phase 8 chat.model_override` made operationally relevant. Recommended `llama-server` startup line in the doc-comment block fixed (`-fa` → `-fa on` per recent llama-server's required value for flash-attn; the bare `-fa` form crashed with `unknown value for --flash-attn: '--port'`) plus added `--no-webui` (disables llama-server's bundled web UI; AndroScan doesn't use it; reduces process surface) and `--cache-reuse 256` (KV-cache prefix reuse; free perf win on AndroScan's stable system-prompt prefix). New doc-comment paragraphs explain three model-sourcing options (HuggingFace cache via `llama-server -cl`, HuggingFace Hub via `-hf` which needs `HF_TOKEN` for private repos, local filesystem via `-m`), why per-request grammar enforcement stays in the LLM client rather than at server startup (`--grammar` / `--json-schema` would force structured-JSON onto chat / prose / report-rendering paths), and a forward-pointer to the LCP.7 model-sourcing UX. **`androscan/config/loader.py`:** `CONFIG_FIELD_MAP` keys renamed (`ollama_model: ("ollama", "model", ...)` → `("ollama", "default_model", ...)`; same for `llamacpp_model`); `cloud_model` / `cloud_api_key` / `cloud_temperature` section labels relabelled `"llm"` → `"cloud"` so they group cleanly under their own `llm.cloud:` sub-section.
