New `_yaml_path_for_section()` + `_walk_yaml_path()` + `_ensure_yaml_path()` helpers translate the flat UI-grouping section labels (`"ollama"` / `"llamacpp"` / `"cloud"` / etc.) to the nested on-disk YAML path (`["llm", "ollama"]` / `["llm", "llamacpp"]` / `["llm", "cloud"]` / `[section]` for everything else). `_merge_from_yaml` rewritten to read from the nested shape (no backwards-compat reader for the pre-DEC-028 flat shape — operator-ratified hard-break migration in single-operator context); `effective_sources` + `dump_to_yaml` walk the dotted YAML path via the helper; `global_view_from_config` produces the deep nested shape (return type widened from `dict[str, dict[str, Any]]` to `dict[str, Any]`). Default values bumped: `Config.default().ollama_model` flipped `qwen3.5:35b` → `qwen3.6:27b` (smaller working set fits 36 GB M3 unified-memory budget — same hardware constraint that motivated the LCP track); `Config.default().llamacpp_model` flipped `""` → `"unsloth/gemma-4-E4B-it-GGUF:Q8_K_XL"` (operator's known-good GGUF spec; matches the recommended `-hf` arg). `_merge_from_yaml`'s default-fallback for `llamacpp_model` updated in lockstep so a missing YAML key resolves to the new default. `with_overrides`'s default-fallback for `ollama_model` updated to `qwen3.6:27b`. **`androscan/web/health_probes.py` `probe_llamacpp` rewrite:** previously hit `GET /v1/models` only; now does a two-step probe — `GET {server-root}/health` for liveness classification (`{"status": "ok"}` → proceed, `{"status": "loading model"}` → return `_PARTIAL_UP` immediately and skip the `/v1/models` call entirely, anything else → `_DOWN`), then `GET {base}/v1/models` on success for the model list used by `llamacpp_model_present`. The `/health` step is purpose-built for liveness, returns smaller responses, and explicitly surfaces the cold-start mmap state which the previous `/v1/models`-only probe could only infer from an empty `data: []` list. The probe's `url` field still reports `/v1/models` so the status-card "open in browser" link stays useful; total wall-clock is sub-millisecond on loopback. **Frontend `androscan/web/frontend/src/tabs/SettingsTab.tsx`:** `LLM_OWNED_SECTIONS` set extended with `"cloud"` (was `{"ollama", "llamacpp", "llm"}`); `LlmProviderRadio`'s `llmCloudFields` resolution flipped from filtering `grouped["llm"]` to reading `grouped["cloud"]` directly — cleaner, no filter predicate. `androscan/web/frontend/src/api/settings.ts` `GlobalSettingsResponse.global` type widened from `Record<string, Record<string, unknown>>` to `Record<string, unknown>` to accommodate the deep nested shape; `AppSettingsResponse.global_view` similarly widened. Form-mode rendering doesn't read `data.global` directly (uses `data.flat` / `data.field_map`), so the type widening is cosmetic — the DiagnosticsPanel still JSON-stringifies the full shape unchanged. **`README.md`:** "Local LLM" section overhauled — Ollama section references `qwen3.6:27b` + the new `llm.ollama:` YAML path with `default_model` key; llama.cpp section's recommended `llama-server` invocation updated with `-fa on` + `--no-webui` + `--cache-reuse 256` + `-hf unsloth/gemma-4-E4B-it-GGUF:Q8_K_XL` as the example model spec; new paragraph documenting the three model-sourcing options (HF cache / HF Hub / local file) plus a forward-pointer to LCP.7; - 2026-05-06 LCP track sub-step **LCP.6** — GBNF grammar enforcement for `skill_requests` / `hypotheses` schema + Ollama JSON-schema mode + `Config.local_grammar_enabled` kill-switch + opportunistic 400-fallback (single commit; **LCP track now fully complete**; +62 new tests; closes `ISSUE-016`). **`androscan/llm/grammar.py` (NEW, ~+330 LOC):** `active_skill_names(only_llm_tier=True)` — registry-driven, sorted, idempotent (calls `discover()` if `_REGISTRY` is empty); `build_response_json_schema(skill_names)` — Ollama 0.5.0+ `format: <schema>` payload mirroring `LLMResponse` shape (`summary` optional string, `skill_requests[*]` discriminated union with `skill` enum + permissive `params` object, `hypotheses[*]` permissive object — `additionalProperties: True`, no required fields, matches parser's fail-soft default-fill posture); top-level `additionalProperties: False`; `build_response_gbnf(skill_names)` — hand-rolled GBNF on top of the standard JSON-value prelude lifted from `llama.cpp/grammars/json.gbnf`; the "any-order, any-subset" outer object expressed as repetition of `response-pair` separated by commas (simplest way to allow LLM to emit `summary` / `skill_requests` / `hypotheses` in any order without combinatorial expansion); `is_grammar_enabled(config)` feature-flag helper (defensive `getattr` fallback to True so MagicMock-based tests pre-dating LCP.6 keep grammar path active by default); `json_schema_for_format_field` + `json_schema_to_wire_str` convenience wrappers. **`androscan/llm/client.py` wiring:** new module-level caches `_OLLAMA_SCHEMA_DISABLED_URLS` + `_LLAMACPP_GRAMMAR_DISABLED_URLS` (per-base-url, per-process; opportunistic fallback when provider rejects the grammar/schema payload); new helpers `_normalise_base_url`, `_looks_like_schema_error` (Ollama 400 heuristic), `_looks_like_grammar_error` (llama.cpp 400 heuristic). `_complete_ollama` swaps `format: "json"` → `format: <schema-dict>` when `Config.local_grammar_enabled` is True and base_url isn't cached; on HTTP 400 matching the heuristic, base_url is cached and the request retries within the same loop iteration with the v1-LCP wire shape (no retry-counter increment). `_complete_llamacpp` attaches `grammar: <gbnf-source>` ALONGSIDE the existing `response_format: {"type": "json_object"}` so a `llama-server` build that ignores the `grammar` field still gets the JSON-mode fallback; on HTTP 400 matching the heuristic, base_url is cached, `grammar` is dropped from the payload, and the request retries within the same loop iteration. Cloud path unchanged. **`androscan/config/loader.py`:** new `local_grammar_enabled: bool = True` field — YAML `llm.local_grammar_enabled`, env `ANDROSCAN_LOCAL_GRAMMAR_ENABLED` (truthy/falsy aliases `true`/`1`/`yes`/`on` ↔ `false`/`0`/`no`/`off`), live-reloadable. Wired through every loader path (`Config.default()`, `_merge_from_yaml`, env overrides, `with_overrides` constructor call, `coerce_yaml_value` bool fields set). **`global_config.yaml`:** new commented `llm:` section with detailed explanation of grammar-mode behavior for Ollama (sends `format: <json-schema>` when on, `format: "json"` when off), llama.cpp (attaches `grammar` field when on, omits when off), opportunistic fallback semantics, and kill-switch usage; cross-references `ISSUE-016` and DEC-027. **Settings UI** (`androscan/web/frontend/src/tabs/SettingsTab.tsx`): `LlmProviderRadio` filter excludes `local_grammar_enabled` from the Cloud subsection's `llmCloudFields`; new `localGrammarField` constant; conditional rendering under BOTH the "Local (Ollama)" and "Local (llama.cpp)" subsections (cross-local-provider knob; cloud providers ignore it because OpenAI-compat `response_format` enforces validity at the SDK layer). No new CSS — reuses the existing generic `SettingsField` renderer. **Tests (+62 net):** new `tests/test_grammar.py` (+38 across 5 classes — `TestActiveSkillNames` skill discovery + sort, `TestBuildResponseJsonSchema` shape + strictness + skill enum match, `TestBuildResponseGbnf` well-formedness + root rule + JSON-prelude inclusion + skill-name alternatives + balanced braces (regex-based whitespace-tolerant matching for the prelude rules to handle right-padded GBNF alignment), `TestParserInvariance` round-trip via `androscan.llm.parser.parse_response`, `TestIsGrammarEnabled` feature-flag); `tests/test_llm_client.py` (+11 across `TestOllamaSchemaFormatMode` + `TestLlamacppGrammarMode` — happy / 400-fallback / kill-switch off / unrelated-400 / cache-pre-seeded / `response_format=None` skip — `_capturing_post` deep-copy idiom for the dict-mutation snapshot trap caught by initial test failure); `tests/test_config.py` (+13 in `TestLocalGrammarEnabled` — default / YAML parse / string truthy/falsy / env override / env-invalid-fallback / live-reloadable / field-map / `with_overrides` boolean + string coercion / `global_view_from_config` round-trip). **6a/6b split decision: single commit, no split.** The LCP.6 planning checkpoint surveyed `SkillMeta.params_schema` across all 9 LLM-tier skills and confirmed it's operator-prose (not typed JSON Schema), so the LCP.0 emitter-scope estimate was over-scoped (~5x); the actual emitter is small enough for a single commit. **Verification:** all 1092 tests pass (1030 → 1092, +62 — grammar +38, llm_client +11, config +13); `tsc --noEmit` clean; `vite build` clean (1235.53 KB JS / 384.66 KB gzip / 93.42 KB CSS / 15.71 KB gzip; +0.40 KB JS over LCP.5, no CSS change since the new toggle reuses the existing `SettingsField` renderer); ReadLints clean. **Closes `ISSUE-016`** — moved to the Resolved section of `docs/KNOWN_ISSUES.md`. **DEC-027 status flipped Active → Closed** with the LCP.6 final closing note appended after the LCP.5 v1-ship note. **Operator impact:** Q4_K_M / IQ4_XS local-LLM quants regain reliable structured-JSON output — the model's logits-sampler can ONLY emit registered skill names + integers in the 1-5 range under grammar-mode; off-vocab / off-range output is impossible at the sampling level. Operators on older Ollama (< 0.5.0) or `llama-server` builds without `grammar:` field support transparently fall back to `response_format`-only via the per-base-url 400-fallback cache (no operator action required); operators wanting to disable grammar enforcement entirely (e.g. for benchmarking or runtime / quant pairings producing sampling failures) can flip `llm.local_grammar_enabled: false` in `global_config.yaml` or set `ANDROSCAN_LOCAL_GRAMMAR_ENABLED=false`. **Next sub-step:** none — LCP track is fully closed. Phase 12 v3 planning checkpoint promotes back to Active when telemetry from Phase 11 v2 + v2.1 + LCP-era use accrues. CI.0 (Phase 4 CI) remains a parallel non-blocking thread.

- 2026-05-06 LCP track sub-step **LCP.4** — Settings UI provider radio + llama.cpp Config knobs (operator-visible LLM-provider chooser + dedicated llama.cpp Config plumbing the LCP.2 dispatcher previously had to synthesise from Ollama fields via `getattr` fallbacks; **single commit, no 4a/4b split — `SettingsTab.tsx` net add hit +251 LOC right at the LCP.0 planning trigger boundary but a split would have produced an awkward intermediate state where "Local (llama.cpp)" radio shows but the sub-knobs don't render**, strictly worse UX than the LCP.3 hand-edit-YAML state). **Settings UI restructure (DEC-027 / Q3 (B) lock-in):** replaced the bare `llm_provider` text input with a top-level 3-way radio "Local (Ollama)" / "Local (llama.cpp)" / "Cloud"; ollama / llamacpp / `llm.cloud_*` sections now render INSIDE the radio block, gated by the active radio value — operators picking a single provider no longer see the other two providers' knobs; "Cloud" radio reveals a nested vendor dropdown (the six existing CLOUD_PROVIDERS entries) plus the cloud_model / cloud_api_key / cloud_temperature fields; "Local (llama.cpp)" radio shows the new llamacpp_* knobs plus a help line clarifying `--ctx-size` lives at server startup; ANDROSCAN_LLM_PROVIDER env-lock disables all radio buttons + surfaces an env-lock pill (parallel to SettingsField behaviour); off-vocab provider values (typos / future vendors) keep rendering in the cloud dropdown as `<value> (unknown)` so operators can spot + fix them. **Backend Config plumbing (mirrors the Ollama schema):** `Config` dataclass gains 3 frozen fields — `llamacpp_base_url` (default sourced from `LLM_PROVIDERS["local"]["llamacpp"]["base_url_default"]` = `http://127.0.0.1:8033/v1`), `llamacpp_model` (free-text label; llama-server ignores the request-body model field — the actual GGUF is set at server startup), `llamacpp_max_tokens` (default 8192). All three wired through `CONFIG_FIELD_MAP` (yaml: `llamacpp.*`; env: `ANDROSCAN_LLAMACPP_*`), `LIVE_RELOADABLE_FIELDS`, `Config.default()`, `_merge_from_yaml()`, the env-override block in `load_config()`, the final `Config(...)` constructor, AND `with_overrides()` (the live-reload path used by Settings UI saves — its missing constructor args were the cause of an initial `test_trace_behavior_skill` failure caught by the full pytest sweep before this commit landed). `Config.active_model` now routes on `provider_kind()` so the CLI banner / Settings status card / run header report `llamacpp_model` (or "(not set)") instead of the unrelated `ollama_model` when llama.cpp is active. `_complete_llamacpp` prefers the new `llamacpp_max_tokens` field; falls back to `ollama_num_predict` when absent so the MagicMock-based unit tests from LCP.2 keep working without per-test edits. `global_config.yaml` gains a commented `llamacpp:` section with the recommended startup line + per-knob rationale. **Tests (+21 net, all passing):** `TestLlamacppBaseUrl` (6: registry-default match / YAML parse / trailing-slash strip / env-override-wins / live-reloadable / field-map round-trip), `TestLlamacppModel` (5: default empty / YAML / env / live / field-map), `TestLlamacppMaxTokens` (6: default 8192 / YAML / env / env-invalid-fallback / live / field-map), `TestLlamacppRoundTrip` (3: `global_view_from_config` + `active_model` for both `llamacpp_model=set` and `=""` paths). `tests/test_llm_client.py`: rename LCP.2 fallback test to `..._falls_back_to_ollama_num_predict_when_llamacpp_field_unset` to reflect post-LCP.4 fallback semantics; add a parallel `..._prefers_dedicated_llamacpp_field_when_set` test for the happy path. `_make_llamacpp_mock_config()` now accepts `**overrides` so future tests can pin the new fields without rewriting the helper. **Verification:** 1030 / 1030 pytest in 13s (+21 since LCP.3's 1009 baseline); `tsc --noEmit` clean; `vite build` clean (JS 1,231.19 KB → 1,234.88 KB = +3.69 KB; CSS 91.34 KB → 93.42 KB = +2.08 KB — both within LCP.0 ceilings of +5 KB JS / +2 KB CSS); ReadLints clean across all 7 modified files. Commit `bbe8c95`.
- 2026-05-06 LCP track sub-step **LCP.3** — `probe_llamacpp()` health probe + Status panel mutual-exclusion routing + provider-tagged LLM card (backend probe + thin frontend addition; **`_gather_global` runs exactly one local-LLM probe per request keyed on `Config.provider_kind()`** — Ollama and llama.cpp probes are mutually exclusive). **Backend.** `androscan/web/health_probes.py` gained `probe_llamacpp(base_url) -> dict` performing a `GET {base_url}/models` (with `/v1` already in the base URL per DEC-027 Q4); curl-first / urllib-fallback pattern; classifies `llama-server` status into `_DOWN` (server unreachable), `_PARTIAL_UP` (server live, models loading), `_UP` (server live, model loaded); structured result mirrors `probe_ollama_tags` (`ok` / `reachable` / `url` / `ping_ms` / `models` / `error`). Companion helper `llamacpp_model_present(tags, model) -> bool` for case-insensitive exact / prefix matching. `androscan/web/status_routes.py` `_gather_global()` now conditionally invokes either `probe_ollama_tags` or `probe_llamacpp` based on `Config.provider_kind()`; LLM card payload gains `provider: "ollama" | "llamacpp"` discriminator + provider-specific label (`"LLM (Ollama)"` / `"LLM (llama.cpp)"`) + provider-specific error messages for missing/unloaded models. **Frontend.** `GlobalStatus.llm` type extended with the `provider` discriminator; `StatusCardView` accepts an optional `extraClassName` prop; the LLM status card renders a `via Ollama` / `via llama.cpp` extras line based on the new field + applies a provider-specific CSS class (`ollama-status` / `llamacpp-status`); `App.css` gains a thin accent-`border-left` for both classes (parallel visual category across surfaces). **Tests (+12 net):** `tests/test_health_probes.py` covers `probe_llamacpp` up/down/partial states + `llamacpp_model_present` matching logic; `tests/test_settings_routes.py` extended with the mutual-exclusion routing (only one local-LLM probe runs per request) + the provider discriminator on the payload; `test_settings_routes.py` fixture extended with a `probe_llamacpp` stub. **Verification:** 1009 / 1009 pytest; `tsc --noEmit` clean; `vite build` clean; ReadLints clean. Commit `c21b639`.
- 2026-05-06 LCP track sub-step **LCP.2** — `_complete_llamacpp()` HTTP path + 3-way `provider_kind()` router + `<think>` strip parity (backend behavior addition; Ollama path untouched). **`androscan/llm/client.py`:** new dedicated `_complete_llamacpp()` function (~250 LOC parallel structure to `_complete_ollama()`); body builder for OpenAI-compat `/v1/chat/completions` with `messages` + `temperature` + `max_tokens` + `response_format: {"type": "json_object"}` when `response_format=="json"`; SSE streaming via `requests.post(..., stream=True)` + `iter_lines()` parsing OpenAI's `data: {...}` chunks; retry logic on transient HTTP errors. Router in `complete()` becomes a 3-way switch on `Config.provider_kind()`: `"local-ollama"` → `_complete_ollama()`; `"local-openai-compat"` → `_complete_llamacpp()`; `"cloud"` → `_complete_cloud()` (also the typo-fallback path). **`<think>...</think>` strip helper applied defensively to BOTH local paths** (Qwen3-family reasoning models can leak think-blocks into the content channel depending on `--reasoning-format` flag); `_strip_think_blocks(text) -> tuple[str, str]` returns `(content_without_think, extracted_thinking)`; both Ollama's `_do_request` (non-streaming) and `_stream_request` (streaming) and the new `_complete_llamacpp()` apply it to merge any leaked thinking content back through `on_thinking`. **New constants:** `_LLAMACPP_TIMEOUT_TIERS`, `_LLAMACPP_RETRYABLE_STATUSES`, `LLAMACPP_DEFAULT_MODEL_LABEL`, `LLAMACPP_SETUP_TIP`. **New helpers:** `_resolve_llamacpp_base_url(config) -> str` (uses `getattr(config, "llamacpp_base_url", None)` for forward-compat with LCP.4 — the dedicated Config field doesn't exist yet at LCP.2 commit time), `_build_llamacpp_messages()` (OpenAI-compatible message builder), `_stream_llamacpp_sse()` (SSE chunk parser). **Tests (+34 net) in new file `tests/test_llm_client.py`:** 8 test classes — `TestStripThinkBlocks`, `TestResolveLlamacppBaseUrl`, `TestLlamacppRequestBodyShape`, `TestLlamacppSseParsing`, `TestLlamacppTransientErrorRetry`, `TestLlamacppNonStreamResponseHandling`, `TestCompleteRouterDispatch`, `TestBuildLlamacppMessages`. Helper `_make_llamacpp_mock_config()` pins `llamacpp_base_url=None` / `llamacpp_model=None` explicitly on the MagicMock config so `getattr(config, "...", None)` returns `None` (without the explicit pin, MagicMock's auto-attribute spawning would shadow the registry-default fallback paths). `tests/test_llm.py`: 4 existing MagicMock configs updated to set `config.provider_kind.return_value = "local-ollama"` so the new router routes them through Ollama; helper `_ollama_mock_config()` introduced for DRY. **Verification:** full pytest green; `tsc --noEmit` clean; `vite build` clean. Commit `fc33658`.
- 2026-05-06 LCP track sub-step **LCP.1** — `LLM_PROVIDERS` table refactor + `Config.provider_kind()` + backwards-compat alias (pure refactor, no behavior change — every existing test continued passing without modifications). **`androscan/config/loader.py`:** flat `CLOUD_PROVIDERS` dictionary replaced with new nested `LLM_PROVIDERS` dictionary containing `local` (for Ollama and llamacpp) and `cloud` sections; each entry gains a `"kind"` field (`"local-ollama"` / `"local-openai-compat"` / `"cloud"`); `CLOUD_PROVIDERS` preserved as a backwards-compat alias to `LLM_PROVIDERS["cloud"]` so existing imports in `androscan/llm/client.py` + elsewhere continue to work without per-call edits. New `Config.provider_kind() -> str` method computed by reverse-lookup on `LLM_PROVIDERS`; `Config.is_cloud` rewired as a shim returning `self.provider_kind() == "cloud"` (was previously `llm_provider != "ollama"` which would have wrongly classified llamacpp as cloud once it became selectable). - 2026-05-05 Phase 11 v2.1 follow-up release sub-step **v2.1.5** — Tier-3 `suggest_trace_entry` skill + skill-response `widgets[]` schema extension + `<TraceEntryCandidateWidget>` chat renderer + bounded chat agentic loop substrate (BE+FE+LLM; biggest sub-step of the v2.1 follow-up release; +37 new tests; **closes ISSUE-009 as a side-effect** — the chat-widget pattern requires an agentic-loop substrate that DEC-022 had recommended but `androscan/web/chat.py` had been single-pass through Phase 11 v2, so v2.1.5 had to ship the loop first as the substrate the widget pattern is built on; this is a notable architectural deviation from the v2.1.0 spec, which had assumed the loop existed). **Backend skill + schema** (`androscan/skills/base.py` + `androscan/skills/suggest_trace_entry.py` + `androscan/skills/__init__.py`): `SkillResult` extended with optional `widgets: tuple[SkillWidget, ...] = field(default_factory=tuple)` field; `SkillWidget` typed union added with `TraceEntryCandidateWidget` as the first member (`@dataclass(frozen=True)` with `kind: Literal["trace_entry_candidate"]` + `smali_id: str` + `rationale: str` + `confidence: float`); new `androscan/skills/suggest_trace_entry.py` (`SKILL_META` with `name="suggest_trace_entry"`, `tier="llm"`, `requires_confirmation=False`) — uses RAG (`search_decompiled_sources`) + call-graph context (`list_methods_on_class`) to gather candidate trace entry methods, then uses `llm.client.complete` (blocking JSON response) with a carefully-crafted system prompt to rank the top-3 candidates (rationale capped at 200 chars, confidence in [0.0, 1.0]); fail-open semantics for various unavailability modes (no app context / no decompile / no RAG / no call graph / LLM timeout); registered in `_SKILL_MODULES`. Tunables: `_RAG_TOP_K`, `_MAX_CANDIDATE_POOL`, `_MAX_CANDIDATES_RETURNED`, `_MAX_RATIONALE_CHARS`, `_MAX_PREVIEW_CHARS`, `_OVERLOADS_PER_CLASS_CAP`. Returns `SkillResult` with `widgets` populated by `TraceEntryCandidateWidget` instances. **Bounded chat agentic loop** (`androscan/web/chat.py`, refactor): new constants `MAX_AGENTIC_TURNS = 5` + `MAX_SKILLS_PER_TURN = 3`; new `validate_chat_request` field `agentic_loop: bool` (optional, defaults to `False` for backward compatibility); `stream_chat_request` dispatches to `_stream_chat_agentic_request` if `body.agentic_loop` is `True`, else falls back to the legacy single-pass streaming path; `_stream_chat_agentic_request` orchestrates multiple blocking `llm.client.complete()` calls within a bounded loop, parses LLM JSON responses (`thinking`, `content`, `skill_requests`), executes non-confirmation skills server-side, emits new SSE events. **New SSE events:** `skill_request` (every skill invocation), `skill_result` (success + `text`), `skill_pending` (consent-class skills that halt the loop — `requires_confirmation=True` skills emit this and halt with explanatory `content` instead of auto-executing; safer than auto-execution), `widget` (every `SkillResult.widgets` item, emitted as a separate SSE event after `skill_result`). Skill results (including `SkillResult.text`) are appended to the message history to provide context for subsequent LLM turns. Telemetry in the `done` event extended to include agentic loop metrics (`skill_calls`, `widgets`, `agentic`, `halted_reason`). **Frontend types + API** (`androscan/web/frontend/src/types.ts` + `androscan/web/frontend/src/api/chat.ts`): `ChatMessage` extended with optional `skill_calls?: ChatSkillCall[]` + `widgets?: ChatWidget[]`; new `ChatSkillCall` interface (`request_id` / `skill` / `params` / `status: "running" | "success" | "failed" | "pending"` / `text` / `reason`); new `ChatWidget` typed union (`TraceEntryCandidateWidgetData` as first member); `ChatSendArgs.agenticLoop` added (mapped to backend `agentic_loop`); `streamChat`'s `parseFrame` switch extended with new `onSkillRequest` / `onSkillResult` / `onSkillPending` / `onWidget` callbacks; `ChatStreamDone` extended with `agentic` / `skill_calls` / `widgets` fields. **New components** (`androscan/web/frontend/src/components/chat/widgets/TraceEntryCandidateWidget.tsx` + `androscan/web/frontend/src/components/chat/widgets/index.tsx`): `<TraceEntryCandidateWidget>` renders a compact card with `smali_id` + `rationale` + bucketed confidence badge ("high" / "medium" / "low") + "Trace this" button that uses `useWorkbench()` to set `pendingTraceEntry` + flip `labMode("trace")` + `setTab("lab")` (auto-fire handoff per Q8: a — same pattern as Inspect → Trace seed); `<ChatWidgetRenderer>` dispatcher uses a `switch` on `widget.kind` to render the appropriate component, gracefully returns `null` for unknown kinds (forward-compatible for version mismatches). **ChatDock wiring** (`androscan/web/frontend/src/components/ChatDock.tsx`): new `AGENTIC_LOOP_TABS = new Set(["lab"])` — Lab tab opts into agentic loop; new callbacks update `ChatMessage` state (record skill calls + append widgets to current assistant message); rendering logic adds a `<details className="chat-skill-calls">` block (auto-opens when skills are running) and a `<div className="chat-widgets">` block (renders `<ChatWidgetRenderer>` per widget below the assistant's main text). **CSS** (`androscan/web/frontend/src/App.css`): new namespaces `.chat-skill-calls*` + `.chat-widget*` + `.chat-widget-trace-entry-candidate*` (including confidence-badge styling). **Tests** (+37 new): `tests/test_suggest_trace_entry_skill.py` (+25 — new file: app-context resolution / decompile-readiness gates / RAG-readiness gates / call-graph-readiness gates / candidate gathering / LLM ranking happy-path / LLM transport-error fallback / malformed-JSON fallback / overload caps / fixture covers `Lcom/example/Greeter;` and its methods); `tests/test_skills.py` (+4 — widgets-default-empty invariant on bare `SkillResult`; `suggest_trace_entry` registration via `_SKILL_MODULES`); `tests/test_chat_stream.py` (+8 — agentic loop dispatch on `agentic_loop: true`; backward-compat single-pass on `agentic_loop: false` / absent; `skill_request` + `skill_result` events forwarded; `widget` event forwarded after `skill_result`; bounded `MAX_AGENTIC_TURNS` halts gracefully; consent-class skills emit `skill_pending` and halt without executing). **Verification:** **935 tests passed** (898 baseline at v2.1.4 + 37 new); `tsc --noEmit` clean; `vite build` clean; ReadLints clean across all 12 modified/new files; CSS bundle +0.80 kB / JS bundle +1.27 kB. **ISSUE-009 → Resolved (Phase 11 v2.1.5)** as a side-effect — the bounded agentic-loop refactor was DEC-022's recommended fix. **Operator impact:** the chat dock in the Lab tab now actually digs deeper than the initial RAG sweep (multi-skill turn-loop with skill-call timeline visible to the operator); the Tier-3 `suggest_trace_entry` skill is the first concrete user of the chat-widget pattern, surfacing top-3 trace entry candidates as clickable cards with auto-fire handoff to Trace mode (operator types a natural-language description in chat, gets back interactive cards, clicks to seed and trace — covers the original v2.1 pain point ("Smali entry-method input is unintuitive") for the hardest case where the operator can't even guess the class name). **Next sub-step:** **v2.1.6** — docs sweep + DEC-025 closing-note v2.1 extension (this commit). CI.0 (Phase 4 CI) remains a parallel non-blocking thread.
- 2026-05-05 Phase 11 v2.1 follow-up release sub-step **v2.1.4** — Tier-2(a) "Ask AI" button + `pendingChatPrefill` plumbing (FE-only). **Frontend** (`androscan/web/frontend/src/components/lab/LabTraceMode.tsx` + `androscan/web/frontend/src/components/ChatDock.tsx` + `androscan/web/frontend/src/components/LabTab.tsx` + `androscan/web/frontend/src/context/WorkbenchContext.tsx` + `androscan/web/frontend/src/App.css`): new "Ask AI" button on the Lab → Trace entry form (sits next to the entry input, alongside the existing v2.1.3 "Find similar classes" suggestion-pills cluster on the ⚠ pill state); on click, builds a pre-filled chat prompt template from the current entry input (`"Help me find a trace entry method matching: <user input>"`) + writes `pendingChatPrefill = {appId, prefillText}` to `WorkbenchContext` + flips to the chat dock's input field. **Two-consumer pattern.** `WorkbenchContext` exposes `pendingChatPrefill` to two consumers: `ChatDock.tsx` (owner — consumes the prefill text into its input field on next render via `useEffect`, then clears via `setPendingChatPrefill(null)`) + `LabTab.tsx` (observer — flips the chat dock open if collapsed, no state mutation; mirrors the existing `pendingTraceEntry` / `pendingHookPrefill` two-consumer patterns). Leverages existing `search_decompiled_sources` + `query_call_graph` skills via the chat dock's standard prompt path; no new backend changes. **Verification:** 898 tests passed (unchanged from v2.1.3 — FE-only); `tsc --noEmit` clean; `vite build` clean; ReadLints clean across the 5 modified files; FE bundle deltas +~0.9 KB JS / +~0.5 KB CSS gzipped (new `.trace-ask-ai-button*` namespace + minor `LabTraceMode.tsx` JSX additions). **Operator impact:** operators can now seed the chat dock with a context-aware prompt template directly from the Trace entry form when the deterministic backbone (v2.1.1) + coalescer (v2.1.2) + Tier-1 fuzzy match (v2.1.3) all fail — bridges the remaining "I don't know what class this lives in" friction signal that motivated the v2.1 follow-up release. **Next sub-step:** **v2.1.5** — Tier-3 skill + skill-response schema extension + chat-widget renderer + auto-fire handoff (BE+FE+LLM, biggest sub-step).
- 2026-05-05 Phase 11 v2.1 follow-up release sub-step **v2.1.3** — Tier-1 "Find similar classes" fuzzy-match suggestions on the ⚠ validation-pill state (BE+FE+LLM; +7 new tests). **Backend** (`androscan/web/trace_routes.py` + tests): new sibling endpoint `POST /api/trace/{app_id}/suggest-similar-classes` factory-built via the existing `build_trace_router(...)` plumbing. Returns up to 5 fuzzy-matched class candidates from the call graph using `difflib.SequenceMatcher` against the `simple_name` field (last segment of the class FQN — operator-friendly surface), sorted by similarity ratio descending, with a minimum threshold of 0.4 to filter out noise. New `SuggestSimilarClassesRequest` Pydantic body model (`class_input: str`). Status codes: 200 with `{candidates: [{class_smali, simple_name, similarity, hops_in_graph, ...}]}` on success; 404 on unknown `app_id`; 409 when call graph isn't ready. Sibling endpoint chosen over extending `/normalise-entry` for cleaner separation of concerns (coalescer = "is this exact class valid?"; fuzzy match = "what classes look like this?"). **Frontend** (`androscan/web/frontend/src/components/lab/LabTraceMode.tsx` + `androscan/web/frontend/src/api/trace.ts` + `androscan/web/frontend/src/App.css`): new `suggestSimilarClasses` API function; new "Find similar classes" button rendered on the ⚠ pill state (visible only when the v2.1.2 coalescer reports `class_exists_in_graph: false`); on click, calls the new endpoint and renders results as clickable suggestion-pills below the entry input — clicking a pill seeds the entry input with the suggested class + re-fires the coalescer (loops back through the v2.1.2 debounced validation flow); empty-result state shows "No similar classes found"; loading state shows inline spinner. **Tests** (+7 new): `tests/test_trace_routes.py::TestSuggestSimilarClasses` (similarity threshold filtering / case-insensitive matching / sort order / empty results on no matches / 404 on unknown app / 409 on call graph not ready / max 5 results capped). **Verification:** 898 tests passed (891 baseline at v2.1.2 + 7 new); `tsc --noEmit` clean; `vite build` clean; ReadLints clean. **Operator impact:** the v2.1.2 ⚠ pill state was previously a dead-end ("class doesn't exist in call graph — now what?"); v2.1.3 turns it into a recovery surface with one-click suggestion pills that re-fire the coalescer, mirroring the IDE typo-correction UX operators already know. **Next sub-step:** **v2.1.4** — Tier-2(a) "Ask AI" button + `pendingChatPrefill` plumbing (FE-only).
- 2026-05-05 Phase 11 v2.1 follow-up release sub-step **v2.1.2** — Backend coalescer endpoint + frontend debounced spinner + ✓/⚠ validation pill (BE+FE; +8 new tests). **Backend** (`androscan/web/trace_routes.py` + tests): new endpoint `POST /api/trace/{app_id}/normalise-entry` factory-built via the existing `build_trace_router(...)` plumbing (no new DI seams). Translates dotted input (`com.example.Foo.onClick` / stack-trace-pasted forms) → Smali class-prefix (`Lcom/example/Foo;->onClick(`) + validates against the call graph via the existing `read_call_graph` reader. Returns `{class_smali, method_name, smali_prefix, class_exists_in_graph: bool, method_count_in_class: int, errors: [...]}`. New `NormaliseEntryRequest` Pydantic body model. New `_coalesce_entry(class_input, method_input)` heuristic: handles dotted FQN / Smali-class-already / stack-trace-paste / dollar-suffix inner classes / leading-trailing whitespace; returns a canonical Smali class-prefix even when the input is already canonical (idempotent). Status codes: 200 on success regardless of `class_exists_in_graph` (the coalescer's job is to translate, validation is a separate signal); 404 on unknown `app_id`; 409 when call graph isn't ready. **Frontend** (`androscan/web/frontend/src/components/lab/LabTraceMode.tsx` + `androscan/web/frontend/src/api/trace.ts` + `androscan/web/frontend/src/App.css`): new `normaliseEntry` API function; debounced 400ms inline coalescer call on entry-input changes via `useEffect` + abort-on-unmount cleanup; inline spinner rendered during coalescer in-flight; ✓ pill on `class_exists_in_graph: true && method_count_in_class >= 1`; ⚠ pill on `class_exists_in_graph: false` (sets the stage for v2.1.3's "Find similar classes" recovery surface); pill placement below the inline form and above `MethodPicker`. Five distinct visual states designed: idle / loading / valid (✓ green) / class-not-found (⚠ amber) / method-not-found (⚠ amber + count=0 sub-message). **Tests** (+8 new): `tests/test_trace_routes.py::TestNormaliseEntry` (dotted FQN translation / Smali idempotent / stack-trace-paste / inner-class dollar-suffix / 404 on unknown app / 409 on call graph not ready / class-not-in-graph still 200 / method-not-in-class returns count=0). **Verification:** 891 tests passed (883 baseline at v2.1.1 + 8 new); `tsc --noEmit` clean; `vite build` clean; ReadLints clean. **Operator impact:** operators get instant feedback ("did I type the class name right?") without having to copy-paste a Smali signature; pasting a Java stack trace line works directly; the ⚠ pill state is a deliberate stepping stone for v2.1.3's "Find similar classes" Tier-1 LLM recovery. **Next sub-step:** **v2.1.3** — Tier-1 "Find similar classes" fuzzy-match suggestions on the ⚠ validation-pill state (BE+FE+LLM).

tighten in 11.7 or a follow-up if measurement shows the bumps weren't enough. **Spec deferrals:** Q1 (B) all-variants depth tagging (would carry depth on success-path Const/Param/Composite terminals — operator argument exists but spec literal won), Q2 (B) descent path tracking (richer tooltip listing methods walked through — depth-only pill is simpler v1 UX), Q3 (B) schema-only YAML changes (preferred surgical inserts since ISSUE-015 doesn't auto-clobber comment blocks added directly to YAML). **Next sub-step:** 11.7 — docs sweep + DEC-025 closing note (Phase 11 v2 wrap-up). - 2026-04-30 Phase 11 sub-step 11.5 — Same-class field-write-site walking (Tier 2 (d) part 2; slicer extension + 8 new tests; closes ISSUE-013's second half): Second half of ISSUE-013 close-out (the first half was 11.4's bounded inter-procedural method descent). **Slicer extension** (`androscan/analysis/slicing.py`, +~280 LOC): refactored `_maybe_descend` into a dispatcher that fans out by origin variant (`MethodCallOrigin` → 11.4's `_maybe_descend_method_call`; `FieldReadOrigin` → new `_maybe_descend_field_read`; terminals are no-ops). New `_walk_field_write_sites(origin, *, current_class_smali, budget, ...)` walker finds the most-recent write to the read field per the **constructor-priority rule** (Q1 (A)) — partitions write sites into `<init>`/`<clinit>` vs other methods, prefers textually-last constructor write, falls back to last non-constructor write only when no constructor write exists. Inner re-slice runs `_slice_register` on the written register at the chosen write site and recurses through `_maybe_descend` so a field-write source that itself resolves to a `MethodCallOrigin` or another `FieldReadOrigin` can be further descended (subject to the shared `_DescentBudget` — closed economy with 11.4's method descent). **New regexes** `_RE_IPUT` / `_RE_SPUT` parse the source register out of write lines (note: `vSrc` is opposite role to iget's vDest). **New helpers** `_class_from_method_sig` + `_java_class_to_smali` thread `current_class_smali` through the descent chain. **Same-class gating** (Q2 (A) strict): no superclass walking in v1. **Receiver-agnostic matching** (Q3 (A)): write sites matched by `(class, field_name)` only — `vObj` ignored; textbook `this.mField` field-cached idiom is correct under this rule. **Lazy walk per descent** (Q4 (A)): no eager indexing pass; bounded by closed-economy budget. **Closed-economy composition**: a top-level slice with `MAX_SLICE_DEPTH = 2` budget can compose any of (2 method hops; 2 field hops; 1 field + 1 method; 1 method + 1 field) — verified end-to-end via the `gateFieldWriteFromMethodCall` and `gateFieldWriteFromDeepChain` fixtures. **Fixture additions** (`Helpers.smali`, +7 methods + 5 fields): instance + static field-cached predicate; multi-write constructor-priority; cross-class skip; declared-but-never-written graceful failure; field-write-from-method-call composition; deep-chain budget exhaustion. **Tests** (`tests/test_decisions_slicing.py` — +8, 51 total slicer tests green): instance via `<init>` (ConstOrigin "0x1"); static via `<clinit>` (ConstOrigin "0x1"); constructor-priority wins (asserts 0x1 not 0x0); cross-class blocked; no-write-site graceful failure; closed-economy 1-field+1-method composition (MethodCallOrigin "pureGetB" at depth-2 cap); budget exhaustion mid-chain (MethodCallOrigin "pureChainHopTwo"); v1 backwards-compat preserved when descent kwargs omitted. **Verification:** all 856 non-device pytest tests pass (848 baseline + 8 new); ReadLints clean; all 99 existing 10.x + 27 11.4 tests still green (additive layering — 11.4's `_maybe_descend_method_call` was extracted unchanged from the dispatcher refactor). **No skill-side wiring changes needed**: `trace_behavior` already passes `classes_by_smali` + `decisions_by_method_sig` + shared `_DescentBudget` from 11.4; field-write descent reached via the unchanged `_maybe_descend` entry point. **Operator impact:** every decision whose v1 slicer terminated at a `FieldReadOrigin` for a same-class field with a constructor-init write now resolves to the deeper terminal (typically `ConstOrigin` for one-time-init flags — the textbook ISSUE-013 false-negative class). Combined with 11.4, the pipeline now resolves the two largest false-negative categories ISSUE-013 documented (helper-method-wrapped + constructor-init field-cached predicates). **Spec deferrals:** superclass walking (Q2 (B)), receiver-aware iput matching (Q3 (B)), eager field-write indexing (Q4 (B)) stay v3 / Phase 12 candidates. **Next sub-step:** 11.6 — bypass-planner re-run + LLM-tier `trace_behavior` budget revisit + cache schema bump + ISSUE-013 close-out + `trace.max_slice_depth` config knob + frontend depth-pill UI. CI.0 (Phase 4 CI) remains a parallel non-blocking thread.
- 2026-04-30 Phase 11 sub-step 11.4 — Bounded inter-procedural slicer descent + type-driven `is_stateless` analyzer (Tier 2 (d) part 1; slicer + skill wiring + 27 new tests): Largest behavioural change of v2 — closes the first half of ISSUE-013 (the second half is 11.5's same-class field-write-site walking). **Slicer extension** (`androscan/analysis/slicing.py`, +~330 LOC over the v1 369 LOC base): new module constants `MAX_SLICE_DEPTH = 2` + `HARD_CAP_DEPTH = 4`; new `_DescentBudget` mutable `@dataclass` (per planning-checkpoint Q1 — "closed economy" semantics in DEC-025 imply same instance flows across passes); new `_STATELESS_LIB_DENYLIST` constant with three-valued lookup (whole-class entry / per-method allowlist / unknown); new `is_stateless(method_sig, ...)` type-driven analyzer (~120 LOC) walking the method's body for `iput-*` / `sput-*` / `aput-*` / `monitor-*` / `throw vN` / non-stateless invokes / reflection — cycles defensively classify as stateful; new `_descend_into_callee` + `_maybe_descend` orchestrators; `slice_predicate_origins` public API extended with four optional kwargs (`classes_by_smali`, `decisions_by_method_sig`, `reflective_method_sigs`, `descent_budget`) — when `classes_by_smali` AND `decisions_by_method_sig` are both supplied, descent is enabled; v1 backwards-compat path preserved. **Deny-list seed** (planning-checkpoint Q2): spec's seed (`Math` whole-class, 8 boxing classes whole, `String` getter allowlist, `Intrinsics` whole-class) + planning-checkpoint addition `Object` getter allowlist (hashCode / equals / toString / getClass — common in `if obj.equals(...)` predicates). **Decompile parser extension** (`androscan/analysis/decisions.py`): `parse_decisions(..., include_branchless=True)` now emits `MethodDecisions` for every method (default behaviour preserved — no test breaks); required so descent can reach pure helper-method bodies. **Skill wiring** (`androscan/skills/trace_behavior.py`): calls `parse_decisions(include_branchless=True)`, builds `classes_by_smali`, fetches `reflective_method_sigs` via the new `call_graph.list_reflective_method_sigs(cache_dir)` helper, instantiates a single shared `_DescentBudget` reused across every per-decision slice in the closure (closed economy — same hub helper isn't redundantly re-descended via different decisions). Branchless methods explicitly `continue`'d in the per-closure loop so v1 aggregation is byte-identical. **New helper** (`androscan/analysis/call_graph.py`): `list_reflective_method_sigs(decompile_cache_dir) -> frozenset[str]` — small SQLite read over `nodes WHERE may_have_unresolved_reflection = 1`. **New fixture** (`tests/fixtures/trace_smali/smali/com/trace/Helpers.smali`, ~26 methods) exercises every descent + statelessness path. **Tests** (`tests/test_decisions_slicing.py` — +27, 43 total slicer tests green): 5 descent positive paths, 3 descent negative paths, 6 `is_stateless` direct unit tests, 5 deny-list short-circuit tests, 2 reflection deny tests, 1 cycle termination test, 4 `_DescentBudget` semantics tests + 1 module-constant pin. **Verification:** all 848 non-device pytest tests pass (821 baseline + 27 new); ReadLints clean; 99 existing 10.x slicer + skill tests all green (descent is a no-op against v1 fixtures since they don't have stateless helper chains — aggregated decisions byte-identical). **Worst-case fixture cost sanity check** (planning-checkpoint Q3): `Log.d` is external → descent terminates immediately, no quadratic blow-up; real worst case (app-internal hub helper called from N decisions) is bounded by `_DescentBudget.visited` (each unique callee descended at most once per top-level slice) — verified in `test_descent_budget_visited_set_prevents_redundant_redescent`. **Operator impact:** every decision whose v1 slicer terminated at a `MethodCallOrigin` for a stateless in-app helper now resolves to the deeper terminal (`ConstOrigin` / `FieldReadOrigin` / `ParamOrigin` — which the bypass planner can target with `force_return_value` directly). Spec deferrals (planning-checkpoint Q4): `trace.max_slice_depth` config knob deferred to 11.6 per the planning checkpoint; 11.4 ships with hardcoded module constant only. **Next sub-step:** 11.5 — Tier 2 (d) part 2, same-class field-write-site walking; reuses the same `_DescentBudget` (closed economy). CI.0 (Phase 4 CI) remains a parallel non-blocking thread.
- 2026-04-30 Phase 11 sub-step 11.3 — `BehaviorAnchor`-aware overlay on Manual Hooks Cytoscape pane (Tier 1 (c); backend route + frontend overlay layer): Third (and final) operator-UX deliverable from DEC-025's Tier 1 polish bundle, and the **first 11.x sub-step that touches the Python tree**. **Backend.** New route `GET /api/trace/{app_id}/anchored-methods` in `androscan/web/trace_routes.py`, factory-built via the existing `build_trace_router(...)` plumbing (no new DI seams). Returns `{app_id, sha, methods: [{class_smali, method_name, hops, created_at}, ...], total, error}` derived from `trace_cache.list_anchors` + a per-row `read_anchor` payload unpack to enumerate every `decisions[*].method`, deduped on `(class_smali, method_name)` keeping the most-recent `(hops, created_at)` per method (most-recent wins on `created_at`; ties broken by larger `hops` since a deeper trace is more thorough). **Status codes per spec:** 404 on unknown `app_id`; 409 when decompile cache isn't ready; **404 on "no `trace.sqlite` exists yet"** (distinguishes from "built then cleared" which returns 200 + empty methods list); 200 + `error` field set when at least one cached anchor's payload fails to decode (partial results still returned so the overlay still renders the methods we *can* read). **No new SQLite schema** — the route stays correct under 11.6's `SCHEMA_VERSION` bump because `read_anchor` already handles both v1 and v2 payload shapes via the additive-defaults pattern. **Backend tests** (`tests/test_trace_routes.py` — +7 tests, 22 total trace-route tests green): unbuilt-cache 404, unknown-app 404, decompile-not-ready 409, happy path after build, empty-after-clear 200, dedupe across two anchors that share methods, payload-decode-failure surfaces `error` field via direct `sqlite3` UPDATE corrupting the row, route-registered smoke. Full pytest suite green: 821 passed. **Frontend.** `androscan/web/frontend/src/api/trace.ts` gains a `listAnchoredMethods(appId)` client + new `AnchoredMethod` / `AnchoredMethodsResponse` types. `CallGraphView.tsx` gains an optional `anchoredMethods?: ReadonlyMap<string, AnchoredMethodMeta> | null` prop (mirrors the `hitsByMethod` pattern, uses the same `hitKey(className, methodName)` join helper); when non-null and a method node's `(class.class_name, method_name)` is in the map, the node gets a new `has-trace-anchor` Cytoscape class (small purple corner badge — inline SVG of a filled `TRACE_PURPLE` circle as `background-image`) and an extra tooltip line `<purple ⚓> trace cached: <hops> hop(s) · <created_at relative time>`. The new Cytoscape stylesheet rule is layered AFTER the `.hit` / `.has-hits` rules per spec so the bold-cyan Frida overlay still wins where both apply (Frida hits beat trace anchors visually because they're live data — DEC-023 / 4.8 sets the same precedence rule); rule is carefully scoped to non-conflicting `background-image` properties so the cascade doesn't fight `.hit`'s border-color/shadow even with later-rule-wins ordering. New `TRACE_PURPLE = "#9d6dff"` colour token shared between the Cytoscape SVG (via `encodeURIComponent`) and the tooltip's `.cy-trace-anchored-glyph` CSS rule. `LabTab.tsx`'s `ManualHooksMode` fetches the anchored set on `appId` change via `useEffect` + `listAnchoredMethods`, builds the `Map` in a `useMemo` keyed on `hitKey(<smali-to-java-converted class>, method_name)`, and threads it through `<CallGraphView />`. Refresh on every successful trace build / delete is handled implicitly by the existing conditional-rendering pattern: `ManualHooksMode` is only mounted when `labMode === "manual-hooks"` (LabTab unmounts it otherwise), so any trace cache mutation that happens in Trace mode is naturally visible the next time the operator switches back. 404 from the route (unbuilt cache) and any non-200 transient error both collapse to overlay-off. **NOTE on two spec-vs-reality divergences (both inline-commented in source):** (1) the 11.3 row in `TASKS.md` types the prop as `ReadonlySet<string>` but the same row mandates a tooltip that needs per-method `hops` + `created_at` metadata — we use a `Map` so the tooltip text actually works; (2) the spec references "the existing `formatRelativeTime` helper" but no such helper exists in the codebase — we add a small local `formatRelativeAge(epochSeconds)` helper next to `hitKey`. On the Cytoscape badge glyph itself, we use a small purple filled circle as `background-image` rather than the literal ⚓ character because rendering a unicode emoji via SVG `<text>` is OS-dependent — the literal ⚓ is preserved in the HTML tooltip where `.cy-trace-anchored-glyph` gives full CSS colour control. **Package-overview mode** accepts the new prop for signature symmetry but doesn't use it in v1 (per-method overlay has no meaningful surface in the package aggregate). **Frontend verification:** `tsc --noEmit` clean; `vite build` clean — 710 modules transformed (unchanged from 11.1 / 11.2; zero new transitive dependencies); JS bundle +~270 bytes gzipped from 11.2; CSS bundle +~140 bytes gzipped from 11.2. ReadLints clean. **Operator impact:** every method node in the call graph now carries a visible signal of whether it's been touched by any cached `BehaviorAnchor`, with hops + relative-age in the tooltip — closes the "I built a trace, but I can't easily see which methods it covered while I'm working in Manual Hooks mode" UX gap. **Next sub-step:** 11.4 — Tier 2 (d) part 1, the bounded inter-procedural slicer descent + type-driven `is_stateless` analyzer (largest behavioural change of v2; starts with a brief Ask-mode planning checkpoint to confirm the `_DescentBudget` shape + `_STATELESS_LIB_DENYLIST` initial seed). CI.0 (Phase 4 CI) remains a parallel non-blocking thread.

Frontend-only addition to `androscan/web/frontend/src/components/CallGraphView.tsx`'s right-click context menu — third button alongside the existing "Focus subgraph here" + "Open in Inspect" entries. **Wiring.** New `traceMethod(node, klass)` `useCallback` sibling of the existing `openInInspect` handler: defensively bails on `!appId || node.is_external`, otherwise writes `setPendingTraceEntry({appId, entryPrefix: node.smali_id, sourceLabel: "Graph → <class>.<method>"})` and chains `setLabMode("trace")` + `setTab("lab")`. Reuses the 10.8 `pendingTraceEntry` plumbing established for the Inspect → Trace handoff — `LabTraceMode.tsx`'s consumer recognises `node.smali_id` (the pre-computed canonical full signature stored on every method node per `call_graph.nodes.smali_id`) as complete via `looksLikeCompleteSmaliSignature` and **auto-fires the trace** on landing, so the operator goes from right-click → trace running in two clicks. **Disabled-state on external nodes.** New menu item is rendered with `disabled={isExternal}` + a class containing `cy-context-menu-item-disabled` when external + tooltip *"External callee — no source available to trace"*. **NOTE on spec-vs-reality:** the 11.2 spec text says the new entry mirrors "the same disabled-state pattern the Cytoscape view already uses for the existing 'Open in Inspect' entry on external nodes" — but the existing "Open in Inspect" entry actually bails *silently* in its handler rather than rendering visibly disabled. We followed the spec's *intent* (visible disabled state + actionable tooltip) rather than mirroring the silent-bail; inline source-comment in `CallGraphView.tsx`'s `traceMethod` callback flags this so the audit trail is explicit. **CSS.** One new namespace rule in `App.css`: `.cy-context-menu-item-disabled { opacity: 0.5; cursor: not-allowed; }` — visually identical to the existing `.callgraph-btn:disabled` pseudo-class rule above it (the menu button is also a `.callgraph-btn`, so the visual treatment cascades regardless), but the namespace exists as a discrete semantic hook so future menu items that *aren't* `.callgraph-btn` instances pick up the same affordance and so the disabled-state semantics are greppable as a concept. **No backend changes, no schema changes, no new components, no new tests** per the 11.2 contract — `node.smali_id` is already pre-computed on every method node by the call-graph indexer and the consumer-side plumbing was locked in 10.8. **Frontend verification:** `tsc --noEmit` clean; `vite build` clean — 710 modules transformed unchanged from 11.1 (zero new transitive dependencies — `setPendingTraceEntry` and `setLabMode` were already exposed by `WorkbenchContext` and pulled into `CallGraphView.tsx` from the existing `useWorkbench()` import, just by adding them to the destructure), 1.22 MB JS / 82 KB CSS / 379 KB gzipped (+~280 bytes gzipped JS for the `traceMethod` callback + the new menu button, +~10 bytes gzipped CSS for the one new namespace rule). **Operator impact:** every method node in the call graph now offers a one-click path into Trace mode with the entry method pre-populated and the trace auto-firing — closes the "Inspect → Trace handoff via the typed entry-method form" UX gap on the call-graph side (operators were typing smali signatures by hand to start a trace from a graph node they could already see). External callees get a visible disabled affordance + tooltip rather than a confusing no-op click. **Next sub-step:** 11.3 — `BehaviorAnchor`-aware overlay on Manual Hooks Cytoscape pane (backend route + frontend overlay; the first 11.x sub-step that touches the Python tree).
