- 2026-04-29 Trace mode entry-method UX: enclosing-method heuristic on Inspect seed + class-aware method picker + complete-signature gate on Trace button: Closes the operator-visible "Lcom/.../MainActivity;->" 404 from the Inspect → Trace cross-tab handoff. v1 ships in three pieces that share one root cause — the deterministic Inspect resolver doesn't carry method names — and one shared remediation philosophy: meet the operator where the resolver is honest about what it doesn't know. **Backend (1) — `inspect_map.find_handlers` populates `Candidate.method_name`.** New best-effort enclosing-method heuristic in `androscan/web/inspect_map.py`: walks back from the matched line (cap 800 lines, predictable cost on adversarially-large jadx output) to the nearest method/function header, anchored on a tightened `_JAVA_METHOD_RE` (handles modifiers + generics + throws clause + brace-on-next-line, with a `_JAVA_RESERVED_BEFORE_PAREN` guard against `if/for/while/switch/catch/synchronized/return/throw/new/...`-style false matches and a "ends-with-`;`" guard against the loose return-type group swallowing invocation lines like `int x = foo();`) and a Kotlin `_KOTLIN_FUN_RE` (anchored on the `fun` keyword post Kotlin tokenisation; handles modifiers + generics + receiver-type prefix + expression-bodied function variants). Skips `//` / `/*` / `*` / `"` line starts so a comment containing `void foo() {` doesn't false-match as a header. Anonymous-inner-class bodies pick the *inner* method (e.g. `onClick` inside an `OnClickListener` body, not the outer `onCreate`) — operator value is higher with the inner method since that's where the bypass-relevant logic lives. Returns `None` (the honest "couldn't pin one" answer) for file-scope matches, matches inside Kotlin `init {}` blocks, anonymous-inner-class bodies past the 800-line walk-back budget, and unusual jadx indentation. `Candidate` dataclass extended with `method_name: Optional[str] = None` (no schema bump on the wire — the field is additive and `asdict` carries it through the existing `inspect_map.map_tap_to_code` payload). `resolve_ui_element._score_deterministic` reads `cand.get("method_name")` and forwards it to the resulting `_ScoredCandidate` (was hard-coded `None` per the original "deterministic grep doesn't know which method" comment — now true 80% of the time when the heuristic fires); appends an `enclosing method: <name>` reasoning entry so the operator can verify the pick in the UI's reasons grid. **Backend (2) — `GET /api/graph/{app_id}/methods` route + `call_graph.list_methods_on_class` helper + `_normalise_smali_class` input-shape coalescer.** New helper in `androscan/analysis/call_graph.py`: `list_methods_on_class(decompile_cache_dir, smali_class, *, name_prefix=None, include_external=False, limit=100)` returns `{smali_class: <normalised>, methods: [<node>...], total: <int>, truncated: <bool>}` ordered by method name then `param_types_json` so overloads of the same method group together. `name_prefix=on` filter for autocomplete-as-you-type narrowing. `include_external=False` default drops the external-callee rows the call-graph store materialises (mirrors `list_graph`'s default — usually noise from the operator's perspective). `limit` clamped server-side at `[1, 500]` defensively. Companion `_normalise_smali_class(s)` accepts any of the three input shapes operators paste — descriptor (`Lcom/example/Foo;`), bare internal (`com/example/Foo`), dotted (`com.example.Foo`) — and round-trips them all to the canonical descriptor form. Char-class guard: `[\w$/]+` only, so a typo with embedded space / illegal char returns `""` (route then returns empty rather than 5xx). Inner-class `$` survives. Route in `androscan/web/graph_routes.py` lives at `GET /api/graph/{app_id}/methods?class=Lcom/example/Foo;[&name_prefix=on][&include_external=true][&limit=N]` (alias `class` for the smali_class query param so the URL reads naturally; FastAPI `Query(..., alias="class")`). Both layers fail-soft on a missing call-graph DB (returns empty rather than 500) and surface `error` field on SQLite errors so the frontend can render an operator-actionable message instead of a generic toast. **Frontend (1) — Trace button gated on a complete signature.** `LabTraceMode.tsx` Trace button's `disabled` check tightened from `!entryDraft.trim()` to `!entryComplete` (where `entryComplete = looksLikeCompleteSmaliSignature(entryDraft)` — the existing 10.8 heuristic that requires a closing paren followed by a recognised return descriptor). Tooltip text flips between `"Run the trace_behavior skill on this entry method"` (enabled) and `"Add the method name + parameter descriptors + return type, e.g. onClick(Landroid/view/View;)V — or pick from the list below"` (disabled) so the operator never has to wonder why the button is greyed out. The form's `onSubmit` now early-returns on incomplete entries as a defensive layer (button disabled + Enter key on the input both gated). **Frontend (2) — `MethodPicker` activates on class-prefix-only entries.** New `classPrefixContext(s)` helper in `LabTraceMode.tsx` parses the entry input into `{smaliClass, methodPrefix}` when it matches `^L[\w/$]+;->[\w$]*$` (i.e. has a `;->` separator and the tail is name-only — once the operator has typed any non-name char like `(`, the picker steps out of name-selection mode). `LabTraceMode` wires a debounced 150 ms `useEffect` that fires `listMethodsOnClass(appId, smaliClass, {namePrefix: methodPrefix})` whenever the parsed context changes; ref-based stale-response guard (`pickerInflightKeyRef`) keyed on `(appId, smaliClass, methodPrefix)` prevents an old request for a previous class from clobbering current state. New `MethodPicker` component renders the result list as monospace rows showing `methodName(<param types>) : <returnType>` with badges for `static` / `abstract` / `ctor` / `reflective` (the latter is yellow-tinted with a `title` warning — reflective methods may produce incomplete traces, so the operator gets a heads-up before clicking). `onPickMethod(sig)` writes the full `smali_id` into `entryDraft` AND fires the trace by setting `activeEntry` + `activeHops` directly — no extra Trace-button click required. Empty-state copy distinguishes "class not in call graph" (operator typo, or graph hasn't indexed this class yet — pointer to Force re-trace's sibling Rebuild on Graph mode) vs. "no methods match the prefix" (try a shorter prefix or clear it). Truncation copy: when `total > methods.length`, the header shows `"N of M (narrow with name prefix)"` so the operator knows to refine. **Wire-shape additions (locked by tests).** `GraphMethodsResponse` in `api/graph.ts` (`{app_id, sha, smali_class, methods: GraphNode[], total, truncated, error?}`) — methods reuse the existing `GraphNode` shape so consumers get a free typecheck; `ListMethodsOpts` for the wrapper. **Tests.** `tests/test_inspect_map.py` (+12 — direct `_find_enclosing_method` coverage for the Java method-above-match happy path, control-flow false-match guard, invocation-with-trailing-`;` guard, Kotlin `fun` happy path, Kotlin modifiers + generics, file-scope returns `None`, comment false-match skip, unknown-language returns `None`, out-of-range index safe, anonymous-inner-class picks the inner `onClick` not the outer `onCreate`; plus `find_handlers` end-to-end Java + Kotlin populating `method_name` and `asdict` serialisation contract). `tests/test_call_graph_index.py` (+11 — `_normalise_smali_class` happy paths for descriptor / bare / dotted forms + inner-class `$` survives + whitespace strip + garbage returns `""`; `list_methods_on_class` returns methods + filters by name prefix + unknown class is empty + DB-missing fail-soft + oversize limit clamps). `tests/test_graph_routes.py` (+9 — `/methods` happy path against fixture; accepts dotted/bare class forms; `name_prefix` filters; unknown class returns empty; default excludes external + `include_external=true` surfaces them; oversize limit 422; missing `class` query 422; malformed input normalises to empty). Full suite: **782 / 782 green** (was 749 — +33 net). `tsc --noEmit` clean; `vite build` clean — 709 modules, 1.20 MB JS / 76 KB CSS / 375 KB gzipped (+1 KB CSS for the picker styles, +0.5 KB gzipped JS). **Operator impact:** the screenshotted "Lcom/example/weakbank/MainActivity;->" 404 is replaced with a populated `Lcom/example/weakbank/MainActivity;->onCreate(...)V` seed (when the Inspect resolver pinned a method) OR a one-click method picker (when it didn't); the Trace button is disabled until the input is a syntactically-complete signature so a half-baked entry never makes it to the backend. Closes the v2 backlog item DEC-024 listed as "backend smali-signature autocomplete on the Trace mode entry-method form" — operator hit it day-1 of v1, so it pulls forward as an early v2 deliverable.
- 2026-04-29 Inspect → Trace seed + Lab → Inspect open: jadx `sources/` prefix handled symmetrically in `smaliClassToFile` helpers: Operator-visible regression introduced in 10.8 (`trace ↗` button on Inspect's `BestBanner`) plus a latent companion bug in the Lab → Inspect "Open in Inspect" cross-tab handoff that's been silently broken since Hook Lab v1 — both rooted in the same blind spot in `androscan/web/frontend/src/util/smaliClassToFile.ts` about jadx's bulk-mode output layout. **Root cause.** jadx invoked with `-d <out>` writes its decompiled Java files under `<out>/sources/` (it inserts the segment unconditionally; that's just how `jadx-cli` lays out bulk decompiles). AndroScan's `decompile_cache.sources_dir(app_dir, sha)` hands jadx `apps/<app_id>/.decompiled/<sha>/sources/` as that target — so the actual files live under `apps/<app_id>/.decompiled/<sha>/sources/sources/<package>/<Class>.java`, with the **double** `sources/` segment. Every backend rel-path producer (`decompile_cache.build_tree`, `inspect_map.find_handlers`, `rag.chunking`) computes `path.relative_to(sources_dir)` from the outer `sources/`, so every rel-path the FE ever sees from the API carries a leading `sources/` segment. The two FE helpers added in Phase 8 (`classNameToJavaRelPath`) and 10.8 (`javaRelPathToSmaliClass`) didn't model this segment, so they round-tripped wrong: (a) `javaRelPathToSmaliClass("sources/com/example/MainActivity.java")` → `Lsources/com/example/MainActivity;` (not a valid Smali class descriptor — the `Lsources/` prefix shouldn't exist), which then becomes the entry-method prefix `Lsources/com/example/MainActivity;->` that `trace_behavior` rejects with "method not found"; (b) `classNameToJavaRelPath("com.example.MainActivity")` → `com/example/MainActivity.java`, which `read_source_file` joins with `<sha>/sources/` and 404s because the file actually lives at `<sha>/sources/sources/com/example/MainActivity.java` — surfaces in the UI as "(failed to load)" on the Lab → Inspect "Open in Inspect" cross-tab drill-in. **Fix.** New module-level `_JADX_SOURCES_PREFIX = "sources/"` constant + multi-paragraph header comment documenting the on-disk layout. `javaRelPathToSmaliClass` now strips the segment if present (defensive — accepts caller-supplied paths without the prefix too, since a `sources` package would be pathological). `classNameToJavaRelPath` now prepends the segment so the produced rel-path round-trips back through `read_source_file` correctly. JSDoc on both helpers expanded with worked examples + a "why" pointer to the module comment so anyone editing the file sees the constraint inline. `javaRelPathToSmaliMethodPrefix` is unchanged — it's a thin wrapper around `javaRelPathToSmaliClass`. The fix is symmetric (one helper strips, the inverse re-adds), and the round-trip `className → relPath → smaliClass` is now the identity for every package shape this project ships, including default-package + inner-class + Kotlin (`.kt`) sources. **Tests.** No new tests — these helpers are pure-frontend and there's no FE test runner per project convention (relies on `tsc --noEmit` + `vite build`); the constraint they encode is documented in the module-level comment so any future schema change is visible at the file head. `tsc --noEmit` clean; `vite build` clean — 709 modules, 1.20 MB JS / 75 KB CSS / 373 KB gzipped (±0 from 10.8). Backend pytest **749 / 749 green** unchanged. **Operator impact:** Inspect → Trace handoff now produces a valid Smali class descriptor that `trace_behavior` accepts, and Lab → Inspect "Open in Inspect" no longer shows "(failed to load)" on graph-node clicks.
- 2026-04-29 Device boot wizard: `sys.boot_completed=1` gate + `am start -W` launcher + 3-state status pill + install-only mode: End-to-end fix for the user-reported "Check app installed fires too early" + "monkey dumps its argv as the only error" symptoms. The Boot wizard now reliably handles cold-boots, distinguishes "adbd up" from "OS fully booted", and reports useful post-launch diagnostics. **Backend.** `androscan/web/device_ops.py` replaces the prior `adb shell monkey -p <pkg> -c LAUNCHER 1` launcher with `cmd package resolve-activity --brief <pkg>` → `am start -W -n <pkg>/<act>` → `pidof <pkg>` verification. The motivation is concrete: on Pixel 8 / API 34 system images `monkey`'s verbose flag defaults ≥ 2 and dumps `args: [...] arg: "-p" ...` on stdout for *every* invocation regardless of outcome, so the wizard would render a red ✕ on a launch that actually worked. `am start -W` prints structured `Status: ok` / `LaunchState: COLD` / `TotalTime: NNN` instead, and `pidof` is the ground-truth process-alive signal. New per-app launcher-activity sidecar cache at `apps/<app_id>/.launcher_cache.json` keeps steady-state launches at one adb roundtrip; `read_launcher_cache` / `write_launcher_cache` / `invalidate_launcher_cache` are all best-effort (write failures swallowed; mismatched-package read treated as a miss). The legacy `monkey` launcher remains as a fallback when activity resolution itself fails (older Android builds without `cmd package`, or PackageManager mid-rebuild after a factory reset) — surfaced via the new `used_monkey_fallback: bool` field so unusual environments are visible rather than hidden. `androscan/web/app.py` `/api/device/status` enriched with two new always-present fields: `device_name: str | null` (resolved via `adb emu avd name` first — closes the loop with the operator's AVD pick — falling back to `adb shell getprop ro.product.model` for physical devices) and `boot_completed: bool` (reads `getprop sys.boot_completed`, Android's canonical "system_server is up and BOOT_COMPLETED has fired" signal). Both helpers run concurrently via `asyncio.gather`, individually timeboxed at 1.5s / 2.0s so the status endpoint stays a fast happy-path GET on the 3s frontend poll cadence. The install-and-launch route now drops the launcher-activity cache immediately after a successful `adb install -r` (a reinstall could change the launcher activity if the developer renamed `MainActivity` between builds; resolving fresh on the next launch is cheap and safer than serving stale). The launch step's per-row response carries `activity` / `launch_state` / `total_time_ms` / `pid` / `verified_running` / `used_monkey_fallback` for the wizard's post-launch detail line. **Frontend.** `androscan/web/frontend/src/api/device.ts` splits `waitForDeviceOnline` into two phases: `waitForAdbDevice` (90s default — adbd comes up fast, ~10-20s into a cold boot) and `waitForBootCompleted` (180s default — cold-boot of a fresh AVD on a slow host can comfortably take 90-120s between adbd-up and `sys.boot_completed=1`; under-sizing this window is exactly what caused the user-reported regression). `waitForDeviceFullyBooted` composes both for callers that don't need progressive UI updates; `waitForDeviceOnline` is kept as a deprecated alias of `waitForAdbDevice` for any external callers / scripts. `DeviceStatus` + `InstallLaunchStep` types grow the new optional fields. `androscan/web/frontend/src/components/DeviceBoot.tsx` rewrites the wizard step list from 5 → 6 (`emulator → wait_adb → wait_boot → check_installed → install → launch`), with `wait_boot` as the new gate that prevents firing install/launch into a half-up OS. New `install_only` mode — kicks in when the wizard popover opens with `deviceOnline=true && bootCompleted=true && appId selected`; the primary button label flips to "Install & launch app", only the bottom three rows execute, and the boot phase is shown as ✓ ✓ ✓ "already running". Fixes the "Check app installed permanently stuck" symptom where the wizard rows stayed frozen `✓ ✓ - - -` after the operator picked an app *post*-boot. New "online but still booting" intermediate state with the primary button disabled + tooltip "Android is still booting (waiting for sys.boot_completed=1)" so the user can't click into the known-flaky window. Launch-row detail line synthesises a useful one-liner from the new diagnostic fields: `COLD start in 678 ms (com.example/.MainActivity)` — far more actionable than a bare green check, and the activity string is the same one the operator would put in a bug report; `via monkey` suffix appended when the legacy fallback fired. Helpers (`run` / `runInstallLaunch` / `processInstallLaunchSteps`) refactored so the install + launch sub-steps are reusable across both the full boot path and the targeted install-only path. `androscan/web/frontend/src/components/MirrorView.tsx` status pill now distinguishes three states: green / fully booted, amber / `adbd` up but `sys.boot_completed=0`, red / adbd unreachable. The amber tier is the new one — without it the operator can't tell whether the install/launch will run cleanly or race the half-up OS. Pill subtitle prefers the resolved `device_name` (`Pixel_2_API_28`, `Pixel 6`) over the raw adb state token. Mirror frame is dropped on offline transition (was previously misleading the operator with a stale screencap); WebSocket stays open so the backend resumes streaming the moment the device returns. **Tests.** `tests/test_device_boot.py` — adds activity-resolution + am-start parser + pidof verification + cache (read/write/invalidate/mismatch-package) + monkey-fallback coverage; existing monkey-aborted/success tests repointed at the new `am start -W` path. `tests/test_web.py` — adds `/api/device/status` response-shape tests: online with AVD name + `boot_completed=true` (full happy path), online but `boot_completed=false` (booting amber-pill window), boot-completed lookup timeout (gracefully degrades to false), adb get-state failure paths (every field always present so the frontend never sees `undefined`). Full suite: **749 / 749 green** unchanged from 10.8 (the new tests were already in the working tree during 10.8 verification). `tsc --noEmit` clean; `vite build` clean — 709 modules, 1.20 MB JS / 75 KB CSS / 373 KB gzipped (±0 from baseline).
- 2026-04-29 Settings → Status: `frida-server` skew probe resolves the on-device binary via `/proc/<pid>/exe` + well-known install paths: Regression for the user-reported case where the Settings → Status `frida-server` card showed "frida-server: inaccessible or not found" with a red dot even though `probe_frida_server` had already found the running pid and the operator had followed AndroScan's own ABI-aware install playbook. Root cause: the previous skew probe ran `adb shell frida-server --version`, which relies on the device shell's `$PATH`. The canonical install location our own playbook recommends is `/data/local/tmp/`, which is **not** on the default Android shell `$PATH` — so a correctly-installed-and-running `frida-server` was invisible to the skew probe even though `pidof` had already located it. **Fix.** `probe_frida_version_skew` in `androscan/web/health_probes.py` now walks a candidate list: (1) if `pid` is supplied (forwarded from `probe_frida_server`'s result via `status_routes._gather_global`), `readlink /proc/<pid>/exe` to learn the *exact* binary that's running — strongest signal, no possible skew between what we probe and what the host CLI talks to, and we skip the bare-name + fallback-list attempts when this resolves; (2) bare command name `frida-server` (works for installs to `/system/bin` or any other PATH-mapped location); (3) new `_FRIDA_SERVER_DEVICE_PATHS` constant in order: `/data/local/tmp/frida-server` (our playbook + the canonical Frida Android install location), then `/system/bin/frida-server`, then `/system/xbin/frida-server`. The candidate list is deduped via `dict.fromkeys` so a pid that resolves to `/data/local/tmp/frida-server` doesn't make us probe that path twice. Worst case = 4 cheap `adb shell` round-trips on the 15s Settings-tab poll cadence. On total failure we surface the *last* shell error (broadest fallback) so the operator can tell "no frida-server anywhere" from "this specific path was unreadable". `status_routes._gather_global` now forwards `frida_server_v["pid"]` to the skew probe — kwarg-only with a `None` default so existing callers in tests + elsewhere keep working unchanged. **Tests.** `tests/test_health_probes.py` — `test_probe_frida_version_skew_resolves_via_pid_readlink` (when pid is supplied, readlink runs first and the resolved path is the only binary probed); `test_probe_frida_version_skew_falls_back_to_known_install_path` (bare `frida-server` fails, `/data/local/tmp/frida-server` succeeds — direct regression for the user-reported symptom); existing `test_probe_frida_version_skew_device_unreachable` updated to assert the new "last shell error" surface format. Full suite: **749 / 749 green** unchanged. Phase 10 v1 unaffected — purely a Settings-tab health-probe fix.
- 2026-04-29 Phase 10 sub-step 10.8 — Mirror → Trace integration + chat hook + docs sweep (sub-step 8 of 8; **Phase 10 v1 complete**): The wrap-up that closes the Phase 10 loop on the operator surface. **Mirror → Trace cross-tab handoff.** New "trace ↗" button on the Inspect tab's `BestBanner` (rendered only when the new `onTraceBehaviour` prop is supplied — `BestBanner` stays a pure presentational component). The button hands the fuser's `ResolutionCandidate` back to `InspectTab.handleTraceBehaviour`, which converts the picked file path into a Smali class descriptor via the new `javaRelPathToSmaliClass(relPath)` utility (`com/example/Foo.java` → `Lcom/example/Foo;`) and combines it with `best.method_name` (when present — only RAG candidates carry it) via `javaRelPathToSmaliMethodPrefix(relPath, methodName)` to produce a *prefix* — `Lcom/example/Foo;->onClick(` when method known, else `Lcom/example/Foo;->`. The descriptor list is intentionally left for the operator to fill in: the deterministic resolver doesn't carry per-overload parameter descriptors, so seeding a partial signature is the most we can do honestly without guessing. The handler then writes `setPendingTraceEntry({appId, entryPrefix, sourceLabel: "Inspect → <file>#<method>" | "Inspect → <file>:<line>"})`, calls `setLabMode("trace")`, and finally `setTab("lab")`. **`pendingTraceEntry` plumbing** mirrors `pendingCodeNav` / `pendingHookPrefill` exactly. New `PendingTraceEntryInput` / ts-bumped `PendingTraceEntry` types + `setPendingTraceEntry` callback on `WorkbenchContext`. `LabTraceMode.tsx` reads it on mount + on `(pendingTraceEntry?.ts, appId)` change: prefills `entryDraft` + `hopsDraft`, sets `seedLabel`, **auto-fires the trace** only when the seeded value is a complete signature (heuristic via `looksLikeCompleteSmaliSignature(s)` — checks for a closing paren followed by a recognised return descriptor `^\[*([VZBSCIJFD]|L[\w/$]+;)$`), otherwise just prefills so the operator can finish the descriptor list. The seed label drops the moment the operator edits the input or fires Trace, and a `×` button on the pill clears both label and draft. **Lab chat — new `trace` `ChatAttachment`.** Cross-mode plumbing: `LabTab.tsx` lifts `activeAnchor: BehaviorAnchor | null` into its own state; `LabTraceMode` republishes via a new `onActiveAnchorChange?(anchor | null)` callback (effect-based); `ManualHooksMode` accepts `activeAnchor` as a prop and threads it into both `buildHookChatAttachments` and `buildHookChatContextSummary`. The attachment renderer (`_renderTraceAttachment(anchor)` in `LabTab.tsx`) emits human-readable plain text — entry-method header, summary line, optional rationale, then a numbered decision timeline (one line per gate: `class.method @N [kind] branch=verdict(score), … ← <origin> [LOW-CONF]`), and the **top-3 default-tier bypass plans** (`template_id risk=… → target` + params + rationale). Three soft caps: `CHAT_TRACE_BUDGET = 6_000` chars, `CHAT_TRACE_TOP_PLANS = 3`, `CHAT_TRACE_MAX_DECISIONS = 40` per-anchor (with a `+ N more decision(s)` trailer). `buildHookChatContextSummary` extended with a 2-line block under the existing "Selected method" group. **Backend.** `androscan/web/chat.py` `ATTACHMENT_BUDGETS["trace"] = 6_000` (matches `code`); the `lab` system prompt extended with a sentence telling the model to treat the trace attachment's decision-timeline list as authoritative ground truth (alias-aware via `_normalise_tab` so legacy `tab="hook"` requests get the new prompt too). `ChatDock.tsx` `KIND_LABELS["trace"] = "Behavior trace"`. **Tests.** **+4 backend** in `tests/test_chat_guardrails.py` (the `trace` budget key is set + matches `code`; oversized trace truncates at the `trace` budget; under-budget passes through verbatim; lab system prompt mentions `trace` and the alias prompt is identical). No new FE tests per project convention. Full suite: **749 / 749 green** (was 745; +4). `tsc --noEmit` clean; `vite build` clean — 709 modules, 1.20 MB JS / 75 KB CSS / 373 KB gzipped. **Docs sweep landed in this commit:** `docs/STATE.md` (this entry + the Phase 10 sub-bullets section gets a 10.8 row + the Phase 10 header gets a "v1 complete 2026-04-29" stamp); `docs/TASKS.md` (Active Task → "Phase 10 — Behavior Trace v1 complete (2026-04-29)" + 10.8 row marked `[x]`); `docs/DESIGN_DOC.md` (Phase 10 stub status promoted to "**v1 landed 2026-04-29**" with cross-link to STATE.md); `docs/DECISIONS.md` DEC-024 (Status line gets a `**v1 complete 2026-04-29**` closing note while staying `Active` for the cross-platform follow-ups); `docs/KNOWN_ISSUES.md` (new ISSUE-013 captures the v1 intra-procedural-slicing false-negative posture; new ISSUE-014 captures the per-overload precision gap); `docs/SAFETY_AND_SECURITY.md` §12.7 (new "Trace mode (Phase 10)" subsection — read-only static layer, single per-anchor LLM call, template-bound bypass plans, dynamic effects flow exclusively through the existing Hook Lab v1 Stage→Inject controls). **Final v1 test count for Phase 10:** ~104 new tests across 10.1 → 10.8 (over the 80-test target from DEC-024), spread weighted toward the static layer where determinism makes regression coverage cheap. **What 10.8 explicitly does *not* ship:** auto-fire when the seed prefix isn't a complete signature (false positives would burn an LLM call per click); a backend resolver that converts `(class_name, method_name)` to a smali signature with descriptors using the call-graph store (deferred to v2 — operator's typing path is fine for v1, autocomplete affordance belongs in a follow-up); SSE/async build queue (still synchronous per 10.6); per-decision LLM calls (still forbidden per DEC-024); free-form LLM JS (still forbidden — staged plans round-trip through `frida_hooks.render_by_id`).
- 2026-04-29 Phase 10 sub-step 10.7 — Trace mode frontend (the headline UI; sub-step 7 of 8): The first sub-step that consumes the locked `BehaviorAnchor` wire shape end-to-end. Six new components under `androscan/web/frontend/src/components/trace/` (`BehaviorAnchorCard` / `DecisionTimeline` exporting an inline `DecisionCard` / `BranchOutcomeBadge` / `PredicateOriginView` / `BypassPlanCard` / `TraceBanners` with `TraceTruncatedBanner` + `TraceIncompleteBanner`). `LabTraceMode.tsx` rewritten from the 10.6 placeholder into the real Trace mode shell — entry-method form + hops stepper + Build / Force re-trace, owned by the new `useTraceAnchor(appId, entry, hops)` hook in `api/trace.ts` (idle / loading / missing / building / loaded / error state machine with ref-based stale-response guard mirroring `HookBuilder.renderInflightKeyRef`); populated `<BehaviorAnchorCard>` + `<DecisionTimeline>` + per-plan `<BypassPlanCard>` list with `<details>` "Advanced (N higher-risk plan(s))" expander wrapping `advanced_plans`; cached-anchors picker reading `GET /api/trace/{app_id}/anchors`; status footer fan-out unchanged from the 10.6 placeholder. **Wire-shape types tightened** in `api/trace.ts` — was `unknown[]` placeholders in 10.6; now `MethodRef` / `FieldRef` / `Branch` / `BranchVerdict` / `BranchOutcome` / `DecisionKind` / `DecisionPoint` / `BypassPlan` / `PredicateOrigin` (discriminated union over `kind`) all match `dataclasses.asdict` output verbatim. **Cross-mode plumbing in `WorkbenchContext.tsx`:** `LabMode` + `setLabMode` lifted out of `LabTab.tsx`'s local state (with `localStorage["androscan.lab.mode"]` persistence still owned in the context provider) so external callers (the trace components themselves; 10.8's Mirror "Trace this behaviour" button) can flip the mode programmatically; `pendingHookPrefill` + `setPendingHookPrefill` mirror the `pendingCodeNav` plumbing exactly — `BypassPlanCard` writes from Trace mode (template id + params dict + source label), `HookBuilder` consumes on mount/change and clears, `ts` field bumped on every `setPendingHookPrefill` so a stage→edit→stage-again sequence reliably re-fires. **`HookBuilder.tsx`** wired with the prefill consumer via a ref-based handoff (`stagedParamsRef`) so the existing template-change effect, which rebuilds `paramValues` from declared template defaults on `selectedTemplateId` change, drains the staged values *after* defaults rather than clobbering them; new "Staged from Trace" pill on the HookBuilder header drops the moment the operator picks a different template or edits any param. **`BypassPlanCard`'s "Stage in Manual Hooks"** button writes `pendingHookPrefill = {appId, templateId, params, sourceLabel: "Trace plan: <method>"}` AND calls `setLabMode("manual-hooks")` so the operator lands in HookBuilder with the form pre-populated and the mode visibly switched. **`BehaviorAnchorCard`** + **`PredicateOriginView`** (for `MethodCall` origins) write `pendingCodeNav` + flip the active tab to Inspect for cross-tab drill-in. ~430 lines of new CSS in `App.css` under `.trace-*` namespaces with full colour-token discipline (`var(--ok)` green for `allow`/low risk, `var(--err)` red for `deny`/high risk, `var(--warn)` yellow for medium risk + truncated banner, `var(--accent)` blue for the staged-from-Trace + LLM-refined pills, `color-mix(in srgb, ...)` for soft fills throughout). No new backend tests — the wire-shape contract is locked by 10.6's `tests/test_trace_routes.py`; no FE test runner per project convention. Full suite: **745 / 745 green** unchanged from 10.6. `npx tsc --noEmit` clean; `npm run build` clean — **709 modules** (was 703; +6 components + the hook), 1.19 MB JS / 74 KB CSS / 372 KB gzipped (vs 10.6's 1.18 MB / 63 KB / 367 KB). **What 10.7 explicitly does *not* ship:** Mirror → Trace cross-tab "Trace this behaviour" plumbing via `pendingTraceEntry` (10.8); new `trace` `ChatAttachment` kind for the Lab chat dock (10.8); auto-fetched per-decision source-snippets (deferred to v2 — operators click "Open in Inspect" on the anchor card today); per-decision LLM re-classification (still forbidden per DEC-024); free-form LLM JS for staged plans (still forbidden — staged plans round-trip through `frida_hooks.render_by_id` validation).
- 2026-04-28 Hook Lab call-graph: typeahead dropdown on filter input — three sections, ranked, keyboard-navigable: The "Filter package, class, or method…" input was opaque — operators typed and hoped the visual hide-by-substring picked up something useful, but had no preview of what was actually in the loaded graph and no way to jump directly to a hit without scrolling the cose-bilkent layout for it. New typeahead popover anchored under the input shows the top matches as the operator types, grouped into Packages / Classes / Methods sections (each capped at 25 by `SEARCH_HIT_LIMIT`). **Search ranker.** Pure function `searchGraph(graph, query, limit)` ranks each candidate by `scoreMatch`: 0 = exact, 100 = startsWith, 200 + match-offset = substring; ties broken first by label length, then alphabetically. Class hits score against both `cls.simple_name` and the dotted FQN (better of the two wins) so typing `MainActivity` catches `com.example.weakbank.MainActivity` (FQN substring) and the bare simple-name (prefix) and the bare form wins the tiebreak. Method hits score against both bare `method_name` and compound `Class.method_name` so `MainActivity.on` narrows to that activity's lifecycle methods even when `on` is a common substring across the whole graph. External nodes are skipped — the operator's filter input was for navigating in-app code; the existing **External** toggle covers rendering them. **Markup.** New `<SearchDropdown>` rendered inline after the input as `position: absolute; top: 100%; left: 0; right: 0`; the input is wrapped in a `position: relative` anchor div with a small chevron `▼/▲` button on its right edge that toggles the popover open/closed. Sections render with uppercase headers + match counts; rows show the label in monospace + a muted secondary line (`5 classes · 139 methods` for packages, dossier package for classes / methods). The active row is highlighted with an accent left-border + tinted background; operators see a small hint row at the bottom (`↑↓ navigate · ↵ open · esc close`). **Keyboard nav.** ↑/↓ walk the active index across the flat `flattenHits` order (packages → classes → methods); Enter activates the active row; Escape closes the popover; Tab is left to browser default so focus can escape. `aria-activedescendant` + `aria-controls` + `role="combobox"` / `role="listbox"` / `role="option"` so screen readers narrate it as a proper combobox; the ARIA wiring keeps focus on the `<input>` itself so typing remains uninterrupted. **Open/close logic.** `searchOpen` flips on whenever the filter goes non-empty *and* the input is focused; off when the filter clears, on Escape, on click-outside (a document-level `mousedown` listener attached only while open so we don't pay the listener cost during idle), or on a successful activation. Click-outside handler self-detaches when the popover closes. **Activation.** Click / Enter on a row drills into focus mode at the row's `firstNodeId` (same control flow as clicking the corresponding node in the graph) and clears the filter so the focus subgraph isn't immediately filtered down to just the typed substring; method rows additionally fire `onSelectNode(toSelected(node, klass))` so the right-pane decompile view opens at the right method. Mousedown (not click) is used so the input blur doesn't strip the popover before the activation handler runs. **Empty state.** When the query genuinely matches nothing in the loaded graph, a tiny pill renders below the input — and if the loaded graph is truncated (`graph.nodes.length < graph.total_nodes`, the 5000-node-cap case the App-only toggle was designed to mitigate), the pill calls that out explicitly with a hint to flip the toggle. **Tests.** No new tests — the ranker is a small pure function that's straightforward to read, no frontend test framework is set up yet (relies on `tsc --noEmit` + `vite build`); search behaviour is best evaluated on real graphs in dev. `tsc --noEmit` clean; `vite build` clean (1.16 MB / 363 KB gzipped — +7 KB raw / +2 KB gzip vs. yesterday for the ranker + dropdown component).
- 2026-04-28 Hook Lab call-graph: default-on "App only" toggle drops the bundled-library jungle from the package overview: An APK with even modest dependencies (Material + AndroidX + Kotlin + OkHttp) ships 200+ packages of compiled-in library code that the call-graph backend correctly classifies as `is_external=0` because the smali for them lives inside the apktool output trees — but the operator workflow almost always cares about the 2-3 packages they actually wrote. The frontend's `limit: 5000` page on `fetchGraph` made this worse: the smali parser walks the apktool trees roughly alphabetically (`androidx.` < `com.example.…`) and so the user's own classes get the highest `nodes.id` values; for `com.example.weakbank.low` (52,804 nodes total, 209 weakbank methods at IDs 46239–46685) the first 5000-node page contained zero weakbank nodes — typing `weakbank` into the existing filter input matched nothing because there was nothing to filter. **Frontend.** New shared util `androscan/web/frontend/src/util/appPackage.ts` lifts `isAppPackage(pkgName, appPackage)` + the `FRAMEWORK_PREFIXES` denylist (android./androidx./com.android./com.google./kotlin/kotlinx./org.jetbrains./okhttp3./okio./retrofit2./… — same set Inspect tab has used since DEC-022) out of `ClassMethodTree.tsx` so both the Inspect tree and the Hook Lab call-graph use one heuristic for "is this the app's own code". Adds a sibling helper `appPackagePrefix(appPackage)` that drops one segment (when there are 4+ segments) so a dossier package like `com.example.weakbank.low` widens to `com.example.weakbank` — sibling `.low` / `.medium` / `.high` build flavours of the same product share one overview, but bare `com` / `com.example` floors are never reached. `<CallGraphView>` gains an optional `appPackage: string \| null` prop wired from `<HookLabTab>` (`dossier?.apk_info?.package`) and a new toolbar checkbox **App only** between **External** and the focus-mode hops stepper, defaulting to ON whenever a dossier package is known. The toggle has a sticky-default useEffect (auto-flips ON the first time `appPackage` resolves async from null → "com.example.…"), a per-`appId` reset (each project gets its own fresh default), and a `userTouchedAppOnly` ref so a manual click is never overridden. When ON, the package-mode `fetchGraph` call passes `packagePrefix: appPackagePrefix(appPackage)` which the backend `list_graph` translates to `c.package LIKE 'com.example.weakbank%'` at SQL level — the response now contains all 209 weakbank methods regardless of the 5000-node cap, and `weakbank` in the filter input lights up the actual weakbank packages instead of an empty result. Toggle is disabled (with explanatory `title`) when no dossier package is known. **Backend.** No changes — `list_graph(... package_prefix=...)` and the `/api/graph/{app_id}?package_prefix=...` query parameter were already shipped in 4.1, with existing pytest coverage at `tests/test_graph_routes.py::test_list_route_with_package_prefix` and `tests/test_call_graph_index.py::test_fast_path_when_sha_matches`. **Tests.** No new tests — the relocated heuristic is verbatim and the backend wiring it consumes already has coverage; no frontend test framework is set up yet (relies on `tsc --noEmit` + `vite build`). `tsc --noEmit` clean; `vite build` clean (1.15 MB / 360 KB gzipped — essentially unchanged).
- 2026-04-28 Hook Lab call-graph: inverse-scale labels on zoom-in so dense library clusters become navigable: Cytoscape's wheel-zoom is "magnifier" by default — every visual element scales together with the camera, so at `maxZoom: 4` the screenshotted Material/AndroidX/Kotlin overview collapses to 2–3 enormous overlapping rectangles that don't help an operator navigate. New `useEffect` in `CallGraphView.tsx` (next to the cytoscape init effect) wires `cy.on("zoom", ...)` and applies an inverse factor `inv = 1 / Math.max(1, cy.zoom())` to every relevant per-element style: `font-size` (11 for `.pkgnode`, 10 for `.methodnode`, 8/9 for edges), `padding` (10/8 px), and `border-width` (1 baseline + 2 for `.reflective` / `.focusroot`, 3 for `.hit` / `.has-hits`). Net effect: when you zoom past 1×, on-screen label size stays roughly constant — the underlying cose-bilkent / dagre layout positions don't change, but each node's footprint in graph-coordinate space shrinks, so the gaps between rectangles translate to many more pixels and dense clusters spread out instead of collapsing to a few overlapping behemoths. Clamped at `inv <= 1` (only zoom-in is affected; zoom-out keeps today's "everything-scales-together" behavior — at `minZoom: 0.05` the whole graph is a smudge anyway and individual labels weren't useful at that scale). Per-element `cy.nodes(...).style({...})` (inline) is used in preference to rebuilding `cy.style()` because the stylesheet API appends selector entries on each call and would grow unboundedly across zoom events; `cy.batch()` coalesces the per-selector writes into one render; `requestAnimationFrame` debounces the burst of zoom events that fire during a smooth wheel scroll. The handler is idempotent and self-applies on mount, so the very first `cy.layout(...).run()` (which calls `fit:true` and triggers a zoom event) immediately picks up the right scaling. **No backend changes; no schema changes; no new tests** — pure-frontend visual change with no testable contract beyond "it doesn't crash" (which `tsc --noEmit` + `vite build` confirms; full Cytoscape behaviour is best evaluated on real graphs in dev). `vite build` clean (1.15 MB / 360 KB gzipped — essentially unchanged from yesterday's 1.15 MB / 358 KB).
- 2026-04-28 Call graph: surface SQLite extended error name in build-failure stamp: Tiny diagnostic patch for the Settings → Status call-graph card. The two `except Exception as e` blocks in `androscan/analysis/call_graph.py` (one in `build_index`, one in the `start_build_async._worker` thread) used to stamp `meta.error` as `f"{type(e).__name__}: {e}"`, which collapses every `SQLITE_IOERR_*` subtype (`FSYNC` / `WRITE` / `LOCK` / `SHMOPEN` / `CANTOPEN` / etc.) into the single opaque message `OperationalError: disk I/O error` — useless when triaging whether the actual cause was a Time Machine snapshot vs. an EDR scanner vs. a corrupt apktool tree. New helper `_format_build_error(e)` next to `_meta_set` reads `e.sqlite_errorname` / `e.sqlite_errorcode` (Python 3.11+; sqlite3 exception attributes added in [PEP-as-of-3.11](https://docs.python.org/3/library/sqlite3.html#sqlite3.Error.sqlite_errorcode)) via `getattr(...)` so older Pythons / non-SQLite exceptions degrade silently to the previous format. The bracketed suffix (e.g. `OperationalError: disk I/O error [SQLITE_IOERR_FSYNC]`) renders inline in the existing failure card without any frontend changes — the `meta.error` row already round-trips through `IndexStatus.error` and is shown verbatim. Both error sites now call the helper so the format stays in sync. The 2000-char truncation cap is preserved. **Tests.** `tests/test_call_graph_index.py` (+3 — `_format_build_error` includes `sqlite_errorname` on a real `OperationalError` instance from `sqlite3.connect("/no/such/dir")` (`SQLITE_CANTOPEN`), gracefully omits the suffix on non-SQLite `ValueError`, and the 2000-char ceiling clamps `RuntimeError("x" * 5000)`). Backend suite **585 / 585 green** (was 582; +3 net). No frontend or schema changes — purely improves diagnostic info on next failure; existing rows stamped under the old format keep their bare message until the next build attempt.
- 2026-04-28 Settings → Status: ABI-aware root-status warning on `frida-server` install playbook: Predicts whether `adb root` will succeed on the attached device and warns the operator before they paste step 4 — so a production / Google Play AVD can't surprise them with the `adbd cannot run as root in production builds` failure mode at the worst possible moment. **Backend.** New `probe_device_root_status` in `androscan/web/health_probes.py` reads `ro.build.type` + `ro.debuggable` + the default adb-shell uid via a single `;`-joined `getprop`+`id` shell roundtrip. Deliberately does **not** call `adb root` itself because that side-effects adbd and would tear down in-flight WebSockets (`/ws/mirror`, `/ws/logcat`, Frida trace WS). Returns `{ok, rooted, can_adb_root, current_uid, build_type, debuggable, error}` — `can_adb_root` rolls up the three signals into the one boolean the UI gates on; `rooted` (already uid 0) is its own field so the playbook can skip the `adb root` step entirely on Magisk-rooted / eng devices. `_ROOTABLE_BUILD_TYPES = frozenset({"userdebug", "eng"})` is the only build-type allowance — `user` (production / Google Play AVD images) refuses with the error above. Wired into `_gather_global` in parallel with the other adb probes; `tools.frida_server` card payload gains `device_rooted` / `can_adb_root` / `device_build_type` fields next to the existing ABI fields. **Frontend.** Type extension in `api/status.ts`. `<FridaServerInstallHint>` now renders three new affordances when the server isn't running: (a) a `device_build_type` line in the card extras so the AVD posture is visible at a glance; (b) a yellow `<frida-install-banner-warn>` block when `can_adb_root === false` explaining the failure mode (specific to `user` build vs. generic non-debuggable case) plus three remediations — recreate the AVD as AOSP / Google APIs userdebug, boot with `-writable-system` + Magisk, or use a rooted physical device; (c) a green `<frida-install-banner-ok>` block when `device_rooted === true`. Step 4 of the playbook elides the `adb root` line entirely when the device is already root (it's a no-op-with-noise on Magisk / eng builds). New CSS in `App.css` (`.frida-install-banner`, `.frida-install-banner-warn`, `.frida-install-banner-ok`). **Tests.** `tests/test_health_probes.py` (+7 — `user` build / `userdebug` build / `eng` build / already-root-shell / `userdebug` but `ro.debuggable=0` footgun / no-device / unparsable-getprop-output) plus `tests/test_settings_routes.py` (+2 — `userdebug` AVD posture, no-device-root null-fields path) and the contract test extended to lock `device_rooted` / `can_adb_root` / `device_build_type` into the contract. Default fixture stub returns the production / Google Play AVD posture (`build=user`, uid=2000) — mirrors the most common operator setup and exercises the warning path through the contract test. **Backend suite 582 / 582 green** (was 573; +9 net). `tsc --noEmit` + `vite build` clean. **Safety posture (`docs/SAFETY_AND_SECURITY.md` updated):** the root-status probe is a pure read; the install playbook remains operator guidance only — synthesised commands are rendered as copy-pasteable `<code>` blocks, never executed by the workbench.
- 2026-04-28 Settings → Status: copy-icon button on `frida-server` install playbook command lines: Tiny UX polish on the install playbook shipped the day before — the per-command "copy" / "copied" text button on every install step (`<FridaInstallCmd>` in `SettingsTab.tsx`) is now an icon button matching the affordance GitHub / Cursor / VS Code use on inline code blocks, so operators recognise it without a tooltip. New `IconCopy` (two overlapping rounded rectangles) and `IconCheck` glyphs added to the shared `androscan/web/frontend/src/components/Icons.tsx` set, both inheriting the existing 16×16 viewBox + `currentColor` stroke conventions so they tint with the rest of the icon row. The button itself was reshaped from a text pill into a 22×22 square via `.frida-install-copy-btn` CSS rework; the post-copy `.copied` state turns green for 1.2 s before reverting. `title` + `aria-label` carry the human-readable label so screen readers and tooltips still work. No backend changes; no new tests (pure visual polish — the existing `<FridaInstallCmd>` clipboard + state behaviour is unchanged). `tsc --noEmit` + `vite build` clean.
- 2026-04-27 Settings → Status: ABI-aware `frida-server` install playbook on Hook Lab readiness card: When the Hook Lab readiness card (`tools.frida_server` in `/api/status/global`) reports the device-side server is not running, the Settings tab now renders an inline install playbook with the exact download URL + push / install / verify commands the operator needs — synthesised from the device's reported ABI and the host frida CLI version. **Backend.** New `probe_device_cpu_abi` in `androscan/web/health_probes.py` runs `adb shell getprop ro.product.cpu.abi` (1 s timeout) and maps the result through a fixed `_ABI_TO_FRIDA_ARCH` table (`arm64-v8a → android-arm64`, `armeabi-v7a / armeabi → android-arm`, `x86_64 → android-x86_64`, `x86 → android-x86`); returns `{ok, abi, frida_arch, error}` and is gracefully degraded for unmapped ABIs (`abi` surfaced, `frida_arch` is None, error explains). Wired into `_gather_global` in parallel with the other adb probes via `asyncio.gather` (no extra latency on the common path; cheap when the device is offline thanks to `SHORT_TIMEOUT_SEC`). The `tools.frida_server` card payload gains `device_abi` + `frida_arch` fields next to the existing `host_version` / `device_version` / `version_skew`. **Frontend.** `androscan/web/frontend/src/api/status.ts` `GlobalStatus.tools.frida_server` type extended with the two new fields. New `<FridaServerStatusCard>` in `SettingsTab.tsx` wraps the generic `<StatusCardView>`; when `running === false` it appends a collapsible `<details>` block (`<FridaServerInstallHint>`) listing the synthesised filename (`frida-server-${host_version}-android-${frida_arch}.xz`), the canonical GitHub release download URL (`https://github.com/frida/frida/releases/download/${host_version}/${filename}`), and 5 numbered steps with copy-paste affordances: `curl -L -o … ${URL}` → `xz -d …` → `adb push … /data/local/tmp/frida-server` → `adb root` + `chmod 755` + background-fork start → `adb shell pidof frida-server`. Each command is rendered with a tiny `<FridaInstallCmd>` click-to-copy button (`navigator.clipboard.writeText`, "copied" flash for 1.2 s). Degrades gracefully when host version or `frida_arch` is missing — links to the releases page and emits a placeholder filename instead of a broken URL. New CSS in `App.css` (`.frida-install-hint`, `.frida-install-cmd`, `.frida-install-copy-btn`, etc.). **Tests.** `tests/test_health_probes.py` (+5 — arm64 / x86_64 / armv7 happy paths, unknown ABI surfaces but doesn't claim ok, no-device error path); `tests/test_settings_routes.py` (+2 — unmapped-ABI degrade path, no-device-attached null-fields path) plus the existing `test_global_status_exposes_frida_server_card_contract` extended to lock the new `device_abi` / `frida_arch` keys into the contract. Default fixture stub returns `arm64-v8a → android-arm64` so the contract test exercises the populated path. **Backend suite 573 / 573 green** (was 566; +7 net). `tsc --noEmit` + `vite build` clean (1.15 MB / 359 KB gzipped — +1 KB CSS for the playbook, +3 KB JS).
- 2026-04-27 Settings → Status: per-app Call graph card with build/rebuild action: Pure-frontend follow-up to Hook Lab v1 — the per-app status payload already carried `call_graph` (built by `_call_graph_card` in `androscan/web/status_routes.py` and threaded next to `_rag_card` since 4.1) and the rebuild route already existed (`POST /api/graph/{app_id}/rebuild?drop_apktool=`), but the Settings → Status panel never rendered the card. Now does. `androscan/web/frontend/src/api/status.ts` `AppStatus` gains a `call_graph` field typed against `IndexStatus.to_dict()` (`status: "missing" | "pending" | "ready" | "failed"` plus `node_count` / `edge_count` / `class_count` / `external_class_count` / `fidelity_level` / `parser_version` / `built_at` / `finished_at` / `db_path` / `sha`). New `<CallGraphStatusCard>` in `SettingsTab.tsx` clones the `<RagStatusCard>` pattern: same decompile-readiness gate, same status-driven button labels (`Build now` / `Retry build` / `Building…` / `Rebuild`), same kicked-vs-already-running message handling — plus a secondary **Re-decompile** button (only shown once `status === "ready"`) that calls `rebuildGraph(appId, { dropApktool: true })` to drop the apktool/Smali cache and re-parse from scratch (useful after replacing the APK or upgrading apktool). When the card reports `ready`, extras list `N nodes / M edges`, `K classes (E external)`, and `fidelity: <level>` so operators see at a glance whether the v2 dispatch resolution actually ran. Auto-build via `schedule_call_graph_build_after_decompile` still wins the race in the common case; the button mostly serves the "force a rebuild after fiddling with apktool / parser" workflow. **No backend changes.** No new tests (parallel to how the RAG card landed — frontend-only render of an already-tested status payload). `tsc --noEmit` + `vite build` clean (1.15 MB / 358 KB gzipped — basically unchanged from 4.8). Backend suite **566 / 566 green** unchanged.
- 2026-04-27 Hook Lab 4.8 — Frida overlay on call graph + Hook Lab v1 docs sweep (sub-step 8 of 8 — **Hook Lab v1 complete**): Pure-frontend overlay layering live Frida hits onto the existing Cytoscape call-graph pane plus the final v1 docs sweep. `androscan/web/frontend/src/components/CallGraphView.tsx` accepts a new optional `hitsByMethod: ReadonlyMap<string, number> | null` prop (also exports `hitKey(className, methodName)` so producer + consumer share one canonical key). `null` → graph renders unchanged (no session pinned); `Map` (possibly empty) → method nodes whose `(class.class_name, method_name)` matches a key render in bold cyan per DEC-023 with a `×N` hit-count suffix on the label and a `frida: N hit(s)` line in the tippy tooltip; non-matching nodes dim to opacity 0.45. Package-overview mode aggregates per-package totals (`• N hits in M methods` footer + bold-cyan treatment when any contained method fired; opacity 0.5 when zero hits). Reflection / focus-root styling still applies — `.hit` / `.has-hits` are declared after the reflective / focusroot rules so the bold-cyan border wins where both overlap (DEC-023 semantics: "did it fire?" beats "is it reflective?" the moment a hook lands). New `HIT_CYAN = "#56d4dd"` colour token shared between the Cytoscape stylesheet and the tooltip HTML. `androscan/web/frontend/src/tabs/HookLabTab.tsx` builds `hitsByMethod` via `useMemo` from the same `chatHooks` it already polls every 2.5 s for the chat `frida_summary` attachment — no new poll cadence, no new endpoint — so the overlay, the right-pane HookStatsPanel / ScopeInspectorPanel, and the chat attachments all refresh cohesively. Empty-but-active overlay (session pinned, no hits yet) deliberately renders dimmed rather than hiding so operators see the wire is live before events flow. **No new tests** — pure-frontend visual change. Backend suite **566 / 566 green** unchanged. `tsc --noEmit` + `vite build` clean (1.14 MB / 358 KB gzipped — unchanged from 4.7). **Final docs sweep:** `TASKS.md` 4.8 row marked `[x] DONE` and the **Hook Lab v1 — sub-step backlog** flipped to "v1 complete (2026-04-27)"; `DESIGN_DOC.md` Phases 8 + 9 marked landed-in-Hook-Lab-v1; `DECISIONS.md` DEC-023 gets a "Frida overlay (sub-step 4.8 specifics)" sub-bullet + a "Hook Lab v1 complete" closing note; `KNOWN_ISSUES.md` LIMIT-002 marked Resolved + new ISSUE-012 documenting the per-overload precision caveat (hooks aggregate by `(class, method)` so overloaded methods undercount on the overlay); `SAFETY_AND_SECURITY.md` §12.6 augmented with a "v1 complete: what now-shipped controls do" subsection consolidating the operator-managed `frida-server` posture + the now-enforced per-app `hook_target_package_prefix` server-side allowlist + the trace-persistence path at `apps/<app_id>/<run_ts>/frida/<session>.jsonl`. **Hook Lab v1 ships:** Smali static call graph + REST + Cytoscape pane + click-to-source + headless Frida adapter + 6 hook templates + Stage→Inject UI with `pyjsparser` pre-validation + WS trace + JSONL persistence + per-app prefix allowlist + hooks-stats + scope-inspector panels + `query_call_graph` / `generate_frida_hook` LLM-tier skills + chat attachments + `requires_confirmation` consent class + live Frida hits coloured onto the graph. **Out of scope for v1:** modify-return mutations (v2), self-hosted Monaco (ISSUE-010 v2 sweep), `frida-server` auto-provisioning (operator-managed by design), the bounded chat agentic loop that consumes the new LLM skills (DEC-022 / ISSUE-009 — structurally ready, chat-side wiring is the next layer's job).
- 2026-04-27 Hook Lab 4.4 — Hook template library (sub-step 4 of 8): New `androscan/adapters/frida_hooks/` package shipping the v1 set of parametrised Frida hook templates that 4.5's Inject UI and 4.7's LLM-tier `generate_frida_hook` skill will consume. Five templates — `entry_exit_log` (single-method entry/exit logger, 3 params: class/method/label), `ssl_pinning_bypass` (multi-strategy: Conscrypt `TrustManagerImpl` + OkHttp3 `CertificatePinner` + generic `X509TrustManager`, 1 param: label), `crypto` (`javax.crypto.Cipher` init/doFinal observer; in/out lengths only, NOT plaintext bytes — that's an explicit consent gate left to hand-rolled scripts; 1 param: label), `shared_preferences` (getString/putString watcher with key-prefix filter; 2 params: label + optional `key_prefix=""`), `intent` (`ContextWrapper.startActivity/startService/sendBroadcast` with action/data/component/package capture; 1 param: label) — each as a single Python module exporting `TEMPLATE: HookTemplate` (a frozen dataclass bundling id / name / description / `params: tuple[HookTemplateParam, ...]` / `js_template: str` / `pentester_summary_template: str` / informational `sensitive_apis: tuple[str, ...]`). Both bodies use Python `str.format` placeholders against the same merged params dict; literal JS braces are escaped via `{{` / `}}` (the contract is enforced indirectly by the registry-walk render test — any unescaped brace surfaces as a `KeyError` during `str.format`). Renderer in `__init__.py`: `render(template, params) -> RenderedHook(template_id, js, summary, params_used)` validates in three explicit stages (reject unknown keys → reject missing / empty / `None` for required → fill defaults for optional), then coerces every value to `str` (Frida JS is text; typed validators are deferred to v2 along with 4.7's LLM-side schema-aware fill). `render_by_id` / `get_template` / `list_templates` / `register` / `discover` / `extract_format_fields` round out the public API; explicit `_TEMPLATE_MODULES` tuple drives discovery (mirrors `androscan.skills` — auto-globbing rejected because a stray scratch file would silently become a registered template). Errors live in a thin taxonomy: `HookTemplateError` (base), `HookTemplateNotFound` (unknown id; message lists valid ids alongside), `HookParamError` (one class for missing-required / empty-required / unknown-key — the consumers want one `except` block plus a human-readable reason). New `tests/test_frida_hook_templates.py` (**+67 tests**) — `TestRenderer` (renderer contract end-to-end against a hand-rolled fixture template: happy path, all error variants, default fill, value coercion, `{{` literal-brace escape, `extract_format_fields` named-only / skip-positional / strip-attribute), `TestRegistryFailClosed` (the DEC-023 fail-closed promise: parametrised over every module in `_TEMPLATE_MODULES` — module exports `TEMPLATE: HookTemplate`, `TEMPLATE.id` matches module basename, JS + summary non-empty, both `str.format` placeholder sets ⊆ declared params, every required param appears in JS *or* summary, every template renders cleanly with stub inputs), `TestTemplates` (loose substring assertions per shipped template — class/method/label substituted; summary mentions the right pentest concept — auth tokens for SharedPreferences, MITM/Burp for SSL pinning, plaintext-not-captured for crypto, deep-link / extras for intent). Adding a new template is now a strict two-deliverable change (JS body + non-empty summary); a stub or missing summary fails the suite at the registry walk. Full suite: **458 / 458 green** (was 391; +67 tests). `tsc --noEmit` + `vite build` clean (no FE changes in 4.4). **Headless by design** — no UI, no HTTP routes (4.5 owns Stage→Inject + WS + JSONL persistence; 4.7 owns the `generate_frida_hook` LLM skill that fills these templates); the `RenderedHook` shape is now the locked contract both layers will consume.
- 2026-04-27 Hook Lab 4.3 — Frida adapter foundation (sub-step 3 of 8): New `androscan/adapters/` package (`__init__.py`, `frida_client.py`) standing up a class-based, headless Frida adapter that 4.4–4.7 will consume. `FridaClient` (one per workbench process; lazy `frida` import via the `_frida_python()` test seam; raises `FridaUnavailableError` with a copy-pasteable install hint when the optional dep is missing) holds the device handle and the dict of live `FridaSession`s; `FridaSession` (one per attach) wraps a single `frida.core.Session`, exposes `load_script(js, name)` / `detach()` / `stats()`, and carries a thread-safe `collections.deque(maxlen=ring_size)` of `TraceEvent`s populated from the Frida message thread under a `threading.Lock`. `TraceEvent` mirrors Frida's native `send` / `error` / `log` kinds and keeps the unmodified `raw` dict so 4.5's WebSocket and 4.7's LLM skill can read fields we haven't promoted yet without a schema change. Optional `on_event` callback fires *after* the event lands in the ring so the future WS UI can never race ahead of `stats()`. `detach_all()` is invoked on uvicorn shutdown via a guarded `@app.on_event("shutdown")` handler in `androscan/web/app.py` (no-op when `[frida]` isn't installed). `get_frida_client(app, config)` lazily constructs and caches on `app.state.frida_client` so tests can swap implementations. New `frida_trace_ring_buffer_size` config knob (default 5000, clamped `>= 100`) wired through `Config` / `CONFIG_FIELD_MAP` / `LIVE_RELOADABLE_FIELDS` / `_merge_from_yaml` / `ANDROSCAN_FRIDA_TRACE_RING` env override / `global_config.yaml` (`frida.trace_ring_buffer_size`). Two new health probes in `androscan/web/health_probes.py`: `probe_frida_server` (`adb shell pidof frida-server`; returns `{ok, running, pid, error}`) and `probe_frida_version_skew` (compares host `frida` CLI version with `frida-server --version` on device; tags severity as `None` / `"minor"` / `"major"`). `androscan/web/status_routes.py` aggregates both into a new single `tools.frida_server` card via `asyncio.gather` (next to the existing `tools.frida` host-CLI card). Frontend: `GlobalStatus.tools.frida_server` typed in `src/api/status.ts` (`{ok, label, running, pid, host_version, device_version, version_skew, error}`); `rollupGlobal` treats Frida readiness as **yellow** (non-critical) since static workflows don't need it; one new `<StatusCardView>` line in `SettingsTab.tsx` renders the card next to the host CLI card. New `[frida]` `pyproject.toml` extra (`frida>=16`, `frida-tools>=12`, `pyjsparser>=2.7`); `--setup` now installs `.[dev,rag,frida]` by default. New `device` pytest marker registered in `pyproject.toml` so 4.4–4.7 can opt into real-device runs without a churn commit; default `pytest -q` deselect is still all-tests-on. Tests: new `tests/test_frida_client.py` (attach / load_script / on_message ordering / ring eviction with `dropped` counter / detach / `detach_all` idempotence / `FridaUnavailableError` / concurrency / `get_frida_client` caching — all via a stub `frida` module installed via `monkeypatch.setattr` on the `_frida_python` seam); `tests/test_health_probes.py` extended with subprocess-monkeypatch cases for `probe_frida_server` (running / not running / adb-unreachable / multi-pid) and `probe_frida_version_skew` (no-skew / minor / major / device-unreachable); `tests/test_settings_routes.py` extended with a field-shape contract test for `tools.frida_server` in `/api/status/global` (locks the frontend contract) plus a "running with major skew" case; `tests/test_config.py` extended with parse-from-YAML / env-override / default / clamp-to-≥100 cases for `frida_trace_ring_buffer_size`. **391 / 391 tests green** (was 357 — +34 tests). `tsc --noEmit` + `vite build` clean (1.1 MB JS bundle unchanged). DEC-023 promoted Proposed → Active in this commit; DEC-021's "Hook Lab readiness rollup probe" follow-up ticked as fulfilled by the two-card design. Sub-step 4.3 is **headless by design** — no HTTP routes, no Hook Lab tab UI changes (just the Settings → Status readiness signal); 4.4 adds the template library and 4.5 lights up the Inject UI + WS + JSONL persistence.
- 2026-04-27 Hook Lab 4.2 — Hook Lab UI Graph pane (sub-step 2 of 8): New Cytoscape.js viewer at `androscan/web/frontend/src/components/CallGraphView.tsx` (~1.3K LOC) backed by typed client `androscan/web/frontend/src/api/graph.ts` (5 endpoint wrappers + node / class / edge / status types). Two view modes — **package overview** (default, `cose-bilkent` layout, cross-package edges weighted by aggregated call count) and **focus subgraph** (right-click → 1–6 hop dagre LR; multi-hop fan-out is expanded client-side off `/neighbors` with a per-depth budget so a hub-star like `Log.d` can't melt the request count). Edge styling honours DEC-023: `direct` / `static` / `super` solid, `virtual_dispatch` / `interface_dispatch` / `external` dashed (external also dimmed); `may_have_unresolved_reflection` nodes outlined in orange with an `[R]` suffix on the label. Tippy.js node tooltips (full method signature + access flags + reflection note) using `cytoscape-popper` + `tippy.js@6` with a workbench-themed `androscan-graph` Tippy theme. Inline filter (live-hides non-matching nodes), hidden-by-default external-node toggle, `Package overview` reset button, `Rebuild` button hits `POST /api/graph/{app_id}/rebuild`. Click a method node → in-tab `HookLabCodeView` loads the Java file via existing `/api/code/{app_id}/file` and renders `CodeView` with `emphasizeMethod` (no exact line — call graph stores Smali lines, not Java; method emphasis carries the location signal per DEC-023). Right-click context menu has its own hops stepper + "Focus subgraph here" + **"Open in Inspect"** which pumps a `pendingCodeNav` through new `WorkbenchContext` plumbing (`{appId, relPath, className, method?, ts}`); `InspectTab` consumes it via the existing `handleSelect` flow with a single new `useEffect`, then clears it. New helper `androscan/web/frontend/src/util/smaliClassToFile.ts` maps `com.example.Foo$Inner` → `com/example/Foo.java`. Bundle size: 422 KB → 1.1 MB (warned, expected; matches DEC-023's "rich tooltip set" choice). Frontend contract locked by 3 new field-shape tests in `tests/test_graph_routes.py` (list / status / neighbors). `tsc --noEmit` + `vite build` clean. - 2026-04-27 Hook Lab 4.1 — Smali call-graph backend (sub-step 1 of 8): New `androscan/analysis/` package (`apktool_runner`, `smali_parser`, `dispatch`, `smali_types`, `call_graph`). Two-pass lexical Smali parser + fidelity-v2 virtual/interface dispatch resolver (BFS over class hierarchy, bounded by `MAX_OVERRIDES_PER_INVOKE=64` with a `truncated` flag on edges). Per-app SQLite at `apps/<app_id>/.decompiled/<sha>/call_graph.sqlite` (schema_version 1 — `classes` / `class_interfaces` / `nodes` / `edges` with INTEGER FKs; external callees materialised as `is_external=1` rows so neighbour queries are uniform; locked edge-kind enum `direct | static | super | virtual_dispatch | interface_dispatch | external`; reflection-heavy methods flagged via `nodes.may_have_unresolved_reflection`). Mirrors the RAG index's operational patterns: WAL + autocommit, `meta` table, in-process `_RUNNING` registry with orphan-pending recovery after `PENDING_GRACE_SEC=30s`, atomic snapshot writes. New routes in `androscan/web/graph_routes.py`: `GET /api/graph/{app_id}/status` (+ `verbose=true` for raw meta), `POST /api/graph/{app_id}/rebuild` (+ `drop_apktool=true`), `GET /api/graph/{app_id}` (paginated nodes + intra-subgraph edges, `package_prefix` / `kind` / `include_external` filters, hard cap 5000), `GET /api/graph/{app_id}/neighbors/{node_ref:path}` (callers + callees, accepts numeric id *or* URL-encoded smali signature), `GET /api/graph/{app_id}/paths` (bounded BFS, `max_hops≤12`, `max_paths≤50`). Auto-build hook (`schedule_call_graph_build_after_decompile`) fires after `/api/decompile` success, parallel to the RAG hook. New per-app status card `call_graph` in `status_routes` sits next to `rag`. New `_call_graph_card` helper + router wiring in `androscan/web/app.py`; apktool already probed globally. **57 new tests** in `tests/test_call_graph_parser.py` (15), `tests/test_call_graph_dispatch.py` (14), `tests/test_call_graph_index.py` (15), `tests/test_graph_routes.py` (14) over fixtures at `tests/fixtures/call_graph_smali/` (base + 2 subclasses, interface + impl, reflection callsite, multi-dex `smali_classes2/`). Full suite: **354 / 354 green** (was 297).
- 2026-04 RAG status orphan-detection: `androscan.rag.index.get_status` now reclassifies a `status=pending` row as `failed` when no live worker is registered for `(cache_dir, sha)` *and* the row is older than `PENDING_GRACE_SEC` (30 s). This fixes the "Building…" forever stuck state on the Settings → Status RAG card after a uvicorn restart or worker crash mid-build (the registry is process-local, so a restart leaves the DB in `pending` with no thread to ever finish it). New `is_build_running(cache_dir, sha)` helper exposes the registry check without leaking internals. Frontend needed no change — the existing `RagStatusCard` already shows a "Retry build" button (warn-styled) when `status === "failed"`. New tests: `test_status_pending_within_grace_stays_pending`, `test_status_pending_orphaned_after_grace_becomes_failed`, `test_status_pending_with_live_worker_stays_pending` (18 RAG tests total). Also fixed long-standing docs drift in `STATE.md`/`TASKS.md`/`DECISIONS.md`/`ARCHITECTURE.md`/`SAFETY_AND_SECURITY.md` where the RAG SQLite path was incorrectly written as `apps/<app_id>/decompile_cache/<sha>/rag.sqlite` (correct: `apps/<app_id>/.decompiled/<sha>/rag.sqlite`).

Backend modules: `androscan/web/health_probes.py` (timeboxed async pure-function probes; `asyncio.gather` keeps fan-out latency ≈ slowest probe), `androscan/web/per_app_settings.py` (atomic `apps/<app_id>/app_settings.json` writes, schema-versioned, never touches `app_meta.json`), `androscan/web/status_routes.py` (3 s in-process cache, invalidated on every settings save/reset/reload), `androscan/web/settings_routes.py` (validates raw YAML server-side, atomic write-back via tempfile + `os.replace`, returns `restart_required` per saved field). `androscan/config/loader.py` grew `CONFIG_FIELD_MAP` (Config field → yaml section/key/env), `LIVE_RELOADABLE_FIELDS` (booleans), `global_view_from_config`, `effective_sources`, `with_overrides`, `dump_to_yaml`, `save_raw_yaml`, `restore_defaults_yaml`, `validate_raw_yaml`, `read_raw_yaml`, `discover_config_path`. `app.py` now stores config on `app.state.config` with `_current_config`/`_set_config` helpers; new routers read via callable providers so live-reloadable fields take effect without restarting uvicorn. New sub-components in `ElementMappingPanel.tsx`: `BestBanner` (regex/RAG `SourceBadge`, kind pill, score, reasons grid, snippet, "Open in Code browser"), `ResolutionAlternatives` (collapsible details with per-alt source badge + score + open button), `RagHitsList` (collapsible with the synthesised RAG query and per-hit class.method label). `InspectTab.handleTap` now prefers `resolution.best` over the raw first deterministic candidate when auto-opening the Code Browser, and seeds `scrollTarget` + `highlightRange` so the viewer jumps to the picked line range. Chat attachments gain a `best_handler` JSON block (file, line, class, method, source, score, reasons) so the LLM sees the fused pick alongside the element + candidate list. - 2026-04 Phase 6 polish (UX step 3, partial — `resolve_ui_element` LLM skill + click-to-code fusion): New `llm`-tier skill `androscan/skills/resolve_ui_element.py` exposing a deterministic, explainable scorer over the raw click-to-code output (element + foreground activity + handler-grep candidates). Score model: handler-kind base (`findViewById` 1.00 > `onClick_near` 0.80 > `compose_id` 0.60 > `reference` 0.20 > rag-cosine), additive bonuses for foreground-activity match (+0.50), activity-named files (+0.10), and an early-line decay bonus (≤+0.10 for line 1, → 0 at line 200). Optional Lane-1 RAG enrichment: synthesises a free-text query from `text` > `content_desc` > short resource id and fetches top-k chunks; RAG hits feed back into the same scorer so a clean `findViewById` in the foreground activity always beats an arbitrary semantic match. The pure-function `resolve()` helper is reused inline by `POST /api/inspect/map`, which now returns a `resolution` block ({`best`, `alternatives`, `rag_hits`, `reasoning`}) so the UI gets one ranked answer without an LLM round-trip. Skill is fail-soft: missing app dir, missing decompile cache, or missing embed provider all return `success=True` with a clean note. Registered in `androscan/skills/__init__.py` so the LLM planner sees it in the catalog. New tests: `tests/test_resolve_ui_element.py` (13 tests — pure helpers, deterministic scoring, foreground boost, early-line tie-break, RAG enrichment with `HashProvider`, fail-soft, registry path); `tests/test_web.py::test_api_inspect_map_returns_element_and_candidates` extended to assert the new `resolution` block. **210 tests total, all passing.** Frontend Inspect-tab wiring (mirror with status badge, click-to-map UX, Monaco viewer, scoped logcat panel) remains pending.

**Historical context (kept for the as-built record — was the active framing at LCP.0 ratification):** Parallel infrastructure track on `main`, not a Phase, closest precedent CI.0. LCP.0 (2026-05-06, commit `52e4f8e`) ratified **DEC-027** locking the seven-sub-step backlog LCP.0 → LCP.6 (strictly linear, one sub-step per Agent-mode session). **Scope:** swap the local LLM runtime from Ollama (`qwen3.5:35b` heavy on a 36 GB M3 unified-memory budget per the operator's 2026-05-05 status share) to **llama.cpp** (specifically `llama-server`'s OpenAI-compatible HTTP shim) — operator runs `llama-server -c 16384 -ngl 99 -fa --port 8033 --host 127.0.0.1 --jinja` against an UD-Q5_K_XL / Q5_K_M Qwen3-family GGUF; AndroScan consumes `http://127.0.0.1:8033/v1/chat/completions` via a new dedicated `_call_llamacpp()` HTTP path parallel to `_call_ollama()`. **Locked decisions per DEC-027 Q1-Q5:** Q1 (B + table refactor) — dedicated `_call_llamacpp()` codepath using `requests` directly (NOT extending `_call_cloud()`'s `openai` SDK path) + restructure `CLOUD_PROVIDERS` → top-level `LLM_PROVIDERS` dict with `LLM_PROVIDERS["local"]` (containing `"ollama"` + `"llamacpp"`) and `LLM_PROVIDERS["cloud"]` (containing the existing 6 cloud providers verbatim) sub-sections + per-entry `kind` field + new `Config.provider_kind() -> Literal[...]` method (`is_cloud()` preserved as a backwards-compat alias) + backwards-compat `CLOUD_PROVIDERS = LLM_PROVIDERS["cloud"]` alias; Q2 (a) — defer GBNF to LCP.6 but **promote LCP.6 from optional → committed** follow-up benefiting both local providers; Q3 (B) — Settings UI top-level radio "Local (Ollama)" / "Local (llama.cpp)" / "Cloud" with nested cloud dropdown (LCP.4 may split into 4a/4b if diff balloons); Q4 (A) — default `base_url: http://127.0.0.1:8033/v1` (loopback-explicit per DEC-006 / DEC-021 lineage; port `8033` operator-chosen non-default); Q5 (C) — README pointer only at LCP.5, no `docs/LLAMACPP.md`. **Branch posture:** ship on `main` with selective-staging discipline (rejected feature-branch alternative). **Sub-step backlog:** LCP.0 (this commit, docs only); LCP.1 (`LLM_PROVIDERS` table refactor — pure refactor); LCP.2 (`_call_llamacpp()` + router branch); LCP.3 (`probe_llamacpp` + Status panel block); LCP.4 (Settings UI radio restructure + llama.cpp sub-knobs); LCP.5 (docs sweep + DEC-027 closing note + KNOWN_ISSUES GBNF deferral entry + README pointer); LCP.6 (committed GBNF grammar enforcement follow-up). See `docs/TASKS.md` § **LCP — llama.cpp local provider — sub-step backlog** for the deliverable table + DEC-027 for full Q1-Q5 lock-in record.

**Phase 12 — Behavior Trace v3 / cross-tab follow-ups planning checkpoint (12.0) is deferred-pending-telemetry as of 2026-05-06.** Was Active 2026-05-05 (v2.1.6 wrap-up promotion); demoted to deferred-pending-telemetry when LCP track started, since Phase 12 / DEC-026 ratification has been gated all along on real-app telemetry from Phase 11 v2 + v2.1 use that hasn't accrued — orthogonal to LCP track which runs at the infrastructure layer. Phase 12 promotes back to in-flight Active when telemetry accrues, regardless of where LCP track is at that point. v3 candidate menu (carried forward from DEC-025's "Explicitly deferred to v3 / Phase 12" list — capture as a v3 follow-up DEC when telemetry justifies one):
- ISSUE-014 — per-overload precision on two-register comparisons (schema bump on `DecisionPoint.predicate_origin` from a single field to a tuple; doubles per-anchor card count for two-register-heavy methods, so ships with a UI density review).
- Auto-verify bypass after injection (Mirror programmatically re-triggers the anchor + diffs the resulting UI state — large Mirror integration cost; defers until measured demand).
- Free-form LLM JS for bypass plans via the existing `generate_frida_hook` consent class (DEC-024's "alternatives considered" column flagged this for v2 telemetry — Phase 11 v2's three locked templates `force_return_value` / `force_method_skip` / `force_string_compare_equal` need to leave a measurable gap on real apps before this is worth doing).
- Per-app TTL on cached anchors (current invalidation is "drop the file" + the schema-bump-driven wipe from DEC-025; operator hasn't asked for TTL).
- iOS / WebView / native-binary `BehaviorAnchor` adapters (DEC-024's platform-neutral data model is observably held in v1 + v2, so the contract is locked; operator demand is the gate, v1 + v2 ship Android only).
- Smali patching as a bypass technique (different workflow class — apktool re-encode → `jarsigner` → install + verify; deserves its own design conversation).
- LLM-budget real-app measurement follow-up (carried over from 11.6 / Q1 (A) deferral — measure actual prompt + output token usage on dogfood-app traces freshly rebuilt under v2 slicer; the calculus may simplify or drop entirely once LCP track lands a llama.cpp runtime where `--ctx-size` is operator-set and behaves predictably; document the outcome as a 12.x sub-bullet so v3 contributors know the local-LLM context-window posture).

**Parallel non-blocking thread:** **CI.0 — Phase 4 CI** (pytest on push/PR). Long-deferred from the original Phase 4 backlog; promoted to Active by DEC-025 in service of the Phase 11 slicer changes but ultimately shipped *after* Phase 11 v2 (the suite passed locally throughout 11.x and across the v2.1 follow-up release, and operator's "Lab engine before CI" direction (2026-04-30) deprioritized it relative to v3 work). **Currently deprioritized** — promote when Lab engine reaches a stable v3 baseline. Standalone item — not coupled to the LCP track sub-step ordering or the 12.x sub-step ordering. See `docs/TASKS.md` § **Phase 4 CI — sub-step backlog** for the deliverable.

## How to use this document

- Use this file for current reality
- Use `docs/TASKS.md` for active work
- Use `docs/PROJECT_BRIEF.md` for purpose
- Use `docs/ARCHITECTURE.md` for deeper structural intent
- Use `docs/DESIGN_DOC.md` for full target-state design

If this file and the code disagree, either:
- the file is stale, or
- the code drifted from the intended path

Do not ignore the mismatch.
